       ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.
       CONFIGURATION SECTION.   
       SOURCE-COMPUTER. UNISYS-2200.
       OBJECT-COMPUTER. UNISYS-2200 MEMORY 3 MODULES.   
       SPECIAL-NAMES.   
               PRINTER IS PRINTER   
185674         PRINTER IS PRT   
               CONSOLE IS CONSOLE.  

       INPUT-OUTPUT SECTION.
       FILE-CONTROL.
           SELECT INPUT-FILE          ASSIGN TO DISC    IN-FILE.
           SELECT OUTPUT-FILE         ASSIGN TO DISC    OUT-FILE.   

       DATA DIVISION.   
       SUBSCHEMA SECTION.   
       INVOKE SUBSCHEMA PRD100UCOBSU
              IN FILE SUBSCHEMAS OF SCHEMA PRD100SCHEMA 
              COPYING RECORDS INTO COMMON   
              COPYING DATA-NAMES INTO COMMON
              OVERLAY PAX-REC WITH PAX-CXF-REC  
                                   PAX-VI-REC   
              SAVE DATA            INCLUDES DEFERRED-UPDATES
              DMCA  COMMON  
              ERROR RECOVERY IS 9100-DATABASE-ERROR 
              ROLLBACK IS 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.  

       FILE SECTION.
       FD  INPUT-FILE   
           LABEL RECORDS ARE STANDARD.  
       01  INPUT-REC                                   PIC X(80).   

       FD  OUTPUT-FILE  
           LABEL RECORDS ARE STANDARD.  
       01  OUTPUT-REC                                  PIC X(150).

       COPY DPP01INT.

           PERFORM DPP12-MCB-INIT THRU DPP12-MCB-INIT-EXIT. 

           PERFORM DPP12-BEGIN-THREAD THRU DPP12-BEGIN-THREAD-EXIT. 

            IF DPP01-SQL-ERROR-IND = 'Y'
               GO TO DPP12-THREAD-ERROR-EXIT.

                          END-IF

                            TO WS-ACTION-CODE (WS-NUM-PAX)

              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB

                   UNTIL WS-SUB > 5 
              IF WS-TRANS-TYPE (WS-SUB) = 'A'   
                 IF WS-AIR-CITY-CNT = 0 
                    MOVE 1  TO WS-AIR-CITY-CNT

                    MOVE WS-CITY-CODE (WS-SUB)  
                      TO WS-AIR-CITY-CODE (WS-AIR-CITY-CNT) 
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 'N' TO WS-CITY-MATCHED-SW  
                    PERFORM VARYING I FROM 1 BY 1   
                            UNTIL I > WS-AIR-CITY-CNT   
                       IF WS-CITY-CODE (WS-SUB) = WS-AIR-CITY-CODE (I)  
                          ADD 1 TO WS-AIR-CITY-REQ (I)  
                          MOVE WS-AIR-CITY-CNT TO I 
                          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CITY-MATCHED-SW
                       END-IF   
                    END-PERFORM 
                    IF NOT CITY-MATCHED 
                       ADD  1  TO WS-AIR-CITY-CNT   
                       MOVE 1  TO WS-AIR-CITY-REQ  (WS-AIR-CITY-CNT)
                       MOVE WS-CITY-CODE (WS-SUB)   
                         TO WS-AIR-CITY-CODE (WS-AIR-CITY-CNT)  
                    END-IF  
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           MOVE 0 TO WS-DUPLICATE-PAX-SEQ-COUNT.
           PERFORM VARYING J FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL J > BKGACT-PAX-CNT   
              PERFORM VARYING I FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL I > BKGACT-PAX-CNT
                IF (BKGACT-PAX-SEQ-NUM (J) =
                    BKGACT-PAX-SEQ-NUM (I)) AND (I NOT = J) 
                      ADD 1 TO WS-DUPLICATE-PAX-SEQ-COUNT   
                END-IF  
              END-PERFORM   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-DUPLICATE-PAX-SEQ-COUNT > 0
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0014       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           PERFORM 4700-CHECK-COUNTRY-STATE-ZIP THRU 4700-EXIT. 

           IF CCMV-ERROR-SUB > ZERO 
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              EVALUATE CCMV-ERROR-SUB   
                  WHEN 1 MOVE 0015 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                  WHEN 2 MOVE 0016 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                  WHEN 3 MOVE 0017 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              END-EVALUATE  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'  
              IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND  
                  SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1) 
                 IF CCP45-SECURITY-ID = 'T' 
                    MOVE SPACES              TO MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID
                    MOVE CCP45-CUSTOMER-NUM  TO MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID
                 END-IF 
                 PERFORM 4850-FETCH-SAIL-SHIP-REC  THRU 4850-EXIT   
                 PERFORM 4900-PROCESS-AGENCY       THRU 4900-EXIT   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 1      TO CCP00-WHOLESALE-ONLY-FLG. 
           MOVE 1      TO CCP00-REQUEST-EMAIL-FLG.  
           MOVE 'B'    TO CCP00-LETTER-TYPE.
           MOVE 1      TO CCP00-REQ-COMMUN-FLG. 
           MOVE 1      TO CCP00-DIRECT-COMM-FLG.
           MOVE 'I'             TO CCP00-EMAIL-TYPE.

           PERFORM VARYING BKGSVC-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
               UNTIL BKGSVC-SUB > BKGSVC-SEGMENT-MAX
               IF  BKGSVC-SEGMENT (BKGSVC-SUB) > SPACES 
                   IF  BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) =   
                       'GSGR' OR 'GGDW' OR 'GAPH'   
                       MOVE 'G' TO CCP00-EMAIL-TYPE 
                       MOVE BKGSVC-SEGMENT-MAX  
                                TO BKGSVC-SUB   
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
           END-PERFORM. 

           MOVE SPACES TO CCP00-INPUT-CURRENCY-CODE.
           IF  BKCRV1-CURRENCY-CODE > SPACES
               MOVE BKCRV1-CURRENCY-CODE     TO 
                                             CCP00-INPUT-CURRENCY-CODE  
           END-IF.  

           IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND 
              SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 0) AND 
              CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1' AND  
              CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM > SPACES  
              MOVE CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM  TO IBR-BOOK-NUM 
              MOVE 'A'                  TO IBR-BOOK-TYPE
              PERFORM IRP66-FETCH-IBR THRU IRP66-FETCH-IBR-EXIT 
              IF IRP66-ERROR-NUM > 0
                 MOVE 2  TO DPP01-MESSAGE-SW
                 MOVE IRP66-ERROR-MESSAGE (IRP66-ERROR-NUM) 
                 TO DPP01-USER-MESSAGE  
                 GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR  
              END-IF
              MOVE IBR-CUSTOMER-NUM TO CCP00-OLD-AGT-CUST-NUM   
              MOVE IBR-ALT-CUSTOMER-NUM TO CCP00-OLD-ALT-CUST-NUM   
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM CCP10-VALIDATE-AGENT THRU CCP10-VALIDATE-AGENT-EXIT. 

           IF CCMV-ERROR-SUB > ZERO 
              PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                 THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
              GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
           END-IF.  

220000*    Modified below check to only stop the creation of TA Booking 
220000*    via .com. It should allow modification of TA booking.

           IF  (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1) AND   
               (SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG         = 1) AND   
220000         (RSP49-O-C-AGENT-DIRECT-IND (1)) = 'N' AND   
220000         (CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1')   
               ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
               MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
               MOVE 0229       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
               MOVE SPACES     TO MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID  
               PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG
                  THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT   
               GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
           END-IF.  

           IF CCL-BOOKING-RATE  
              IF SSP03-O-PPC-COMPLAINT-FLG = 0  
                   ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                   MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                                     (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                   MOVE 8001       TO   
                                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                   PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG
                      THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT   
                   GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF  ( RSP50-O-CURRENCY-CODE NOT = SSP05-O-CURRENCY-CODE )
               IF  SSP03-O-MULTICURRENCY-FLG NOT = 1
                   ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                   MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                                     (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                   MOVE 0135       TO   
                                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                   PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG
                      THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT   
                   GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
               END-IF   
           END-IF   

           IF RSP49-O-C-AGENT-DIRECT-IND (1) > SPACES   
              IF RSP49-O-C-AGENT-DIRECT-IND (1) = 'Y'   
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-AGENT-DIRECT-IND
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 'N' TO WS-AGENT-DIRECT-IND
              END-IF
           END-IF.  
           IF WS-AGENT-DIRECT-IND = 'Y' AND 
              RSP49-O-COMMUN-AGT-CNT > 1
              IF RSP49-O-C-AGENT-DIRECT-IND (2) > SPACES
                 IF RSP49-O-C-AGENT-DIRECT-IND (2) = 'Y'
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-AGENT-DIRECT-IND 
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 'N' TO WS-AGENT-DIRECT-IND 
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF BKTAGI-TAKEOVER-ID > SPACES AND   
              RSP50-O-CUSTOMER-CODE NOT = 'C'   
                 ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                 MOVE 0096    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
           END-IF.  

           IF BKTAGI-TAKEOVER-ID > SPACES AND   
              BKTAGI-TAKEOVER-CODE NOT = 'A' AND
              BKTAGI-TAKEOVER-CODE NOT = 'P'
                 ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                 MOVE 0092    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
           END-IF.  

           IF BKTAGI-TAKEOVER-ID > SPACES   
              IF SSP03-O-TAKEOVER-FLG     NOT = 1 OR
                 CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'   
                 ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                 MOVE 0093    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF BKTAGI-TAKEOVER-CODE > SPACES AND 
              BKTAGI-TAKEOVER-ID   NOT > SPACES 
                 ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                 MOVE 0094    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 0 TO WS-TAKEOVER.   
           IF BKTAGI-TAKEOVER-ID > SPACES AND   
              CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'  
              IF BKTAGI-TAKEOVER-CODE = 'P' 
               MOVE BKTAGI-TAKEOVER-ID TO MSGHDR-AGENT-PHONE-NUM
               MOVE SPACES             TO MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID  
              END-IF
              IF BKTAGI-TAKEOVER-CODE = 'A' 
               MOVE BKTAGI-TAKEOVER-ID TO MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID  
               MOVE SPACES             TO MSGHDR-AGENT-PHONE-NUM
              END-IF
              MOVE SPACES              TO CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM   
              MOVE 1                   TO WS-TAKEOVER   
              PERFORM CCP10-VALIDATE-AGENT  
                 THRU CCP10-VALIDATE-AGENT-EXIT 
              IF CCMV-ERROR-SUB > ZERO  
                 PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG  
                    THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT 
                 GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-TAKEOVER = 1   
              IF RSP50-O-CUSTOMER-CODE NOT = 'A'
               ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
               MOVE 0095    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
               GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-TAKEOVER = 1   
              MOVE RSP50-O-CUSTOMER-NUM  TO MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID
           END-IF.  

       COPY RSP86INT.   
           MOVE RSP50-O-LOCALITY-CODE   TO RSP86-I-LOCALITY-CODE.   

           CALL 'RSC86GLD' USING  DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS  
                                  DPP01-COMMON-INFO 
                                  RSP86-INPUT-PARAMS
                                  RSP86-OUTPUT-PARAMS.  

           IF (DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > ZERO AND 
              DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1) 
              ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) TO  
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1)  TO  
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-NUM (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                 THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
              GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
           END-IF.  

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1' AND  
              BKCRV1-ATTEMPT-CNT > 1
              PERFORM 5400-CHK-FOR-EXISTING-BOOKING THRU 5500-EXIT  
              IF ACBR-BOOKING-FOUND 
                 MOVE IBR-BOOK-NUM TO RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW   
                 GO TO 4100-GET-CURRENT-BOOKING 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) = 'CSV'  
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0026       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'  
              GO TO 0700-EDIT-CONTINUE-CHG  
           END-IF.  

           IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND 
               SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1) AND
               CCP45-SECURITY-ID = 'T'  
              MOVE 'F' TO WS-CONF-IND   
           END-IF.  

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'  
              GO TO 0900-AFTER-AER-UPDATE   
           END-IF.  

       0700-EDIT-CONTINUE-CHG.  
           IF BKCRV1-SYNCHRONIZATION-ID > ZERO  
              IF IBR-LAST-UPDATE-NUM NOT = BKCRV1-SYNCHRONIZATION-ID
                 ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                 MOVE 0018       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF IBR-BOOK-NUM > SPACES 
              MOVE IBR-SAIL-DATE        TO WS-SAIL-DATE 
              MOVE IBR-SHIP-CODE        TO BKCRV1-SHIP-CODE 
              MOVE IBR-DURATION-DAYS    TO BKCRV1-DURATION-DAYS 
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM VARYING J FROM 1 BY 1
                   UNTIL J > BKGACT-PAX-CNT OR CCMV-ERROR-SUB = 1   
              PERFORM VARYING I FROM 1 BY 1 
                      UNTIL I > BKGINF-SUB  
                   OR BKGACT-PAX-SEQ-NUM (J) = BKGINF-PAX-SEQ-NUM (I)   
                   MOVE 0 TO CCMV-ERROR-SUB 
              END-PERFORM   
              IF I > BKGINF-SUB 
                 MOVE 1 TO CCMV-ERROR-SUB   
              ELSE  
                 IF BKGACT-ACTION-CODE (J) NOT = 'D'
                    IF BKGINF-LAST-NAME     (I) NOT > SPACES OR 
                       BKGINF-FIRST-NAME    (I) NOT > SPACES OR 
                       BKGINF-TITLE-CODE    (I) NOT > SPACES OR 
                       BKGINF-GENDER-CODE   (I) NOT > SPACES OR 
                       BKGINF-AGE-CODE      (I) NOT > SPACES OR 
                       BKGINF-INSURANCE-IND (I) NOT > SPACES

                       MOVE 1 TO CCMV-ERROR-SUB 
                    END-IF  
                    IF BKGACT-ACTION-CODE(J) = 'A'  
                       ADD 1 TO WS-PAX-CNT-ADD  
                    END-IF  
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF CCMV-ERROR-SUB > ZERO 
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0020       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-TAKEOVER = 1   
              GO 0900-AFTER-AER-UPDATE  
           END-IF.  

           IF  (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND
                SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1) AND   
               CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1' 
               GO TO 0700-CREATE-AER
           ELSE 
              GO TO 0900-AFTER-AER-UPDATE   
           END-IF.  

       0700-CREATE-AER. 
           MOVE WS-EMAIL-ADDRESS    TO AER-EMAIL-ADDRESS.   
           MOVE MSGHDR-UI-CODE      TO AER-COMPANY-CODE.
           MOVE AGENT-CUSTOMER-NUM  TO AER-CUSTOMER-NUM.
           MOVE 0                   TO AER-DELIVERY-ERROR-COUNT 
                                       AER-MESSAGE-IND. 

           IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND 
               SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1)
               MOVE 0               TO WS-AER-MESG-IND-2
               MOVE 1               TO WS-AER-MESG-IND-1
               MOVE WS-AER-MESSAGE-IND  
                                    TO AER-MESSAGE-IND  
           END-IF.  

           STORE AGENT-EMAIL-REC
              ON ERROR GO TO 0800-ERROR.

           GO TO 0900-AFTER-AER-UPDATE. 

       0800-ERROR.  
           IF ERROR-NUM NOT = '0005'
               GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB. 
           MOVE 0021       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB).
           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.

      *=================================================================
       0900-AFTER-AER-UPDATE.   
           COPY RSP51INT.   

           MOVE WS-SAIL-DATE            TO CAH-SAIL-DATE.   
           MOVE BKCRV1-SHIP-CODE        TO CAH-SHIP-CODE.   
           IF BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) = 'GUAR' 
              MOVE 'TBA  '              TO CAH-CABIN-NUM
           ELSE 
              MOVE BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) TO CAH-CABIN-NUM
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 1                       TO RSP51-I-CABIN-REQUESTS.  
           FETCH CABIN-HOLD-REC RECORD  
                 ON ERROR GO TO 1000-CABIN-HOLD-ERROR.  

           MOVE 0                       TO RSP51-I-CABIN-REQUESTS.  
           GO TO 1000-CONTINUE. 

       1000-CABIN-HOLD-ERROR.   

           IF  CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM > SPACES 
               MOVE 0                   TO RSP51-I-CABIN-REQUESTS   
           ELSE 
               MOVE 1                   TO RSP51-I-CABIN-REQUESTS   
           END-IF.  

       1000-CONTINUE.   
           IF  CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM > SPACES 

               IF  IBR-BOOK-TYPE = 'I'  
                   MOVE CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM TO RSP51-I-INDV-BOOK-NUM
               ELSE 
                   MOVE IBR-GROUP-BOOK-NUM  TO RSP51-I-GRP-BOOK-NUM 
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF    MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID           NOT > SPACES
             AND (   MSGHDR-AGENT-PHONE-NUM NOT > SPACES
                  OR MSGHDR-AGENT-PHONE-NUM NOT > ZEROES)   
             AND (   MSGHDR-AGENT-ARC-NUM   NOT > SPACES
                  OR MSGHDR-AGENT-ARC-NUM   NOT > ZEROES)   
             AND (   MSGHDR-AGENT-CLIA-NUM  NOT > SPACES
                  OR MSGHDR-AGENT-CLIA-NUM  NOT > ZEROES)   
               MOVE 1 TO RSP51-OVR-AGT-NEW-BOOK-FLG 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE WS-SAIL-DATE            TO RSP51-I-SAIL-DATE.   
           MOVE BKCRV1-SHIP-CODE        TO RSP51-I-SHIP-CODE.   
           MOVE BKCRV1-DURATION-DAYS    TO RSP51-I-DURATION-DAYS.   
           MOVE WS-NUM-PAX              TO RSP51-I-NUM-PAX. 

           IF CCL-CORP-GRP-IND  
              MOVE 'Y'                  TO RSP51-I-PROT-POLICY-PHASE-IND
              MOVE 'G'                  TO RSP51-I-BOOK-TYPE
              MOVE GBR1-BOOK-NUM        TO RSP51-I-GRP-BOOK-NUM 
              MOVE GBR1-POLICY-PHASE-NUM
                                        TO RSP51-I-POLICY-PHASE-NUM 
              MOVE GBR1-POLICY-TYPE     TO RSP51-I-POLICY-TYPE  
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'N'                  TO RSP51-I-PROT-POLICY-PHASE-IND
              MOVE 'I'                  TO RSP51-I-BOOK-TYPE
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'B'                     TO RSP51-I-REQUEST-TYPE.

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1' AND  
              WS-PAX-CNT-ADD  = 0   
              MOVE 'U'                  TO RSP51-I-REQUEST-TYPE 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'N'                     TO RSP51-I-DURATION-TYPE-CODE.  
           MOVE 'N'                     TO RSP51-I-ALT-DURATION-IND.
           MOVE 'N'                     TO RSP51-I-ADD-PAX-IND. 
           MOVE 'Y'                     TO RSP51-I-ELEC-DIST-IND.   
           MOVE 'N'                     TO RSP51-I-SAILING-IS-ALT-IND.  

           CALL 'RSO51SAV' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 RSP51-SAILING-AVAIL-INPUT  
                                 RSP51-SAILING-AVAIL-OUTPUT 
                                 RSP50-AGENCY-OUTPUT
                                 RSP90-OVERRIDE-FLAGS-INFO. 

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > ZERO AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1 
              ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) TO  
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1)  TO  
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-NUM (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                 THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
              GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE TDP01-FINANCIAL-COMPANY-CODE
                                        TO CCP00-FIN-COMPANY-CODE.  
           MOVE BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE  
                                        TO CCP00-DINING-CODE.   
           PERFORM CCP11-GET-DINING-CODE THRU CCP11-DINING-EXIT.
           MOVE CCP00-DINING-CODE       TO BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE.  
           IF BKDINI-W-DINING-SEATING-CODE > SPACES 
              MOVE BKDINI-W-DINING-SEATING-CODE 
                                        TO CCP00-DINING-CODE
              PERFORM CCP11-GET-DINING-CODE THRU CCP11-DINING-EXIT  
              MOVE CCP00-DINING-CODE    TO BKDINI-W-DINING-SEATING-CODE 
           END-IF.  

           EXEC SQL 
                SELECT SCHEDULED_PAYMENT_IND
                  INTO WS-SCHEDULED-PAYMENT-IND 
                  FROM COMPANY  
                 WHERE COMPANY_CODE = :TDP01-COMPANY-CODE   
                 FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
           END-EXEC.

       1100-FETCH-BOOKING.  

           MOVE ALL 'N'          TO RSP06-EXIST-INDS OF RSP06-OLD   
                                    RSP06-READ-INDS  OF RSP06-OLD.  
           MOVE LOW-VALUES       TO RSP06-ENTRY-CNTS OF RSP06-OLD.  
           INITIALIZE RSP06-BOOKING-REC OF RSP06-OLD.   
           MOVE SPACES           TO RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD.
           INITIALIZE RSP06-FEE-INFO    OF RSP06-OLD.   
           INITIALIZE RSP06-MISC-INFO   OF RSP06-OLD.   
           INITIALIZE RSP06-I-PIAR-INFO OF RSP06-OLD.   
           INITIALIZE RSP06-I-EMAIL-INFO OF RSP06-OLD.  
           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL WS-SUB > 5 
              INITIALIZE RSP06-PAX-ENTRY OF RSP06-OLD (WS-SUB)  
           END-PERFORM. 

           MOVE ALL 'N'          TO RSP06-EXIST-INDS OF RSP06-NEW   
                                    RSP06-READ-INDS  OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE LOW-VALUES       TO RSP06-ENTRY-CNTS OF RSP06-NEW.  
           INITIALIZE RSP06-BOOKING-REC OF RSP06-NEW.   
           MOVE SPACES        TO RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW.   
           INITIALIZE RSP06-FEE-INFO    OF RSP06-NEW.   
           INITIALIZE RSP06-MISC-INFO   OF RSP06-NEW.   
           INITIALIZE RSP06-I-PIAR-INFO OF RSP06-NEW.   
           INITIALIZE RSP06-I-EMAIL-INFO OF RSP06-NEW.  
           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL WS-SUB > 5 
              INITIALIZE RSP06-PAX-ENTRY OF RSP06-NEW (WS-SUB)  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF NOT CCL-CORP-GRP-IND AND CCP00-GROUP-BOOK-NUM NOT > SPACES
              GO TO 1150-CALL-RSO23RGC  
           END-IF.  

           COPY RSP15INT.   
           MOVE GBR1-BOOK-NUM   TO RSP15-I-GRP-BOOK-NUM.
           MOVE 'Y'             TO RSP15-I-CALC-PAYMENT-IND.

           CALL 'RSO15BCR' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 RSP15-CABIN-COUNTS-INPUT   
                                 RSP15-CABIN-COUNTS-OUTPUT  
                                 RSP90-OVERRIDE-FLAGS-INFO. 

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > ZERO AND  
              DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1  
              ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) TO  
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1) TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE 
              MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE TO 
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                 THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
              GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
           END-IF.  

       1150-CALL-RSO23RGC.  

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'  
              GO TO 1200-VALIDATE-AIR-CITY  
           END-IF.  

       COPY RSP23INT.   

           MOVE IBR-BOOK-NUM     TO RSP23-I-INDV-BOOK-NUM.  
           MOVE 'A'              TO RSP23-I-BOOK-TYPE.  
           MOVE 'Y'              TO RSP23-I-READ-IBR-IND
                                    RSP23-I-READ-IFR-IND
                                    RSP23-I-READ-I-PIAR-IND 
                                    RSP23-I-READ-I-PIER-IND 
                                    RSP23-I-READ-PIER-IND   
                                    RSP23-I-READ-PAX-IND
                                    RSP23-I-READ-PTFR-IND   
                                    RSP23-I-READ-PPR-IND
                                    RSP23-I-READ-TIR-IND
                                    RSP23-I-READ-PXFR-IND   
                                    RSP23-I-READ-PIAR-IND   
                                    RSP23-I-READ-PIID-IND   
                                    RSP23-I-READ-PICR-IND   
                                    RSP23-I-READ-PPIR-IND   
                                    RSP23-I-READ-CIF-IND
                                    RSP23-I-READ-I-TOAR-IND 
                                    RSP23-I-READ-IBPIR-IND  
                                    RSP23-I-READ-PPHOR-IND  
                                    RSP23-I-READ-POBC-IND   
                                    RSP23-I-READ-PICC-IND   
                                    RSP23-I-READ-CSSXR-IND  
                                    RSP23-I-READ-GST-OFR-IND
                                    RSP23-I-READ-GST-OFR-QUL-IND
                                    RSP23-I-READ-PICF-IND.  

           CALL 'RSO23RGC'    USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS
                                    DPP01-COMMON-INFO   
                                    RSP23-RGC-INPUT 
                                    RSP06-OLD.  

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > ZERO AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1 
              ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) TO  
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1)  TO  
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-NUM (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                 THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
              GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-STATUS OF RSP06-OLD = 'C' OR   
              RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD > 0
              ADD 1              TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
              MOVE 0024          TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD
             MOVE RSP06-PAX-CITY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD           (WS-SUB)   
                                 TO WS-PAX-CITY-CODE-OLD     (WS-SUB)   
           END-PERFORM. 

           PERFORM VARYING BKGSVC-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL BKGSVC-SUB > BKGSVC-SEGMENT-MAX
              IF BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) (1:2) 
                                                     = 'XF' OR 'TF' 
                 PERFORM VARYING WS-OLD-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                         UNTIL WS-OLD-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD  
                    IF RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB) =
                                 BKGSVC-PAX-SEQ-NUM (BKGSVC-SUB)
                       IF ( RSP06-PAX-AIR-TYPE OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)
                            = 'F' OR 'R' ) AND  
                          ( BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) (3:3)  
                            = 'FFF'      )  
                            ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                            MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE 
                                                      (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                            MOVE 0177       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   
                                                      (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                            PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG   
                               THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
                            GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB   
                       END-IF   
                       IF ( RSP06-PAX-AIR-TYPE OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)
                            = 'A' ) AND 
                          ( BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) (3:3)  
                            NOT = 'FFF'      )  
                            ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                            MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE 
                                                      (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                            MOVE 0178       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   
                                                      (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                            PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG   
                               THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
                            GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB   
                       END-IF   
                    END-IF  
                 END-PERFORM
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM  

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1' AND  
              BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) = 'GUAR' 
              IF  (RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (1:1) = 'T' OR 'C') 
                  MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM  OF RSP06-OLD
                                            TO CAH-CABIN-NUM
                  FETCH CABIN-HOLD-REC RECORD   
                        ON ERROR GO TO 1200-VALIDATE-AIR-CITY   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

       1200-VALIDATE-AIR-CITY.  
           IF WS-AIR-CITY-CNT NOT > 0   
              GO TO 1500-AFTER-CITY-AVAIL.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL WS-SUB > WS-AIR-CITY-CNT   
              MOVE 'N'                       TO WS-AIR-CITY-CHANGED 
              IF  CCMH-MESSAGE-ID  NOT = 'CCNEWBK1' 
                  MOVE WS-SUB                TO WS-SUB1 
                  PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB1 FROM 1 BY 1   
                          UNTIL ((WS-SUB1 > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD))
                       PERFORM VARYING PSUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                              UNTIL PSUB > WS-NUM-PAX   
                          IF (WS-BKGTRN-PAX-SEQ-NUM (PSUB) =
                              RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-SUB1)) 
                               AND (WS-TRANS-TYPE (PSUB)
                                                  NOT = 'O' AND SPACES) 
                              IF WS-PAX-CITY-CODE-OLD (WS-SUB1) 
                                              NOT = WS-CITY-CODE (PSUB) 
                                 ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                                 MOVE 0174       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE  
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                                 PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG  
                                    THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT 
                                 GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB  
                              END-IF
                          END-IF
                       END-PERFORM  
                  END-PERFORM   
                  IF  RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD NOT = WS-NUM-PAX   
                      PERFORM VARYING I FROM 1 BY 1 
                             UNTIL I > WS-NUM-PAX   
                        IF  WS-ACTION-CODE (I) = 'A' AND
                            WS-TRANS-TYPE  (I) = 'A'
                            ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                            MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE 
                                                     (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                            MOVE 0176       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   
                                                     (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                            PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG   
                               THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
                            GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB   
                        END-IF  
                      END-PERFORM   
                  END-IF
                  MOVE 'N' TO WS-CITY-FOUND 
                  PERFORM VARYING WS-AIR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL  
                          WS-AIR-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD OR
                          WS-CITY-FOUND = 'Y'   
                          IF  WS-TRANS-TYPE (WS-AIR-SUB) = 'A'  
                             MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CITY-FOUND  
                          END-IF
                  END-PERFORM   
                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-AIR-SUB
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

      *=================================================================
       1500-AFTER-CITY-AVAIL.   

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'  
              GO TO 2000-PROCESS-CABIN-HOLD 
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (1:1) = 'T' OR 'C' OR 'W'
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD TO DPP34-CABIN-NUM  
              PERFORM DPP33-FETCH-CABIN THRU DPP33-EXIT 
              IF DPP34-ERROR-SUB > ZEROES   
                 GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR  
              END-IF
              IF AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'Y' OR 'P' OR 'S' 
                                              OR 'L' OR 'R' 
                 PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                         UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD  

                    IF (RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (WS-SUB 1)  
                              = 'W' OR 'U') OR  
                       (RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (WS-SUB 2)  
                              = 'W' OR 'U') OR  
                       (RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (WS-SUB 3)  
                              = 'W' OR 'U') OR  
                       (RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (WS-SUB 4)  
                              = 'W' OR 'U') OR  
                       (RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (WS-SUB 5)  
                              = 'W' OR 'U') 
                       IF BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) NOT =
                                       RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD 
                       ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                       MOVE 0076 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                       GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
                       END-IF   
                    END-IF  
                 END-PERFORM
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CHECK-OLD-PAX-COMMENT-SW. 
           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD
              MOVE SPACES TO WS-PAX-COMMENT-INHOUSE (WS-SUB)
              MOVE SPACES         TO WS-PAX-COMMENT (WS-SUB)
              PERFORM VARYING WS-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
                      UNTIL (WS-IDX > 5)
                         OR (RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE 
                              OF RSP06-OLD (WS-SUB WS-IDX) NOT > SPACES)
                 MOVE RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE OF RSP06-OLD   
                                        (WS-SUB WS-IDX) TO WS-CHECK-CODE
                                                           WS-FOR-CODE  
                 PERFORM 5840-FETCH-FOR-RECORD THRU 5840-EXIT   
                 IF VALID-INHOUSE-FEATURE   
                    INSPECT WS-PAX-COMMENT-INHOUSE (WS-SUB) REPLACING   
                            FIRST SPACE BY WS-CHECK-CODE
                    MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-INHOUSE-FEATURE-SW 
                 END-IF 
              END-PERFORM   
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-COMMENT OF RSP06-OLD   
                                     (WS-SUB) TO WS-PAX-COMMENT(WS-SUB) 
           END-PERFORM. 

           MOVE RSP06-BOOKING-REC OF RSP06-OLD TO   
                RSP06-BOOKING-REC OF RSP06-NEW. 
           MOVE RSP06-TPR-INFO    OF RSP06-OLD TO   
                RSP06-TPR-INFO    OF RSP06-NEW. 
           MOVE RSP06-MISC-INFO   OF RSP06-OLD TO   
                RSP06-MISC-INFO   OF RSP06-NEW. 
           MOVE RSP06-EXIST-INDS  OF RSP06-OLD TO   
                RSP06-EXIST-INDS  OF RSP06-NEW. 
           MOVE RSP06-READ-INDS   OF RSP06-OLD TO   
                RSP06-READ-INDS   OF RSP06-NEW. 
           MOVE RSP06-ENTRY-CNTS  OF RSP06-OLD TO   
                RSP06-ENTRY-CNTS  OF RSP06-NEW. 
           MOVE RSP06-FEE-INFO    OF RSP06-OLD TO   
                RSP06-FEE-INFO    OF RSP06-NEW. 
           MOVE RSP06-I-TOAR-INFO OF RSP06-OLD TO   
                RSP06-I-TOAR-INFO OF RSP06-NEW. 
           MOVE RSP06-IBPIR-INFO  OF RSP06-OLD TO   
                RSP06-IBPIR-INFO  OF RSP06-NEW. 
           MOVE RSP06-CABIN-OFFER OF RSP06-OLD TO   
                RSP06-CABIN-OFFER OF RSP06-NEW. 
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-OBC-INFO OF RSP06-OLD TO  
                RSP06-IBR-OBC-INFO OF RSP06-NEW.

           PERFORM VARYING WS-GCNT FROM 1 BY 1  
                UNTIL WS-GCNT > RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT OF RSP06-OLD  
                MOVE RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-TABLE OF RSP06-OLD (WS-GCNT) TO  
                RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-TABLE OF RSP06-NEW (WS-GCNT)  
           END-PERFORM. 

           PERFORM VARYING WS-GCNT FROM 1 BY 1  
                UNTIL WS-GCNT > RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-CNT  OF RSP06-OLD  
                MOVE RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-QUAL OF RSP06-OLD (WS-GCNT) TO   
                     RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-QUAL OF RSP06-NEW (WS-GCNT)  
           END-PERFORM. 


           MOVE RSP51-O-SAIL-DATE TO DPP02-SAIL-DATE.   

           MOVE 0 TO RSP06-IBR-NUM-ROLLAWAYS OF RSP06-NEW   
                     RSP06-IBR-NUM-CRIBS     OF RSP06-NEW.  

      *=================================================================
       2000-PROCESS-CABIN-HOLD. 

           MOVE SPACES                  TO DPP34-EDIT-TYPE. 
           MOVE RSP51-O-SHIP-CODE       TO DPP34-SHIP-CODE. 
           MOVE RSP51-O-SAIL-DATE       TO DPP34-SAIL-DATE  
           MOVE RSP51-O-DURATION-DAYS   TO DPP34-DURATION-DAYS. 
           IF BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) = 'GUAR' 
              MOVE 'TBA  '              TO DPP34-CABIN-NUM  
           ELSE 
              MOVE BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) TO DPP34-CABIN-NUM  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE (1) TO DPP34-SELECT-CATEGORY-CODE. 
           MOVE SPACES                   TO DPP34-SHARE-CODE.   

           MOVE RSP51-I-BOOK-TYPE        TO DPP34-BOOK-TYPE.
           MOVE RSP51-I-NUM-PAX          TO DPP34-NUM-PAX.  

           PERFORM DPP33-FETCH-CABIN THRU DPP33-EXIT.   

           IF DPP34-ERROR-SUB > ZERO
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0026       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           IF BKCRV1-UPGRADE-CODE (1) > SPACES AND  
              (NOT CCL-GRP-RATE-WITH-VERSION)   
              IF DPP34-CABIN-NUM (1:1) NOT = 'T' AND 'C'
                 MOVE DPP34-CATEGORY-CODE TO BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE (1)   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'  
              MOVE ZEROES TO BKGACT-SUB 
              PERFORM VARYING I FROM 1 BY 1 
                        UNTIL I > BKGACT-PAX-CNT OR 
                              I > BKGACT-PAX-MAX
                 IF BKGACT-ACTION-CODE (I) NOT = 'D' AND
                    BKGINF-SEGMENT (I) > SPACES 
                    ADD 1 TO BKGACT-SUB 
                 END-IF 
              END-PERFORM   
              IF BKGACT-SUB NOT > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD
                 PERFORM VARYING I FROM 1 BY 1  
                           UNTIL I > BKGACT-PAX-CNT OR  
                                 I > BKGACT-PAX-MAX 
                    IF BKGACT-ACTION-CODE (I) = 'A' AND 
                       BKGINF-SEGMENT     (I) > SPACES  
                       ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                       MOVE 0090 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                       GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
                    END-IF  
                 END-PERFORM
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'  
              PERFORM 6800-DETERMINE-RATE-CHANGE THRU 6800-EXIT 
              IF NOT RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CAH-NOT-NEEDED-SW   
                 GO TO 3000-PROCESS-RSP06   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1' AND  
              BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) = 'GUAR' 
              IF  (RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (1:1) = 'T' OR 'C') 
                  MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM  OF RSP06-OLD
                                            TO DPP34-CABIN-NUM  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE DPP34-CABIN-NUM TO WS-CABIN-NUM.
           PERFORM DPP33-FETCH-CABIN-HOLD THRU DPP33-EXIT.  
           IF DPP34-ERROR-SUB > ZEROS   
              IF DPP34-ERROR-SUB = 4
                 MOVE 'TBA' TO DPP34-CABIN-NUM  
                 PERFORM DPP33-FETCH-CABIN-HOLD THRU DPP33-EXIT 
                 MOVE WS-CABIN-NUM TO DPP34-CABIN-NUM   
                 IF DPP34-ERROR-SUB = 4 
                    GO TO 2200-CABIN-HOLD-NOT-GOOD  
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                 MOVE 0027       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE SPACES TO WS-CAH-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION.  
           STRING MSGHDR-SYSTEM-ID (1:3)
                  MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID (1:10)   
                  MSGHDR-USER-ID (1:6)  
                  DELIMITED BY SIZE 
             INTO WS-CAH-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION.  

       2050-VALIDATE-CAH-OWNER. 

           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD NOT > SPACES AND  
              MSGHDR-USER-ID (1:10) = 'NEWCONSUME' AND  
              ( CAH-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION = 'CCL          NEWCON' OR 
                CAH-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION = 'CUK          NEWCON' OR 
                CAH-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION = 'MCL          NEWCON' OR 
                CAH-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION = 'CAU          NEWCON')   
              GO TO 2100-FOUND-CABIN-HOLD   
           END-IF.  

           IF CAH-OPER-INITIALS  = DPP01-3CHAR-USERID AND   
              CAH-BOOK-TYPE      = DPP34-BOOK-TYPE       AND
              CAH-CURRENCY-CODE  = RSP50-O-CURRENCY-CODE AND
              CAH-CABIN-BOOK-NUM = RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD AND  
              CAH-GRP-BOOK-NUM   = RSP51-I-GRP-BOOK-NUM AND 
              CAH-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION = WS-CAH-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION
              IF DPP34-CABIN-NUM (1:1) = 'T' OR 'C' 
                 IF CAH-REQ-CAT-CODE = BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE (1) 
                    GO TO 2100-FOUND-CABIN-HOLD 
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 2100-FOUND-CABIN-HOLD.   

           FETCH NEXT DUPLICATE CABIN-HOLD-REC RECORD   
               ON ERROR GO TO 2070-CHECK-CABIN-HOLD-ERROR.  

           GO TO 2050-VALIDATE-CAH-OWNER.   

       2070-CHECK-CABIN-HOLD-ERROR. 

           IF DPP34-CABIN-NUM (1:1) = 'T' OR 'C'
              GO TO 2200-CABIN-HOLD-NOT-GOOD
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB. 
           MOVE 0028       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB).
           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.

       2100-FOUND-CABIN-HOLD.   

           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CABIN-HELD-IND.   

           IF WS-OFFER-CODE > SPACES
              IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1' AND   
                 ((NOT RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT) OR CHANGE-CABIN-ONLY)   
                 IF CAH-OFFER-CODE NOT =
                              RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (1) OR  
                    CAH-OFFER-ID (1) NOT =  
                              RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-OLD (1)   
                    GO TO 2200-CABIN-HOLD-NOT-GOOD  
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 IF CAH-OFFER-CODE NOT = WS-OFFER-CODE  
                    GO TO 2200-CABIN-HOLD-NOT-GOOD  
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF CCL-CORP-GRP-IND AND CCL-GRP-RATE-WITH-VERSION
              MOVE CCR-GRP-VERSION-NUM TO WS-RATE-VERSION-NUM   
              IF CAH-RATE-VERSION-NUM NOT = WS-RATE-VERSION-NUM 
                 GO TO 2200-CABIN-HOLD-NOT-GOOD 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF BKCRV1-UPGRADE-CODE (1) > SPACES  
              IF CAH-REQ-CPA NOT = BKCRV1-UPGRADE-CODE (1)  
                 GO TO 2200-CABIN-HOLD-NOT-GOOD 
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              IF CAH-REQ-CPA NOT = BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE (1) 
                 AND CAH-BOOK-TYPE = 'G'
                 GO TO 2200-CABIN-HOLD-NOT-GOOD 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

       2150-CONTINUE.   
           IF CAH-NUM-PAX NOT = RSP51-I-NUM-PAX 
              GO TO 2200-CABIN-HOLD-NOT-GOOD
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 3000-PROCESS-RSP06.

      *=================================================================
       2200-CABIN-HOLD-NOT-GOOD.

           IF CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM > SPACES AND CHANGE-CABIN-ONLY
              GO TO 2500-CALL-RSO60CBV  
           END-IF.  

       COPY RSP10PII.   
           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL WS-SUB > BKGINF-SEGMENT-MAX OR 
                       BKGINF-PAX-SEQ-NUM ( WS-SUB ) NOT > 0
             IF BKGINF-PAST-PAX-NUM (WS-SUB) > SPACES   
                MOVE BKGINF-PAST-PAX-NUM (WS-SUB)   
                                       TO WS-NEW-PAST-GUEST-NUM 
                PERFORM 3295-VALIDATE-PAST-GUEST-NUM
                   THRU 3295-EXIT   
                ADD 1                  TO  RSP10-I-LOYALTY-CNT  
                MOVE WS-NEW-PAST-GUEST-TEN-NUM  
                                       TO RSP10-I-LOYALTY-NUM   
                                         (RSP10-I-LOYALTY-CNT)  

                IF BKGINF-TIER-CODE (WS-SUB) > SPACES   
                   MOVE BKGINF-TIER-CODE   (WS-SUB) 
                                       TO RSP10-I-TIER-CODE 
                                         (RSP10-I-LOYALTY-CNT)  
                   IF BKGINF-TRUST-ME (WS-SUB) > SPACES 
                      MOVE BKGINF-TRUST-ME (WS-SUB) 
                                       TO RSP10-I-TRUST-ME-IND  
                                         (RSP10-I-LOYALTY-CNT)  
                   ELSE 
                      MOVE 'N'         TO RSP10-I-TRUST-ME-IND  
                                         (RSP10-I-LOYALTY-CNT)  
                   END-IF   
                ELSE
                   MOVE 'N'            TO RSP10-I-TRUST-ME-IND  
                                         (RSP10-I-LOYALTY-CNT)  
                END-IF  
             ELSE   
                IF CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM > SPACES 
                    PERFORM VARYING WS-SUBP FROM 1 BY 1 
                      UNTIL WS-SUBP > RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-CNT OF RSP06-OLD 
                     IF (RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-OLD(1) =   
                        RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-OLD(WS-SUBP))
                         AND
                        (RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD(WS-SUB) =   
                        RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD(WS-SUBP)) 
                        PERFORM VARYING WS-SUBQ FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL   
                            WS-SUBQ >   
                         RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-INFO-CNT OF RSP06-OLD(WS-SUBP)
                         IF RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM OF RSP06-OLD   
                            (WS-SUB) > SPACES   
                            ADD 1                 TO RSP10-I-LOYALTY-CNT
                            MOVE RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM OF RSP06-OLD  
                            (WS-SUB) TO RSP10-I-LOYALTY-NUM 
                                                  (RSP10-I-LOYALTY-CNT) 
                            IF RSP06-QUAL-TIER-CODE OF RSP06-OLD
                               (WS-SUBP WS-SUBQ) > SPACES   
                             MOVE RSP06-QUAL-TIER-CODE OF RSP06-OLD 
                             (WS-SUBP WS-SUBQ) TO RSP10-I-TIER-CODE 
                                                  (RSP10-I-LOYALTY-CNT) 
                             MOVE 'Y'         TO RSP10-I-TRUST-ME-IND   
                                              (RSP10-I-LOYALTY-CNT) 
                            ELSE
                             MOVE 'N'         TO RSP10-I-TRUST-ME-IND   
                                              (RSP10-I-LOYALTY-CNT) 
                            END-IF  
                         END-IF 
                        END-PERFORM 
                      END-IF
                      END-PERFORM   
                  END-IF
             END-IF 
           END-PERFORM. 

           MOVE 'PG'                   TO RSP10-I-IDENTIFER-TYPE.   
           MOVE 'O'                    TO RSP10-I-OFFER-STATUS-CODE.

           CALL 'RSO10PGI' USING        DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS
                                        DPP01-COMMON-INFO   
                                        RSP10-INPUT-PARMS   
                                        RSP10-OUTPUT-PARMS. 

           IF  DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > ZERO AND 
               DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1 
              ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) TO  
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1)  TO  
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-NUM (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                 THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
              GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
           END-IF.  

           IF BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) = 'GUAR' 
              MOVE 'TBA  '              TO DPP34-CABIN-NUM  
           ELSE 
              MOVE BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) TO DPP34-CABIN-NUM  
           END-IF.  
           PERFORM DPP33-FETCH-CABIN THRU DPP33-EXIT
           IF DPP34-ERROR-SUB > ZEROES  
              GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR 
           END-IF   

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'  
              IF RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT
                 IF ADD-PAX-ONLY
                    GO TO 2500-CALL-RSO04APX
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 2500-CALL-RSO60CBV   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF CCL-GRP-RATE-WITH-VERSION 
              GO TO 2400-CALL-RSO57GOA  
           END-IF.  

       2300-CALL-RSO54OAV.  
       COPY RSP54OAVINT.

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUBB FROM 1 BY 1  
                                   UNTIL WS-SUBB > WS-NUM-PAX   

              MOVE ZEROS TO WS-QUAL-SUB 

              IF ((BKGINF-HOME-STATE-CODE (WS-SUBB) > SPACES) AND   
                        (BKGINF-HOME-STATE-CODE (WS-SUBB) NOT = 'XX'))  

                  ADD 1               TO WS-QUAL-SUB
                  MOVE 'RS'           TO RSP54-I-QUALIFICATION-CODE 
                                                  (WS-SUBB WS-QUAL-SUB) 
                  EXEC SQL  
                      SELECT COUNTRY_CODE,  
                             CCL_STATE_CODE 
                        INTO :RSP54-I-COUNTRY-CODE (WS-SUBB),   
                             :RSP54-I-STATE-CODE   (WS-SUBB)
                        FROM VALID_COUNTRY_STATE
                       WHERE CCL_STATE_CODE =   
                             :BKGINF-HOME-STATE-CODE (WS-SUBB)  
                       FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY   
                  END-EXEC  
                  IF SQLCODE < 0 OR SQLCODE = 100   
                     SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-QUAL-SUB
                  END-IF
              END-IF

              IF BKGINF-PAST-PAX-NUM (WS-SUBB) > SPACES OR  
                 BKGINF-TIER-CODE (WS-SUBB) > SPACES
                  ADD 1               TO WS-QUAL-SUB
                  MOVE 'PG'           TO RSP54-I-QUALIFICATION-CODE 
                                          (WS-SUBB WS-QUAL-SUB) 
              END-IF

              IF  BKGINF-INTERLINE-IND (WS-SUBB)  = 'Y' 
                  ADD 1 TO WS-QUAL-SUB  
                  MOVE 'IL'             TO RSP54-I-QUALIFICATION-CODE   
                                               (WS-SUBB WS-QUAL-SUB)
              END-IF

              IF  BKGINF-MILITARY-IND  (WS-SUBB) = 'Y'  
                  ADD 1 TO WS-QUAL-SUB  
                  MOVE 'ML'             TO RSP54-I-QUALIFICATION-CODE   
                                               (WS-SUBB WS-QUAL-SUB)
              END-IF

              IF (BKGINF-AGE-CODE (WS-SUBB) = 'Y' AND   
                  BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT (WS-SUBB) > SPACES) 
                 MOVE BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT (WS-SUBB)   
                                      TO WS-PAX-AGE 
              ELSE  
                 IF BKGINF-BIRTH-DATE (WS-SUBB) > ZEROES
                    MOVE BKGINF-BIRTH-DATE (WS-SUBB)
                                      TO DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE
                    PERFORM DPP10-MMDDYYYY-TO-YYYYMMDD  
                    MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE
                                      TO DPP02-BD-YYYYMMDD-DATE 
                    MOVE TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD   
                                      TO DPP02-SAIL-DATE
                    PERFORM DPP10-COMPUTE-GUEST-AGE 
                    IF  DPP02-IS-DATE-VALID = 'N'   
                        ADD 1         TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                        MOVE 0102     TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE 
                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                        MOVE 'CCR08UGC' 
                                      TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE   
                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                        PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG   
                           THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
                        GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB   
                    END-IF  
                    MOVE DPP02-YEAR-CNT TO WS-PAX-AGE   
                 ELSE   
                    ADD 1              TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                    MOVE 0102          TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE
                                           (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                    MOVE 'CCR08UGC'    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                                           (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                    PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG   
                       THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
                    GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB   
                 END-IF 
              END-IF

              IF  WS-PAX-AGE >= TDP01-MIN-SENIOR-AGE
                  ADD 1               TO WS-QUAL-SUB
                  MOVE 'SN'           TO RSP54-I-QUALIFICATION-CODE 
                                          (WS-SUBB WS-QUAL-SUB) 
                  MOVE 'Y' TO WS-SN-FOUND   
              END-IF

              IF CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM > SPACES   
               PERFORM VARYING WS-SUBP FROM 1 BY 1  
                 UNTIL WS-SUBP > RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-CNT OF RSP06-OLD  
                 IF RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-OLD(1) =
                    RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-OLD(WS-SUBP) AND 
                    RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD(WS-SUBB) =   
                    RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD(WS-SUBP)  
                     PERFORM VARYING WS-SUBQ FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL  
                           WS-SUBQ >
                        RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-INFO-CNT OF RSP06-OLD(WS-SUBP) 

                      IF RSP06-QUALIFICATION-CODE OF RSP06-OLD  
                         (WS-SUBP WS-SUBQ) = 'SN' AND WS-SN-FOUND = 'N' 
                         ADD 1          TO WS-QUAL-SUB  
                         MOVE 'SN'      TO RSP54-I-QUALIFICATION-CODE   
                                           (WS-SUBB WS-QUAL-SUB)
                      END-IF

                      IF RSP06-QUALIFICATION-CODE OF RSP06-OLD  
                         (WS-SUBP WS-SUBQ) = 'RS' AND   
                        (BKGINF-HOME-STATE-CODE (WS-SUBB) <= SPACES)
                         ADD 1          TO WS-QUAL-SUB  
                         MOVE 'RS'      TO RSP54-I-QUALIFICATION-CODE   
                                             (WS-SUBB WS-QUAL-SUB)  
                        MOVE RSP06-QUAL-STATE-CODE OF RSP06-OLD 
                        (WS-SUBP WS-SUBQ) TO
                                        RSP54-I-STATE-CODE (WS-SUBB)
                        MOVE RSP06-QUAL-COUNTRY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD   
                        (WS-SUBP WS-SUBQ)   
                                   TO RSP54-I-COUNTRY-CODE (WS-SUBB)
                      END-IF

                      IF RSP06-QUALIFICATION-CODE OF RSP06-OLD  
                         (WS-SUBP WS-SUBQ) = 'PG' AND   
                         (BKGINF-PAST-PAX-NUM (WS-SUBB) <= SPACES   
                          AND BKGINF-TIER-CODE (WS-SUBB) <= SPACES) 
                         ADD 1          TO WS-QUAL-SUB  
                         MOVE 'PG'      TO RSP54-I-QUALIFICATION-CODE   
                                           (WS-SUBB WS-QUAL-SUB)
                      END-IF

                      IF RSP06-QUALIFICATION-CODE OF RSP06-OLD  
                         (WS-SUBP WS-SUBQ) = 'IL' AND   
                         BKGINF-INTERLINE-IND (WS-SUBB) NOT = 'Y'   
                         ADD 1          TO WS-QUAL-SUB  
                         MOVE 'IL'      TO RSP54-I-QUALIFICATION-CODE   
                                           (WS-SUBB WS-QUAL-SUB)
                      END-IF

                      IF RSP06-QUALIFICATION-CODE OF RSP06-OLD  
                         (WS-SUBP WS-SUBQ) = 'ML' AND   
                         BKGINF-MILITARY-IND  (WS-SUBB) NOT = 'Y'   
                         ADD 1          TO WS-QUAL-SUB  
                         MOVE 'ML'      TO RSP54-I-QUALIFICATION-CODE   
                                           (WS-SUBB WS-QUAL-SUB)
                      END-IF

                     END-PERFORM
                 END-IF 
               END-PERFORM  
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF  BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) > SPACES AND
               BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) NOT = 'GUAR'
               IF AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'Y'  
                   MOVE 'Y'             TO RSP54-I-ADA-CABIN-IND
               ELSE 
                  IF AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'P'   
                     MOVE 'P'           TO RSP54-I-ADA-CABIN-IND
                  ELSE  
                     IF AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'L' OR 'R' OR 'S'  
                        MOVE 'S'        TO RSP54-I-ADA-CABIN-IND
                     END-IF 
                  END-IF
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP51-O-SAIL-ID         TO RSP54-I-SAIL-ID. 
           MOVE WS-NUM-PAX              TO RSP54-I-OCCUPANCY.   
           MOVE RSP51-I-SHIP-CODE       TO RSP54-I-SHIP-CODE.   
           MOVE 'B'                     TO RSP54-I-REQUEST-TYPE.
           MOVE CCP00-GROUP-BOOK-NUM    TO RSP54-I-GROUP-BOOK-NUM   
           MOVE RSP51-I-BOOK-TYPE       TO RSP54-I-BOOK-TYPE.   
           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'  
              MOVE 'Y'                  TO RSP54-I-NEW-BOOKING-IND  
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'N'                  TO RSP54-I-NEW-BOOKING-IND  
           END-IF.  
           MOVE BKCRV1-COUPON-CODE      TO RSP54-I-COUPON-CODE. 
PRCCMB     MOVE 1                       TO RSP54-I-SPLIT-TGO-FLG
           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'  
              IF SSP03-O-PPC-COMPLAINT-FLG = 1  
                 IF CCL-BOOKING-RATE
                    IF NOT RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT  
                        MOVE RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-OLD (1)
                                        TO RSP54-I-OFFER-ID 
                    END-IF  
                    MOVE RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (1)  
                                        TO RSP54-I-OFFER-CODE   
PRCCMB              MOVE 0              TO RSP54-I-SPLIT-TGO-FLG
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE WS-OFFER-CODE  TO RSP54-I-OFFER-CODE   
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 MOVE WS-OFFER-CODE     TO RSP54-I-OFFER-CODE   
                 IF NOT RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT 
                    MOVE RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-OLD (1)
                                        TO RSP54-I-OFFER-ID 
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              MOVE WS-OFFER-CODE        TO RSP54-I-OFFER-CODE   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'N'                     TO RSP54-I-INTERNAL-ACCESS-IND  
                                           RSP54-I-PRICE-PROTECT-IND
                                           RSP54-I-PRICE-ELIGIBLE-IND.  
           MOVE RSP51-O-PRC-XCHG-RATE   TO RSP54-I-PRC-XCHG-RATE.   
           IF  SSP03-O-COUPON-ONLY-FLG = 1  
               MOVE 1                   TO RSP54-I-SPLIT-COUPON-FLG 
               MOVE BKCRV1-COUPON-CODE  TO RSP54-I-SUBSET-COUPON-CODE   
           ELSE 
               MOVE 0                   TO RSP54-I-SPLIT-COUPON-FLG 
           END-IF.  
PRCCMB*    MOVE 1                       TO RSP54-I-SPLIT-TGO-FLG
PRCCMB*                                    RSP54-I-SPLIT-WEB-ONLY-FLG.  

           IF WS-AB-CODE > SPACES   
              MOVE WS-AB-CODE           TO RSP54-I-SUBSET-TGO-ENT-CODE  
              MOVE 'ADB'                TO RSP54-I-SUBSET-TGO-ENT-TYPE  
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES               TO RSP54-I-SUBSET-TGO-ENT-CODE  
              MOVE 'OFF'                TO RSP54-I-SUBSET-TGO-ENT-TYPE  
           END-IF.  

           IF SSP03-O-PPC-COMPLAINT-FLG = 0 
              MOVE 1                    TO RSP54-OVERRIDE-INTERLINE-FLG 
                                           RSP54-OVERRIDE-MILITARY-FLG  
           END-IF.  

           IF BKCRV1-UPGRADE-CODE (1) > SPACES  
              MOVE BKCRV1-UPGRADE-CODE (1)  
                                      TO RSP54-I-CPA-CAT-CODE   
           ELSE 
              MOVE BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE (1) 
                                      TO RSP54-I-CPA-CAT-CODE   
           END-IF.  
           IF RSP54-I-ADA-CABIN-IND > SPACES
              MOVE SPACES             TO RSP54-I-CATEGORY-CODE  
           ELSE 
              MOVE BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE (1) 
                                      TO RSP54-I-CATEGORY-CODE  
           END-IF.  

           IF (CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM > SPACES)
             AND (CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM = RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD)
             AND (CCS-SHIP-CODE = RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-OLD) 
             AND (CCS-SAIL-DATE = RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD) 
             AND (CCS-DURATION-DAYS = RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF
                                      RSP06-OLD)
             AND (RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD NOT > 0)   
             AND (RSP06-IBR-BOOK-STATUS OF RSP06-OLD NOT = 'C') 
              IF RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD > SPACES  
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD  
                                      TO RSP54-I-HELD-CPA   
              ELSE  
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD  
                                      TO RSP54-I-HELD-CPA   
             END-IF 
             IF RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (1:3) = 'TBA' OR 'CSV' 
                                                               OR 'WAI' 
                MOVE 'G'              TO RSP54-I-HELD-CABIN-TYPE
                MOVE SPACES           TO RSP54-I-HELD-CABIN-NUM 
                MOVE RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD   
                                      TO RSP54-I-HELD-CATEGORY  
             ELSE   
                IF RSP06-IBR-GTEE-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD > SPACES   
                   MOVE 'G'           TO RSP54-I-HELD-CABIN-TYPE
                   MOVE SPACES        TO RSP54-I-HELD-CABIN-NUM 
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-GTEE-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD   
                                      TO RSP54-I-HELD-CATEGORY  
               ELSE 
                  MOVE 'C'            TO RSP54-I-HELD-CABIN-TYPE
                  MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD 
                                      TO RSP54-I-HELD-CABIN-NUM 
                  MOVE RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD 
                                      TO RSP54-I-HELD-CATEGORY  
               END-IF   
             END-IF 
           END-IF.  

           CALL 'RSO54OAV' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 RSP54-INPUT-PARMS  
                                 RSP54-OUTPUT-PARMS 
                                 RSP50-AGENCY-OUTPUT
                                 RSP90-OVERRIDE-FLAGS-INFO. 
            IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > 0 AND
               DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1 
               ADD 1                   TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
               MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1)
                                       TO   
                    MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
               MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1) 
                                       TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE 
               MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE  TO   
                    MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
               PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG
                  THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT   
               GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
            END-IF. 

       2350-LOOP-THRU-BEST-FARE-TBL.

           IF BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) = 'GUAR' OR 'TBA'
              MOVE 2                  TO WS-CAB-SUB 
           ELSE 
              MOVE 1                  TO WS-CAB-SUB 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE ZEROS                  TO WS-CAT-SUB.   
           MOVE 'N'                    TO WS-CAT-FOUND-SW.  
           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP54-O-CATEGORY-CNT OR   
                   WS-CAT-FOUND 
               IF RSP54-OC-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE (WS-SUB WS-CAB-SUB) =  
                  RSP54-I-CPA-CAT-CODE  
                  MOVE WS-SUB         TO WS-CAT-SUB 
                  MOVE 'Y'            TO WS-CAT-FOUND-SW
               END-IF   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF NOT WS-CAT-FOUND  
              ADD 1                   TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0009               TO
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC'         TO
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                 THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
              GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP54-OC-CAT-AVAIL-IND (WS-CAT-SUB WS-CAB-SUB) = 'N'  
              ADD 1                   TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0010               TO
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC'         TO
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                 THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
              GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP54-OC-OFFER-ID (WS-CAT-SUB WS-CAB-SUB)   
                                     TO WS-OFFER-ID.
           MOVE RSP54-OC-OFFER-CODE (WS-CAT-SUB WS-CAB-SUB) 
                                     TO WS-OFFER-CODE.  
           GO TO 2500-CALL-RSO60CBV.

       2400-CALL-RSO57GOA.  
       COPY RSP57GOAINT.
           MOVE RSP51-O-SAIL-ID         TO RSP57-I-SAIL-ID. 
           MOVE WS-NUM-PAX              TO RSP57-I-OCCUPANCY.   
           MOVE RSP51-I-SHIP-CODE       TO RSP57-I-SHIP-CODE.   
           MOVE 'B'                     TO RSP57-I-REQUEST-TYPE.
           MOVE 'G'                     TO RSP57-I-BOOK-TYPE.   
           MOVE 'N'                     TO RSP57-I-INTERNAL-ACCESS-IND. 
           MOVE CCR-GRP-VERSION-NUM     TO RSP57-I-GRP-RATE-VER-NUM.
           MOVE RSP51-O-PRC-XCHG-RATE   TO RSP57-I-PRC-XCHG-RATE.   
           MOVE GBR1-POLICY-TYPE        TO RSP57-I-POLICY-CODE. 
           MOVE CCP00-GROUP-BOOK-NUM    TO RSP57-I-GROUP-BOOK-NUM.  
           IF SSP03-O-PPC-COMPLAINT-FLG = 0 
              MOVE 1                    TO RSP57-OVERRIDE-INTERLINE-FLG 
                                           RSP57-OVERRIDE-MILITARY-FLG  
           END-IF.  

           IF  BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) > SPACES AND
               BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) NOT = 'GUAR'
               IF AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'Y'  
                  MOVE 'Y'              TO RSP57-I-ADA-CABIN-IND
               ELSE 
                  IF AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'P'   
                     MOVE 'P'           TO RSP57-I-ADA-CABIN-IND
                  ELSE  
                     IF AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'L' OR 'R' OR 'S'  
                        MOVE 'S'        TO RSP57-I-ADA-CABIN-IND
                     END-IF 
                  END-IF
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF (CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM > SPACES) AND
              (CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM = RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF  
                                     RSP06-OLD) AND 
              (CCS-SHIP-CODE = RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-OLD) AND
              (CCS-SAIL-DATE = RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD) AND
              (CCS-DURATION-DAYS = RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-OLD)
              IF RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (1:3) = 'TBA' OR 'CSV'
                                                                OR 'WAI'
                 MOVE 'G'               TO RSP57-I-HELD-CABIN-TYPE  
                 MOVE SPACES            TO RSP57-I-HELD-CABIN-NUM   
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD  
                                        TO RSP57-I-HELD-CATEGORY
              ELSE  
                 IF RSP06-IBR-GTEE-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD > SPACES  
                    MOVE 'G'            TO RSP57-I-HELD-CABIN-TYPE  
                    MOVE SPACES         TO RSP57-I-HELD-CABIN-NUM   
                    MOVE RSP06-IBR-GTEE-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD  
                                        TO RSP57-I-HELD-CATEGORY
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 'C'            TO RSP57-I-HELD-CABIN-TYPE  
                    MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD   
                                        TO RSP57-I-HELD-CABIN-NUM   
                    MOVE RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD   
                                        TO RSP57-I-HELD-CATEGORY
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           CALL 'RSO57GOA' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 RSP57-INPUT-PARAMS 
                                 RSP57-RATE-OUTPUT-PARMS
                                 RSP51-SAILING-AVAIL-OUTPUT 
                                 RSP50-AGENCY-OUTPUT
                                 RSP90-OVERRIDE-FLAGS-INFO. 

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > 0 AND 
              DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1  
              ADD 1                   TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) 
                                      TO
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1)  
                                      TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE  
              MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE  TO
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                 THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
              GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
           END-IF.  

       2450-LOOP-THRU-RSP57-TBL.

           MOVE ZEROS                 TO WS-CAT-SUB.
           MOVE 'N'                   TO WS-CAT-FOUND-SW.   
           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP57-O-CATEGORY-CNT OR   
                   WS-CAT-FOUND 
               IF RSP57-O-CATEGORY-CODE (WS-SUB) =  
                  BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE (1)  
                  MOVE WS-SUB         TO WS-CAT-SUB 
                  MOVE 'Y'            TO WS-CAT-FOUND-SW
               END-IF   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF NOT WS-CAT-FOUND  
              ADD 1                   TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0011               TO
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC'         TO
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                 THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
              GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
           END-IF.  

           IF BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) = 'GUAR' 
              MOVE 2                  TO WS-CAB-SUB 
           ELSE 
              MOVE 1                  TO WS-CAB-SUB 
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP57-O-CAT-AVAIL-IND (WS-CAT-SUB WS-CAB-SUB) = 'N'   
              ADD 1                   TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0012               TO
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC'         TO
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                 THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
              GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP57-O-FSG-OFFER-PTR(1)
                                      TO RSP57-FSG-OFFER-PTR.   
           MOVE RSP57-O-OFFER-ID (WS-CAT-SUB WS-CAB-SUB)
                                      TO WS-OFFER-ID.   
           MOVE RSP57-FSG-OFFER-CODE  TO WS-OFFER-CODE. 

           GO TO 2500-CALL-RSO60CBV.

       2500-CALL-RSO04APX.  
           COPY RSP04INT.   

           MOVE TDP01-SAIL-ID         TO RSP04-I-SAIL-ID.   
           MOVE RSP51-I-NUM-PAX       TO RSP04-I-REQ-OCCUPANCY. 
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-REQ-RATE-VERSION OF RSP06-OLD 
                                      TO RSP04-I-RATE-VERSION-NUM.  
           MOVE 'N'                   TO RSP04-I-OVERRIDE-IND   
                                         RSP04-I-INTERNAL-ACCESS-IND.   
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD 
                                      TO RSP04-I-BOOK-NUM.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-GROUP-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD   
                                      TO RSP04-I-GROUP-BOOK-NUM.
           MOVE RSP06-COUPON-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (1)  
                                      TO RSP04-I-COUPON-CODE.   
           IF SSP03-O-PPC-COMPLAINT-FLG = 0 
              MOVE 1                    TO RSP04-OVR-INTERLINE-FLG  
                                           RSP04-OVR-MILITARY-FLG   
           END-IF.  
           IF RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD > SPACES 
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD 
                                      TO RSP04-I-CPA-CAT-CODE   
           ELSE 
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD 
                                      TO RSP04-I-CPA-CAT-CODE   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE DPP34-CABIN-NUM       TO RSP04-I-CABIN-NUMBER.  

           CALL 'RSO04APX' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 RSP04-ADD-PAX-INPUT
                                 RSP51-SAILING-AVAIL-OUTPUT 
                                 RSP50-AGENCY-OUTPUT
                                 RSP06-OLD  
                                 RSP15-CABIN-COUNTS-OUTPUT  
                                 RSP04-ADD-PAX-OUTPUT.  

           IF  DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR        > ZERO AND  
               DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1 
               ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
               MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) TO 
                    MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
               MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX  (1) TO 
                    CCP00-ADVISE-CODE   
               MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE        TO 
                    MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
               PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG
                  THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT   
               GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP04-O-NO-RATE-IND      = 'Y' OR 
              RSP04-O-CHANGE-RATE-IND  = 'Y' OR 
              RSP04-O-CHANGE-CAT-IND   = 'Y'
              ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE 0211 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                 THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
              GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
           END-IF.  

       2500-CALL-RSO60CBV.  
           COPY RSP60INT.   
           COPY RSP60INF.   
           MOVE WS-SAIL-DATE          TO RSP60-I-SAIL-DATE. 
           MOVE RSP51-O-SAIL-ID       TO RSP60-I-SAIL-ID.   
           MOVE BKCRV1-DURATION-DAYS  TO RSP60-I-DURATION-DAYS. 
           MOVE RSP50-O-CUSTOMER-NUM  TO RSP60-I-CUSTOMER-NUM.  
           MOVE WS-NUM-PAX            TO RSP60-I-NUM-PAX.   
           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1' AND  
              ((NOT RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT) OR 
                   (ADD-PAX-ONLY) OR (CHANGE-CABIN-ONLY))   
              IF RSP06-IBR-REQ-RATE-VERSION OF RSP06-OLD > 0
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-REQ-RATE-VERSION OF RSP06-OLD   
                                     TO RSP60-I-RATE-VERSION-NUM
              END-IF
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-OLD (1)  
                                      TO RSP60-I-OFFER-ID   
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (1)
                                      TO RSP60-I-OFFER-CODE 
              MOVE 1 TO RSP60-OVERRIDE-OFFER-CLS-FLG
              EXEC SQL  
                   SELECT RESTRICTED_CABIN_ONLY_IND 
                     INTO WS-RST-CAB-IND
                     FROM RATE_MASTER   
                    WHERE PRP_OWNER_ID = :RSP06-ENTITY-OWN-ID OF
                                          RSP06-OLD(1 1)
                      AND PRP_OWNER_CODE = 'RTE'
                    FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY  
              END-EXEC  
              IF SQLCODE = 100  
                 MOVE SPACES TO RSP60-I-CABIN-RATE-CODE 
              END-IF
              IF WS-RST-CAB-IND = 'Y'   
                 MOVE RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE OF RSP06-OLD(1) TO   
                                              RSP60-I-CABIN-RATE-CODE   
              ELSE  
                 MOVE SPACES         TO RSP60-I-CABIN-RATE-CODE 
              END-IF
              MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-OWN-ID OF RSP06-OLD(1 1)
                                     TO RSP60-I-RATE-MASTER-OWNER-ID

           ELSE 
              MOVE WS-OFFER-ID        TO RSP60-I-OFFER-ID   
              MOVE WS-OFFER-CODE      TO RSP60-I-OFFER-CODE 
           END-IF.  
           IF BKCRV1-UPGRADE-CODE (1) > SPACES  
              MOVE BKCRV1-UPGRADE-CODE (1)  
                                      TO RSP60-I-CPA-CAT-CODE   
           ELSE 
              MOVE BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE (1) 
                                      TO RSP60-I-CPA-CAT-CODE   
           END-IF.  
           MOVE BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE (1)
                                      TO RSP60-I-CATEGORY-CODE. 
           IF CCL-GRP-RATE-WITH-VERSION 
              MOVE CCR-GRP-VERSION-NUM  
                                      TO RSP60-I-RATE-VERSION-NUM   
           END-IF.  
           MOVE RSP51-I-BOOK-TYPE     TO RSP60-I-BOOK-TYPE. 
           MOVE 999                   TO RSP60-I-CABINS-PER-CAT 
                                         RSP60-I-CABINS-LIST-MAX.   
           MOVE BKCRV1-SHIP-CODE      TO RSP60-I-SHIP-CODE. 

           IF  BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) > SPACES AND
               BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) NOT = 'GUAR'
               IF  AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'Y' OR 'P' OR 'S'   
                                                OR 'L' OR 'R'   
                   MOVE 'A'           TO RSP60-I-WHEELCHAIR-IND 
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF CCP00-GROUP-BOOK-NUM > SPACES 
              MOVE CCP00-GROUP-BOOK-NUM
