                                      TO RSP60-I-GRP-BOOK-NUM   
           ELSE 
              MOVE IBR-GROUP-BOOK-NUM TO RSP60-I-GRP-BOOK-NUM   
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-IBR-REQ-CPA-CATEGORY OF RSP06-NEW > SPACES  
              MOVE 1                  TO RSP60-PROTECT-HELD-UPG-FLG 
           END-IF.  
           MOVE RSP50-O-CUSTOMER-NUM  TO RSP60-I-CUSTOMER-NUM.  
           MOVE 'N'                   TO RSP60-I-ALT-CABIN-IND. 

           IF DPP34-CABIN-NUM = RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD 
                                      TO RSP60-I-HELD-CABIN 
              MOVE 1 TO RSP60-RETURN-HELD-CABIN-FLG 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE DPP34-CABIN-NUM       TO RSP60-I-CABIN-NUM. 

           CALL 'RSO60CBV' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 RSP60-I-CABIN-AVAIL-INPUT  
                                 RSP60-O-CABIN-AVAIL-OUTPUT 
                                 RSP60-I-CABIN-AVAIL-FILTERS
                                 RSP90-OVERRIDE-FLAGS-INFO. 

           IF  DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR        > ZERO AND  
               DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1 
               ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
               MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) TO 
                    MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
               MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX  (1) TO 
                    CCP00-ADVISE-CODE   
               MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE        TO 
                    MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
               PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG
                  THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT   
               GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
           END-IF.  

           IF (DPP34-CABIN-NUM (1:1) NOT = 'T' AND 'C') AND 
              (RSP60-O-CABIN-NUM (1) NOT = RSP60-I-CABIN-NUM)   
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0029       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           IF (DPP34-CABIN-NUM (1:1) = 'T' OR 'C') AND  
               RSP60-O-GTEE-ALLOW-IND NOT = 'Y' 
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0214       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

       2500-CALL-RSO39CAH.  
       COPY RSP39INT.   
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD 
                                        TO RSP39-I-CABIN-BOOK-NUM.  
           IF CCL-CORP-GRP-IND  
              MOVE GBR1-BOOK-NUM        TO RSP39-I-GRP-BOOK-NUM 
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES               TO RSP39-I-GRP-BOOK-NUM 
           END-IF.  
           MOVE RSP51-I-BOOK-TYPE       TO RSP39-I-BOOK-TYPE.   
           MOVE BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE(1) TO RSP39-I-CATEGORY-CODE
                                           RSP39-I-REQ-CAT-CODE.
           IF DPP34-CABIN-NUM (1:1) = 'T'   
              MOVE DPP34-CABIN-NUM         TO RSP39-I-CABIN-NUMBER  
           ELSE 
              MOVE RSP60-I-CABIN-NUM       TO RSP39-I-CABIN-NUMBER  
           END-IF.  
           MOVE RSP51-I-NUM-PAX         TO RSP39-I-NUM-PAX. 
           MOVE WS-OFFER-CODE           TO RSP39-I-OFFER-CODE.  
           IF CCL-GRP-RATE-WITH-VERSION 
               MOVE CCR-GRP-VERSION-NUM TO RSP39-I-FF-RATE-VERSION  
           END-IF.  
           MOVE 'Y'                     TO RSP39-I-ELECT-DIST-IND.  
           MOVE 'Y'                     TO RSP39-I-UPDATE-IND.  
           MOVE 'N'                     TO RSP39-I-MUST-HOLD.   

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1' AND  
             ((NOT RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT) OR  
                   (ADD-PAX-ONLY) OR (CHANGE-CABIN-ONLY))   
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE OF RSP06-OLD(1) 
                                      TO RSP39-I-OFFER-CODE 
              MOVE ZEROES             TO RSP39-I-OFFER-ID(1)
                                         RSP39-I-OFFER-ID(2)
                                         RSP39-I-OFFER-ID(3)
                                         RSP39-I-OFFER-ID(4)
                                         RSP39-I-OFFER-ID(5)
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-REQ-RATE-VERSION OF RSP06-OLD  
                                      TO RSP39-I-FF-RATE-VERSION
              IF RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD < RSP51-I-NUM-PAX   
                 MOVE 'Y'              TO RSP39-I-ADD-PAX-IND   
              END-IF
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD TO RSP39-I-OCCUPANCY  
              IF  RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-OLD  = 'G'   
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-REQ-CATEGORY OF RSP06-OLD 
                                       TO RSP39-I-REQ-CAT-CODE  
              ELSE  
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD
                                       TO RSP39-I-REQ-CAT-CODE  
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
                  UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP51-I-NUM-PAX OR 
                        WS-SUB > 5  
                  MOVE WS-OFFER-ID    TO RSP39-I-OFFER-ID(WS-SUB)   
              END-PERFORM   
           END-IF.  

           STRING MSGHDR-SYSTEM-ID (1:3)
                  MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID (1:10)   
                  MSGHDR-USER-ID (1:6)  
                  DELIMITED BY SIZE 
             INTO RSP39-I-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION. 

           STRING '@' SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE DELIMITED BY SIZE   
                  INTO RSP39-I-OPER-INITIALS.   

           MOVE RSP39-I-OPER-INITIALS   TO RSP39-I-USERID   
                                         DPP01-USERID.  
           STRING '@' SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE DELIMITED BY SIZE   
                      INTO RSP39-I-USERID.  
           MOVE RSP39-I-USERID TO DPP01-USERID. 

207024*    IF RSP39-I-USERID = '@IA' OR '@UA'   
207024     IF RSP39-I-USERID = '@IA' OR '@UA' OR '@AU'  
              MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID          TO RSP39-I-GOCCL-ID  
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES                  TO RSP39-I-GOCCL-ID  
           END-IF.  

           IF BKCRV1-UPGRADE-CODE (1) > SPACES  
              MOVE BKCRV1-UPGRADE-CODE (1) TO RSP39-I-CPA-CAT-CODE  
           ELSE 
              MOVE BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE(1) TO RSP39-I-CPA-CAT-CODE  
           END-IF.  

           IF  BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) > SPACES AND
               BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) NOT = 'GUAR'
               IF  AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'Y' OR 'P' OR 'S'   
                                                OR 'L' OR 'R'   
                   MOVE '1'          TO RSP39-OVERRIDE-OCC-REST-FLG 
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           CALL 'RSO39CAH' USING  DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS  
                                  DPP01-COMMON-INFO 
                                  RSP39-CABIN-HOLD-INPUT
                                  RSP57-RATE-OUTPUT-PARMS   
                                  RSP54-OUTPUT-PARMS
                                  RSP54-INPUT-PARMS 
                                  RSP04-ADD-PAX-OUTPUT  
                                  RSP51-SAILING-AVAIL-OUTPUT
                                  RSP50-AGENCY-OUTPUT   
                                  RSP90-OVERRIDE-FLAGS-INFO 
                                  RSP89-SEC-RULE-TBL.   

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > ZERO AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1 
              ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) TO  
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1)  TO  
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-NUM (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                 THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
              GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
           END-IF.  

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > ZERO AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) = 1 
              IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1) = 21   
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CAH-NOT-NEEDED-SW   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

PPC470     MOVE 'Y'                    TO WS-CABIN-HOLD-ESTABLISH.  
           IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND 
               SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1)
              MOVE BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE (1) TO DPP80-CATEGORY-CODE  
              MOVE 'P'                      TO DPP80-EDIT-TYPE  
              PERFORM DPP80-GET-SAIL-CAT-CODE THRU DPP80-EXIT   
              IF TDP01-SPA-IND  (DPP80-SAIL-CAT-SUB) = 'Y'  
                 ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                 MOVE 0136       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC'
                                 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              IF AVL-CAB-SMOKING-FLG = 0
                 IF TDP01-BASE-CURRENCY-CODE = 'AUD'
                    ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                    MOVE 0209    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                    MOVE 'CCR08UGC' 
                                 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                 ELSE   
                    ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                    MOVE 0089    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                    MOVE 'CCR08UGC' 
                                 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
               UNTIL WS-SUB > TDP01-SAIL-CAT-CNT
               IF  IBR-CATEGORY-CODE =  
                   TDP01-CATEGORY-CODE (WS-SUB) 
                   IF TDP01-AGE-RESTRICTION-NI (WS-SUB) = 0 
                     MOVE 1  TO WS-APPLY-AGE-FLAG   
                     MOVE TDP01-AGE-RESTRICTION (WS-SUB) TO 
                          WS-RESTRICTION-AGE
                   ELSE 
                     MOVE ZEROES TO WS-APPLY-AGE-FLAG   
                                    WS-RESTRICTION-AGE  
                   END-IF   
                   MOVE TDP01-SAIL-CAT-CNT TO WS-SUB
               END-IF   
           END-PERFORM.

       3000-PROCESS-RSP06.  
           MOVE SPACES TO WS-FOR-CODE.  
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-INHOUSE-FEATURE-SW  
                       WS-CHECK-OLD-PAX-COMMENT-SW

                       RSP06-I-TOAR-READ-IND OF RSP06-OLD.  

           MOVE SPACES                   TO WS-AGT-CONTACT.

               MOVE MSGHDR-USER-NAME     TO WS-AGT-CONTACT

               MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID       TO WS-AGT-CONTACT

           IF SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1  
              MOVE INFO-USERID  
                TO RSP06-IBR-CPA-APPROVAL-USERID OF RSP06-NEW   
           ELSE 
              MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID   
                TO RSP06-IBR-CPA-APPROVAL-USERID OF RSP06-NEW

           IF  RSP06-IBR-ALLOW-REFUND-IND OF RSP06-OLD NOT > SPACES 
               IF  RSP51-I-BOOK-TYPE = 'G'

                     TO RSP06-IBR-ALLOW-REFUND-IND OF RSP06-NEW

           END-IF.  

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'  
              GO TO 3100-LOAD-RSP06-IBR-CHG-ONLY.   

           MOVE CAH-BOOK-TYPE   
             TO RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE          OF RSP06-NEW.

             TO RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE          OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE RSP51-O-SAIL-DATE   
             TO RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE          OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE RSP51-O-DURATION-DAYS   
             TO RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS      OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE DPP34-CABIN-NUM 
             TO RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM          OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE 'O' 
             TO RSP06-IBR-BOOK-STATUS        OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE   
             TO RSP06-IBR-STATUS-DATE        OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE DPP02-SYS-TIME-REDEF
             TO WS-STATUS-TIME. 
           MOVE WS-STATUS-TIME-HHMN 
             TO RSP06-IBR-STATUS-TIME        OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE 'N' 
             TO RSP06-IBR-WAITLIST-IND       OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE 'E' 
             TO RSP06-IBR-LANGUAGE-CODE      OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE RSP50-O-CUSTOMER-NUM
             TO RSP06-IBR-CUSTOMER-NUM       OF RSP06-NEW.  

           MOVE CAH-CATEGORY-CODE   
             TO RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE      OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE CAH-RATE-VERSION-NUM
             TO RSP06-IBR-REQ-RATE-VERSION   OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE CAH-PRC-XCHG-RATE (1)   
             TO RSP06-IBR-PRC-XCHG-RATE      OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE CAH-ACCT-XCHG-RATE  
             TO RSP06-IBR-ACCT-XCHG-RATE     OF RSP06-NEW.

             TO RSP06-IBR-REGION-CODE        OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE RSP50-O-SALES-REP-CODE  
             TO RSP06-IBR-SALES-REP-CODE     OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE 'N' 
             TO RSP06-IBR-WAIVE-PROG-FEE-IND OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE RSP50-O-COMPANY-CODE
             TO RSP06-IBR-COMPANY-CODE       OF RSP06-NEW.  

           IF RSP50-O-CUSTOMER-CODE = 'C'   
              MOVE 'Y' TO RSP06-IBR-DIRECT-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'N' TO RSP06-IBR-DIRECT-IND OF RSP06-NEW

194565                 WS-BTH-HOURS WS-BTH-HOURS-TYPE DELIMITED 
194565                 ' ' ' + '                      DELIMITED SIZE
194565                 WS-BTH-MAIL-DAYS WS-BTH-MAIL-TYPE
194565                                                DELIMITED ' ' 
194565                                           INTO WS-PAY-COMMENT
194565          END-IF  
194565        END-IF

194565        IF WS-BTH-DAYS > 0  AND WS-BTH-MAIL-DAYS = 0  
194565           STRING ' '                           DELIMITED SIZE
194565                  WS-BTH-DAYS WS-BTH-DAYS-TYPE  DELIMITED ' ' 
194565                                           INTO WS-PAY-COMMENT
194565        ELSE  
194565          IF WS-BTH-HOURS > 0  AND WS-BTH-MAIL-DAYS = 0   
194565             STRING ' '                         DELIMITED SIZE
194565                WS-BTH-HOURS WS-BTH-HOURS-TYPE  DELIMITED ' ' 
194565                                           INTO WS-PAY-COMMENT
194565          END-IF  
194565        END-IF
194565        MOVE WS-PAY-COMMENT TO RSP06-IBR-PAY-COMMENT OF RSP06-NEW 
194565     END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-IBR-USERID OF RSP06-NEW NOT > SPACES
              STRING '@' SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE DELIMITED BY SIZE
                         INTO RSP06-IBR-USERID OF RSP06-NEW 
           END-IF.  
           IF RSP06-IBR-AGENCY-CONN-CODE OF RSP06-NEW NOT > SPACES  
              MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE 
                  TO RSP06-IBR-AGENCY-CONN-CODE OF RSP06-NEW
           END-IF.  
           IF RSP06-IBR-LOCATION-CODE OF RSP06-NEW    NOT > SPACES  
              MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE
                   TO RSP06-IBR-LOCATION-CODE OF RSP06-NEW  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKCRV1-REMOTE-BOOKING-ID
             TO RSP06-IBR-AGENCY-CONN-ID     OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE RSP50-O-CURRENCY-CODE   
             TO RSP06-IBR-CURRENCY-CODE      OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE RSP50-O-LOCALITY-CODE   
             TO RSP06-IBR-LOCALITY-CODE      OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID (1:10)   
             TO RSP06-IBR-INTERNET-ID        OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE CAH-REQ-CPA 
             TO RSP06-IBR-REQ-CPA-CATEGORY   OF RSP06-NEW.  

           MOVE CAH-REQ-CPA 
             TO RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE  OF RSP06-NEW.  

           MOVE CAH-REQ-CAT-CODE
             TO RSP06-IBR-REQ-CATEGORY       OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE 1   
             TO RSP06-IBR-LAST-UPDATE-NUM    OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE CAH-CABIN-POOL-CODE 
             TO RSP06-IBR-CABIN-SOURCE-CODE  OF RSP06-NEW.  
           IF CCL-CORP-GRP-IND  
              MOVE GBR1-BOOK-NUM
                TO RSP06-IBR-GROUP-BOOK-NUM  OF RSP06-NEW   
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES   
                TO RSP06-IBR-GROUP-BOOK-NUM  OF RSP06-NEW   
           END-IF.  
           MOVE MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID
             TO RSP06-IBR-REMOTE-AGENCY-ID   OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE 'N' 
             TO RSP06-IBR-FULL-PENALTY-IND   OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE RSP50-O-ALT-COMM-CODE   
             TO RSP06-IBR-ALT-COMM-CODE      OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE RSP51-O-INDV-POLICY-PHASE-NUM   
             TO RSP06-IBR-POLICY-PHASE-NUM   OF RSP06-NEW.  

           GO TO 3150-PROCESS-PAX.  

       3100-LOAD-RSP06-IBR-CHG-ONLY.

           IF RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (1:1)
              = DPP34-CABIN-NUM (1:1) AND   
              RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM   OF RSP06-OLD (1:1) = 'T'
              IF CAH-NOT-NEEDED OR WS-NUM-PAX-TWO = 'Y' 
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM          OF RSP06-OLD 
                   TO RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM          OF RSP06-NEW 
              ELSE  
                 MOVE DPP34-CABIN-NUM   
                   TO RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM          OF RSP06-NEW 
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              IF RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (1:1) = 'C' AND   
                 DPP34-CABIN-NUM (1:1)  = 'C' OR 'T'
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM          OF RSP06-OLD 
                   TO RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM          OF RSP06-NEW 
              ELSE  
                 MOVE DPP34-CABIN-NUM   
                   TO RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM          OF RSP06-NEW.

           IF NOT CAH-NOT-NEEDED
              MOVE CAH-CATEGORY-CODE
                TO RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE      OF RSP06-NEW
              MOVE CAH-REQ-CPA  
                TO RSP06-IBR-REQ-CPA-CATEGORY   OF RSP06-NEW
              MOVE CAH-RATE-VERSION-NUM 
                TO RSP06-IBR-REQ-RATE-VERSION   OF RSP06-NEW
              IF CAH-CATEGORY-CODE NOT = CAH-REQ-CPA
                 MOVE CAH-REQ-CPA   
                   TO RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE      OF RSP06-NEW 
              ELSE  
                 MOVE CAH-REQ-CPA   
                   TO RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE      OF RSP06-NEW 
              END-IF
              MOVE CAH-REQ-CAT-CODE 
                TO RSP06-IBR-REQ-CATEGORY       OF RSP06-NEW
              MOVE CAH-CABIN-POOL-CODE  
                TO RSP06-IBR-CABIN-SOURCE-CODE  OF RSP06-NEW
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-TAKEOVER = 1   
              MOVE 'N' TO RSP06-IBR-DIRECT-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
              MOVE RSP50-O-CUSTOMER-NUM TO RSP06-IBR-CUSTOMER-NUM   
                                               OF RSP06-NEW 
              MOVE RSP50-O-ALT-CUSTOMER-NUM TO  
                   RSP06-IBR-ALT-CUSTOMER-NUM  OF RSP06-NEW.

219891     IF ((SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND
               SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 0) AND
              (MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID (1:7)  =
219891          CCP00-OLD-AGT-CUST-NUM )) OR
219891        (MSGHDR-SYSTEM-ID = 'NLG')
              IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW  = 'I'
                 IF MSGHDR-ALT-AGENCY-ID (1:10) > SPACE 
                    MOVE MSGHDR-ALT-AGENCY-ID (1:10)
                              TO WS-MSG-ID-AN   
219891              MOVE RSP50-O-ALT-CUSTOMER-NUM TO
219891              RSP06-IBR-ALT-CUSTOMER-NUM  OF RSP06-NEW
                 ELSE   
                  MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-IBR-ALT-CUSTOMER-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW  = 'G' 
                    IF GBR1-ALT-CUSTOMER-NUM > SPACES   
                       CONTINUE 
                    ELSE
                       IF MSGHDR-ALT-AGENCY-ID (1:10) > SPACE   
                             MOVE RSP50-O-ALT-CUSTOMER-NUM    TO
                             RSP06-IBR-ALT-CUSTOMER-NUM  OF RSP06-NEW   
                       ELSE 
                          IF RSP50-O-WHOLESALE-IND = 'Y' OR 'G' OR 'O'  
                             MOVE RSP06-IBR-CUSTOMER-NUM
                                   OF RSP06-NEW TO  
                               RSP06-IBR-ALT-CUSTOMER-NUM OF RSP06-NEW  
                          END-IF
                       END-IF   
                    END-IF  
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

      *=================================================================
       3150-PROCESS-PAX.
           MOVE ZEROS TO NEW-PAX-SUB.   
           MOVE ZEROS TO WS-CXP-CNT.
           MOVE ZEROS TO BKGINF-SUB.
           MOVE ZEROS TO BKGACT-SUB.
           MOVE 'N'   TO WS-RATE-CHANGED-SW 
                         WS-PAX-ADDED-SW.   

       3200-LOAD-RSP06-PAX. 

           ADD 1 TO BKGACT-SUB. 

           IF BKGACT-SUB > BKGACT-PAX-CNT   
              GO TO 3580-VALIDATE-MOB-TEXT-IND  
           END-IF.  

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'  
              MOVE ZEROS TO OLD-PAX-SUB 
              MOVE ZEROS TO BKGINF-SUB  
              IF BKGACT-ACTION-CODE (BKGACT-SUB) = 'D'  
                 ADD 1            TO WS-CXP-CNT 
                 GO TO 3200-LOAD-RSP06-PAX  
              END-IF
              PERFORM VARYING I FROM 1 BY 1 
                      UNTIL I > BKGACT-PAX-CNT  
                 IF BKGACT-PAX-SEQ-NUM (BKGACT-SUB) =   
                    BKGINF-PAX-SEQ-NUM (I)  
                    MOVE I              TO BKGINF-SUB   
                    MOVE BKGACT-PAX-CNT TO I
                 END-IF 
              END-PERFORM   
              IF BKGINF-SUB = ZEROS 
                 ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                 MOVE 0031       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
              ELSE  
                 ADD 1 TO NEW-PAX-SUB   
              END-IF
              IF BKGACT-ACTION-CODE (BKGACT-SUB) = 'A'  
                 MOVE 'Y'               TO WS-PAX-ADDED-SW  
                 GO TO 3250-LOAD-CONTINUE   
              END-IF
              PERFORM VARYING I FROM 1 BY 1 
                      UNTIL I > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD  
                 IF BKGACT-PAX-SEQ-NUM (BKGACT-SUB) =   
                    RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (I)  
                    MOVE RSP06-PAX-ENTRY OF RSP06-OLD (I)   
                      TO RSP06-PAX-ENTRY OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                    MOVE RSP06-PAX-EXIST-INDS OF RSP06-OLD (I) TO   
                         RSP06-PAX-EXIST-INDS OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                    MOVE RSP06-PAX-READ-INDS  OF RSP06-OLD (I) TO   
                         RSP06-PAX-READ-INDS  OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                    MOVE RSP06-N-PAX-PARAMS   OF RSP06-OLD (I) TO   
                         RSP06-N-PAX-PARAMS   OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                    MOVE I                             TO OLD-PAX-SUB   
                    MOVE RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD    TO I 
                 END-IF 
              END-PERFORM   
           ELSE 
              ADD 1 TO NEW-PAX-SUB  
              ADD 1 TO BKGINF-SUB   
           END-IF.  

       3250-LOAD-CONTINUE.  

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'  
              MOVE ALL 'Y' TO RSP06-READ-INDS OF RSP06-NEW  
                              RSP06-READ-INDS OF RSP06-OLD  
           END-IF.  

           IF PAX-ADDED 
              MOVE 'Y' TO RSP06-PTFR-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                          RSP06-PPR-READ-IND  OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                          RSP06-PXFR-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                          RSP06-TIR-READ-IND  OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                          RSP06-PIAR-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                          RSP06-PIER-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                          RSP06-PIID-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                          RSP06-PICF-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                         RSP06-PPHOR-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                          RSP06-PPIR-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
147834                    RSP06-POBC-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
           END-IF.  

           MOVE NEW-PAX-SUB 
             TO RSP06-PAX-CABIN-SEQ-NUM      OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           IF BKGINF-NATIONALITY-CODE (BKGINF-SUB) > SPACES 
              MOVE BKGINF-NATIONALITY-CODE (BKGINF-SUB) 
                TO RSP06-PAX-NATIONALITY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES   
                TO RSP06-PAX-NATIONALITY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PAX-NATIONALITY-CODE      OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                                             > SPACES   
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              IF RSP50-O-NATIONALITY-CODE > SPACES  
                 MOVE RSP50-O-NATIONALITY-CODE  
                   TO RSP06-PAX-NATIONALITY-CODE       OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                  (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 'US'  
                   TO RSP06-PAX-NATIONALITY-CODE       OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                  (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKGINF-LAST-NAME (BKGINF-SUB)   
             TO RSP06-PAX-LAST-NAME          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE BKGINF-FIRST-NAME (BKGINF-SUB)  
             TO RSP06-PAX-FIRST-NAME         OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           MOVE SPACES        TO WS-STRING-AREA.
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-FIRST-NAME         OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                              TO WS-STRING-AREA.
           MOVE 10            TO WS-STRING-LENGTH.  
           PERFORM 8800-CONVERT-CASE THRU 8800-EXIT.
           MOVE WS-STRING-AREA  
             TO RSP06-PAX-FIRST-NAME         OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           MOVE SPACES        TO WS-STRING-AREA.
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-LAST-NAME          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                              TO WS-STRING-AREA.
           MOVE 15            TO WS-STRING-LENGTH.  
           PERFORM 8800-CONVERT-CASE THRU 8800-EXIT.
           MOVE WS-STRING-AREA  
             TO RSP06-PAX-LAST-NAME          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           IF NEW-PAX-SUB = 1   
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-LAST-NAME       OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                TO RSP06-IBR-PRIMARY-NAME    OF RSP06-NEW   
           END-IF.  

           IF  BKGINF-GENDER-CODE (BKGINF-SUB) = 'M'
               IF  BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT  (BKGINF-SUB) <= 23 AND
                   BKGINF-AGE-CODE    (BKGINF-SUB) = 'M'
                   MOVE 'INFT'  
                          TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                             CCP00-TITLE-CODE   
               ELSE 
                   IF  BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT  (BKGINF-SUB) < 2  
                       MOVE 'INFT'  
                          TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                             CCP00-TITLE-CODE   
                   ELSE 
                      IF  ((BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT  (BKGINF-SUB) >= 2) AND   
                           (BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT    (BKGINF-SUB) <= 12))   
                           MOVE 'MSTR'  
                           TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                              CCP00-TITLE-CODE  
                      ELSE  
                           IF BKGINF-TITLE-CODE (BKGINF-SUB) = 'MSTR'   
                              MOVE 'MR' TO  
                              RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                              CCP00-TITLE-CODE  
                           ELSE 
                              MOVE BKGINF-TITLE-CODE (BKGINF-SUB)   
                              TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF 
                                 RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                              CCP00-TITLE-CODE  
                           END-IF   
                      END-IF
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
           ELSE 
               IF  BKGINF-GENDER-CODE (BKGINF-SUB) = 'F'
                   IF  BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT  (BKGINF-SUB) <= 23 AND
                       BKGINF-AGE-CODE    (BKGINF-SUB) = 'M'
                       MOVE 'INFT'  
                        TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                           CCP00-TITLE-CODE 
                   ELSE 
                       IF  BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT  (BKGINF-SUB) < 2  
                           MOVE 'INFT'  
                           TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                              CCP00-TITLE-CODE  
                       ELSE 
                          IF ((BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT  (BKGINF-SUB) >= 2) AND
                              (BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT    (BKGINF-SUB) <= 12))
                           MOVE 'MISS'  
                           TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                              CCP00-TITLE-CODE  
                          ELSE  
                           IF BKGINF-TITLE-CODE (BKGINF-SUB) = 'MISS'   
                              MOVE 'MS' TO  
                              RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                              CCP00-TITLE-CODE  
                           ELSE 
                              MOVE BKGINF-TITLE-CODE (BKGINF-SUB) TO
                              RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                              CCP00-TITLE-CODE  
                           END-IF   
                          END-IF
                       END-IF   
                   END-IF   
               ELSE 
                   IF BKGINF-GENDER-CODE (BKGINF-SUB) = 'I' 
                      IF (BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT  (BKGINF-SUB) <= 23 AND 
                          BKGINF-AGE-CODE    (BKGINF-SUB) = 'M') OR 
                         (BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT  (BKGINF-SUB) < 2)  
                         MOVE 'INFT'
                          TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                             CCP00-TITLE-CODE   
                      END-IF
                   ELSE 
                      IF BKGINF-GENDER-CODE (BKGINF-SUB) = 'C'  
                      IF  ((BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT  (BKGINF-SUB) >= 2) AND   
                           (BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT    (BKGINF-SUB) <= 12))   
                           MOVE BKGINF-TITLE-CODE (BKGINF-SUB)  
                           TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                              CCP00-TITLE-CODE  
                      END-IF
                      END-IF
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE SPACES        TO WS-STRING-AREA.
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-TITLE              OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
             TO WS-STRING-AREA. 
           MOVE 4             TO WS-STRING-LENGTH.  
           PERFORM 8800-CONVERT-CASE THRU 8800-EXIT.
           MOVE WS-STRING-AREA  
             TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE              OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                CCP00-TITLE-CODE.   

           PERFORM CCP10-GET-TITLE THRU CCP10-TITLE-EXIT.   
           IF CCP00-SUB NOT > ZERO  
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0023       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  
           IF BKGINF-EREF (BKGINF-SUB) > SPACES 
              MOVE BKGINF-EREF (BKGINF-SUB) 
                TO RSP06-PIER-EMAIL-ADDR OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
              MOVE 'Y' TO RSP06-PIER-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                         RSP06-PIER-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                         RSP06-PIAR-READ-IND  OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                         RSP06-PIAR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES   
                TO RSP06-PIER-EMAIL-ADDR OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
              MOVE 'Y' TO RSP06-PIER-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                         RSP06-PIAR-READ-IND  OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                         RSP06-PIAR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
              MOVE 'N' TO   
                        RSP06-PIER-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGINF-BIRTH-DATE (BKGINF-SUB) > ZERO 
              MOVE BKGINF-BIRTH-DATE (BKGINF-SUB)   
                TO DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE  
              PERFORM DPP10-MMDDYYYY-TO-YYYYMMDD
              MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE  
                TO RSP06-PAX-BIRTH-DATE      OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                   DPP02-BD-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
              MOVE RSP51-O-SAIL-DATE TO DPP02-SAIL-DATE 
              PERFORM DPP10-COMPUTE-GUEST-AGE   
              IF  DPP02-IS-DATE-VALID = 'N' 
                  ADD 1              TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                  MOVE 0150          TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE  
                                                         (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                  GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              MOVE ZEROES TO DPP02-YEAR-CNT 
                             DPP02-MONTH-CNT
                   RSP06-PAX-BIRTH-DATE      OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKGINF-AGE         (BKGINF-SUB) TO CCMV-PAX-AGE-X.  
           MOVE BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT  (BKGINF-SUB) TO CCMV-PAX-AGE.
           MOVE BKGINF-AGE-CODE    (BKGINF-SUB) TO CCMV-AGE-CODE.   
           MOVE BKGINF-GENDER-CODE (BKGINF-SUB) TO  
                RSP06-PAX-SEX                OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           MOVE SPACES        TO WS-STRING-AREA.
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-SEX                OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                              TO WS-STRING-AREA.
           MOVE  1            TO WS-STRING-LENGTH.  
           PERFORM 8800-CONVERT-CASE THRU 8800-EXIT.
           MOVE WS-STRING-AREA  
             TO RSP06-PAX-SEX                OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           IF DPP02-YEAR-CNT > 1
              MOVE 'Y'                          TO CCMV-AGE-CODE
              MOVE DPP02-YEAR-CNT               TO CCMV-PAX-AGE 
           ELSE 
              IF DPP02-MONTH-CNT > ZERO 
                 MOVE 'M'                       TO CCMV-AGE-CODE
                 MOVE DPP02-MONTH-CNT           TO CCMV-PAX-AGE 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM CCP75-DETERMINE-SEX-AGE THRU CCP75-DSA-EXIT. 

           IF CCMV-PAX-AGE > 99 
              MOVE 99   
                TO RSP06-PAX-AGE             OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
           ELSE 
              MOVE CCMV-PAX-AGE 
                TO RSP06-PAX-AGE             OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           IF CCMV-PAX-SEX > SPACE  
              MOVE CCMV-PAX-SEX 
                TO RSP06-PAX-SEX             OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE CCMV-AGE-CODE   
             TO RSP06-PAX-AGE-CODE           OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           IF  BKGINF-GENDER-CODE (BKGINF-SUB) = 'M' AND
               BKGINF-AGE-CODE    (BKGINF-SUB) = 'Y'
               IF  ((RSP06-PAX-AGE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) >= 2) AND 
                   (RSP06-PAX-AGE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) <= 12))
                   MOVE 'MSTR'  
                       TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
               ELSE 
                   IF  RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) = 
                       'MSTR'   
                       MOVE 'MR'
                       TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                   ELSE 
                       MOVE BKGINF-TITLE-CODE (BKGINF-SUB)  
                       TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
           ELSE 
               IF  BKGINF-GENDER-CODE (BKGINF-SUB) = 'F' AND
                   BKGINF-AGE-CODE    (BKGINF-SUB) = 'Y'
                   IF  ((RSP06-PAX-AGE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) >= 2) 
                       AND  
                       (RSP06-PAX-AGE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) <= 12))
                       MOVE 'MISS'  
                          TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                   ELSE 
                       IF BKGINF-TITLE-CODE (BKGINF-SUB) = 'MISS'   
                          MOVE 'MS' 
                          TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                       ELSE 
                          MOVE BKGINF-TITLE-CODE (BKGINF-SUB)   
                          TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                       END-IF   
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PAX-AGE-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) = 'Y'   
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'INFT'   
                          TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE SPACES    TO WS-STRING-AREA.
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-TITLE              OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                          TO WS-STRING-AREA.
           MOVE 4         TO WS-STRING-LENGTH.  
           PERFORM 8800-CONVERT-CASE THRU 8800-EXIT.
           MOVE WS-STRING-AREA  
                          TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           IF WS-APPLY-AGE-FLAG = 1 AND RSP25-OVR-AGE-RESTRICT-IND = 0  
              IF RSP06-PAX-AGE-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) = 'M'
                 COMPUTE WS-MIN-AGE =   
                        RSP06-PAX-AGE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) / 12   
              ELSE  
                 MOVE RSP06-PAX-AGE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                        TO WS-MIN-AGE   
              END-IF
              IF WS-MIN-AGE < WS-RESTRICTION-AGE
                  ADD 1          TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                  MOVE 0172      TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                  IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'   
                     MOVE 0171   TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                  END-IF
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.  

           MOVE ZEROS TO WS-STD-INS-CNT WS-GOLD-INS-CNT.
           MOVE 'N' TO RSP06-PAX-PP-GRATS-IND OF RSP06-NEW  
                                                       (NEW-PAX-SUB).   
           MOVE SPACES  
             TO RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-TABLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                RSP06-PAX-COMMENT       OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB). 

           IF TDP01-TRANSFER-CUTOFF-DATE-NI = TDP01-NULL-IND
              MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TRANSFER-CUTOFF-DAYS
           ELSE 
              EXEC SQL  
                   SELECT DATEDIFF('DAY',:TDP01-TRANSFER-CUTOFF-DATE,   
                                    :TDP01-SAIL-DATE)   
                     INTO :WS-TRANSFER-CUTOFF-DAYS  
                     FROM RDMS.RDMS_DUMMY   
PPC470          FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY  
              END-EXEC  
              IF SQLCODE < 0 OR SQLCODE = 100   
                 GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
              END-IF
           END-IF   

           MOVE TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD
                                       TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE.  
           MOVE WS-TRANSFER-CUTOFF-DAYS 
                                       TO DPP02-NUMBER-DAYS.
           PERFORM DPP10-DEMINISH-YYYYMMDD-DATE.
           IF DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE >= DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE  
              MOVE 'Y'                 TO WS-WITHIN-XFER-DAYS-SW
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'N'                 TO WS-WITHIN-XFER-DAYS-SW
           END-IF.  

           IF NOT WITHIN-XFER-DAYS AND  
              RSP06-PICF-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) NOT = 'Y' 
              MOVE SPACES   
                TO RSP06-PAX-ARRIVAL-CITY  OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                   RSP06-PAX-DEPART-CITY   OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
           END-IF.  

           IF OLD-PAX-SUB > 0   
              IF WS-PAX-COMMENT-INHOUSE (OLD-PAX-SUB) > SPACES  
                 MOVE WS-PAX-COMMENT-INHOUSE (OLD-PAX-SUB)  
                   TO RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-TABLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
              END-IF.   

           IF  OLD-PAX-SUB > 0  
               IF  WS-PAX-COMMENT (OLD-PAX-SUB) > SPACES
                   MOVE WS-PAX-COMMENT (OLD-PAX-SUB)
                     TO RSP06-PAX-COMMENT OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           INITIALIZE RSP06-PTFR-INFO OF RSP06-NEW  
                      (NEW-PAX-SUB) 

           PERFORM VARYING WS-BKPUCC-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL  
                            (WS-BKPUCC-SUB > BKPUCC-SEGMENT-MAX) OR 
                 (BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (WS-BKPUCC-SUB) = 'Y' OR 'N') 
              CONTINUE  
           END-PERFORM. 
           IF WS-BKPUCC-SUB > 0 AND WS-BKPUCC-SUB <= BKPUCC-SEGMENT-MAX 
               IF  (BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (WS-BKPUCC-SUB) =   
                                                            'Y' OR 'N') 
                   PERFORM VARYING WS-BKGSVC-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL  
                            (WS-BKGSVC-SUB > BKGSVC-SEGMENT-MAX) OR 
                      (BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (WS-BKGSVC-SUB) (1:2) = 'FP')
                       CONTINUE 
                   END-PERFORM  
                   IF WS-BKGSVC-SUB > 0 
                      IF  (WS-BKGSVC-SUB > BKGSVC-SEGMENT-MAX)  
                          ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                          MOVE 0068       TO
                                 MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                          GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
                      END-IF
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF  IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE = 'A' or 'R'
               PERFORM VARYING WS-BKGSVC-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL  
                        (WS-BKGSVC-SUB > BKGSVC-SEGMENT-MAX) OR 
                   (BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (WS-BKGSVC-SUB) (1:2) = 'FP')   
                   CONTINUE 
               END-PERFORM  
               IF  WS-BKGSVC-SUB > BKGSVC-SEGMENT-MAX   
                   PERFORM 5800-CANCEL-AUTOPAY THRU 
                                     5800-CANCEL-AUTOPAY-EXIT   
               ELSE 
                   CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
               CONTINUE 
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM 5800-PROCESS-SPC-SERV THRU 5800-EXIT.

           MOVE 'N'                    TO WS-PYMT-MADE. 

           PERFORM VARYING WS-BKPUCC-SUB2 FROM 1 BY 1   
               UNTIL WS-BKPUCC-SUB2 > BKPUCC-SEGMENT-MAX
               IF  BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (WS-BKPUCC-SUB2) > 0  
                   MOVE 'Y'            TO WS-PYMT-MADE  
               END-IF   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-CONF-REQ NOT = 'Y' 
              IF  ( NOT RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT ) AND   
                  ( WS-PYMT-MADE = 'Y' )
                    CONTINUE
              ELSE  
                  IF RSP06-IBR-GTEE-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD > SPACES 
                     IF RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (1:3) NOT =
                                                        'TBA' AND 'CSV' 
                        IF RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD NOT =   
                           RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                           ADD 1       TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                           MOVE 0030   TO   
                                       MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
                        END-IF  
                     END-IF 
                  END-IF
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF  WS-PTFR-SUB > ZEROS AND OLD-PAX-SUB > 0  
               MOVE WS-PTFR-SUB 
                   TO RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
               IF  RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                      (NEW-PAX-SUB) > 0 
                   MOVE 'Y' 
                   TO RSP06-PAX-CRUO-TRANSFER-IND OF RSP06-NEW  
                                                           (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                   MOVE 'Y' TO  
                        RSP06-PTFR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
               END-IF   
               MOVE RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT OF RSP06-OLD(OLD-PAX-SUB)   
                   TO DPP03-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT   
               MOVE RSP06-PTFR-INFO OF RSP06-OLD        (OLD-PAX-SUB)   
                   TO DPP03-PTFR-INFO   
               MOVE 'Y' 
                   TO RSP06-PTFR-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW     (NEW-PAX-SUB)
               MOVE DPP03-PTFR-CALC-KEY 
                   TO RSP06-PTFR-CALC-KEY OF RSP06-NEW     (NEW-PAX-SUB)
               MOVE DPP03-PTFR-PRE-DECLINE-IND  
                   TO RSP06-PTFR-PRE-DECLINE-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
                                                           (NEW-PAX-SUB)
               MOVE DPP03-PTFR-POST-DECLINE-IND 
                   TO RSP06-PTFR-POST-DECLINE-IND OF RSP06-NEW  
                                                           (NEW-PAX-SUB)

               PERFORM VARYING WS-XFR FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL 
                   WS-XFR > RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT 
                                              OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                   IF  ( RSP06-PTFR-PRE-POST-IND       OF RSP06-NEW 
                         (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-XFR) = 'I' )   
                       MOVE 1          TO DPP03-PTFR-SUB
                       PERFORM UNTIL
                          ( DPP03-PTFR-SUB > DPP03-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT )  
                       OR (    ( DPP03-PTFR-PRE-POST-CODE   
                                            (DPP03-PTFR-SUB) = 'I' )
                            AND(   ( WS-TRANS-TYPE (WS-TRANS-SUB) = 'A')
                                OR ( DPP03-PTFR-LOC-CODE
                                            (DPP03-PTFR-SUB) =  
                                     RSP06-PTFR-LOC-CODE OF RSP06-NEW   
                                            (NEW-PAX-SUB, WS-XFR)   
                                   )
                               )
                          ) 
                           ADD 1                 TO DPP03-PTFR-SUB  
                       END-PERFORM  
                       IF  ( DPP03-PTFR-SUB <= DPP03-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT )
                            MOVE DPP03-PTFR-DETAIL (DPP03-PTFR-SUB) 
                                       TO RSP06-PTFR-DETAIL OF RSP06-NEW
                                                   (NEW-PAX-SUB, WS-XFR)
                           IF  DPP03-PTFR-CANCEL-DATE (DPP03-PTFR-SUB)  
                                                           > 0  
                               MOVE 0 TO RSP06-PTFR-CANCEL-DATE OF  
                                         RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB, WS-XFR)
                               MOVE RSP06-IBR-CURRENCY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW
                                 TO RSP06-PTFR-CURRENCY-CODE OF 
                                    RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB, WS-XFR) 
                               MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-PTFR-PURCHASE-SRC OF
                                        RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB, WS-XFR) 
                           END-IF   
                       END-IF   
                   END-IF   
                   IF  ( RSP06-PTFR-PRE-POST-IND       OF RSP06-NEW 
                         (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-XFR) = 'O' )   
                        MOVE 1         TO DPP03-PTFR-SUB
                       PERFORM UNTIL
                          ( DPP03-PTFR-SUB > DPP03-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT )  
                       OR (    ( DPP03-PTFR-PRE-POST-CODE   
                                            (DPP03-PTFR-SUB) = 'O' )
                            AND(   ( WS-TRANS-TYPE (WS-TRANS-SUB) = 'A')
                                OR ( DPP03-PTFR-LOC-CODE
                                            (DPP03-PTFR-SUB) =  
                                     RSP06-PTFR-LOC-CODE OF RSP06-NEW   
                                            (NEW-PAX-SUB, WS-XFR)   
                                   )
                               )
                          ) 
                           ADD 1       TO DPP03-PTFR-SUB
                       END-PERFORM  
                       IF  ( DPP03-PTFR-SUB <= DPP03-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT )
                           MOVE DPP03-PTFR-DETAIL (DPP03-PTFR-SUB)  
                                       TO RSP06-PTFR-DETAIL OF RSP06-NEW
                                                   (NEW-PAX-SUB, WS-XFR)
                           IF  DPP03-PTFR-CANCEL-DATE (DPP03-PTFR-SUB)  
                                                           > 0  
                               MOVE 0 TO RSP06-PTFR-CANCEL-DATE OF  
                                         RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB, WS-XFR)
                               MOVE RSP06-IBR-CURRENCY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW
                                 TO RSP06-PTFR-CURRENCY-CODE OF 
                                    RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB, WS-XFR) 
                               MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-PTFR-PURCHASE-SRC OF
                                        RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB, WS-XFR) 
                           END-IF   
                       END-IF   
                   END-IF   
               END-PERFORM  
               IF  NOT WITHIN-XFER-DAYS 
                   CONTINUE 
               ELSE 
                   IF  ( RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT OF RSP06-NEW   
                         (NEW-PAX-SUB) > 0 )
                                                                     AND
                       ( RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT OF 
                         RSP06-OLD (OLD-PAX-SUB) < 1 )  
                         ADD 1         TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                         MOVE 0087     TO   
                                       MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                       GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF  WITHIN-XFER-DAYS                                      AND
               OLD-PAX-SUB > 0  
               MOVE RSP06-PTFR-INFO OF RSP06-OLD (OLD-PAX-SUB)  
                                       TO   
                    RSP06-PTFR-INFO OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
           ELSE 
              IF  OLD-PAX-SUB > 0   
                MOVE 0                   TO WS-OXFR 
                                            WS-NXFR 
                PERFORM VARYING WS-OXFR FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL   
                   WS-OXFR > RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT OF 
                                    RSP06-OLD (OLD-PAX-SUB) 
                   IF  RSP06-PTFR-CANCEL-DATE OF
                              RSP06-OLD (OLD-PAX-SUB WS-OXFR) > 0   
                       MOVE 0            TO WS-XFR  
                       PERFORM VARYING WS-NXFR FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL
                          WS-NXFR > RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT OF  
                                           RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                          IF  RSP06-PTFR-PRE-POST-IND OF
                                      RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-NXFR)   
                                        =   
                              RSP06-PTFR-PRE-POST-IND OF
                                      RSP06-OLD (OLD-PAX-SUB WS-OXFR)   
                              MOVE WS-NXFR  
                                         TO WS-XFR  
                              MOVE RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT OF RSP06-NEW 
                                   (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                                         TO WS-NXFR 
                          END-IF
                       END-PERFORM  
                       IF  WS-XFR = 0   
                           ADD 1         TO RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT OF  
                                                 RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                           MOVE RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT OF  
                                        RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                                         TO WS-NXFR 
                           MOVE RSP06-PTFR-DETAIL OF
                                        RSP06-OLD (OLD-PAX-SUB WS-OXFR) 
                                         TO 
                                RSP06-PTFR-DETAIL OF
                                        RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-NXFR) 
                       END-IF   
                   END-IF   
                END-PERFORM 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGINF-INSURANCE-IND (BKGINF-SUB) = 'Y'   
              AND (RSP50-O-CURRENCY-CODE = SSP05-O-CURRENCY-CODE)   
              IF WS-STD-INS-CNT = 1 AND WS-GOLD-INS-CNT = 1 
                 ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                 MOVE 0032       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
              ELSE  
                 IF WS-STD-INS-CNT = 1 AND WS-GOLD-INS-CNT = 0  
                    MOVE 'S' TO RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND OF RSP06-NEW
                                                        (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                 ELSE   
                    IF WS-GOLD-INS-CNT = 1 AND WS-STD-INS-CNT = 0   
                       MOVE 'G' TO RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                        (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                    ELSE
                       IF WS-GOLD-INS-CNT = 0 AND WS-STD-INS-CNT = 0
                          IF ((OLD-PAX-SUB = 0) OR  
                              (RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND OF RSP06-OLD 
                               (OLD-PAX-SUB) NOT = 'S' AND 'G') )   
                          MOVE TDP01-VPP-DEFAULT-CODE   
                            TO RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                        (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                         END-IF 
                       END-IF   
                    END-IF  
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              IF BKGINF-INSURANCE-IND (BKGINF-SUB) = 'N'
                 OR (RSP50-O-CURRENCY-CODE NOT = SSP05-O-CURRENCY-CODE) 
                 IF WS-STD-INS-CNT = 1 AND WS-GOLD-INS-CNT = 1  
                    ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                    MOVE 0033    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                    GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR
                 ELSE   
                    IF WS-STD-INS-CNT = 1 AND WS-GOLD-INS-CNT = 0   
                       ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                       MOVE 0034 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
PRCCMB                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC'  
PRCCMB                           TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                       MOVE 'N' TO RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                        (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                    ELSE
                       IF WS-GOLD-INS-CNT = 1 AND WS-STD-INS-CNT = 0
                          ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                          MOVE 0034 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
PRCCMB                    MOVE 'CCR08UGC'   
PRCCMB                              TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE 
PRCCMB                                                    (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                          MOVE 'N'  
                            TO RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                        (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                       ELSE 
                          IF WS-GOLD-INS-CNT = 0 AND WS-STD-INS-CNT = 0 
                             MOVE 'N'   
                              TO RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
                                                        (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                          END-IF
                       END-IF   
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-NUM-I-XFRS > 1 OR WS-NUM-O-XFRS > 1
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0035       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           MOVE 'C' 
             TO RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           IF PAX-ADDED OR CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1' OR  
              OLD-PAX-SUB NOT > ZERO
              MOVE 'C'  
             TO RSP06-PAX-AIR-TYPE           OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
           ELSE 
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-AIR-TYPE        OF RSP06-OLD (OLD-PAX-SUB) 
             TO RSP06-PAX-AIR-TYPE           OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE ZERO   TO BKGTRN-SUB.   
           IF NEW-PAX-SUB > 1   
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (1) TO 
                   RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1) TO
                  RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
              GO TO 3300-FIND-BKGTRN-ENTRY  
           END-IF.  

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'  
             IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND   
                 SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 0)  
                 IF ((BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE =  
                      RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1))   
                                 OR 
                     (BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE =  
                      RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (1)) AND
                    (BKDINI-W-DINING-SEATING-CODE = 
                     RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1)) AND
                    (RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1) > SPACE))   
                    GO TO 3300-FIND-BKGTRN-ENTRY
                 END-IF 
             ELSE   
                IF ((BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE =   
                     RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1))
                                OR  
                  ((BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE =
                    RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (1)) AND  
                   (RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1) = SPACE)))   
                   GO TO 3300-FIND-BKGTRN-ENTRY 
                END-IF  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

       3255-CHECK-DINING.   
           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'  
             IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND   
                 SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 0)  
                IF BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE = 
                      RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (1) AND 
                   RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1) = 
                      BKDINI-W-DINING-SEATING-CODE  
                    GO TO 3300-FIND-BKGTRN-ENTRY
                END-IF  
             ELSE   
              IF BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE =   
                  RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (1) AND 
                  BKDINI-W-DINING-SEATING-CODE  NOT > SPACES
                  MOVE SPACE TO RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1)  
                  GO TO 3300-FIND-BKGTRN-ENTRY  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM 3260-INIT-DINING.
           IF  RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW > SPACES 
               IF  RSP06-IB-DINING-PROCESS-CODE OF RSP06-NEW = 'C'  
                   MOVE  'Y'   TO  IRP00-OVERRIDE-DIN-CNF-IND   
               END-IF   
           ELSE 
               MOVE    'N'     TO WS-TIER-DATA-IND  
               PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                       UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP51-I-NUM-PAX   
                  IF  BKGINF-PAST-PAX-NUM (WS-SUB) > '0'
                      PERFORM 3280-VALIDATE-TIER-CODE   
                        THRU  3280-EXIT 
                  END-IF
               END-PERFORM. 
           IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND 
                 SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 0)  
                 GO TO 3255-HANDLE-GO-CCL-DINING.   

           MOVE BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE TO IRP00-DIN-CNF.
           PERFORM 3270-VALIDATE-DINING.
           IF IRP00-DINING-ERROR NOT > 0
              MOVE BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE   
                   TO RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (1) 
              MOVE SPACE TO RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1)  
           ELSE 
              MOVE BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE   
                  TO RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1) 
              IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'   
                 CONTINUE   
              ELSE  
                PERFORM 3260-INIT-DINING
                IF BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE = '1' 
                   MOVE '2' TO IRP00-DIN-CNF
                ELSE
                   MOVE '1' TO IRP00-DIN-CNF
                END-IF  
                PERFORM 3270-VALIDATE-DINING
                IF IRP00-DINING-ERROR NOT > 0   
                   MOVE IRP00-DIN-CNF   
                    TO RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (1)
                ELSE
                   PERFORM 3260-INIT-DINING 
                   IF BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE NOT = 'O'  
                     MOVE 'O' TO IRP00-DIN-CNF  
                   ELSE 
                     MOVE '2' TO IRP00-DIN-CNF  
                   END-IF   
                   PERFORM 3270-VALIDATE-DINING 
                   IF IRP00-DINING-ERROR > 0
                      MOVE 'U' TO   
                         RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (1)  
                   ELSE 
                      MOVE IRP00-DIN-CNF TO 
                         RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (1)  
                   END-IF   
                   END-IF   
                END-IF  
           END-IF.  
           GO TO 3300-FIND-BKGTRN-ENTRY.

       3255-HANDLE-GO-CCL-DINING.   
           MOVE BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE TO IRP00-DIN-CNF.
           PERFORM 3270-VALIDATE-DINING.
           IF IRP00-DINING-ERROR NOT > 0
              MOVE BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE   
                   TO RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (1) 
              IF BKDINI-W-DINING-SEATING-CODE > Spaces  
                 MOVE BKDINI-W-DINING-SEATING-CODE TO   
                      RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1)
              ELSE  
                 MOVE SPACE TO RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1)   
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              MOVE BKDINI-W-DINING-SEATING-CODE 
                  TO RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1) 
              IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'   
                 CONTINUE   
              ELSE  
                PERFORM 3260-INIT-DINING
                IF BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE = '1' 
                   MOVE '2' TO IRP00-DIN-CNF
                ELSE
                   MOVE '1' TO IRP00-DIN-CNF
                END-IF  
                PERFORM 3270-VALIDATE-DINING
                IF IRP00-DINING-ERROR NOT > 0   
                   MOVE IRP00-DIN-CNF   
                    TO RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (1)
                  IF BKDINI-W-DINING-SEATING-CODE > SPACES AND  
                     BKDINI-W-DINING-SEATING-CODE NOT = IRP00-DIN-CNF   
                     MOVE BKDINI-W-DINING-SEATING-CODE TO   
                          RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1)
                  ELSE  
                     MOVE SPACES TO 
                          RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1)
                  END-IF
                ELSE
                   PERFORM 3260-INIT-DINING 
                   IF BKDINI-DINING-SEATING-CODE NOT = 'O'  
                     MOVE 'O' TO IRP00-DIN-CNF  
                   ELSE 
                     MOVE '2' TO IRP00-DIN-CNF  
                   END-IF   
                   PERFORM 3270-VALIDATE-DINING 
                   IF IRP00-DINING-ERROR > 0
                      MOVE 'U' TO   
                         RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (1)  
                      MOVE SPACES TO
                            RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1)  
                   ELSE 
                      MOVE IRP00-DIN-CNF TO 
                         RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (1)  
                     IF BKDINI-W-DINING-SEATING-CODE > SPACES AND   
                        BKDINI-W-DINING-SEATING-CODE NOT =  
                                          IRP00-DIN-CNF 
                        MOVE BKDINI-W-DINING-SEATING-CODE TO
                        RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1)  
                     ELSE   
                        MOVE SPACES TO  
                             RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1) 
                     END-IF 
                   END-IF   
                   END-IF   
                END-IF  
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 3300-FIND-BKGTRN-ENTRY.

       3260-INIT-DINING.
           COPY IRP00INT.   

       3270-VALIDATE-DINING.
           MOVE 0 TO WS-GTEE-SEATS. 
           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'G'
              COMPUTE WS-GTEE-SEATS = GBR1-GTEE-DINING-SEAT-CNT (1) +   
                                      GBR1-GTEE-DINING-SEAT-CNT (2) +   
                                      GBR1-GTEE-DINING-SEAT-CNT (3) +   
                                      GBR1-GTEE-DINING-SEAT-CNT (4) +   
                                      GBR1-GTEE-DINING-SEAT-CNT (5) +   
                                      GBR1-GTEE-DINING-SEAT-CNT (6) 
              IF WS-GTEE-SEATS > 0  
                 MOVE 'G' TO IRP00-DIN-TABLE-CODE   
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 'L' TO IRP00-DIN-TABLE-CODE   
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'L'                        TO IRP00-DIN-TABLE-CODE   
           END-IF.  
           MOVE WS-NUM-PAX TO IRP00-DIN-NUM-PAX.
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW TO IRP00-BOOK-TYPE.
           PERFORM IRP00-DIN-VAL THRU IRP00-EXIT.   

       3280-VALIDATE-TIER-CODE. 
LOYLTY     IF BKGINF-TRUST-ME (WS-SUB) = 'Y'
LOYLTY        IF BKGINF-TIER-CODE (WS-SUB) > SPACES 
LOYLTY           MOVE  BKGINF-TIER-CODE (WS-SUB) TO WS-TIER-CODE
LOYLTY        ELSE  
LOYLTY           MOVE SPACES                     TO WS-TIER-CODE
LOYLTY        END-IF
           ELSE 
               MOVE BKGINF-PAST-PAX-NUM (WS-SUB) TO 
                                                 WS-NEW-PAST-GUEST-NUM  
               PERFORM 3295-VALIDATE-PAST-GUEST-NUM 
                  THRU 3295-EXIT
               MOVE WS-NEW-PAST-GUEST-TEN-NUM    TO CIF-CUSTOMER-ID 
               PERFORM 3285-FETCH-CIF   
                  THRU 3285-FETCH-CIF-EXIT  
               MOVE  CIF-TIER-CODE               TO WS-TIER-CODE.   
LTPROD     IF WS-LOYALTY-PILOT-IND = 'Y'
LTTIER        IF WS-TIER-CODE > SPACES  
LTTIER           PERFORM 3290-CALL-RSO91GLP 
LTTIER              THRU 3290-CALL-RSO91GLP-EXIT
LTTIER        END-IF
LTTIER     ELSE 
LTTIER        PERFORM 3290-CALL-RSO91GLP
LTTIER           THRU 3290-CALL-RSO91GLP-EXIT   
LTTIER     END-IF.  

           IF RSP91-O-DINING-CODE = 'C' 
              MOVE  'Y' TO  
                    IRP00-OVERRIDE-DIN-CNF-IND. 

       3280-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       3285-FETCH-CIF.  
           MOVE 'CIF'                        TO CIF-CALCAREA.   
           MOVE CIF-CUSTOMER-ID (10:1)       TO CIF-CALCAREA (4:1)  
           FETCH CIF-REC RECORD 
              ON ERROR GO TO 3285-CIF-ERROR.
           GO TO 3285-FETCH-CIF-EXIT.   

       3285-CIF-ERROR.  
           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB. 
           MOVE 0137       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB).
           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.

       3285-FETCH-CIF-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

       3290-CALL-RSO91GLP.  
       COPY RSP91INT.   

           MOVE WS-TIER-CODE      TO RSP91-I-TIER-CODE. 

           CALL 'RSC91GLD' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 RSP91-INPUT-PARAMS 
                                 RSP91-OUTPUT-PARAMS.   

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > ZERO AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1 
              ADD 1                               TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX  (1)       TO
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

       3290-CALL-RSO91GLP-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

       3295-VALIDATE-PAST-GUEST-NUM.
           MOVE SPACES  
                    TO WS-NEW-PAST-GUEST-TEN-NUM.   
           MOVE 10  TO WS-IN-SUB1.  
           PERFORM VARYING WS-IN-SUB FROM 10 BY -1  
                   UNTIL WS-IN-SUB = 0  
              IF  WS-IN-SUB > 0 
                  IF  WS-NEW-PAST-GUEST-DIGIT (WS-IN-SUB) NOT = SPACE   
                      MOVE WS-NEW-PAST-GUEST-DIGIT (WS-IN-SUB)  
                      TO  WS-NEW-PAST-GUEST-TEN-DIGIT (WS-IN-SUB1)  
                      SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-IN-SUB1
                  END-IF
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 
           INSPECT WS-NEW-PAST-GUEST-TEN-NUM
                              REPLACING LEADING ' ' BY '0' .

       3295-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       3300-FIND-BKGTRN-ENTRY.  

           ADD 1 TO BKGTRN-SUB. 
           IF BKGTRN-SUB > BKGTRN-SEGMENT-MAX   
              GO TO 3350-CONTINUE   
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGTRN-SEGMENT (BKGTRN-SUB) NOT > SPACES  
              GO TO 3350-CONTINUE   
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGTRN-PAX-SEQ-NUM (BKGTRN-SUB) NOT = 
              BKGINF-PAX-SEQ-NUM (BKGINF-SUB)   
               GO TO 3300-FIND-BKGTRN-ENTRY 
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGTRN-DIRECTION-CODE (BKGTRN-SUB) = 'R' OR 'O'   
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 3300-FIND-BKGTRN-ENTRY  
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGTRN-TRANS-TYPE (BKGTRN-SUB) NOT = 'A' AND 'B' AND 'O'  
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0036       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           MOVE 'C'   TO RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           IF BKGTRN-TRANS-TYPE (BKGTRN-SUB) = 'A'  
              MOVE 'F' TO RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGTRN-TRANS-TYPE (BKGTRN-SUB) = 'B'  
              MOVE 'B' TO RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) = 'C'  
              MOVE 'C' TO RSP06-PAX-AIR-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKGTRN-CITY-CODE (BKGTRN-SUB)   
                       TO RSP06-PAX-ORIGINATING-CITY-CD 
                       OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).  

           IF RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) = 'F'  
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-FLYA-IND   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE SPACES  
             TO RSP06-PAX-CITY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB). 

       3350-CONTINUE.   

           MOVE 'N' TO RSP06-PAX-PACKAGE-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           INITIALIZE RSP06-PPR-INFO OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-PAX-PRE-PACKAGE-CODE OF 
                RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-PAX-POST-PACKAGE-CODE OF
                RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           PERFORM 5900-PROCESS-PACKAGES THRU 5900-EXIT.
           MOVE WS-PPR-SUB TO RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB). 
           IF RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) > 0  
              MOVE 'Y'  
                TO RSP06-PAX-PACKAGE-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                   RSP06-PPR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'N'  
                TO RSP06-PPR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PAX-CITY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) > SPACES OR
              RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) = 'C'  
                GO TO 3400-CONTINUE-AFTER-CITY  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKGTRN-CITY-CODE (BKGTRN-SUB) TO CCS-CC-IATA-CODE   
                                                 CCP00-REQ-CITY-CODE.   
           IF CCS-CC-IATA-CHR (4) > SPACES  
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0037       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           MOVE BKGTRN-CITY-CODE (BKGTRN-SUB)   
             TO RSP06-PAX-CITY-CODE          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

       3400-CONTINUE-AFTER-CITY.
           MOVE DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE   
             TO RSP06-PAX-CHANGE-DATE        OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           IF BKGINF-PAST-PAX-NUM (BKGINF-SUB) > SPACES AND 
              BKGINF-PAST-PAX-NUM (BKGINF-SUB) NOT = '0'
              MOVE 'Y' TO RSP06-PAX-PAST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
              MOVE BKGINF-PAST-PAX-NUM (BKGINF-SUB) 
                       TO WS-NEW-PAST-GUEST-NUM 
              PERFORM 3295-VALIDATE-PAST-GUEST-NUM  
                 THRU 3295-EXIT 
              MOVE WS-NEW-PAST-GUEST-TEN-NUM
                TO RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PAST-GUEST-INPUT-SW
              IF BKGINF-TRUST-ME (BKGINF-SUB) = 'Y' 

                  MOVE 1 TO RSP25-TRUST-ME-FLAG (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                  MOVE BKGINF-TIER-CODE (BKGINF-SUB)
                      TO RSP25-I-TRUST-ME-TIER-CODE (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                         RSP06-PAX-RATE-TIER-CODE OF RSP06-NEW  
                                                    (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
              ELSE  
                  MOVE 0 TO RSP25-TRUST-ME-FLAG (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                  MOVE WS-NEW-PAST-GUEST-TEN-NUM
                       TO CIF-CUSTOMER-ID   
                  PERFORM 3285-FETCH-CIF
                     THRU 3285-FETCH-CIF-EXIT   
                  MOVE 'Y'  
                       TO RSP06-CIF-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                  MOVE CIF-REC  
                       TO RSP06-CIF-REC OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                  MOVE RSP06-CIF-TIER-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                       TO RSP06-PAX-RATE-TIER-CODE OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                    (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES   
                TO RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM  OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
              MOVE 'N' TO RSP06-PAX-PAST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
              MOVE 0   TO RSP25-TRUST-ME-FLAG (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKGINF-PHONE-CODE (BKGINF-SUB)  TO CCP00-PHONE-CODE.
           PERFORM CCP11-GET-PHONE-CODE THRU CCP11-PHONE-EXIT.  

           MOVE ZEROES                        TO WS-MOBILE-NUM. 
           IF SSP03-O-CHK-TXT-PREF-FLG = 1  
              MOVE BKGINF-MOBILE-PHONE-NUM (BKGINF-SUB) 
                                              TO WS-PHONE-ARRAY 
              INSPECT WS-PHONE-ARRAY REPLACING ALL  
                     '(' BY SPACE   
                     ')' BY SPACE   
                     '-' BY SPACE   
                     '.' BY SPACE   

              PERFORM 6000-EDIT-PHONE THRU 6000-EXIT
              MOVE WS-PHONE-NUM               TO WS-MOBILE-NUM  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKGINF-PHONE-NUM (BKGINF-SUB) TO WS-PHONE-ARRAY.
           INSPECT WS-PHONE-ARRAY REPLACING ALL 
                  '(' BY SPACE  
                  ')' BY SPACE  
                  '-' BY SPACE  
                  '.' BY SPACE. 

           PERFORM 6000-EDIT-PHONE THRU 6000-EXIT.  

           PERFORM 8200-CHECK-PPHOR-EXIST   
              THRU 8200-CHECK-PPHOR-EXIST-EXIT. 

           MOVE BKGINF-PRIMARY-CONTACT-IND (BKGINF-SUB) 
             TO RSP06-PAX-PRIMARY-CONTACT-IND OF RSP06-NEW  
                                                 (NEW-PAX-SUB). 

           IF BKGINF-COUNTRY-CODE (BKGINF-SUB) > SPACES 
              MOVE BKGINF-COUNTRY-CODE (BKGINF-SUB) 
                TO RSP06-PAX-RESIDENCE-COUNTRY OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                        (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PIAR-COUNTRY OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) NOT > SPACES
              MOVE SPACES   
                TO RSP06-PIAR-COUNTRY OF RSP06-NEW      (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'N' 
             TO RSP06-PAX-DAMAGE-DEPOSIT-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           IF CCL-CORP-GRP-IND  
              IF GBR1-DAMAGE-DEPOSIT-IND = 'Y'  
                 IF RSP06-PAX-AGE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) < 21 OR
                    RSP06-PAX-AGE-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) = 'M' 
                    MOVE 'Y'
                      TO RSP06-PAX-DAMAGE-DEPOSIT-IND OF RSP06-NEW  
                                                         (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PAX-AGE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) >= 21
              MOVE 'N' TO RSP06-PAX-DAMAGE-DEPOSIT-IND  OF RSP06-NEW
                                                        (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP51-O-SHIP-CODE   
             TO RSP06-TIR-SHIP-CODE          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE RSP51-O-SAIL-DATE   
             TO RSP06-TIR-SAIL-DATE          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE DPP34-CABIN-NUM 
             TO RSP06-TIR-CABIN-NUM          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE                     OF RSP06-NEW
             TO RSP06-TIR-BOOK-TYPE          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'  
              GO TO 3500-LOAD-RSP06-PAX-CHG-ONLY.   

           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'G'
              MOVE GBR1-BOOK-NUM
                TO RSP06-PAX-BOOK-NUM        OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE TDP01-PAX-SLOT-NUM  
             TO RSP06-PAX-SLOT-NUM           OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE RSP51-O-SHIP-CODE   
             TO RSP06-PAX-SHIP-CODE          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE RSP51-O-SAIL-DATE   
             TO RSP06-PAX-SAIL-DATE          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE RSP51-O-DURATION-DAYS   
             TO RSP06-PAX-DURATION-DAYS      OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE 'N' 
             TO RSP06-PAX-BTB-IND            OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE          OF RSP06-NEW   
             TO RSP06-PAX-BOOK-TYPE          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE CAH-PRC-XCHG-RATE (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
             TO RSP06-PAX-PRC-XCHG-RATE      OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE   
             TO RSP06-PAX-BOOK-DATE          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE 'N' 
             TO RSP06-PAX-MAILED-IND         OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE 1   
             TO RSP06-PAX-LAST-UPDATE-NUM    OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           IF (CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1' AND 
              CAH-OFFER-ID (NEW-PAX-SUB) > ZEROES) OR   
              CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'  
              MOVE CAH-OFFER-CODE   
              TO RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE   OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
              MOVE CAH-OFFER-ID (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
              TO RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID  OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
              MOVE CAH-CRUISE-RATE (NEW-PAX-SUB) TO 
                 RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
              MOVE CAH-NON-COMM-AMT (NEW-PAX-SUB) TO
                 RSP06-PAX-PTCHG-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
              MOVE CAH-PRC-XCHG-RATE (NEW-PAX-SUB) TO   
                  RSP06-PAX-PRC-XCHG-RATE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
              MOVE CAH-SINGLES-PCT (NEW-PAX-SUB) TO 
                  RSP06-PAX-SINGLES-PCT OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
              MOVE CAH-REQ-SUGG-POINTS (NEW-PAX-SUB)    TO  
                  RSP06-PAX-SUGG-POINTS OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
           END-IF.  
           MOVE 'N' 
             TO RSP06-PAX-INS-INFO-IND       OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                RSP06-PAX-COP-INFO-IND       OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           MOVE 'N' 
             TO RSP06-PAX-EMAIL-ADDR-IND     OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           GO TO 3550-CHECK-NEW-BOOKING-ONLY.   

       3500-LOAD-RSP06-PAX-CHG-ONLY.

           IF NOT CAH-NOT-NEEDED
              IF (CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1' AND  
                 CAH-OFFER-ID (NEW-PAX-SUB) > ZEROES) OR
                 CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'   
                 IF ADD-PAX-ONLY
PPC470              MOVE SPACES              TO WS-APX-OFFER-CODE   
PPC470              EXEC SQL
PPC470                   SELECT OFFER_CODE  
PPC470                     INTO WS-APX-OFFER-CODE   
PPC470                     FROM OFFER_ID_STATIC_INFO
PPC470                    WHERE OFFER_ID = :CAH-OFFER-ID (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
PPC470                    FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
PPC470              END-EXEC
                    IF  RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD < NEW-PAX-SUB
                        EVALUATE NEW-PAX-SUB
                           WHEN 2   
                            MOVE CAH-OFFER-CODE 
                            TO RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE  OF RSP06-NEW (2)   
                           WHEN 3   
                              MOVE WS-APX-OFFER-CODE
                              TO RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (3)  
                           WHEN 4   
                              MOVE WS-APX-OFFER-CODE
                              TO RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (4)  
                           WHEN 5   
                              MOVE WS-APX-OFFER-CODE
