                              TO RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (5)  
                        END-EVALUATE
                    END-IF  
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE CAH-OFFER-CODE 
                    TO RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE  OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                 END-IF 
                 MOVE CAH-OFFER-ID (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                 TO RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID  OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                 MOVE CAH-CRUISE-RATE (NEW-PAX-SUB) TO  
                    RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                 MOVE CAH-NON-COMM-AMT (NEW-PAX-SUB) TO 
                    RSP06-PAX-PTCHG-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                 MOVE CAH-PRC-XCHG-RATE (NEW-PAX-SUB) TO
                     RSP06-PAX-PRC-XCHG-RATE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                 MOVE CAH-SINGLES-PCT (NEW-PAX-SUB) TO  
                     RSP06-PAX-SINGLES-PCT OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                 MOVE CAH-REQ-SUGG-POINTS (NEW-PAX-SUB)    TO   
                     RSP06-PAX-SUGG-POINTS OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-IBR-REQ-CPA-CATEGORY OF RSP06-OLD   
                                        NOT =   
              RSP06-IBR-REQ-CPA-CATEGORY OF RSP06-NEW   
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-RATE-CHANGED-SW
              GO TO 3550-CHECK-NEW-BOOKING-ONLY 
           END-IF.  

           IF OLD-PAX-SUB > 0   
           IF RSP06-PAX-SUGG-POINTS OF RSP06-OLD (OLD-PAX-SUB) NOT =
              RSP06-PAX-SUGG-POINTS OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-RATE-CHANGED-SW
              GO TO 3550-CHECK-NEW-BOOKING-ONLY 
           END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF OLD-PAX-SUB > 0   
              IF RSP06-PAX-SINGLES-PCT OF RSP06-OLD (OLD-PAX-SUB) NOT = 
                 RSP06-PAX-SINGLES-PCT OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-RATE-CHANGED-SW 
                 GO TO 3550-CHECK-NEW-BOOKING-ONLY  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

       3550-CHECK-NEW-BOOKING-ONLY. 

           MOVE 'N'                 TO WS-NEW-BOOKING-ONLY-IND. 
           EXEC SQL 
                SELECT NEW_BOOKING_ONLY_IND 
                  INTO WS-NEW-BOOKING-ONLY-IND  
                  FROM OFFER_ID_STATIC_INFO 
                 WHERE OFFER_ID = :RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-NEW  
                                                          (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                 FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'  
              IF WS-NEW-BOOKING-ONLY-IND = 'Y'  
                 IF RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-OLD (OLD-PAX-SUB) NOT = 
                    RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                    ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                    MOVE 0084 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                    GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGINF-HOME-STATE-CODE (NEW-PAX-SUB) = 'XX' OR
              BKGINF-HOME-STATE-CODE (NEW-PAX-SUB) <= SPACES
              MOVE SPACES   
                TO RSP06-PAX-STATE     OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
           ELSE 
              MOVE BKGINF-HOME-STATE-CODE (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                TO RSP06-PAX-STATE     OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
           END-IF.  

           IF   RSP06-PAX-STATE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) NOT  =   
                RSP06-PAX-STATE OF RSP06-OLD (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-STATE-CHG-IND 
           END-IF.  

188007     IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'  
188007        MOVE 'N' TO WS-STATE-CHG-IND  
188007     END-IF.  

           PERFORM 6300-PROCESS-BKGADI-BKGIMI THRU 6300-EXIT.   

           GO TO 3200-LOAD-RSP06-PAX.   

      *=================================================================
       3580-VALIDATE-MOB-TEXT-IND.  

           IF SSP03-O-CHK-TXT-PREF-FLG = 1  
              PERFORM 8600-FIND-CHG-IN-MOB  
                 THRU 8600-FIND-CHG-IN-MOB-EXIT 
           END-IF.  

       3600-AFTER-RSP06-LOADING.

           MOVE NEW-PAX-SUB TO RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL   WS-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW  
                   OR      WS-WHEELCHAIR-CMT = 'N'  
                 MOVE ZERO       TO WS-COUNT
                 INSPECT RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-TABLE OF RSP06-NEW (WS-SUB)  
                     TALLYING WS-COUNT FOR ALL 'W'  
                 IF  WS-COUNT NOT > ZERO
                     MOVE 'Y'    TO WS-WHEELCHAIR-CMT   
                 ELSE   
                     MOVE 'N'    TO WS-WHEELCHAIR-CMT   
                 END-IF 
           END-PERFORM. 
           IF RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM  OF RSP06-OLD (1:1) = 'T' OR  
                                                        'C' OR 'W'  
              NEXT SENTENCE 
           ELSE 
               MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO DPP34-CABIN-NUM 
               PERFORM DPP33-FETCH-CABIN THRU DPP33-EXIT
               IF DPP34-ERROR-SUB > ZEROES  
                  GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR 
               END-IF   
               IF AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'Y' OR 'P' OR 'S'
                                               OR 'L' OR 'R'
                 IF WS-WHEELCHAIR-CMT = 'Y' 
                    ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                    MOVE 0086    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                    MOVE 'CCR08UGC' 
                                 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB).

           IF MSGHDR-UI-CODE = 'C'  
              IF BKDDLV-DOC-DELIVERY-LOC-CODE  = 'A'
                 IF (BKDDLV-AGENCY-PHONE-NUM (1:10) NOT > SPACES) OR
                    (BKDDLV-AGENCY-PHONE-NUM (1:10) = '0000000000') 
                    ADD 1      TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                    MOVE 0041  TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                    GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.   

           IF CSMADR-COUNTRY-CODE = 'US' OR 'CA'
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PAX-DOMESTIC-IND   
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'N' TO WS-PAX-DOMESTIC-IND   
           END-IF.  

           IF MSGHDR-UI-CODE = 'C'  
              IF BKDDLV-DOC-DELIVERY-LOC-CODE  = 'A'
                 MOVE BKDDLV-AGENCY-PHONE-NUM   
                                       TO WS-PHONE-ARRAY
                 INSPECT WS-PHONE-ARRAY REPLACING ALL   
                        '(' BY SPACE
                        ')' BY SPACE
                        '-' BY SPACE
                        '.' BY SPACE
                 PERFORM 6000-EDIT-PHONE THRU 6000-EXIT 
                 MOVE WS-PHONE-NUM     TO APR-PHONE-NUM 
                 MOVE 001              TO APR-COUNTRY-NUM   
                 FETCH AGENT-PHONE-REC RECORD   
                       ON ERROR GO TO 3650-INVALID-ALT-ADDRESS  
                 FETCH OWNER RECORD WITHIN AGENT-PHONE-SET SET  
                       ON ERROR GO TO 3650-INVALID-ALT-ADDRESS  
              END-IF
           ELSE 
               IF BKDDLV-DOC-DELIVERY-LOC-CODE  = 'A'   
                  IF CSMINF-FIRST-NAME       > SPACES AND   
                     CSMINF-LAST-NAME        > SPACES AND   
                     CSMADR-ADDRESS1         > SPACES AND   
                     CSMADR-CITY             > SPACES AND   
                     CSMADR-COUNTRY-CODE     > SPACES AND   
                     ((WS-PAX-DOMESTIC                AND   
                       CSMADR-ZIP-CODE       > SPACES AND   
                       CSMADR-STATE-CODE     > SPACES   
                      ) OR (WS-PAX-INTL)
                     )  
                     IF RSP06-PAX-STATE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                                                 > SPACES   
                        AND WS-STATE-CHG-IND = 'Y'  
                        IF RSP06-PAX-STATE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                                            NOT  = CSMADR-STATE-CODE
                           ADD 1  TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                           MOVE 0082
                                  TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
                        END-IF  
                     END-IF 
                     IF ((CSMINF-PHONE-NUM (1) > SPACES) OR 
                         (CSMINF-PHONE-NUM (2) > SPACES))   
                          PERFORM 6200-EDIT-CSMINF-PHONE
                             THRU 6200-EXIT 
                     END-IF 
                   ELSE 
                       GO TO 3650-INVALID-ALT-ADDRESS   
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 3700-CONTINUE-RSP06-PROCESS.   

       3650-INVALID-ALT-ADDRESS.

           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
           MOVE 0042       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.

       3700-CONTINUE-RSP06-PROCESS. 

           IF WS-PP-GRATS-CNT > ZERO
              PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
                      UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW 

                 IF RSP06-PAX-PP-GRATS-IND OF RSP06-NEW (WS-SUB)
                                   = 'N'
                    MOVE 'Y'
                      TO RSP06-PAX-PP-GRATS-IND OF RSP06-NEW (WS-SUB)   
                    ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                    MOVE 0069       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                    MOVE 'CCR08UGC' 
                      TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                    MOVE RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW TO WS-SUB   
                 END-IF 

              END-PERFORM   
           END-IF.  

           IF (DPP34-NORMAL-CAPACITY < DPP34-MAX-CAPACITY) AND  
              (RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW > (DPP34-NORMAL-CAPACITY +
                                 RSP06-IBR-NUM-CRIBS OF RSP06-NEW)) 
              COMPUTE RSP06-IBR-NUM-ROLLAWAYS OF RSP06-NEW =
                RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW - (DPP34-NORMAL-CAPACITY 
                   + RSP06-IBR-NUM-CRIBS OF RSP06-NEW)  
              IF (RSP06-IBR-NUM-ROLLAWAYS OF RSP06-NEW NOT =
                  RSP06-IBR-NUM-ROLLAWAYS OF RSP06-OLD) AND 
                 (RSP06-IBR-NUM-ROLLAWAYS OF RSP06-NEW > 0) 
                 ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                 MOVE 0114       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC'
                      TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF ( (CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1') AND  
                ( RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW > 2) AND   
              (RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (1:3) = 'TBA' OR 'CSV'))
              IF (( RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW NOT =
                    RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD) OR  
                   (RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (1:3) NOT =
                                                       'TBA' AND 'CSV') 
                OR (RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD NOT =  
                   RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW) )  
                 ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                 MOVE 0122       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC'
                      TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM 7000-PROCESS-OTHER-PAYMENT THRU 7000-EXIT.   

           PERFORM VARYING WS-OLD-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL  WS-OLD-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD   
              PERFORM VARYING WS-O-PPR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                      UNTIL WS-O-PPR-SUB > RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-OLD   
                                                            (WS-OLD-SUB)
                 PERFORM VARYING WS-NEW-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                         UNTIL WS-NEW-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW  
                    IF (RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB)   
                                                              = 'F') AND
                       (RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) =   
                            RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB))
                       MOVE 'N' TO WS-PAX-PKG-FOUND-IND 
                       PERFORM VARYING WS-N-PPR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                               UNTIL WS-N-PPR-SUB > 
                                 RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB)
                          IF RSP06-PPR-PKG-TYPE OF RSP06-OLD
                                            (WS-OLD-SUB WS-O-PPR-SUB) = 
                                        RSP06-PPR-PKG-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW 
                                            (WS-NEW-SUB WS-N-PPR-SUB)   
                             MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PAX-PKG-FOUND-IND   
                             IF RSP06-PPR-PKG-CODE OF RSP06-OLD 
                                            (WS-OLD-SUB WS-O-PPR-SUB)   
                                NOT = RSP06-PPR-PKG-CODE OF RSP06-NEW   
                                            (WS-NEW-SUB WS-N-PPR-SUB)   
                                ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                                MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO  
                                              MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                                                       (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                                MOVE 0175       TO  
                                                MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE  
                                                       (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                                PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG   
                                   THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
                                GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB   
                             END-IF 
                          END-IF
                       END-PERFORM  
                       IF WS-PAX-PKG-FOUND-IND NOT = 'Y'
                          ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                          MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE   
                                                    (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                          MOVE 0175       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE 
                                                    (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                          PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                             THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
                          GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
                       END-IF   
                    END-IF  
                 END-PERFORM
              END-PERFORM   
           END-PERFORM  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-NEW-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL  WS-NEW-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW   
              PERFORM VARYING WS-N-PPR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                      UNTIL WS-N-PPR-SUB > RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-NEW   
                                                            (WS-NEW-SUB)
                 PERFORM VARYING WS-OLD-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                         UNTIL WS-OLD-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD  
                    IF (RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)   
                                                              = 'F') AND
                       (RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB) =   
                            RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB))
                       MOVE 'N' TO WS-PAX-PKG-FOUND-IND 
                       PERFORM VARYING WS-O-PPR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                               UNTIL WS-O-PPR-SUB > 
                                 RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)
                          IF RSP06-PPR-PKG-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW
                                            (WS-NEW-SUB WS-N-PPR-SUB) = 
                                        RSP06-PPR-PKG-TYPE OF RSP06-OLD 
                                            (WS-OLD-SUB WS-O-PPR-SUB)   
                             MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PAX-PKG-FOUND-IND   
                             IF RSP06-PPR-PKG-CODE OF RSP06-NEW 
                                            (WS-NEW-SUB WS-N-PPR-SUB)   
                                NOT = RSP06-PPR-PKG-CODE OF RSP06-OLD   
                                            (WS-OLD-SUB WS-O-PPR-SUB)   
                                ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                                MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO  
                                              MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                                                       (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                                MOVE 0175       TO  
                                                MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE  
                                                       (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                                PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG   
                                   THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
                                GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB   
                             END-IF 
                          END-IF
                       END-PERFORM  
                       IF WS-PAX-PKG-FOUND-IND NOT = 'Y'
                          ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                          MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE   
                                                    (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                          MOVE 0175       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE 
                                                    (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                          PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                             THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
                          GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
                       END-IF   
                    END-IF  
                 END-PERFORM
              END-PERFORM   
           END-PERFORM  

           MOVE 'N'                       TO WS-XFR-MISMATCH-IND.   
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW
                                          TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE.   
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW
                                          TO DPP02-NUMBER-DAYS. 
           PERFORM DPP10-ADVANCE-YYYYMMDD-DATE. 
           MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE       TO WS-SAIL-END-DATE.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-PAX-SUB1 FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL WS-PAX-SUB1 > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW   
              IF  RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW  (WS-PAX-SUB1) = 'F' 
                                                                    AND 
                  RSP06-PTFR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (WS-PAX-SUB1) = 'Y' 
                                                                    AND 
                  RSP06-PICF-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (WS-PAX-SUB1) = 'Y' 
                                                                    AND 
                  RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-PAX-SUB1) >  
                  RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (WS-PAX-SUB1)
                  PERFORM 3710-AIR-XFR-MIS-ADV THRU 
                          3710-AIR-XFR-MIS-ADV-EXIT 
                  IF  WS-XFR-MISMATCH-IND = 'Y' 
                      ADD 1               TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                      MOVE 'CCR08UGC'     TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE   
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                      MOVE 0208           TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE 
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                      MOVE RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW   
                                          TO WS-PAX-SUB1
                  END-IF
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           GO TO 3800-CALL-RSO25-SUBROUTINE.

       3710-AIR-XFR-MIS-ADV.

           MOVE SPACES                    TO WS-PICF-ARRIVAL-CITY   
                                             WS-PICF-DEPART-CITY.   

           PERFORM VARYING WS-PCF-SUB FROM RSP06-PICF-CNT OF RSP06-NEW  
                   (WS-PAX-SUB1) BY -1 UNTIL WS-PCF-SUB < 1 
              EVALUATE RSP06-PICF-ARRIVE-DATE OF RSP06-NEW  
                                                (WS-PAX-SUB1 WS-PCF-SUB)
                  WHEN RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW 
                       MOVE RSP06-PICF-TO-CITY OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                (WS-PAX-SUB1 WS-PCF-SUB)
                                          TO WS-PICF-ARRIVAL-CITY   
                       MOVE 1             TO WS-PCF-SUB 
              END-EVALUATE  
           END-PERFORM. 

           PERFORM VARYING WS-PCF-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL WS-PCF-SUB > RSP06-PICF-CNT OF RSP06-NEW   
                                                           (WS-PAX-SUB1)
              EVALUATE RSP06-PICF-DEPART-DATE OF RSP06-NEW  
                                                (WS-PAX-SUB1 WS-PCF-SUB)
                  WHEN WS-SAIL-END-DATE 
                       MOVE RSP06-PICF-FROM-CITY OF RSP06-NEW   
                                                (WS-PAX-SUB1 WS-PCF-SUB)
                                          TO WS-PICF-DEPART-CITY
                       MOVE RSP06-PICF-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-PAX-SUB1)   
                                          TO WS-PCF-SUB 
              END-EVALUATE  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF  WS-PICF-ARRIVAL-CITY > SPACES                         OR 
               WS-PICF-DEPART-CITY  > SPACES
               CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
               GO TO 3710-AIR-XFR-MIS-ADV-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           COMPUTE WS-OLD-XFR-SUB = RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT OF RSP06-OLD
                                                           (WS-PAX-SUB1)
                                  + 1.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-XFR-SUB FROM WS-OLD-XFR-SUB BY 1  
                   UNTIL WS-XFR-SUB > RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT OF
                                                 RSP06-NEW (WS-PAX-SUB1)
              IF  RSP06-PTFR-PRE-POST-IND OF RSP06-NEW  
                                    (WS-PAX-SUB1 WS-XFR-SUB) = 'I'  AND 
                  RSP06-PTFR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW   
                                    (WS-PAX-SUB1 WS-XFR-SUB) = ZERO AND 
                  WS-PICF-ARRIVAL-CITY                       > SPACES   
                  IF  WS-PICF-ARRIVAL-CITY               NOT =  
                      RSP06-PTFR-LOC-CODE OF RSP06-NEW  
                                                (WS-PAX-SUB1 WS-XFR-SUB)
                      MOVE 'Y'            TO WS-XFR-MISMATCH-IND
                      GO TO 3710-AIR-XFR-MIS-ADV-EXIT   
                  END-IF
              END-IF
              IF  RSP06-PTFR-PRE-POST-IND OF RSP06-NEW  
                                    (WS-PAX-SUB1 WS-XFR-SUB) = 'O'  AND 
                  RSP06-PTFR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW   
                                    (WS-PAX-SUB1 WS-XFR-SUB) = ZERO AND 
                  WS-PICF-DEPART-CITY                        > SPACES   
                  IF  WS-PICF-DEPART-CITY                NOT =  
                      RSP06-PTFR-LOC-CODE OF RSP06-NEW  
                                                (WS-PAX-SUB1 WS-XFR-SUB)
                      MOVE 'Y'            TO WS-XFR-MISMATCH-IND
                      GO TO 3710-AIR-XFR-MIS-ADV-EXIT   
                  END-IF
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

       3710-AIR-XFR-MIS-ADV-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       3800-CALL-RSO25-SUBROUTINE.  

PPC470     IF WS-CABIN-HOLD-ESTABLISH = 'Y'        AND  
PPC470        CCMH-MESSAGE-ID         = 'CCNEWBK1' AND  
PPC470        CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM NOT > SPACES  
PPC470        PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT.  

           MOVE 1                       TO RSP25-UPDATE-FLAG.   
           MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE
                                        TO RSP25-I-CONNECTION-CODE. 
           MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE   TO RSP25-I-LOCATION-CODE.   
           MOVE 0                       TO RSP25-CALL-CENTER-FLAG.  
           MOVE SPACES                  TO RSP25-I-AGENT-USERID.

           IF MSGHDR-USER-NAME > SPACES 
              MOVE MSGHDR-USER-NAME     TO RSP25-I-UPDATE-CONTACT   
           ELSE 
              MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID       TO RSP25-I-UPDATE-CONTACT   
           END-IF.  

           STRING MSGHDR-SYSTEM-ID (1:3)
                  MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID (1:10)   
                  MSGHDR-USER-ID (1:6)  
                  DELIMITED BY SIZE 
             INTO RSP25-I-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION. 

           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD NOT > SPACES AND  
              MSGHDR-USER-ID (1:10) = 'NEWCONSUME' AND  
              ( CAH-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION = 'CCL          NEWCON' OR 
                CAH-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION = 'CUK          NEWCON' OR 
                CAH-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION = 'MCL          NEWCON' OR 
                CAH-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION = 'CAU          NEWCON')   
              MOVE CAH-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION 
                TO RSP25-I-AGENCY-CONN-LOCATION 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE SPACES TO DPP01-USERID. 
           STRING '@' SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE DELIMITED BY SIZE   
                      INTO DPP01-USERID.

           MOVE 0                        TO RSP25-REPRICE-FLAG. 
           MOVE 'N'   TO RSP25-I-EXTEND-CODE.   
           PERFORM VARYING BKGSVC-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL 
              BKGSVC-SUB NOT < BKGSVC-SEGMENT-MAX   
              IF BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) = 'OPTEXT'
                 ACCEPT DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE FROM DATE YYYYMMDD  
                 PERFORM DPP10-YYYYMMDD-WEEK-DAY
                 IF DPP02-DAY-OF-WEEK = 'FRIDAY' OR 'SATURDAY'  
                    MOVE 'M'              TO RSP25-I-EXTEND-CODE
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 'Y'              TO RSP25-I-EXTEND-CODE
                 END-IF 
                 IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IC' OR 'IM' OR 'CF'
                    MOVE 1                   TO RSP25-I-OVR-ONE-HR-RULE 
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 0                   TO RSP25-I-OVR-ONE-HR-RULE 
                 END-IF 
                 MOVE BKGSVC-SEGMENT-MAX TO BKGSVC-SUB  
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-CONF-REQ = 'Y' AND 
              (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND 
               SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 0)
              MOVE 0         TO RSP25-UPDATE-FLAG   
              IF (RSP06-IBR-GTEE-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD > SPACES)   
                 AND (RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (1:3) NOT =  
                                                     'TBA' AND 'CSV')   
                 MOVE 1         TO RSP25-I-OVR-GTEE-CAT-FLAG
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 1                       TO RSP25-CHK-VPP-STATE-FLAG.

           IF   (    (CCMH-MESSAGE-ID              = 'CCNEWBK1')
                 AND (CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM      NOT > SPACES    )
                 AND (RSP50-O-ALLOW-NEW-BKG-IND    = 'Y'       )
                 AND (RSP50-O-ALLOW-MODIFY-BKG-IND = 'N'       ))   
                MOVE 1                  TO RSP25-OVR-AGT-MOD-BOOK-FLG   
           END-IF.  

           IF    MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID           NOT > SPACES
             AND (   MSGHDR-AGENT-PHONE-NUM NOT > SPACES
                  OR MSGHDR-AGENT-PHONE-NUM NOT > ZEROES)   
             AND (   MSGHDR-AGENT-ARC-NUM   NOT > SPACES
                  OR MSGHDR-AGENT-ARC-NUM   NOT > ZEROES)   
             AND (   MSGHDR-AGENT-CLIA-NUM  NOT > SPACES
                  OR MSGHDR-AGENT-CLIA-NUM  NOT > ZEROES)   

               MOVE 1 TO RSP25-OVR-AGT-NEW-BOOK-FLG 
                         RSP25-OVR-AGT-MOD-BOOK-FLG 
           END-IF.  


           IF  CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1' AND 
               BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) = 'GUAR'
               IF  (RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (1:1) NOT = 'T'
                                                            AND  'C')   
                   MOVE 'TBA'    TO RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
               ELSE 
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD
                                 TO RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
               END-IF   
           ELSE 
               IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'  
                  IF  BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) = 'GUAR' 
                      MOVE 'TBA' TO RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                  ELSE  
                      MOVE BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1) 
                                 TO RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                  END-IF
               ELSE 
                   MOVE BKCRV1-CABIN-NUM (1)
                                 TO RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF  (( RSP06-IBR-GTEE-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD > SPACES )  
               AND (( RSP06-IBR-GTEE-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD =   
                     RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW ) 
               OR CAH-NOT-NEEDED))  
               IF  RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (1:1)  = 'T'
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD         TO 
                              RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW  
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD TO 
                              RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW  
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD     TO 
                              RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW  
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF  CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1' 
               IF  BKCRV1-BED-CONFIGURATION-PREF = 'Y'  
                   MOVE '1'             TO RSP06-IBR-CNV-KING-CODE  
                                           OF RSP06-NEW 
               ELSE 
                  IF  BKCRV1-BED-CONFIGURATION-PREF = 'N'   
                      MOVE '0'          TO RSP06-IBR-CNV-KING-CODE  
                                           OF RSP06-NEW 
                  END-IF
               END-IF   
           ELSE 
               IF  CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1' 
                   MOVE ' '             TO RSP06-IBR-CNV-KING-CODE  
                                           OF RSP06-NEW 
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 1                       TO RSP25-SKIP-PG-TITLE-FLAG.
           MOVE 0                       TO RSP25-REPRICE-FLAG.  
           MOVE SPACES                  TO RSP25-I-AGENT-USERID.

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'  
              IF BKCRV1-COURTESY-HOLD-IND = 'Y' 
                 MOVE 1                 TO RSP25-I-COURTESY-HOLD-FLG
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 0                 TO RSP25-I-COURTESY-HOLD-FLG
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE ZEROS TO WS-FORCED-DATE 
                         RSP25-I-FORCED-CH-OPT-FLG. 
           IF BKCRV1-COURTESY-HOLD-NEWDATE > 0  
              MOVE BKCRV1-COURTESY-HOLD-NEWDATE 
                TO DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE  
              PERFORM DPP10-MMDDYYYY-TO-YYYYMMDD
              MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE TO WS-FORCED-DATE.   

           IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND 
               SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1) AND
               CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1' 
              IF WS-FORCED-DATE  > 0 AND
                 WS-FORCED-DATE  >= DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE   
                 MOVE WS-FORCED-DATE               TO   
                      RSP06-IBR-OPTION-DATE OF RSP06-NEW
                 MOVE 1 TO RSP25-I-FORCED-CH-OPT-FLG
              END-IF

              IF BKCRV1-COURTESY-HOLD-NEWTIME > 0 AND   
                 WS-FORCED-DATE  >= DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE   
                 MOVE BKCRV1-COURTESY-HOLD-NEWTIME (3:4) TO 
                      RSP06-IBR-OPTION-TIME OF RSP06-NEW
                 MOVE 1 TO RSP25-I-FORCED-CH-OPT-FLG
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP50-O-COUNTRY-CODE   TO RSP25-I-COUNTRY-CODE. 
207024     IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IA' OR 'UA' OR 'AU'  
              MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID      TO RSP25-I-GOCCL-ID  
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES              TO RSP25-I-GOCCL-ID  
           END-IF.  

           CALL 'RSO25GCU'      USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS  
                                      DPP01-COMMON-INFO 
                                      RSP25-UPDATE-GST-CAB-INPUT
                                      RSP25-UPDATE-GST-CAB-OUTPUT   
                                      RSP06-NEW 
                                      RSP06-OLD 
                                      RSP90-OVERRIDE-FLAGS-INFO 
                                      RSP89-SEC-RULE-TBL.   

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > ZERO AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1 
              ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) TO  
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-PAX-AGE-CODE (1) TO CCP00-AGE-CODE 
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-PAX-AGE (1)      TO CCP00-AGE-MIN  
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-PAX-SEQ-NUM (1)  TO CCP00-PAX-NUM  
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1)  TO  
                   MSGHDR-ADVISE-NUM (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                 THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
              GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB.


           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > ZERO  
            PERFORM VARYING WS-MSG-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                      UNTIL WS-MSG-SUB > DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR  
             IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (WS-MSG-SUB) = 1   
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (WS-MSG-SUB)
                                        TO WS-SUB-MSG-SOURCE
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (WS-MSG-SUB) 
                                        TO WS-SUB-MSG-INDEX 
              IF VALID-ADVISORIES   
                AND CCMV-ADV-SUB < 5
                 ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                 MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (WS-MSG-SUB) TO  
                      MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (WS-MSG-SUB)  TO  
                      MSGHDR-ADVISE-NUM (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                 IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (WS-MSG-SUB) = 'RSO25GCU' AND  
                    DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (WS-MSG-SUB) = 626  
                    MOVE DPP01-SUB-PAX-SEQ-NUM (WS-MSG-SUB) TO WS-SIGNED
                    MOVE WS-UNSIGNED TO CCP00-PAX-NUM   
                    MOVE DPP01-SUB-PAX-AGE (WS-MSG-SUB) TO WS-SIGNED
                    MOVE WS-UNSIGNED TO CCP00-AGE-MIN   
                 END-IF 
                 IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (WS-MSG-SUB) = 'RSO25GCU' AND  
                    DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (WS-MSG-SUB) =  
                                        1104 OR 1107 OR 1108 OR 1109
                    MOVE DPP01-SUB-PAX-SEQ-NUM (WS-MSG-SUB) TO WS-SIGNED
                    MOVE WS-UNSIGNED TO CCP00-PAX-NUM   
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
             END-IF 
            END-PERFORM 
           END-IF.  
           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'  
              IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND  
                  SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1)  AND
                 (RSP49-O-C-AGENT-DIRECT-IND (1)) = 'N' 
                 MOVE ZERO                   TO WS-REQ-PAYMENT  
                                                WS-GROSS-DIFF   
                 IF  (RSP06-IBR-GROSS-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-OLD) NOT =  
                     (RSP06-IBR-GROSS-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-NEW)
                     IF (RSP06-IBR-GROSS-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-NEW) >   
                        (RSP06-IBR-GROSS-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-OLD) 
                        PERFORM VARYING WS-BKPUCC-SUB2 FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL
                                    WS-BKPUCC-SUB2 > BKPUCC-SEGMENT-MAX 
                           IF  BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (WS-BKPUCC-SUB2) > 0  
                               ADD BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (WS-BKPUCC-SUB2)  
                                          TO WS-REQ-PAYMENT 
                           END-IF   
                        END-PERFORM 
                        COMPUTE WS-GROSS-DIFF = 
                             RSP06-IBR-GROSS-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-NEW -
                             RSP06-IBR-GROSS-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-OLD  
                        IF WS-REQ-PAYMENT = WS-GROSS-DIFF   
                           CONTINUE 
                        ELSE
                           ADD 1          TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                           MOVE 0210      TO
                                     MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO   
                                    MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                           MOVE SPACES    TO MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID   
                           PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG
                              THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT   
                           GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
                        END-IF  
                     ELSE   
                        ADD 1             TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                        MOVE 0211         TO
                                      MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                        MOVE 'CCR08UGC'   TO
                                    MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                        MOVE SPACES       TO MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID   
                        PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG   
                           THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
                        GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB   
                     END-IF 
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
              IF RSP06-IBR-ADMIN-FEE-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW >   
                 RSP06-IBR-ADMIN-FEE-AMT   OF RSP06-OLD 
                 ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                 MOVE 0071       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              END-IF
              IF RSP06-IBR-PENALTY-AMT     OF RSP06-NEW >   
                 RSP06-IBR-PENALTY-AMT     OF RSP06-OLD 
                 ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                 MOVE 0072       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

       4000-AFTER-RSO25-CALL.   

PPCTST     IF RSP25-UPDATE-FLAG = 1 
              PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT.  

           IF  BKDINP-DINING-WITH-BOOK-NUM (1) > SPACES 
               CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
              IF RSP06-IBR-TRVL-WITH-NUM OF RSP06-NEW > 0   
                 CONTINUE   
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 4050-AFTER-TRAVEL-WITH   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP06-NEW TO RSP06-NEW-B4-TW.   
           MOVE RSP06-OLD TO RSP06-OLD-B4-TW.   
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW
                                         TO WS-INP-SAIL-DUR-DAYS.   
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE  OF RSP06-NEW   
                                         TO WS-INP-SAIL-DATE.   
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE  OF RSP06-NEW   
                                         TO WS-INP-SHIP-CODE.   

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-TRVL-WITH-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                                         TO WS-REQ-TRVL-NUM 
           IF RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1) > SPACE
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-NEW (1)   
                                          TO WS-NEWBK-DINING-CODE   
                                             WS-NEWBK-DINING-PREF   
           ELSE 
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (1)
                                          TO WS-NEWBK-DINING-CODE   
           END-IF.  
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-NEW (1)   
                                          TO WS-NEWBK-DINING-CNF.   
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                                          TO WS-IBR-1ST-BOOK-NUM.   
           PERFORM 0001-TW-PROCESS-INIT.
           MOVE 'N' TO  WS-TWR-ADV-SHOWN.   
           PERFORM 8400-CHECK-TRAVEL-WITH-LOOP THRU 8400-EXIT   
               VARYING WS-BKG-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
               UNTIL WS-BKG-SUB > 20.   
           IF  WS-TWR-ADV-SHOWN = 'Y'   
               MOVE RSP06-NEW-B4-TW TO RSP06-NEW
               MOVE RSP06-OLD-B4-TW TO RSP06-OLD
               GO TO 4050-AFTER-TRAVEL-WITH.

           PERFORM VARYING WS-BKG-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL 
                WS-BKG-SUB > 20 OR  
                (WS-INP-BOOK-NUM (WS-BKG-SUB) = SPACES OR '000000') 
                CONTINUE
           END-PERFORM. 
           MOVE WS-IBR-1ST-BOOK-NUM TO  
                             WS-INP-BOOK-NUM (WS-BKG-SUB)   
                             WS01-BOOK-NUM(WS-BKG-SUB)  
                             WS-BOOK-NUM.   
           MOVE MSGHDR-SYSTEM-ID    TO WS-SYSTEM-ID.
           PERFORM 7500-CALL-RSO23-FOR-TW THRU 7500-EXIT.   
           IF WS-NEWBK-DINING-PREF > SPACES 
              MOVE WS-NEWBK-DINING-PREF TO  
                 WS-INP-PAX-DINING-PREF (WS-BKG-SUB)
           END-IF.  
           MOVE WS-NEWBK-DINING-CNF     TO  
                  WS-INP-PAX-DINING-CNF (WS-BKG-SUB).   

           MOVE WS-REQ-TRVL-NUM TO WS01-REQ-TRVL-NUM.   
           PERFORM 2000-PROCESS-UPDATE THRU 2200-END-OF-TW. 
           MOVE RSP06-NEW-B4-TW TO RSP06-NEW .  
           MOVE RSP06-OLD-B4-TW TO RSP06-OLD.   

       4050-AFTER-TRAVEL-WITH.  

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'  
              GO TO 4100-GET-CURRENT-BOOKING.   

           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'G'
              GO TO 4100-GET-CURRENT-BOOKING.   

           FETCH CURRENT AGENCY-ASSOCIATION-REC RECORD. 
           IF AAR-LAST-BOOK-DATE NOT = DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE
              MOVE DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE TO AAR-LAST-BOOK-DATE  
              MODIFY AGENCY-ASSOCIATION-REC RECORD  
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-CONF-IND = 'M' 
              MOVE LOW-VALUES                TO ITRF-REC
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE       OF RSP06-NEW   
                TO DPP04-TRF-SAIL-DATE  
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE       OF RSP06-NEW   
                TO DPP04-TRF-SHIP-CODE  
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS   OF RSP06-NEW   
                TO DPP04-TRF-DURATION-DAYS  
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM       OF RSP06-NEW   
                TO DPP04-TRF-CABIN-NUM  
              MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE     TO DPP04-TRF-LOCATION-CODE 
              MOVE 'CRQ'                     TO DPP04-HIST-ACTIVITY-TYPE
              MOVE INFO-USERID               TO DPP04-HIST-OPER-INITIAL 
              MOVE 'Y'                       TO DPP04-MAIL-IND  
              MOVE ZERO                      TO DPP04-TRF-PAX-SEQ-NUM   
              MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE      TO 
                                             DPP04-HIST-AGENCY-CONN-CODE
              PERFORM IRP15-CREATE-TRF-RECORD THRU IRP15-TRF-EXIT   
           END-IF.  

      *=================================================================
           MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID          TO IRP75-USERID.
           MOVE MSGHDR-SESSION-ID       TO IRP75-SESSION-ID.
           MOVE MSGHDR-UI-CODE          TO IRP75-USER-INTERFACE-TYPE.   
           MOVE RSP50-O-PHONE-NUM       TO IRP75-PHONE-NUM. 
           MOVE RSP50-O-CUSTOMER-NUM    TO IRP75-CUSTOMER-NUM.  
           MOVE RSP50-O-CURRENCY-CODE   TO IRP75-CURRENCY-CODE. 
           MOVE 1                       TO IRP75-SEQ-NUM.   
           MOVE 'I'                     TO IRP75-BOOK-TYPE. 

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW     TO 
                                              IRP75-REQ-BOOK-CAT-CODE.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW TO 
                                            IRP75-QUOTED-CPA-CAT-CODE.  

           EXEC SQL 
                SELECT PHASE_NUM,   
                       RATE_CODE
                  INTO WS-PHASE-NUM,
                       WS-RATE1-CODE
                  FROM RATE_MASTER  
                 WHERE PRP_OWNER_ID = :RSP06-ENTITY-OWN-ID OF   
                                       RSP06-NEW(1 1)   
                   AND PRP_OWNER_CODE = 'RTE'   
PPC470           FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE WS-PHASE-NUM            TO  IRP75-QUOTED-1-2-PHASE-NUM. 

           STRING RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW (1) DELIMITED BY SIZE
                  WS-RATE1-CODE DELIMITED BY SIZE   
                  INTO WS-FARE-CODE.
           MOVE  WS-FARE-CODE TO IRP75-QUOTED-1-2-FARE-CODE.

           PERFORM VARYING SUB1 FROM 1 BY 1 
                    UNTIL SUB1 > RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT OF RSP06-NEW 
                IF RSP06-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-NEW(SUB1) =  
                                     RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-NEW(3) 
                   AND RSP06-GUEST-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW(SUB1) = 
                                      RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW(3) 
                    PERFORM VARYING ESUB FROM 1 BY 1
                      UNTIL ESUB > RSP06-ENTITY-CNT OF RSP06-NEW(SUB1)  
                      IF RSP06-ENTITY-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                 (SUB1 ESUB) = 'ADB'
                         AND RSP06-ENTITY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                (SUB1 ESUB)(1:2)= 'EG'  
                          MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-OWN-ID OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                         (SUB1 ESUB)
                                                    TO WS-ENTITY-OWN-ID 
                          MOVE  'Y'                 TO WS-GUEST-FOUND   
                      END-IF
                    END-PERFORM 
                END-IF  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-GUEST-FOUND NOT = 'Y'  
              PERFORM VARYING SUB1 FROM 1 BY 1  
                       UNTIL SUB1 > RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT OF RSP06-NEW  
                   IF RSP06-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-NEW(SUB1) =   
                                    RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-NEW(3)  
                      AND RSP06-GUEST-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW(SUB1)  = 0   
                      PERFORM VARYING ESUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                       UNTIL ESUB > RSP06-ENTITY-CNT OF RSP06-NEW(SUB1) 
                       IF RSP06-ENTITY-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW
                                                (SUB1 ESUB) = 'ADB' 
                          AND RSP06-ENTITY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW
                                                (SUB1 ESUB)(1:2)= 'EG'  
                           MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-OWN-ID OF RSP06-NEW
                                                 (SUB1 ESUB)
                                                   TO WS-ENTITY-OWN-ID  
                       END-IF   
                      END-PERFORM   
                   END-IF   
              END-PERFORM   
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-ENTITY-OWN-ID > ZERO   
              EXEC SQL  
                   SELECT PHASE_NUM,
                          ADD_BENEFIT_CODE  
                     INTO WS-AB-PHASE-NUM,  
                          WS-ADB-CODE   
                     FROM ADD_BENEFIT_MASTER
                    WHERE PRP_OWNER_ID   = :WS-ENTITY-OWN-ID
                      AND PRP_OWNER_CODE = 'ADB'
                      AND ADD_BENEFIT_TYPE = 'E'
PPC470              FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY  
              END-EXEC  
              MOVE WS-AB-PHASE-NUM      TO IRP75-QUOTED-3-4-PHASE-NUM   
              STRING RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW(3)
                     WS-ADB-CODE(3:3) DELIMITED BY SIZE 
                INTO WS-EXTRA-FARE-CODE 
              MOVE  WS-EXTRA-FARE-CODE  TO IRP75-QUOTED-3-4-FARE-CODE   
           END-IF.  

           IF INFO-PROG-APP = 'CC'  
              MOVE 'E'                  TO IRP75-TEST-IND   
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'T'                  TO IRP75-TEST-IND   
           END-IF.  
           ACCEPT IRP75-ACTIVITY-DATE   FROM DATE YYYYMMDD. 
           ACCEPT IRP75-ACTIVITY-TIME   FROM TIME.  

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-PRC-XCHG-RATE      OF RSP06-NEW   
             TO IRP75-PRC-XCHG-RATE.
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-DIRECT-IND         OF RSP06-NEW   
             TO IRP75-DIRECT-IND.   
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-AGENCY-CONN-CODE   OF RSP06-NEW   
             TO IRP75-AGENCY-CONN-CODE. 
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-LOCATION-CODE      OF RSP06-NEW   
             TO IRP75-LOCATION-CODE.
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM           OF RSP06-NEW   
             TO IRP75-BOOK-NUM. 
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE          OF RSP06-NEW   
             TO IRP75-SHIP-CODE.
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE          OF RSP06-NEW   
             TO IRP75-SAILING-DATE. 
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS      OF RSP06-NEW   
             TO IRP75-DURATION-DAYS.
           MOVE TDP01-ITINERARY-CODE
             TO IRP75-REQ-ITIN-CODE.
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE      OF RSP06-NEW   
             TO IRP75-REQ-BOOK-CAT-CODE.
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE  OF RSP06-NEW   
             TO IRP75-QUOTED-CPA-CAT-CODE.  

           COMPUTE IRP75-NUM-PAX-REQ =  
                         RSP06-IBR-NUM-ADULTS   OF RSP06-NEW +  
                         RSP06-IBR-NUM-CHILDREN OF RSP06-NEW.   

           IF  RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW =  1 OR 2 

               IF IRP75-VAC-CODE > SPACES   
                  COMPUTE IRP75-QUOTED-1-2-RATE-AMT =   
                  RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW (1) + 
                  RSP06-PAX-UPGRADE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (1) + 
                  RSP06-PAX-PACKAGE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (1) - 
                  RSP06-PAX-DISCOUNT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (1)   
               ELSE 
                  COMPUTE IRP75-QUOTED-1-2-RATE-AMT =   
                  RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW (1) + 
                  RSP06-PAX-UPGRADE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (1) + 
                  RSP06-PAX-DISCOUNT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (1)   
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF  RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW =  3  
               IF IRP75-VAC-CODE > SPACES   
                  COMPUTE IRP75-QUOTED-3-4-RATE-AMT =   
                  RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW (3) + 
                  RSP06-PAX-UPGRADE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (3) + 
                  RSP06-PAX-PACKAGE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (3) - 
                  RSP06-PAX-DISCOUNT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (3)   
               ELSE 
                  COMPUTE IRP75-QUOTED-3-4-RATE-AMT =   
                  RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW (3) + 
                  RSP06-PAX-UPGRADE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (3) + 
                  RSP06-PAX-DISCOUNT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (3)   
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF  RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW =  4  

               COMPUTE IRP75-QUOTED-3-4-ADDON-AMT = 
                  (RSP06-PAX-ADDON-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (3) +   
                   RSP06-PAX-ADDON-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (4))
               IF IRP75-VAC-CODE > SPACES   
                  COMPUTE IRP75-QUOTED-3-4-RATE-AMT =   
                  (RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW (3) +
                   RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW (4) +
                   RSP06-PAX-UPGRADE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (3) +
                   RSP06-PAX-UPGRADE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (4) +
                   RSP06-PAX-PACKAGE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (3) +
                   RSP06-PAX-PACKAGE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (4) -
                   RSP06-PAX-DISCOUNT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (3) -
                   RSP06-PAX-DISCOUNT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (4)) / 2 
               ELSE 
                  COMPUTE IRP75-QUOTED-3-4-RATE-AMT =   
                  (RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW (3) +
                   RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW (4) +
                   RSP06-PAX-UPGRADE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (3) +
                   RSP06-PAX-UPGRADE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (4) +
                   RSP06-PAX-DISCOUNT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (3) -
                   RSP06-PAX-DISCOUNT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (4)) / 2 
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF  RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW =  5  
               COMPUTE IRP75-QUOTED-3-4-ADDON-AMT = 
                  (RSP06-PAX-ADDON-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (3) +   
                   RSP06-PAX-ADDON-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (4) +   
                   RSP06-PAX-ADDON-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (5) )   

               IF IRP75-VAC-CODE > SPACES   
                  COMPUTE IRP75-QUOTED-3-4-RATE-AMT =   
                  (RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW (3) +
                   RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW (4) +
                   RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW (5) +
                   RSP06-PAX-UPGRADE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (3) +
                   RSP06-PAX-UPGRADE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (4) +
                   RSP06-PAX-UPGRADE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (5) +
                   RSP06-PAX-PACKAGE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (3) +
                   RSP06-PAX-PACKAGE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (4) +
                   RSP06-PAX-PACKAGE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (5) -
                   RSP06-PAX-DISCOUNT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (3) -
                   RSP06-PAX-DISCOUNT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (4) -
                   RSP06-PAX-DISCOUNT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (5)) / 3 
               ELSE 
                  COMPUTE IRP75-QUOTED-3-4-RATE-AMT =   
                  (RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW (3) +
                   RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW (4) +
                   RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW (5) +
                   RSP06-PAX-UPGRADE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (3) +
                   RSP06-PAX-UPGRADE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (4) +
                   RSP06-PAX-UPGRADE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW (5) +
                   RSP06-PAX-DISCOUNT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (3) -
                   RSP06-PAX-DISCOUNT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (4) -
                   RSP06-PAX-DISCOUNT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (5)) / 3 
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF IRP75-DIRECT-IND NOT     = 'N' AND 'Y' AND 'U'
              MOVE 'U' TO IRP75-DIRECT-IND. 

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                     UNTIL WS-SUB > IRP75-NUM-PAX-REQ   
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-STATE OF RSP06-NEW (WS-SUB)
                TO IRP75-RESIDENT-STATE-CODE (WS-SUB)   
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-SUB)   
                TO IRP75-PAST-GUEST-NUM (WS-SUB)
           END-PERFORM. 
           IF IRP75-TEST-IND = 'E' OR 'T'   
              PERFORM IRP75-MOVE-TO-SPILL THRU IRP75-EXIT   
              IF IRP75-ERROR-CODE > 0   
                 DISPLAY 'ERROR STORING SPILL DATA' UPON PRINTER.   

      *=================================================================
       4100-GET-CURRENT-BOOKING.

           IF (RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW NOT = IBR-BOOK-NUM) OR   
              (CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1' AND BKCRV1-ATTEMPT-CNT > 1) 

              MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW  
                TO RSP23-I-INDV-BOOK-NUM
              MOVE 'A'                  TO RSP23-I-BOOK-TYPE
              MOVE 'Y'                  TO RSP23-I-READ-IBR-IND 
                                           RSP23-I-READ-IFR-IND 
                                           RSP23-I-READ-I-PIAR-IND  
                                           RSP23-I-READ-I-PIER-IND  
                                           RSP23-I-READ-PAX-IND 
                                           RSP23-I-READ-PTFR-IND
                                           RSP23-I-READ-PPR-IND 
                                           RSP23-I-READ-TIR-IND 
                                           RSP23-I-READ-PXFR-IND
                                           RSP23-I-READ-PIAR-IND
                                           RSP23-I-READ-PIID-IND
                                           RSP23-I-READ-PICR-IND
                                           RSP23-I-READ-CIF-IND 
                                           RSP23-I-READ-I-TOAR-IND  
                                           RSP23-I-READ-PPIR-IND
                                           RSP23-I-READ-IBPIR-IND   
                                           RSP23-I-READ-PIER-IND
                                           RSP23-I-READ-PPHOR-IND   
                                           RSP23-I-READ-POBC-IND
                                           RSP23-I-READ-PICF-IND
                                           RSP23-I-READ-PICC-IND
                                           RSP23-I-READ-CSSXR-IND   
                                           RSP23-I-READ-GST-OFR-IND 
                                           RSP23-I-READ-GST-OFR-QUL-IND 
              CALL 'RSO23RGC'        USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS 
                                           DPP01-COMMON-INFO
                                           RSP23-RGC-INPUT  
                                           RSP06-NEW

              IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > ZERO AND   
                 DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1   
                 ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                 MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) TO   
                      MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1)  TO   
                      MSGHDR-ADVISE-NUM (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                 PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG  
                    THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT 
                 GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB  
              END-IF

           END-IF.  

           IF WS-CONF-IND = 'F' AND 
              RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'G'
              MOVE ZERO           TO WS-EMAIL-SUB1  
              PERFORM VARYING WS-EMAIL-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                 UNTIL WS-EMAIL-SUB > RSP49-O-COMMUN-ENTRY-CNT  
                 IF RSP49-O-C-EMAIL (WS-EMAIL-SUB) > SPACES 
                    MOVE WS-EMAIL-SUB   
                                  TO WS-EMAIL-SUB1  
                    MOVE RSP49-O-COMMUN-ENTRY-CNT   
                                  TO WS-EMAIL-SUB   
                 END-IF 
              END-PERFORM   
              IF  WS-EMAIL-SUB1 = ZERO  
              AND RSP49-O-PAX-EMAIL-CNT NOT > ZEROES
                  ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                  MOVE 0079       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                  MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                  GO TO 4300-FINISH-REPLY-BLOCK 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-EMAIL-CNT > 0  
              MOVE 0 TO WS-FCNF-FLAG
              PERFORM VARYING WS-EML-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL  
                              WS-EML-SUB > WS-EMAIL-CNT 
                 IF (WS-FCNF-FLAG = 0) OR   
                    (WS-EML-CONF-IND (WS-EML-SUB) NOT = 'F')
                 MOVE 'Y' TO IRP03-CRS-IND  
                 MOVE WS-EML-FAX-TYPE (WS-EML-SUB)  
                      TO IRP03-FAX-TYPE 
                 MOVE WS-EML-ADDR (WS-EML-SUB)  
                      TO IRP03-EMAIL-OR-FAX-NUM 
                 PERFORM IRP29-FAX-PASSOFF THRU IRP29-FXP-EXIT  
                    IF WS-EML-CONF-IND (WS-EML-SUB) = 'F'   
                       MOVE 1 TO WS-FCNF-FLAG   
                    END-IF  
                 END-IF 
              END-PERFORM   
           END-IF.  
           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'G'
              GO TO 4300-FINISH-REPLY-BLOCK.

       4300-FINISH-REPLY-BLOCK. 
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO BKCRV2-BOOK-NUM. 
           MOVE 11                              TO BKCRV2-STATUS-CODE.  

           MOVE RSP51-O-DURATION-DAYS  TO ITR-DURATION-DAYS.
           MOVE TDP01-EMBARK-PORT-CODE TO ITR-PORT-CODE.
           MOVE TDP01-ITINERARY-CODE   TO ITR-ITINERARY-CODE.   


           FETCH ITINERARY-REC RECORD.  

           MOVE ITR-EMBARK-BEG-TIME TO BKCRV2-ITIN-ACTION-TIME (1). 
           MOVE 'Emb begins'        TO BKCRV2-ITIN-ACTION-TEXT (1). 
           MOVE ITR-EMBARK-END-TIME TO BKCRV2-ITIN-ACTION-TIME (2). 
           MOVE 'Emb ends  '        TO BKCRV2-ITIN-ACTION-TEXT (2). 

           MOVE ZERO TO WS-PAX-SUB. 

       4400-PAX-INFO-RESPONSE.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-PAX-SUB. 

           IF WS-PAX-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW   
               GO TO 4500-SEND-RESPONSE.

           MOVE WS-PAX-SUB  
             TO BKGID2-PAX-SEQ-NUM (WS-PAX-SUB).
           IF SSP03-O-PPC-COMPLAINT-FLG = 0 
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (1)
                TO BKGID2-RATE-CODE (WS-PAX-SUB)
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'BKGRTE' 
                TO BKGID2-RATE-CODE (WS-PAX-SUB)
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
             TO BKGID2-BOOK-NUM (WS-PAX-SUB).   
           MOVE 11  
             TO BKGID2-STATUS-CODE (WS-PAX-SUB).

           EXEC SQL 
                SELECT DESCRIPTION  
                  INTO WS-PD-DESCRIPTION
                  FROM PRP_DESCRIPTION  
                 WHERE PRP_OWNER_CODE = 'RTE'   
                   AND PRP_OWNER_ID = :RSP06-PAX-RATE-OWNER-ID  
                                   OF RSP06-NEW (WS-PAX-SUB)
                   AND LANGUAGE_CODE = 'ENG'
                   AND (LOCALITY_CODE IS NULL   
                    OR  LOCALITY_CODE = :RSP06-IBR-LOCALITY-CODE OF 
                                         RSP06-NEW) 
                 FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0 OR SQLCODE = 100  
              MOVE 0100 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (1)   
PRCCMB        MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (1)   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  
           MOVE WS-PD-DESCRIPTION   
             TO BKGID2-RATE-NAME (WS-PAX-SUB).  
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-ORIGINAL-GUEST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (WS-PAX-SUB)  
             TO BKGID2-ORIG-GUEST-IND (WS-PAX-SUB)  

           GO TO 4400-PAX-INFO-RESPONSE.

       4500-SEND-RESPONSE.  

           PERFORM 6600-GET-ADDITIONAL-INFO THRU 6600-EXIT. 

           MOVE 'N' TO CCP45-SP-TAKEOVER-SW.
           MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID TO CCP45-USER-ID.

           COMPUTE WS-TOTAL-PAYMENT-AMT =   
                                 RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-UCC-PMT   OF RSP06-OLD 
                               + RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-CASH-PMT  OF RSP06-OLD 
                               + RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-OLD 
                               + RSP06-IBR-CABIN-UCC-PMT   OF RSP06-OLD 
                               + RSP06-IBR-CABIN-CASH-PMT  OF RSP06-OLD 
                               + RSP06-IBR-CABIN-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-OLD.

           IF BKCRV1-ATTEMPT-CNT > 1
               NEXT SENTENCE
           ELSE 
               PERFORM 7100-PROCESS-PAYMENTS  THRU 7600-EXIT.   

           IF   RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (1:1) =  'C' OR 'T' AND
              ( RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (1:1)  
                                                 NOT =  'C' AND 'T' )   
                                                                     AND
              RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-1 OF RSP06-NEW <= SPACES        AND
              RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-2 OF RSP06-NEW <= SPACES        AND
              RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-AMT    OF RSP06-NEW  > 0             AND
              RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-AMT    OF RSP06-NEW  >  
              WS-TOTAL-PAYMENT-AMT  
              ADD 1             TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE 0091         TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC'   TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
           END-IF.  

           IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND 
               SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1)                   AND  
              CCP45-SECURITY-ID = 'T'                  AND  
              BKDDLV-DOC-DELIVERY-LOC-CODE = 'A'       AND  
              BKDDLV-AGENCY-PHONE-NUM > SPACES  
              NEXT SENTENCE 
           ELSE 
              GO TO 4600-CONTINUE.  

           IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND 
               SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1)                   AND  
              CCP45-SECURITY-ID = 'T'   
              IF CCP45-CUSTOMER-NUM = RSP06-IBR-CUSTOMER-NUM
                                                       OF RSP06-NEW 
                 GO TO 4600-CONTINUE
              ELSE  
                 ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                 MOVE 0066 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                 GO TO 4600-CONTINUE.   

       4600-CONTINUE.   
           IF  (WS-AUTOPAY-MSG-CANC      OR 
                WS-AUTOPAY-MSG-DEPSAT    OR 
                WS-AUTOPAY-MSG-DEPNOTSAT)   AND 
               (WS-DENIED-MESSAGE NOT = 'Y')
               ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
               MOVE WS-AUTOPAY-MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE TO
                           MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
               MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
               GO TO 4600-BUILD-OUTPUT  
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-DENIED-MESSAGE = 'Y'   
              GO TO 4600-BUILD-OUTPUT   
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-SPLITPAY-SCHD-REQUEST = 'Y'
              ADD 1              TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
              MOVE 0202          TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC'    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
           ELSE 
              IF WS-SPLITPAY-CANCEL-REQUEST = 'Y'   
                 ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                 MOVE 0198       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              ELSE  
                 IF WS-SPLITPAY-CC-CHG-REQUEST = 'Y'
                    ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                    MOVE 0205    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                    MOVE 'CCR08UGC' 
                                 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

       4600-BUILD-OUTPUT.   
           IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND 
               SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1) AND
              CCP45-SECURITY-ID = 'T'   
               ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
               MOVE 0067 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
               MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB).  

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'  
              IF TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD = 20220331 AND
                 TDP01-SHIP-CODE = 'PR' 
                 ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                 MOVE 0212 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              ELSE  
                IF TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD <= 20251231 
                   ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                   MOVE 0213 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                   MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO   
                                    MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                END-IF  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF CCP45-SECURITY-ID = 'T'   
              MOVE SPACES TO MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID   
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM CCP78-OUTPUT-CCNEWBK2
              THRU CCP78-OUTPUT-CCNEWBK2-EXIT.  

           GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB.   

      *=================================================================
       4700-CHECK-COUNTRY-STATE-ZIP.

           PERFORM VARYING J FROM 1 BY 1
                   UNTIL J > BKGADI-SUB OR CCMV-ERROR-SUB > ZERO

              MOVE BKGADI-NATIONALITY-CODE (J) TO WS-COUNTRY-CODE   
              MOVE BKGADI-STATE-CODE       (J) TO WS-STATE-CODE 
              MOVE BKGADI-ZIP-CODE         (J) TO WS-ZIP-CODE   
              PERFORM 4750-WS-STATE-ZIP-CHECK THRU 4750-EXIT
              PERFORM 4800-WS-STRT-CITY-CNTRY THRU 4800-EXIT

           END-PERFORM. 
           IF CCMV-ERROR-SUB > 0
              GO TO 4700-EXIT.  

           PERFORM VARYING J FROM 1 BY 1
                   UNTIL J > BKPUCC-SUB  OR  CCMV-ERROR-SUB > ZERO  

              MOVE BKPUCC-COUNTRY-CODE    (J) TO WS-COUNTRY-CODE
              MOVE BKPUCC-STATE-CODE      (J) TO WS-STATE-CODE  
              MOVE BKPUCC-HOLDER-ZIP-CODE (J) TO WS-ZIP-CODE
              MOVE BKPUCC-CID             (J) TO WS-CID 
              PERFORM 4750-WS-STATE-ZIP-CHECK THRU 4750-EXIT

           END-PERFORM. 
           IF CCMV-ERROR-SUB > 0
              GO TO 4700-EXIT.  

           IF BKPUCC-SEGMENT (1) > SPACES   
              PERFORM VARYING USUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL
                              USUB > BKPUCC-SEGMENT-MAX OR  
                         (BKPUCC-CURRENCY-CODE (USUB) NOT > SPACES) 
                IF  SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1
                    IF BKPUCC-PMT-REF-NUM (USUB) > SPACES   
                       PERFORM 9800-VALIDATE-REF-NUM
                          THRU 9800-VALIDATE-REF-NUM-EXIT   
                    END-IF  
                ELSE
                    IF BKPUCC-PMT-REF-NUM (USUB) > SPACES   
                       ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                       MOVE 0153 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                       GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
                    END-IF  
                END-IF  
              END-PERFORM   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE AGCINF-NATIONALITY-CODE TO WS-COUNTRY-CODE. 
           MOVE AGCINF-STATE-CODE       TO WS-STATE-CODE.   
           MOVE AGCINF-ZIP-CODE         TO WS-ZIP-CODE. 
           PERFORM 4750-WS-STATE-ZIP-CHECK  THRU 4750-EXIT. 
           IF CCMV-ERROR-SUB > 0
              GO TO 4700-EXIT.  

           MOVE CSMADR-COUNTRY-CODE  TO WS-COUNTRY-CODE.
           MOVE CSMADR-STATE-CODE    TO WS-STATE-CODE.  
           MOVE CSMADR-ZIP-CODE      TO WS-ZIP-CODE.
           PERFORM 4750-WS-STATE-ZIP-CHECK THRU 4750-EXIT.  
           IF CCMV-ERROR-SUB > 0
              GO TO 4700-EXIT.  

       4700-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       4750-WS-STATE-ZIP-CHECK. 

           IF WS-COUNTRY-CODE = 'US' OR 'CA'
               IF WS-STATE-CODE NOT > SPACES
                   MOVE 1    TO CCMV-ERROR-SUB  
               ELSE 
                   IF WS-ZIP-CODE NOT > SPACES  
                       MOVE 2    TO CCMV-ERROR-SUB. 

       4750-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       4800-WS-STRT-CITY-CNTRY. 

           IF BKGADI-ADDRESS1 (J) = SPACES OR   
              BKGADI-CITY (J)     = SPACES OR   
              BKGADI-NATIONALITY-CODE (J) = SPACES  
                MOVE 3  TO    CCMV-ERROR-SUB.   

       4800-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

      *=================================================================
       4850-FETCH-SAIL-SHIP-REC.

           MOVE WS-SAIL-DATE         TO TDP01-I-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD. 
           MOVE BKCRV1-SHIP-CODE     TO TDP01-I-SHIP-CODE.  
           MOVE BKCRV1-DURATION-DAYS TO TDP01-I-DUR-DAYS.   


           PERFORM TDP01-RET-SAIL-DETAILS THRU  
                   TDP01-RET-SAIL-DETAILS-EXIT. 

           IF TDP01-SAIL-SQLERR 
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF TDP01-SAIL-NOTFND OR  
              TDP01-PROC-ERROR > ZEROES 
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF TDP01-SCAT-SAIL-ID = TDP01-SAIL-ID AND
              TDP01-SAIL-CAT-CNT > 1
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              PERFORM TDP01-RET-ALL-SCAT THRU   
                      TDP01-RET-ALL-SCAT-EXIT   
              IF TDP01-SCAT-SQLERR  
                 GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF (TDP01-STATUS-CODE = 'C' AND  
               TDP01-SPECIAL-SAIL-IND  NOT = 'D') OR
              (TDP01-STATUS-CODE NOT = 'O' AND 'R' AND 'W' AND 'D') OR  
              (TDP01-STATUS-CODE = 'O' AND  
               TDP01-SPECIAL-SAIL-IND > SPACES AND  
               TDP01-SPECIAL-SAIL-IND NOT = 'C')
              ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE 0043 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           IF TDP01-STATUS-CODE = 'R' OR TDP01-SPECIAL-SAIL-IND = 'C'   
              ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE 0134 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           IF (TDP01-STATUS-CODE = 'D' OR 'W') OR   
              (TDP01-STATUS-CODE = 'C' AND TDP01-SPECIAL-SAIL-IND = 'D')
              ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE 0133 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           IF TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD  NOT > DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE 
              ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE 0044 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

       4850-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

      *=================================================================
       4900-PROCESS-AGENCY. 

           MOVE SPACES TO CCP45-ALT-AGY-FOUND   
                          WS-AGENT-REC-STORED-IND.  
           MOVE 'N'    TO WS-USING-DOC-DELV-PHONE-SW.   

           MOVE 'N'   TO CCP45-ALT-STORE.   
           PERFORM CCP45-CHECK-ALT-AGY THRU CCP45-CHECK-ALT-AGY-EXIT.   
           MOVE 'Y'   TO CCP45-ALT-STORE.   

           IF CCP45-ALT-AGY-FOUND = 'Y' 
              IF DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE >= TDP01-FINAL-PAYMENT-DATE  
                 ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                 MOVE 0045       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                 MOVE 'N'      TO CCP45-ALT-STORE   
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 'Y'      TO WS-USING-DOC-DELV-PHONE-SW
                 PERFORM 5150-FETCH-DEFAULT-AAR-REC   THRU 5100-EXIT
                 GO TO 4900-CONTINUE
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF  MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID       NOT > SPACES AND  
               MSGHDR-AGENT-PHONE-NUM NOT > SPACES AND  
               MSGHDR-AGENT-ARC-NUM   NOT > SPACES AND  
               MSGHDR-AGENT-CLIA-NUM  NOT > SPACES  

               PERFORM 5000-STORE-AGENT THRU 5000-EXIT  

               IF AGENT-REC-STORED-OR-FOUND 
                  GO TO 4900-CONTINUE   
               ELSE 
                  GO TO 5300-AGENCY-ERROR   
               END-IF   

           END-IF.  

           IF MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID NOT > SPACES AND 
              MSGHDR-AGENT-PHONE-NUM > SPACES   

              PERFORM 5100-FETCH-AGENT-USING-PHONE THRU 5100-EXIT   

              IF AGENT-REC-NOT-STORED-OR-FOUND  
                 PERFORM 5000-STORE-AGENT THRU 5000-EXIT
                 IF AGENT-REC-STORED-OR-FOUND   
                    GO TO 4900-CONTINUE 
                 ELSE   
                    GO TO 5300-AGENCY-ERROR 
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 4900-CONTINUE
              END-IF

           END-IF.  

       4900-CONTINUE.   

           PERFORM 5200-VALIDATE-AGENT-FIELDS   THRU 5200-EXIT. 
           PERFORM 5250-SET-AGENT-MSGHDR-VALUES THRU 5250-EXIT. 

       4900-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

      *=================================================================
       5000-STORE-AGENT.

           MOVE SPACE TO CCP45-AGY-FOUND.   

           MOVE 'Y' TO CCP45-DIRECT-IND.

           PERFORM CCP45-STORE-AGENT-REC
              THRU CCP45-STORE-AGENT-REC-EXIT.  

           IF CCP45-PHONE-NUM NOT NUMERIC   
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0046       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           MOVE SPACES        TO WS-AGENT-VALID.
           IF CCP45-AGY-FOUND = 'Y' 
              PERFORM 5050-CHK-OTHER-PARAMETERS THRU 5050-CHK-EXIT  
              IF WS-AGENT-VALID = 'Y'   
                 MOVE 'Y'     TO WS-AGENT-REC-STORED-IND
                 GO TO 5000-EXIT
              ELSE  
                 ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                 MOVE 0047       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.  

           MOVE CCP45-AGY-FOUND TO WS-AGENT-REC-STORED-IND. 

           IF CSMINF-AGENT-EREF > SPACES
              MOVE FUNCTION UPPER-CASE (CSMINF-AGENT-EREF)  
                                      TO CSMINF-AGENT-EREF  
              MOVE CSMINF-AGENT-EREF  TO WS-EMAIL-ADDRESS   
              INSPECT WS-EMAIL-ADDRESS REPLACING FIRST ' ' BY '_'   
              INSPECT WS-EMAIL-ADDRESS REPLACING ALL '_ ' BY '  '   
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5000-EXIT   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE WS-EMAIL-ADDRESS   TO AER-EMAIL-ADDRESS.
           MOVE MSGHDR-UI-CODE     TO AER-COMPANY-CODE. 
           MOVE AGENT-CUSTOMER-NUM TO AER-CUSTOMER-NUM. 
           MOVE 0                  TO AER-DELIVERY-ERROR-COUNT  
                                      AER-MESSAGE-IND.  

           IF  (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND
                SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1)   
               MOVE 0              TO WS-AER-MESG-IND-2 
               MOVE 1              TO WS-AER-MESG-IND-1 
               MOVE WS-AER-MESSAGE-IND  
                                   TO AER-MESSAGE-IND   
           END-IF.  

           STORE AGENT-EMAIL-REC
              ON ERROR GO TO 5000-ERROR.

           GO TO 5000-EXIT. 

       5000-ERROR.  

           IF ERROR-NUM NOT = '0005'
              GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.

           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB. 
           MOVE 0048       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB).  
           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.

       5000-EXIT.   
           EXIT.
       5050-CHK-OTHER-PARAMETERS.   

           MOVE AGENT-PHONE-NUM    TO ATCR-PHONE-NUM.   
           MOVE 'C'                TO ATCR-COMPANY-CODE.
           FETCH AGENT-TERMS-COMPANY-REC RECORD 
              ON ERROR GO TO 5070-CHK-PHONE-NAME.   

       5060-FETCH-NEXT-AER. 

           FETCH NEXT AGENT-EMAIL-REC WITHIN AGENT-EMAIL-SET SET
              ON ERROR GO TO 5070-CHK-PHONE-NAME.   
           MOVE FUNCTION UPPER-CASE (CSMINF-AGENT-EREF) 
                                   TO CSMINF-AGENT-EREF 
           IF CSMINF-AGENT-EREF = AER-EMAIL-ADDRESS 
              MOVE 'Y'             TO WS-AGENT-VALID
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5060-FETCH-NEXT-AER 
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 5050-CHK-EXIT. 

       5070-CHK-PHONE-NAME. 
           MOVE 0                  TO WS-AGENT-LENGTH   
                                      WS-AGENT-LEN  
                                      WS-AGENT-LAST.
           MOVE SPACES             TO WS-AGENT-NAME 
                                      WS-AGENT-CHARS
                                      WS-AGENT-LAST-NAME
                                      WS-AGENT-FIRST-INITIAL.   
           MOVE AGENT-NAME         TO WS-AGENT-NAME.
           MOVE WS-AGENT-NAME      TO WS-AGENT-CHARS.   

           MOVE 30                 TO WS-AGENT-LENGTH.  
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-AGENT-CHAR (WS-AGENT-LENGTH) NOT = SPACES   
               SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-AGENT-LENGTH  
           END-PERFORM. 

           MOVE WS-AGENT-LENGTH    TO WS-AGENT-LEN. 
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-AGENT-LENGTH <= 0 OR
                         WS-AGENT-CHAR (WS-AGENT-LENGTH) = SPACES   
               SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-AGENT-LENGTH  
           END-PERFORM. 

           COMPUTE WS-AGENT-LAST = WS-AGENT-LEN - WS-AGENT-LENGTH.  
           COMPUTE WS-AGENT-LENGTH = WS-AGENT-LENGTH + 1.   
           MOVE WS-AGENT-NAME (WS-AGENT-LENGTH:WS-AGENT-LAST)   
                                   TO WS-AGENT-LAST-NAME.   
           MOVE WS-AGENT-NAME (1:1) 
                                   TO WS-AGENT-FIRST-INITIAL.   
           MOVE FUNCTION UPPER-CASE (CSMINF-FIRST-NAME) 
                                   TO CSMINF-FIRST-NAME.
           MOVE FUNCTION UPPER-CASE (CSMINF-LAST-NAME)  
                                   TO CSMINF-LAST-NAME. 
           MOVE FUNCTION UPPER-CASE (CSMADR-ADDRESS1)   
                                   TO CSMADR-ADDRESS1.  
           MOVE FUNCTION UPPER-CASE (CSMADR-ADDRESS2)   
                                   TO CSMADR-ADDRESS2.  
           IF WS-AGENT-LAST-NAME = CSMINF-LAST-NAME 
              IF (WS-AGENT-FIRST-INITIAL = CSMINF-FIRST-NAME (1:1)) OR  
                 (CSMADR-ADDRESS1 = AGENT-ADDRESS1 AND  
                 CSMADR-ADDRESS2 = AGENT-ADDRESS2)  
                 MOVE 'Y'          TO WS-AGENT-VALID
                 GO TO 5050-CHK-EXIT
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

       5050-CHK-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

      *=================================================================
       5100-FETCH-AGENT-USING-PHONE.

           MOVE SPACE                  TO WS-AGENT-REC-STORED-IND.  
           MOVE MSGHDR-AGENT-PHONE-NUM TO WS-AGENCY-PHONE-X.
           MOVE 001                    TO APR-COUNTRY-NUM.  
           MOVE WS-AGENCY-PHONE        TO APR-PHONE-NUM.
           FETCH AGENT-PHONE-REC RECORD 
              ON ERROR GO TO 5100-EXIT. 
           FETCH OWNER WITHIN AGENT-PHONE-SET SET   
              ON ERROR GO TO 5100-EXIT. 

           PERFORM 5150-FETCH-DEFAULT-AAR-REC THRU 5100-EXIT.
