           MOVE 'Y'  TO WS-AGENT-REC-STORED-IND.

       5100-EXIT.   
           EXIT.


       5150-FETCH-DEFAULT-AAR-REC.  

           MOVE AGENT-CUSTOMER-NUM   TO AAR-CUSTOMER-NUM.   
           MOVE SPACES               TO AAR-CUSTOMER-FILL.  
           MOVE 'CM'                 TO AAR-TYPE.   
           FETCH AGENCY-ASSOCIATION-REC RECORD. 

       5150-EXIT.   
           EXIT.


       5200-VALIDATE-AGENT-FIELDS.  

           IF AGENT-PHONE-NUM = 3055201212  
              MOVE 90 TO DPP02-NUMBER-DAYS  
              MOVE TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD 
                                 TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE 
              PERFORM DPP10-DEMINISH-YYYYMMDD-DATE  
              IF DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE >= DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
                 ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                 MOVE 0049       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

       5200-EXIT.   
           EXIT.


       5250-SET-AGENT-MSGHDR-VALUES.

           IF AGENT-REC-STORED-OR-FOUND 
              MOVE AGENT-CUSTOMER-NUM TO MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID   
              MOVE APR-PHONE-NUM      TO MSGHDR-AGENT-PHONE-NUM 
           ELSE 
              MOVE AGENT-PHONE-NUM    TO MSGHDR-AGENT-PHONE-NUM 
           END-IF.  

       5250-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       5300-AGENCY-ERROR.   

           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB. 
           MOVE 0050       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB).  
           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.

      *=================================================================
       5400-CHK-FOR-EXISTING-BOOKING.   

           IF RSP50-O-COMPANY-CODE = ATCR-COMPANY-CODE AND  
              RSP50-O-PHONE-NUM    = ATCR-PHONE-NUM 
              FIND CURRENT AGENT-TERMS-COMPANY-REC RECORD   
                   ON ERROR GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR   
           ELSE 
              MOVE RSP50-O-COMPANY-CODE TO ATCR-COMPANY-CODE
              MOVE RSP50-O-PHONE-NUM    TO ATCR-PHONE-NUM   
              FETCH AGENT-TERMS-COMPANY-REC RECORD  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'L'                     TO WS-ACBR-RECS-IND.

           IF AGENT-MO-YR-SET SET EMPTY 
              MOVE 'E'                  TO WS-ACBR-RECS-IND 
           ELSE 
              ACCEPT DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE FROM DATE YYYYMMDD 
              MOVE TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD(1:4)
                                        TO AMYR-YEAR
              MOVE TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD(5:2)
                                        TO AMYR-MONTH   
              FETCH AGENT-MO-YR-REC VIA AGENT-MO-YR-SET 
                    USING AMYR-CCYYMM   
                    ON ERROR GO TO 5450-CHECK-AMYR-ERROR.   

           PERFORM 5600-WALK-AGENT-COMP-BKNG-RECS THRU 5650-EXIT
                   UNTIL ACBR-BOOK-DATE-NOT-EQUAL-TODAY 
                      OR ACBR-BOOKING-FOUND 
                      OR ACBR-END-OF-RECORDS.   
           GO TO 5500-EXIT. 

       5450-CHECK-AMYR-ERROR.   
           IF ERROR-NUM = '0013' OR '0007'  
               CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
               GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR
           END-IF.  
           MOVE 'E'   TO WS-ACBR-RECS-IND.  

       5500-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       5600-WALK-AGENT-COMP-BKNG-RECS.  

           IF ACBR-LAST-RECORD  
              FETCH LAST AGENT-COMP-BOOKINGS-REC
                  WITHIN AGENT-ACTIVE-BKGS-SET SET  
                 ON ERROR GO TO 5630-END-OF-RECORDS 
              MOVE 'P'      TO WS-ACBR-RECS-IND 
           ELSE 
              FETCH PRIOR AGENT-COMP-BOOKINGS-REC   
                   WITHIN AGENT-ACTIVE-BKGS-SET SET 
                 AT END GO TO 5630-END-OF-RECORDS.  

           IF ACBR-BOOK-DATE = DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE  
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'D' TO WS-ACBR-RECS-IND  
              GO TO 5650-EXIT   
           END-IF.  

           IF  ACBR-SHIP-CODE       = TDP01-SHIP-CODE          AND  
               ACBR-SAIL-DATE       = TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD AND  
               ACBR-DURATION-DAYS   = TDP01-DURATION-DAYS   
               NEXT SENTENCE
           ELSE 
               GO TO 5650-EXIT. 

           MOVE ACBR-BOOK-NUM        TO IBR-BOOK-NUM.   
           MOVE 'I'                  TO IBR-BOOK-TYPE.  
           MOVE 'Y'                  TO IRP66-FETCH-FEES-IND.   
           PERFORM IRP66-FETCH-IBR THRU IRP66-FETCH-IBR-EXIT.   

           IF IRP66-ERROR-NUM > 0   
              MOVE 2  TO DPP01-MESSAGE-SW   
              MOVE IRP66-ERROR-MESSAGE (IRP66-ERROR-NUM)
                TO DPP01-USER-MESSAGE   
                   DPP42-HOLD-MSG-DESC  
              GO TO 5650-EXIT.  

           IF IBR-AGENCY-CONN-CODE = 'IC' OR 'IA'   
              MOVE ZERO              TO BKPUCC-SEGMENT-MAX  
                                        BKPCHK-SEGMENT-MAX  
              MOVE 'B'               TO WS-ACBR-RECS-IND
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 5650-EXIT. 

       5630-END-OF-RECORDS. 

           IF ERROR-NUM = '0007' OR '0013'  
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'E' TO WS-ACBR-RECS-IND.

       5650-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

      *=================================================================
       5800-PROCESS-SPC-SERV.   
           MOVE ZERO   TO BKGSVC-SUB
                          WS-PTFR-SUB.  

           MOVE ZERO   TO WS-NUM-I-XFRS 
                          WS-NUM-O-XFRS.

147461     INSPECT RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-TABLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
147461             REPLACING FIRST 'T' BY SPACE.


       5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.  

           ADD 1 TO BKGSVC-SUB. 

           IF BKGSVC-SUB > BKGSVC-SEGMENT-MAX   
              GO TO 5800-EXIT   
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGSVC-SEGMENT (BKGSVC-SUB) NOT > SPACES  
              GO TO 5800-EXIT   
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGSVC-PAX-SEQ-NUM (BKGSVC-SUB) NOT = 
              BKGINF-PAX-SEQ-NUM (BKGINF-SUB)   
              GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP  
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
             UNTIL WS-SUB > WS-NUM-PAX  

              IF BKGINF-PAX-SEQ-NUM (BKGINF-SUB) =  
                 WS-BKGTRN-PAX-SEQ-NUM (WS-SUB) 
                 MOVE WS-SUB       TO WS-TRANS-SUB  
                 MOVE WS-NUM-PAX   TO WS-SUB
              END-IF

           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-TRANS-SUB > 0  

              IF WS-TRANS-TYPE (WS-TRANS-SUB) = 'O' 
                 IF (BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) (1:2) = 'XF'  
                                                           OR 'TF') AND 
                    BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) (3:3) = 'FFF'  
                    ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                    MOVE 0132    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                    GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) (1:2) = 'XF' OR 'TF' 
              ADD 1 TO WS-PTFR-SUB  
                       RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT OF RSP06-NEW 
                               (NEW-PAX-SUB)
               MOVE 'Y' TO  
                  RSP06-PTFR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                  RSP06-PAX-CRUO-TRANSFER-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
              IF BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) (3:3) = 'FFF' 
                 MOVE SPACES  TO BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) (3:3) 
              END-IF

              IF BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) (6:1) = 'I'   
                 IF  RSP06-PICF-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                     NOT = 'Y'  
                     AND RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                      = 'C' 
                     MOVE BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) (3:3) TO 
                     RSP06-PAX-ARRIVAL-CITY OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                 END-IF 
                 ADD 1 TO WS-NUM-I-XFRS 
              MOVE 'I'  
                TO RSP06-PTFR-PRE-POST-IND         OF RSP06-NEW 
                           (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PTFR-SUB)
              MOVE BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) (3:3)   
                TO RSP06-PTFR-LOC-CODE             OF RSP06-NEW 
                          (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PTFR-SUB) 
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-CURRENCY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW TO  
                   RSP06-PTFR-CURRENCY-CODE        OF RSP06-NEW 
                          (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PTFR-SUB) 
              ELSE  
                 IF BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) (6:1) = 'O'
                    IF  RSP06-PICF-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                        NOT = 'Y'   
                     AND RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                      = 'C' 
                        MOVE BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) (3:3) TO  
                        RSP06-PAX-DEPART-CITY OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                    END-IF  
                    ADD 1 TO WS-NUM-O-XFRS  
                     MOVE 'O'   
                       TO RSP06-PTFR-PRE-POST-IND       OF RSP06-NEW
                           (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PTFR-SUB)
                     MOVE BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) (3:3)
                       TO RSP06-PTFR-LOC-CODE           OF RSP06-NEW
                           (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PTFR-SUB)
                    MOVE RSP06-IBR-CURRENCY-CODE        OF RSP06-NEW
                      TO RSP06-PTFR-CURRENCY-CODE       OF RSP06-NEW
                          (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PTFR-SUB) 
                 ELSE   
                    ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                    MOVE 0051    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                    DISPLAY '**EXPECTED CRUO-XFR INPUT DATA ERROR!' 
                       UPON PRINTER 
                    DISPLAY '** DATA: "'
                            BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) '"'
                       UPON PRINTER 
                    GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
              GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP  
           END-IF.  

           IF MSGHDR-SYSTEM-ID = 'AMA'  
              IF BKGSVC-SERVICE-TYPE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) = 'MED' OR 'DIE' 
                                                               OR 'OCC' 
                 MOVE BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) TO WS-FOR-CODE   
                 PERFORM 5840-FETCH-FOR-RECORD  
                    THRU 5840-EXIT  
                 IF (FOR-SUB-GROUP =
                         BKGSVC-SERVICE-TYPE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB)) OR  
                    ((FOR-SUB-GROUP = 'SPA' OR 'SPB' OR 'SPC') AND  
                     (BKGSVC-SERVICE-TYPE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) = 'OCC'))   
                     CONTINUE   
                 ELSE   
                    ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                    MOVE 0068 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                    GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 'OTH' TO BKGSVC-SERVICE-TYPE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB)
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) TO WS-SPC-SVC-CODE.
           MOVE BKGSVC-SERVICE-TYPE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB)   
             TO CCP00-SERVICE-TYPE. 
           PERFORM CCP11-GET-SERVICE THRU CCP11-SERVICE-EXIT.   

           IF MSGHDR-SYSTEM-ID = 'AMA'  
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              PERFORM 5820-VALIDATE-ENTRY-FOR    THRU 5820-EXIT.

           IF CCP00-SERVICE-TYPE = '1' OR '2' OR '3'
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5800-PP-GRATS   
           END-IF.  

           INSPECT RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-TABLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                   REPLACING FIRST SPACE BY WS-COMMENT-CODE.

           IF CCP00-SERVICE-TYPE = '1'  
              IF WS-MED-CHECK = 'Y' 
                 ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                 MOVE 'N'        TO WS-MED-CHECK
                 MOVE 0085       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              END-IF
              MOVE ZERO TO WS-TALLY 
              INSPECT RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-TABLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
                   TALLYING WS-TALLY FOR ALL WS-PREG-CODE-X 
              IF  WS-TALLY > ZERO   
                  ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                  MOVE 0083 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
PRCCMB            MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF (   (BKGSVC-SERVICE-TYPE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) = 'OCC')   
               OR (WS-FOR-SUB-GROUP = 'OCC' )  )     AND
              (WS-COMMENT-CODE = 'E' OR 'F')                AND 
              (BKGSVC-SERVICE-DATE (BKGSVC-SUB) > ZEROES)   
              IF RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-CODE     OF RSP06-NEW   
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB 1) = SPACES   
                 IF RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-CODE  OF RSP06-NEW   
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB 2) = SPACES   
                    MOVE WS-COMMENT-CODE
                      TO RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-CODE OF RSP06-NEW   
                                                  (NEW-PAX-SUB 1)   
                    MOVE BKGSVC-SERVICE-DATE (BKGSVC-SUB)   
                      TO RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-DATE OF RSP06-NEW   
                                                  (NEW-PAX-SUB 1)   
                 ELSE   
                    IF WS-COMMENT-CODE =
                              RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-CODE  OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                  (NEW-PAX-SUB 2)   
                       IF BKGSVC-SERVICE-DATE (BKGSVC-SUB) =
                              RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-DATE  OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                  (NEW-PAX-SUB 2)   
                          CONTINUE  
                       ELSE 
                          MOVE BKGSVC-SERVICE-DATE(BKGSVC-SUB)  
                            TO RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-DATE OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                  (NEW-PAX-SUB 2)   
                       END-IF   
                    ELSE
                       CONTINUE 
                    END-IF  
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 IF WS-COMMENT-CODE =   
                              RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-CODE  OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                  (NEW-PAX-SUB 1)   
                    IF BKGSVC-SERVICE-DATE (BKGSVC-SUB) =   
                              RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-DATE  OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                  (NEW-PAX-SUB 1)   
                       CONTINUE 
                    ELSE
                       MOVE BKGSVC-SERVICE-DATE (BKGSVC-SUB)
                         TO RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-DATE    OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                  (NEW-PAX-SUB 1)   
                    END-IF  
                 ELSE   
                    IF RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-CODE    OF RSP06-NEW  
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB 2) = SPACES   
                       MOVE WS-COMMENT-CODE 
                         TO RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-CODE    OF RSP06-NEW 
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB 2)
                       MOVE BKGSVC-SERVICE-DATE (BKGSVC-SUB)
                         TO RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-DATE    OF RSP06-NEW 
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB 2)
                    ELSE
                        IF WS-COMMENT-CODE =
                              RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-CODE  OF RSP06-NEW 
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB 2)
                           IF BKGSVC-SERVICE-DATE (BKGSVC-SUB) =
                              RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-DATE  OF RSP06-NEW 
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB 2)
                              CONTINUE  
                           ELSE 
                              MOVE BKGSVC-SERVICE-DATE (BKGSVC-SUB) TO  
                               RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-DATE OF RSP06-NEW 
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB 2)
                           END-IF   
                        ELSE
                           CONTINUE 
                        END-IF  
                    END-IF  
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  
           GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.

       5800-PP-GRATS.   

           IF WS-SPC-SVC-CODE = 'GRATS' 
              MOVE 'Y'  
                TO RSP06-PAX-PP-GRATS-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
              ADD 1 TO WS-PP-GRATS-CNT  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5800-CRIBS  
           END-IF.  
           GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.

       5800-CRIBS.  

           IF WS-SPC-SVC-CODE = 'CRIB'  
             IF WS-NUM-CRIB-REQUESTS > 0
               MOVE WS-NUM-CRIB-REQUESTS TO 
                    RSP06-IBR-NUM-CRIBS OF RSP06-NEW
             ELSE   
              MOVE 1 TO RSP06-IBR-NUM-CRIBS            OF RSP06-NEW 
             END-IF 
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5800-ROLLAWAY   
           END-IF.  
           GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.

       5800-ROLLAWAY.   

           IF WS-SPC-SVC-CODE = 'RWAY'  
             COMPUTE RSP06-IBR-NUM-ROLLAWAYS OF RSP06-NEW = 
                RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW - (DPP34-NORMAL-CAPACITY 
                   + RSP06-IBR-NUM-CRIBS OF RSP06-NEW)  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5800-STD-INS
           END-IF.  
           GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.

       5800-STD-INS.

           IF WS-SPC-SVC-CODE = 'STD'   
              MOVE 1 TO WS-STD-INS-CNT  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5800-GOLD-INS   
           END-IF.  
           GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.

       5800-GOLD-INS.   

           IF WS-SPC-SVC-CODE = 'GOLD'  
              MOVE 1 TO WS-GOLD-INS-CNT 
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5800-GROUP-LEADER   
           END-IF.  
           GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.

       5800-GROUP-LEADER.   

           IF WS-SPC-SVC-CODE = 'GLDR'  
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5800-ONITE-HOTEL
           END-IF.  
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-GLDR-FLAG.

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB4 FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL WS-SUB4 > 5
               IF RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE OF 
                  RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-SUB4) = 'T' 
                      MOVE 6 TO WS-SUB4 
                      MOVE 'Y' TO WS-GLDR-FLAG  
               END-IF   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-GLDR-FLAG NOT = 'Y'
             INSPECT RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-TABLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                   REPLACING FIRST SPACE BY 'T' 
           END-IF.  
           GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.

       5800-ONITE-HOTEL.

           IF WS-SPC-SVC-CODE = 'OVNHTL'
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
               GO TO 5800-AUTOPAY   
           END-IF.  
           INSPECT RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-TABLE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
                   REPLACING FIRST SPACE BY 'O'.
           GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.

       5800-AUTOPAY.

           IF  WS-SPC-SVC-CODE (1:2) = 'FP' 
               CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
               GO TO 5800-CONFIRMATION  
           END-IF.  

           IF  NEW-PAX-SUB = 1  
               CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
               GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP 
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-BKPUCC-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL  
                            (WS-BKPUCC-SUB > BKPUCC-SEGMENT-MAX) OR 
                 (BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (WS-BKPUCC-SUB) = 'Y' OR 'N') 
              CONTINUE  
           END-PERFORM. 
           IF  WS-BKPUCC-SUB > BKPUCC-SEGMENT-MAX   
               IF  IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE = 'A' or 'R' or 'P' 
                   GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP 
               ELSE 
                   MOVE ZERO TO WS-BKPUCC-COUNT WS-BKPUCC-CC-SUBSCRIPT  
                   PERFORM VARYING WS-BKPUCC-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL  
                            (WS-BKPUCC-SUB > BKPUCC-SEGMENT-MAX)
                    IF (BKPUCC-CARD-NUM    (WS-BKPUCC-SUB) > SPACES OR  
                         BKPUCC-TOKEN-NUM (WS-BKPUCC-SUB) > SPACES) AND 
                         BKPUCC-CARD-EXP-MONTH (WS-BKPUCC-SUB) > ZERO   
                         AND BKPUCC-CARD-EXP-YEAR (WS-BKPUCC-SUB) > ZERO
                         ADD 1 TO WS-BKPUCC-COUNT   
                         MOVE WS-BKPUCC-SUB TO WS-BKPUCC-CC-SUBSCRIPT   
                     END-IF 
                   END-PERFORM  
                   IF  WS-BKPUCC-COUNT = 1  
                       MOVE WS-BKPUCC-CC-SUBSCRIPT TO WS-BKPUCC-SUB 
                       MOVE 'Y' TO  
                                 BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (WS-BKPUCC-SUB)
                   ELSE 
                       IF  WS-BKPUCC-COUNT = 0  
                           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                           MOVE 0129    TO  
                                       MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
                       ELSE 
                           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                           MOVE 0130    TO  
                                       MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
                       END-IF   
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
           ELSE 
               CONTINUE 
           END-IF.  
           IF WS-BKPUCC-SUB > 0 

              IF  BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (WS-BKPUCC-SUB) = 'Y' 
                  PERFORM 5800-SCHEDULE-AUTOPAY THRU
                             5800-SCHEDULE-AUTOPAY-EXIT 
              ELSE  
                  IF  BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'N'
                      PERFORM 5800-CANCEL-AUTOPAY THRU  
                                        5800-CANCEL-AUTOPAY-EXIT
                  END-IF
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.

       5800-SCHEDULE-AUTOPAY.   
           EXEC SQL 
                SELECT SCHEDULED_PAYMENT_IND
                  INTO WS-SCHEDULED-PAYMENT-IND 
                  FROM COMPANY  
                 WHERE COMPANY_CODE = :TDP01-COMPANY-CODE   
                 FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
           END-EXEC.

           IF  BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (WS-BKPUCC-SUB) = 'Y'
               IF  WS-SCHEDULED-PAYMENT-IND NOT = 'Y'   
                   ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                   MOVE 0123    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                   GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
               ELSE 
                   IF  IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE = 'A' OR 'R' OR 'P' 
                       ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                       MOVE 0124    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                       GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
                   ELSE 
                       IF  ( IBR-FINAL-DATE < DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE )   
                           OR ( IBR-BOOK-STATUS = 'P' ) 
                           OR ( IBR-FINAL-DATE = DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE AND  
                               ( DPP02-SYSTEM-TIME > 16000000) )
                           ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                           MOVE 0125    TO  
                                       MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
                       ELSE 
                           PERFORM 5800-CHK-FOR-FULL-DEPOSIT
                                   THRU 5800-CHK-FOR-FULL-DEPOSIT-EXIT  
                           MOVE SPACES TO WS-AUTOPAY-HIST-CONTACT   
                           IF MSGHDR-USER-NAME > SPACES 
                              MOVE MSGHDR-USER-NAME 
                                        TO WS-AUTOPAY-HIST-CONTACT  
                           ELSE 
                               MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID  
                                        TO WS-AUTOPAY-HIST-CONTACT  
                           END-IF   
                           IF  IBR-BOOK-TYPE = 'I'  
                               MOVE 'APR'   
                                        TO DPP04-HIST-ACTIVITY-TYPE 
                               MOVE SPACES  
                                        TO DPP04-TRF-COMMENT1   
                                           DPP04-TRF-COMMENT2   
                               MOVE 'Auto Payment Request'  
                                        TO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT   
                                           DPP04-TRF-COMMENT1   
                               MOVE INFO-USERID 
                                        TO DPP04-HIST-OPER-INITIAL  
                               MOVE WS-AUTOPAY-HIST-CONTACT 
                                        TO DPP04-HIST-CONTACT   
                               MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW
                                        TO DPP04-TRF-SAIL-DATE  
                               MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW
                                        TO DPP04-TRF-SHIP-CODE  
                               MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW
                                        TO DPP04-TRF-DURATION-DAYS  
                               MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE   
                                        TO DPP04-TRF-LOCATION-CODE  
                               MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE
                                        TO DPP04-HIST-AGENCY-CONN-CODE  
                               PERFORM IRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY 
                                  THRU IRP15-TRF-EXIT   
                                IF  (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1   
                                     AND SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1)  
                                     MOVE 'SYS' 
                                        TO DPP04-HIST-OPER-INITIAL  
                                     MOVE 'SPR' 
                                        TO DPP04-HIST-ACTIVITY-TYPE 
                                     MOVE SPACES
                                        TO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT   
                                           DPP04-TRF-COMMENT1   
                                           DPP04-TRF-COMMENT2   
                                     MOVE 'Schedule AutoPmt Request'
                                        TO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT,  
                                           DPP04-TRF-COMMENT1   
                                     MOVE IBR-CABIN-NUM 
                                        TO DPP04-HIST-CABIN-NUM 
                                     MOVE IBR-AGENT-CONTACT 
                                        TO DPP04-HIST-CONTACT   
                                     MOVE IBR-SAIL-DATE 
                                        TO DPP04-TRF-SAIL-DATE  
                                     MOVE IBR-SHIP-CODE 
                                        TO DPP04-TRF-SHIP-CODE  
                                     MOVE IBR-DURATION-DAYS 
                                        TO DPP04-TRF-DURATION-DAYS  
                                     MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE 
                                        TO DPP04-TRF-LOCATION-CODE  
                                     MOVE 'IR'  
                                        TO DPP04-HIST-AGENCY-CONN-CODE  
                                     MOVE ZEROES
                                        TO DPP04-TRF-PAX-SEQ-NUM
                                   PERFORM IRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY 
                                     THRU IRP15-TRF-EXIT
                                END-IF  
                           ELSE 
                               INITIALIZE GRP15-TRF-COMMENTS
                               MOVE SPACES  
                                        TO GRP15-HISTORY-INFORMATION
                               MOVE 'G' TO GRP15-TYPE   
                               MOVE 'APR'   
                                        TO GRP15-ACTIVITY-CODE  
                               MOVE 'Auto Payment Request'  
                                        TO GRP15-COMMENT
                                           GRP15-TRF-COMMENT1   
                               ADD 1    TO GRP15-ITEM-NUM   
                               MOVE INFO-USERID 
                                        TO GRP15-USERID 
                               MOVE WS-AUTOPAY-HIST-CONTACT 
                                        TO GRP15-AGENCY-CONTACT 
                               MOVE PYDR-PAYMENT-AMT
                                        TO GRP15-AMT
                               MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                                        TO GRP15-IBR-BOOK-NUM   
                               MOVE GBR1-BOOK-NUM   
                                       TO GRP15-BOOK-NUM
                               MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                                       TO GRP15-CABIN-NUM   
                               MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE
                                       TO GRP15-AGENCY-CONNECT-CODE 
                               MOVE RSP06-IBR-SYSTEM-ID OF RSP06-NEW
                                       TO GRP15-AGENCY-SYSTEM-ID
                               MOVE RSP06-IBR-AGENCY-CONN-ID
                                       OF RSP06-NEW 
                                       TO GRP15-AGENCY-CONNECT-ID   
                               MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE   
                                       TO GRP15-LOCATION-CODE   
                               IF GRP15-USERID = '@IA' OR '@UA' 
                                  MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID   
                                       TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID
                               ELSE 
                                  MOVE SPACES   
                                       TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID
                               END-IF   
                               PERFORM GRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY 
                                  THRU GRP15-GTRF-EXIT  
                           END-IF   
                       END-IF   
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  
           GO TO 5800-SCHEDULE-AUTOPAY-EXIT.

       5800-CHK-FOR-FULL-DEPOSIT.   
           MOVE ZERO TO WS-BKG-PYMT-TOT 
           PERFORM VARYING WS-BKPUCC-SUB2 FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL 
                            WS-BKPUCC-SUB2 > BKPUCC-SEGMENT-MAX 
              IF  BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (WS-BKPUCC-SUB2) > 0   
                  ADD BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (WS-BKPUCC-SUB2)   
                                                 TO WS-BKG-PYMT-TOT 
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 
           MOVE IBR-BOOK-NUM       TO PHR-PRODUCT-NUM   
                                      ARP90-BOOK-NUM.   
           PERFORM ARP90-LOAD-CALC-AREA-NAME THRU   
                   ARP90-LOAD-CALC-AREA-NAME-EXIT.  
           FIND PAYMENT-HEADER-REC RECORD   
               ON ERROR GO TO 5800-CHK-DEPOSIT. 
       5800-FETCH-NEXT-PYDR.
           FETCH NEXT PAYMENT-REC WITHIN PMT-SET SET
               AT END GO TO 5800-CHK-DEPOSIT
               ON ERROR GO TO 5800-CHK-DEPOSIT. 
           IF  (PYDR-AUTH-BOOK-IND = 'B')                 AND   
               (PYDR-CARD-APPROVAL-DENIAL-CODE NOT = 'D') AND   
               (PYDR-PRODUCT-TYPE = 'I' OR 'G') 
               ADD PYDR-PAYMENT-AMT TO WS-BKG-PYMT-TOT  
           END-IF.  
           GO TO 5800-FETCH-NEXT-PYDR.  
       5800-CHK-DEPOSIT.
           IF  ERROR-STATUS = '000307' OR '000313'  
               CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
               GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR
           END-IF.  

           IF  RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW > WS-BKG-PYMT-TOT 
               MOVE 0126    TO WS-AUTOPAY-MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE
           ELSE 
               MOVE 0128    TO WS-AUTOPAY-MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE
           END-IF.  
       5800-CHK-FOR-FULL-DEPOSIT-EXIT.  
           EXIT.
       5800-SCHEDULE-AUTOPAY-EXIT.  
           EXIT.

       5800-CANCEL-AUTOPAY. 
           MOVE 0       TO WS-AP-CANCEL-REST.   
           MOVE SPACE TO WS-AUTOPAY-CANCELLED-FLAG. 
           MOVE IBR-FINAL-DATE TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE.  
           MOVE 1              TO DPP02-NUMBER-DAYS.
           PERFORM DPP10-DEMINISH-YYYYMMDD-DATE.
           IF  DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE > DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE  
               MOVE 1       TO WS-AP-CANCEL-REST
           END-IF.  
           IF  DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE = DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE  AND 
                   DPP02-SYSTEM-TIME > 16000000 
               MOVE 1       TO WS-AP-CANCEL-REST
           END-IF.  
           IF  WS-AP-CANCEL-REST = 1
               ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
               MOVE 0127    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
               GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE IBR-BOOK-NUM       TO PHR-PRODUCT-NUM   
                                    ARP90-BOOK-NUM. 
           PERFORM ARP90-LOAD-CALC-AREA-NAME THRU   
                   ARP90-LOAD-CALC-AREA-NAME-EXIT.  
           FIND PAYMENT-HEADER-REC RECORD   
               ON ERROR GO TO 5800-ERR-CHK. 
           MOVE 'A'                TO PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE.
           FETCH PAYMENT-REC VIA PMT-SET
                 USING PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE
                 ON ERROR GO TO 5800-ERR-CHK.   
           MOVE 'C'                TO PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE.
           MOVE 'Y'                TO WS-AUTOPAY-CANCELLED-FLAG.

           MODIFY PAYMENT-REC RECORD.   
           GO TO 5800-CANCEL-AUTOPAY-IBR.   
       5800-ERR-CHK.
           IF  ERROR-STATUS = '000307' OR '000313'  
               CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
               GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR
           END-IF.  
       5800-CANCEL-AUTOPAY-IBR. 
           IF  IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE = 'A' OR 'R' OR 
                                                   WS-AUTOPAY-CANCELLED 
               MOVE 'Y' TO WS-AUTOPAY-CANCELLED-FLAG
               MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW
                                        TO TDP01-I-SHIP-CODE
               MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW
                                        TO TDP01-I-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD   
               MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW
                                        TO TDP01-I-DUR-DAYS 

               PERFORM TDP01-RET-SAIL-DETAILS THRU  
                       TDP01-RET-SAIL-DETAILS-EXIT  

               IF TDP01-SAIL-SQLERR 
                  GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
               END-IF   

               IF TDP01-SCAT-SAIL-ID = TDP01-SAIL-ID AND
                  TDP01-SAIL-CAT-CNT > 1
                  CONTINUE  
               ELSE 
                  PERFORM TDP01-RET-ALL-SCAT THRU   
                          TDP01-RET-ALL-SCAT-EXIT   
                  IF TDP01-SCAT-SQLERR  
                     GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
                  END-IF
               END-IF   

               MOVE 'C' TO IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE 
                           RSP06-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE OF RSP06-NEW  
               PERFORM DPP16-MODIFY-SAIL-INDIVIDUAL 
                       THRU DPP16-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           IF  WS-AUTOPAY-CANCELLED 
               MOVE SPACES TO WS-AUTOPAY-HIST-CONTACT   
               IF MSGHDR-USER-NAME > SPACES 
                  MOVE MSGHDR-USER-NAME     TO WS-AUTOPAY-HIST-CONTACT  
               ELSE 
                   MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID      TO WS-AUTOPAY-HIST-CONTACT  
               END-IF   
               MOVE 0131                    TO  
                                         WS-AUTOPAY-MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE  
               IF  IBR-BOOK-TYPE = 'I'  
                   MOVE 'CSP'               TO DPP04-HIST-ACTIVITY-TYPE 
                   MOVE SPACES              TO DPP04-TRF-COMMENT1   
                                               DPP04-TRF-COMMENT2   
                   MOVE 'Cancel Scheduled Autopay'  
                                            TO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT   
                                               DPP04-TRF-COMMENT1   
                   MOVE INFO-USERID         TO DPP04-HIST-OPER-INITIAL  
                   MOVE WS-AUTOPAY-HIST-CONTACT 
                                            TO DPP04-HIST-CONTACT   
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW
                                            TO DPP04-TRF-SAIL-DATE  
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW
                                            TO DPP04-TRF-SHIP-CODE  
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW
                                            TO DPP04-TRF-DURATION-DAYS  
                   MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE   
                                            TO DPP04-TRF-LOCATION-CODE  
                   MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE
                                            TO  
                                           DPP04-HIST-AGENCY-CONN-CODE  
                   PERFORM IRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY 
                      THRU IRP15-TRF-EXIT   
               ELSE 
                   INITIALIZE GRP15-TRF-COMMENTS
                   MOVE SPACES              TO GRP15-HISTORY-INFORMATION
                   MOVE 'G'                 TO GRP15-TYPE   
                   MOVE 'CSP'               TO GRP15-ACTIVITY-CODE  
                   MOVE 'Cancel Scheduled Autopay'  
                                            TO GRP15-COMMENT
                                               GRP15-TRF-COMMENT1   
                   ADD 1                    TO GRP15-ITEM-NUM   
                   MOVE INFO-USERID         TO GRP15-USERID 
                   MOVE WS-AUTOPAY-HIST-CONTACT 
                                            TO GRP15-AGENCY-CONTACT 
                   MOVE PYDR-PAYMENT-AMT    TO GRP15-AMT
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                                            TO GRP15-IBR-BOOK-NUM   
                   MOVE GBR1-BOOK-NUM       TO GRP15-BOOK-NUM   
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                                            TO GRP15-CABIN-NUM  
                   MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE
                                            TO GRP15-AGENCY-CONNECT-CODE
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-SYSTEM-ID OF RSP06-NEW
                                            TO GRP15-AGENCY-SYSTEM-ID   
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-AGENCY-CONN-ID OF RSP06-NEW   
                                            TO GRP15-AGENCY-CONNECT-ID  
                   MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE   
                                            TO GRP15-LOCATION-CODE  
                   IF GRP15-USERID = '@IA' OR '@UA' OR '@AU'
                      MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID   TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID   
                   ELSE 
                      MOVE SPACES           TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID   
                   END-IF   
                   PERFORM GRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY 
                      THRU GRP15-GTRF-EXIT  
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

       5800-CANCEL-AUTOPAY-EXIT.
           EXIT.

       5800-CONFIRMATION.   
           IF WS-CONF-CODE = 'MCNF' 
              GO TO 5800-MAIL   
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-CONF-CODE = 'FCNF' OR 'ECNF'   
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5800-GROUP-STMT-REQ.

           IF IBR-CUSTOMER-NUM = SPACES AND 
              IBR-ALT-CUSTOMER-NUM = SPACES 
              GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP  
           END-IF.  
           MOVE 'Y'             TO WS-CONF-REQ. 
           MOVE WS-COMMENT-CODE TO WS-CONF-IND. 
           IF RSP49-O-CONF-ALLOW-IND NOT = 'Y'  
              ADD 1      TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
              MOVE 0109  TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  
           IF BKGSVC-EREF-FAX-NUM (BKGSVC-SUB) > SPACES 
              MOVE BKGSVC-EREF-FAX-NUM (BKGSVC-SUB) TO  
                   IRP03-EMAIL-OR-FAX-NUM   
           ELSE 
              IF WS-CONF-CODE = 'ECNF'  
              ADD 1      TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
              MOVE 0110  TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  
           IF ((SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND
                SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 0) OR
               (WS-CONF-TYPE-IND = 'P'  )    )  
              MOVE WS-CONF-TYPE-IND TO IRP03-FAX-TYPE   
           ELSE 
             IF ( (WS-CONF-CODE = 'ECNF') OR
                  ((BKGSVC-EREF-FAX-NUM (BKGSVC-SUB) (1:10) NOT NUMERIC)
               AND (BKGSVC-EREF-FAX-NUM (BKGSVC-SUB) > SPACES))      )  
              MOVE BKGSVC-EREF-FAX-NUM (BKGSVC-SUB) TO  
                   AER-EMAIL-ADDRESS
              FETCH AGENT-EMAIL-REC RECORD  
                ON ERROR GO TO 5800-AER-ERROR   
               IF AER-CUSTOMER-NUM = IBR-ALT-CUSTOMER-NUM OR
                                     IBR-CUSTOMER-NUM   
                 MOVE WS-CONF-TYPE-IND TO IRP03-FAX-TYPE
               ELSE 
                 MOVE 'P' TO IRP03-FAX-TYPE 
               END-IF   
             ELSE   
                IF WS-CONF-CODE = 'FCNF'
                   MOVE WS-CONF-TYPE-IND TO IRP03-FAX-TYPE  
                ELSE
                   MOVE 'P' TO IRP03-FAX-TYPE   
                END-IF  
             END-IF 
           END-IF.  
           ADD 1 TO WS-EMAIL-CNT.   
           MOVE WS-CONF-IND TO WS-EML-CONF-IND (WS-EMAIL-CNT).  
           MOVE IRP03-FAX-TYPE TO WS-EML-FAX-TYPE (WS-EMAIL-CNT).   
           MOVE IRP03-EMAIL-OR-FAX-NUM TO WS-EML-ADDR (WS-EMAIL-CNT).   
           GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.

       5800-MAIL.   
           IF WS-CONF-CODE = 'MCNF' 
              MOVE 'Y'             TO WS-CONF-REQ   
              MOVE WS-COMMENT-CODE TO WS-CONF-IND   
           END-IF.  
           GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.

       5800-AER-ERROR.  
           ADD 1 TO WS-EMAIL-CNT.   
           MOVE 'P' TO IRP03-FAX-TYPE  WS-EML-FAX-TYPE (WS-EMAIL-CNT).  
           MOVE WS-CONF-IND TO WS-EML-CONF-IND (WS-EMAIL-CNT).  
           MOVE IRP03-EMAIL-OR-FAX-NUM TO WS-EML-ADDR (WS-EMAIL-CNT).   
           GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.

       5800-GROUP-STMT-REQ. 
           IF WS-SPC-SVC-CODE = 'GSGR'  
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5800-GROUP-GDW-REQ. 

           IF CCP00-GROUP-BOOK-NUM > SPACES AND 
               CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM > SPACES AND 
                RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'G'  
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP. 

           IF SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1  
              GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP. 

           IF ( RSP49-O-COMMUN-ENTRY-CNT = 0       ) AND
              ( BKCRV1-GRP-RPTS-SEC-EMAIL = SPACES ) AND
              ( BKCRV1-GRP-RPTS-SEC-FAX = ZEROS    )
              GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP. 

           MOVE ALL ',' TO DPP01-MSG-BODY.  
           MOVE CCP00-GROUP-BOOK-NUM TO MSMQ-GRP-BKG-NUM.   

           MOVE 'CNF' TO MSMQ-REPORT-IND.   
           STRING 'EDIST' MSGHDR-SYSTEM-ID RSP50-O-CUSTOMER-CODE
              MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID (1:10)  'CCL'
                DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO MSMQ-AGT-CONN.   
           MOVE SPACES TO WS-EMAIL-ADD. 

           IF INFO-PROG-APP = 'CC' OR '72' OR 'WO'  
              MOVE 'CCL' TO CSF-FILE-QUAL   
           ELSE 
              IF INFO-PROG-APP = '70' OR 'XO'   
                 MOVE 'UAT' TO CSF-FILE-QUAL
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 'DEV' TO CSF-FILE-QUAL
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE CSF-FILE-NAME-IN TO CSFA-ASG-FILE-NAME-IN   
                                 CSFA-USE-FILE-NAME-IN  
                                 CSFA-FREE-FILE-NAME-IN.

           CALL 'ERACSF' USING CSF-ASGA-IMAGE-IN CSF-STATUS.

           IF CSF-STATUS NOT EQUAL 0
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0120       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           CALL 'ERACSF' USING CSF-USE-IMAGE-IN CSF-STATUS. 

           IF CSF-STATUS NOT EQUAL 0
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0120       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           OPEN INPUT INPUT-FILE.   
           READ INPUT-FILE INTO WS-MSMQ-PARAM-REC AT END
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0120       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 
           CLOSE INPUT-FILE.

           CALL 'ERACSF' USING CSF-FREE-IMAGE-IN CSF-STATUS.

           IF CSF-STATUS NOT EQUAL 0
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0120       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           MOVE LOW-VALUES              TO DPP01-CONNECT-CLEAR. 
           MOVE 1800                    TO DPP01-TIMEOUT.   
           MOVE 'CARNIVAL'              TO DPP01-DOMAIN.
           MOVE 'MSMQGW_JJM'            TO DPP01-USERNAME.  
           MOVE 'Unisys_MQ'             TO DPP01-PASSWORD.  
           MOVE  1                      TO DPP01-ALTERNATE-ROUTING. 

           PERFORM DPP12-CONNECT-MSMQ THRU DPP12-CONNECT-MSMQ-EXIT. 

           MOVE LOW-VALUES       TO DPP01-QUEUE-CLEAR.  
           MOVE PONGQ-NAME       TO DPP01-QUEUE-NAME.   

           PERFORM DPP12-OPEN-QUEUE THRU DPP12-OPEN-QUEUE-EXIT. 

           MOVE WS-MSMQ-OUT-REC-LNG TO DPP01-MSG-LENGTH.

           PERFORM 7600-SEND-MESSAGES THRU  
                   7600-SEND-MESSAGES-EXIT. 

           PERFORM DPP12-CLOSE-QUEUE THRU DPP12-CLOSE-QUEUE-EXIT.   

           PERFORM DPP12-DISCON-MSMQ THRU DPP12-DISCON-MSMQ-EXIT.   

           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB. 
           MOVE 0117       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB).
           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB).  

           MOVE SPACES TO GRP15-HISTORY-INFORMATION.
           MOVE 'RSGR' TO GRP15-ACTIVITY-CODE.  
           MOVE INFO-USERID TO GRP15-USERID.
           MOVE ZEROS TO GRP15-AMT. 
           ADD 1 TO GRP15-ITEM-NUM. 
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO GRP15-IBR-BOOK-NUM.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO GRP15-CABIN-NUM.
           MOVE 'STMT GROUP REQUEST SENT' TO GRP15-COMMENT  
                                             GRP15-TRF-COMMENT2.
           MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE         TO GRP15-LOCATION-CODE.   
           MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE   TO GRP15-AGENCY-CONNECT-CODE.

           IF GRP15-USERID = '@IA' OR '@UA' OR '@AU'
              MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID          TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES                  TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM GRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY THRU GRP15-GTRF-EXIT.   

           GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.

       5800-GROUP-GDW-REQ.  
           IF WS-SPC-SVC-CODE = 'GGDW'  
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5800-GROUP-ALPHA-LIST-REQ.  

           IF CCP00-GROUP-BOOK-NUM > SPACES AND 
               CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM > SPACES AND 
                RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'G'  
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP. 

           IF SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1  
              GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP. 

           IF ( RSP49-O-COMMUN-ENTRY-CNT = 0       ) AND
              ( BKCRV1-GRP-RPTS-SEC-EMAIL = SPACES ) AND
              ( BKCRV1-GRP-RPTS-SEC-FAX = ZEROS    )
              GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP. 

           MOVE ALL ',' TO DPP01-MSG-BODY.  
           MOVE CCP00-GROUP-BOOK-NUM TO MSMQ-GRP-BKG-NUM.   

           MOVE 'GDW' TO MSMQ-REPORT-IND.   

           STRING 'EDIST' MSGHDR-SYSTEM-ID RSP50-O-CUSTOMER-CODE
              MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID (1:10)  'CCL'
                DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO MSMQ-AGT-CONN.   

           MOVE SPACES TO WS-EMAIL-ADD. 

           IF INFO-PROG-APP = 'CC' OR '72' OR 'WO'  
              MOVE 'CCL' TO CSF-FILE-QUAL   
           ELSE 
              IF INFO-PROG-APP = '70' OR 'XO'   
                 MOVE 'UAT' TO CSF-FILE-QUAL
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 'DEV' TO CSF-FILE-QUAL
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE CSF-FILE-NAME-IN TO CSFA-ASG-FILE-NAME-IN   
                                 CSFA-USE-FILE-NAME-IN  
                                 CSFA-FREE-FILE-NAME-IN.

           CALL 'ERACSF' USING CSF-ASGA-IMAGE-IN CSF-STATUS.

           IF CSF-STATUS NOT EQUAL 0
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0120       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           CALL 'ERACSF' USING CSF-USE-IMAGE-IN CSF-STATUS. 

           IF CSF-STATUS NOT EQUAL 0
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0120       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           OPEN INPUT INPUT-FILE.   
           READ INPUT-FILE INTO WS-MSMQ-PARAM-REC AT END
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0120       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 
           CLOSE INPUT-FILE.

           CALL 'ERACSF' USING CSF-FREE-IMAGE-IN CSF-STATUS.

           IF CSF-STATUS NOT EQUAL 0
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0120       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           MOVE LOW-VALUES              TO DPP01-CONNECT-CLEAR. 
           MOVE 1800                    TO DPP01-TIMEOUT.   
           MOVE 'CARNIVAL'              TO DPP01-DOMAIN.
           MOVE 'MSMQGW_JJM'            TO DPP01-USERNAME.  
           MOVE 'Unisys_MQ'             TO DPP01-PASSWORD.  
           MOVE  1                      TO DPP01-ALTERNATE-ROUTING. 

           PERFORM DPP12-CONNECT-MSMQ THRU DPP12-CONNECT-MSMQ-EXIT. 

           MOVE LOW-VALUES       TO DPP01-QUEUE-CLEAR.  
           MOVE PONGQ-NAME       TO DPP01-QUEUE-NAME.   

           PERFORM DPP12-OPEN-QUEUE THRU DPP12-OPEN-QUEUE-EXIT. 

           MOVE WS-MSMQ-OUT-REC-LNG TO DPP01-MSG-LENGTH.

           PERFORM 7600-SEND-MESSAGES THRU  
                   7600-SEND-MESSAGES-EXIT. 

           PERFORM DPP12-CLOSE-QUEUE THRU DPP12-CLOSE-QUEUE-EXIT.   

           PERFORM DPP12-DISCON-MSMQ THRU DPP12-DISCON-MSMQ-EXIT.   

           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB. 
           MOVE 0117       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB).
           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB).  

           MOVE SPACES TO GRP15-HISTORY-INFORMATION.
           MOVE 'RGDW' TO GRP15-ACTIVITY-CODE.  
           MOVE INFO-USERID TO GRP15-USERID.
           MOVE ZEROS TO GRP15-AMT. 
           ADD 1 TO GRP15-ITEM-NUM. 
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO GRP15-IBR-BOOK-NUM.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO GRP15-CABIN-NUM.
           MOVE 'GROUP DETAIL WORKUP REQUEST SENT' TO GRP15-COMMENT 
                                                  GRP15-TRF-COMMENT2.   
           MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE           TO GRP15-LOCATION-CODE. 
           MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE        TO  
                                              GRP15-AGENCY-CONNECT-CODE.
           IF GRP15-USERID = '@IA' OR '@UA' OR '@AU'
              MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID               TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID   
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES                       TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID   
           END-IF.  
           PERFORM GRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY THRU GRP15-GTRF-EXIT.   

           GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.

       5800-GROUP-ALPHA-LIST-REQ.   
           IF WS-SPC-SVC-CODE = 'GAPH'  
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP. 

           IF CCP00-GROUP-BOOK-NUM > SPACES AND 
               CCP00-INDV-BOOK-NUM > SPACES AND 
                RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'G'  
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP. 

           IF SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1  
              GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP. 

           IF ( RSP49-O-COMMUN-ENTRY-CNT = 0       ) AND
              ( BKCRV1-GRP-RPTS-SEC-EMAIL = SPACES ) AND
              ( BKCRV1-GRP-RPTS-SEC-FAX = ZEROS    )
              GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP. 

           MOVE ALL ',' TO WS-OUTPUT-REC.   
           MOVE BKCRV1-SHIP-CODE     TO O-SHIP-CODE.
           MOVE RSP51-O-SAIL-DATE    TO O-SAIL-DATE.
           MOVE BKCRV1-DURATION-DAYS TO O-DURATION-DAYS.
           MOVE CCP00-GROUP-BOOK-NUM TO O-GRP-BKG-NUM.  

           MOVE CSF-FILE-NAME TO CSFA-ASG-FILE-NAME 
                                 CSFA-USE-FILE-NAME 
                                 CSFA-FREE-FILE-NAME.   

           CALL 'ERACSF' USING CSF-ASGA-IMAGE CSF-STATUS.   

           IF CSF-STATUS NOT EQUAL 0
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0119       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           CALL 'ERACSF' USING CSF-USE-IMAGE CSF-STATUS.

           IF CSF-STATUS NOT EQUAL 0
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0119       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           OPEN EXTEND OUTPUT-FILE. 

           IF  BKCRV1-GRP-RPTS-SEC-EMAIL > SPACES OR
               BKCRV1-GRP-RPTS-SEC-FAX   > ZEROS
               MOVE BKCRV1-GRP-RPTS-SEC-EMAIL   
                           TO O-EMAIL-ADD   
               MOVE BKCRV1-GRP-RPTS-SEC-FAX 
                           TO O-FAX-NUM 
               IF  O-EMAIL-ADD > SPACES 
                   MOVE 'E' 
                           TO O-DELIVERY-IND
               ELSE 
                   MOVE 'F' 
                           TO O-DELIVERY-IND
               END-IF   
               WRITE OUTPUT-REC FROM WS-OUTPUT-REC  
           ELSE 
               PERFORM VARYING WS-EMAIL-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                   UNTIL WS-EMAIL-SUB > RSP49-O-COMMUN-ENTRY-CNT
                   MOVE RSP49-O-C-EMAIL (WS-EMAIL-SUB)  
                           TO O-EMAIL-ADD   
                   MOVE RSP49-O-C-FAX-NUM (WS-EMAIL-SUB)
                           TO O-FAX-NUM 
                   IF  O-EMAIL-ADD > SPACES 
                       MOVE 'E' 
                           TO O-DELIVERY-IND
                   ELSE 
                       MOVE 'F' 
                           TO O-DELIVERY-IND
                   END-IF   
                   WRITE OUTPUT-REC FROM WS-OUTPUT-REC  
               END-PERFORM  
           END-IF.  

           CLOSE OUTPUT-FILE.   

           CALL 'ERACSF' USING CSF-FREE-IMAGE CSF-STATUS.   

           IF CSF-STATUS NOT EQUAL 0
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0119       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB. 
           MOVE 0117       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB).
           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB).  

           MOVE SPACES TO GRP15-HISTORY-INFORMATION.
           MOVE 'RAPH' TO GRP15-ACTIVITY-CODE.  
           MOVE INFO-USERID TO GRP15-USERID.
           MOVE ZEROS TO GRP15-AMT. 
           ADD 1 TO GRP15-ITEM-NUM. 
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO GRP15-IBR-BOOK-NUM.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO GRP15-CABIN-NUM.
           MOVE 'GROUP ALPHA LIST REQUEST SENT' TO GRP15-COMMENT
                                                  GRP15-TRF-COMMENT2.   
           MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE            TO GRP15-LOCATION-CODE.
           MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE       TO   
                                              GRP15-AGENCY-CONNECT-CODE.
           IF GRP15-USERID = '@IA' OR '@UA' OR '@AU'
              MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID              TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES                      TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID
           END-IF.  
           PERFORM GRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY THRU GRP15-GTRF-EXIT.   

           GO TO 5800-SPC-SERV-LOOP.
       5800-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

      *=================================================================
       5820-VALIDATE-ENTRY-FOR. 

           MOVE SPACES              TO WS-FOR-SUB-GROUP.
           IF CCP00-SERVICE-TYPE = '1'  
              MOVE 'MED'            TO WS-FOR-SUB-GROUP.

           IF CCP00-SERVICE-TYPE = '2'  
              MOVE 'DIE'            TO WS-FOR-SUB-GROUP.

           IF CCP00-SERVICE-TYPE = '3'  
              MOVE 'OCC'            TO WS-FOR-SUB-GROUP.

           IF (WS-FOR-SUB-GROUP = 'MED' OR 'DIE' OR 'OCC')  
               MOVE BKGSVC-SERVICE-CODE (BKGSVC-SUB) TO WS-FOR-CODE 
               PERFORM 5840-FETCH-FOR-RECORD
                  THRU 5840-EXIT
               IF (FOR-SUB-GROUP = WS-FOR-SUB-GROUP)  OR
                  ((FOR-SUB-GROUP = 'SPA' OR 'SPB' OR 'SPC') AND
                    WS-FOR-SUB-GROUP = 'OCC')   
                   CONTINUE 
               ELSE 
                   ADD 1      TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                   MOVE 0068  TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                   GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

       5820-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

      *=================================================================
       5840-FETCH-FOR-RECORD.   
           MOVE SPACES               TO FOR-TYPE
                                        FOR-GROUP   
                                        FOR-SUB-GROUP   
                                        FOR-CODE.   

           FETCH FEATURES-OPTIONS-REC RECORD
               ON ERROR GO TO 5840-FOR-FETCH-ERROR. 

           GO TO 5840-CHECK-FOR-CODE.   

       5840-FETCH-NEXT-FOR. 
           FETCH NEXT FEATURES-OPTIONS-REC RECORD   
               AT END   GO TO 5840-FOR-FETCH-ERROR  
               ON ERROR GO TO 5840-FOR-FETCH-ERROR. 

       5840-CHECK-FOR-CODE. 
           IF  WS-FOR-CODE = FOR-CODE AND   
              (FOR-SUB-GROUP = 'MED' OR 'DIE' OR 'OCC' OR   
                               'SPA' OR 'SPB' OR 'SPC') 
               GO TO 5840-EXIT  
           ELSE 
               GO TO 5840-FETCH-NEXT-FOR
           END-IF.  

       5840-FOR-FETCH-ERROR.
           IF  ERROR-NUM  = '0013' OR '0007'
               IF CHECK-OLD-PAX-COMMENT 
                  MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VALID-INHOUSE-FEATURE-SW   
                  GO TO 5840-EXIT   
               END-IF   
               ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
               MOVE 0068 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
               GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
           ELSE 
               GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR
           END-IF.  

       5840-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

      *=================================================================
       5900-PROCESS-PACKAGES.   

           MOVE ZEROS TO BKGPKG-SUB.
           MOVE ZEROS TO WS-PPR-SUB.


       5900-PACKAGE-LOOP.   

           ADD 1 TO BKGPKG-SUB. 

           IF BKGPKG-SUB > BKGPKG-SEGMENT-MAX   
              GO TO 5900-EXIT   
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGPKG-PAX-SEQ-NUM (BKGPKG-SUB) NOT = 
              BKGINF-PAX-SEQ-NUM (BKGINF-SUB)   
              GO TO 5900-PACKAGE-LOOP   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKGPKG-PKG-CODE (BKGPKG-SUB) TO WS-CC-PACKAGE-CODE. 

           IF WS-PACKAGE-CODE = 'FLY'   
              MOVE WS-PKG-CCL-CITY-CODE 
                TO RSP06-PAX-CITY-CODE       OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
              GO TO 5900-PACKAGE-LOOP   
           ELSE 
              IF BKGPKG-OPTION-CODE (BKGPKG-SUB) > SPACES   
                 MOVE BKGPKG-OPTION-CODE (BKGPKG-SUB) TO WS-PKG-OPTION  
              END-IF
              IF WS-PKG-OPTION NOT > SPACES 
                 MOVE 'N' TO WS-PKG-OPTION  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-PACKAGE-CODE NOT = 'FLY' AND   
              BKGPKG-OPTION-CODE (BKGPKG-SUB) NOT > SPACES  
              MOVE WS-PACKAGE-CODE            TO DPP30-PKG-CODE 
              MOVE ZEROES                     TO DPP30-PKG-PHASE-NUM
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-CURRENCY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW 
                TO DPP30-CURRENCY-CODE  
              MOVE 'P'                        TO DPP30-EDIT-TYPE
              IF RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW > SPACES  
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW  
                   TO DPP30-CATEGORY-CODE   
              ELSE  
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW  
                   TO DPP30-CATEGORY-CODE   
              END-IF
              PERFORM DPP30-FETCH-PDR-REC THRU DPP30-EXIT   
              IF PDR1-TRANSFER-IND = 'I'
                 MOVE 'T' TO WS-PKG-OPTION  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGPKG-PKG-TYPE (BKGPKG-SUB) = 'B '   
              MOVE WS-PACKAGE-CODE  
                TO RSP06-PAX-PRE-PACKAGE-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
           ELSE 
              IF BKGPKG-PKG-TYPE (BKGPKG-SUB) = 'A '
                 MOVE WS-PACKAGE-CODE   
                   TO RSP06-PAX-POST-PACKAGE-CODE OF RSP06-NEW  
                                                     (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 5900-PACKAGE-LOOP
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-PPR-SUB. 

           MOVE WS-PACKAGE-CODE 
             TO RSP06-PPR-PKG-CODE           OF RSP06-NEW   
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPR-SUB).  
           MOVE BKGPKG-PKG-TYPE (BKGPKG-SUB)
             TO RSP06-PPR-PKG-TYPE           OF RSP06-NEW   
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPR-SUB).  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE          OF RSP06-NEW   
             TO RSP06-PPR-BOOK-TYPE          OF RSP06-NEW   
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPR-SUB).  
           MOVE 'O' 
             TO RSP06-PPR-PKG-PRICE-TYPE     OF RSP06-NEW   
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPR-SUB).  

           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD > SPACES  

              MOVE 'N' TO WS-PPR-EXIST-SW   
              PERFORM VARYING I FROM 1 BY 1 
                      UNTIL I > RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OLD-PAX-SUB)

                 IF (RSP06-PPR-PKG-TYPE      OF RSP06-NEW   
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPR-SUB) = 
                     RSP06-PPR-PKG-TYPE      OF RSP06-OLD   
                                             (OLD-PAX-SUB I)
                    ) AND   
                    (RSP06-PPR-PKG-CODE      OF RSP06-NEW   
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPR-SUB) = 
                     RSP06-PPR-PKG-CODE      OF RSP06-OLD   
                                             (OLD-PAX-SUB I)
                    ) AND   
                    (RSP06-PPR-BOOK-TYPE     OF RSP06-NEW   
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPR-SUB) = 
                     RSP06-PPR-BOOK-TYPE     OF RSP06-OLD   
                                             (OLD-PAX-SUB I)
                    )   
                      AND   
                    (RSP06-PPR-CANCEL-DATE   OF RSP06-NEW   
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPR-SUB) = 
                     RSP06-PPR-CANCEL-DATE   OF RSP06-OLD   
                                             (OLD-PAX-SUB I)
                    )   

                    MOVE RSP06-PPR-ENTRY     OF RSP06-OLD   
                                             (OLD-PAX-SUB I)
                      TO RSP06-PPR-ENTRY     OF RSP06-NEW   
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPR-SUB)   

                    MOVE RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OLD-PAX-SUB) TO I  
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PPR-EXIST-SW 

                 END-IF 

              END-PERFORM   

              IF PAX-ADDED OR   
                 ((OLD-PAX-SUB > 0) AND (NOT PPR-EXIST))

                 MOVE 'N' TO WS-PPR-EXIST-SW
                 PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                    UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD   

                    PERFORM VARYING I FROM 1 BY 1   
                       UNTIL I > RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (WS-SUB)

                       IF (RSP06-PPR-PKG-TYPE     OF RSP06-NEW  
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPR-SUB) = 
                           RSP06-PPR-PKG-TYPE     OF RSP06-OLD  
                                             (WS-SUB I) 
                          ) AND 
                          (RSP06-PPR-PKG-CODE     OF RSP06-NEW  
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPR-SUB) = 
                           RSP06-PPR-PKG-CODE     OF RSP06-OLD  
                                             (WS-SUB I) 
                          ) AND 
                          (RSP06-PPR-BOOK-TYPE    OF RSP06-NEW  
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPR-SUB) = 
                           RSP06-PPR-BOOK-TYPE    OF RSP06-OLD  
                                             (WS-SUB I) 
                          ) 
                            AND 
                          (RSP06-PPR-CANCEL-DATE  OF RSP06-NEW  
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPR-SUB) = 
                           RSP06-PPR-CANCEL-DATE  OF RSP06-OLD  
                                             (WS-SUB I) 
                          ) 

                          MOVE RSP06-PPR-ENTRY    OF RSP06-OLD  
                                             (WS-SUB I) 
                            TO RSP06-PPR-ENTRY    OF RSP06-NEW  
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPR-SUB)   

                          MOVE RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (WS-SUB) TO I 
                          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PPR-EXIST-SW   

                       END-IF   

                    END-PERFORM 

                    IF PPR-EXIST
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD TO WS-SUB
                    END-IF  

                 END-PERFORM


              END-IF

           END-IF.  

           MOVE WS-PKG-OPTION   
                TO RSP06-PPR-PKG-CAR-IND        OF RSP06-NEW
                                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPR-SUB)   

           IF WS-PKG-OPTION = 'C'   
              MOVE 1 TO RSP06-TPR-NUM-CAR    OF RSP06-NEW   
                                             (WS-PPR-SUB)   
           ELSE 
              MOVE 0 TO RSP06-TPR-NUM-CAR    OF RSP06-NEW   
                                             (WS-PPR-SUB).  

           IF WS-PKG-OPTION = 'T'   
              MOVE 1 TO RSP06-TPR-NUM-XFR    OF RSP06-NEW   
                                             (WS-PPR-SUB)   
           ELSE 
              MOVE 0 TO RSP06-TPR-NUM-XFR    OF RSP06-NEW   
                                             (WS-PPR-SUB).  

           GO TO 5900-PACKAGE-LOOP. 

       5900-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

      *=================================================================
       6000-EDIT-PHONE. 

           MOVE 21 TO WS-FROM-SUB   
                      WS-TO-SUB.

       6000-PHONE-LOOP. 

           SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-FROM-SUB. 

           IF WS-FROM-SUB < 1   
              GO TO 6000-ZERO-FILL-PHONE
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-PHONE-NUMBER (WS-FROM-SUB) = SPACES
              GO TO 6000-PHONE-LOOP 
           END-IF.  

           SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-TO-SUB.   
           MOVE WS-PHONE-NUMBER (WS-FROM-SUB) TO
                WS-PHONE-NUMBER (WS-TO-SUB).

           GO TO 6000-PHONE-LOOP.   

       6000-ZERO-FILL-PHONE.
           SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-TO-SUB.   

           IF WS-TO-SUB < 1 
              GO TO 6000-CONTINUE   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE ZERO TO WS-PHONE-NUMBER (WS-TO-SUB).
           GO TO 6000-ZERO-FILL-PHONE.  

       6000-CONTINUE.   

           IF WS-PHONE-NUM NOT NUMERIC  
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0052       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

       6000-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

      *=================================================================
       6200-EDIT-CSMINF-PHONE.  

           MOVE 0  TO WS-CSINF-SUB. 

       6200-NEXT-PHONE-NUM. 

           ADD 1   TO WS-CSINF-SUB. 

           IF WS-CSINF-SUB > 2  
              GO TO 6200-EXIT   
           END-IF.  

           IF CSMINF-PHONE-NUM (WS-CSINF-SUB) > SPACES  
              MOVE CSMINF-PHONE-NUM (WS-CSINF-SUB) TO WS-PHONE-ARRAY
           ELSE 
               GO TO 6200-NEXT-PHONE-NUM
           END-IF.  

           INSPECT WS-PHONE-ARRAY REPLACING ALL 
                  '(' BY SPACE  
                  ')' BY SPACE  
                  '-' BY SPACE  
                  '.' BY SPACE. 

           PERFORM 6000-EDIT-PHONE THRU 6000-EXIT.  
           MOVE WS-PHONE-NUM TO CSMINF-PHONE-NUM (WS-CSINF-SUB).

           GO TO 6200-NEXT-PHONE-NUM.   

       6200-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

      *=================================================================
       6300-PROCESS-BKGADI-BKGIMI.  

           MOVE ZERO   TO BKGADI-SUB.   
           MOVE ZERO   TO BKGIMI-SUB.   

       6300-BKGADI-LOOP.

           ADD 1 TO BKGADI-SUB. 

           IF BKGADI-SUB > BKGADI-SEGMENT-MAX   
              GO TO 6300-BKGIMI-LOOP
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGADI-SEGMENT (BKGADI-SUB) NOT > SPACES  
              GO TO 6300-BKGIMI-LOOP
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGADI-PAX-SEQ-NUM (BKGADI-SUB) NOT = 
              BKGINF-PAX-SEQ-NUM (BKGINF-SUB)   
              GO TO 6300-BKGADI-LOOP
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'Y' 
             TO RSP06-PIAR-READ-IND         OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB). 
           MOVE 'Y' 
             TO RSP06-PIAR-EXIST-IND         OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE                     OF RSP06-NEW
             TO RSP06-PIAR-BOOK-TYPE         OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE BKGADI-ADDRESS1 (BKGADI-SUB)
             TO RSP06-PIAR-ADDR1             OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE BKGADI-ADDRESS2 (BKGADI-SUB)
             TO RSP06-PIAR-ADDR2             OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE BKGADI-CITY (BKGADI-SUB)
             TO RSP06-PIAR-CITY              OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           IF RSP06-PAX-STATE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) > SPACES   
              AND WS-STATE-CHG-IND = 'Y'
              IF BKGADI-STATE-CODE (BKGADI-SUB) NOT = RSP06-PAX-STATE   
                                             OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                 ADD 1  TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                 MOVE 0082  
                        TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKGADI-STATE-CODE (BKGADI-SUB)  
             TO RSP06-PIAR-STATE          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).   
           MOVE BKGADI-ZIP-CODE (BKGADI-SUB)
             TO RSP06-PIAR-ZIP-CODE          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE BKGADI-NATIONALITY-CODE (BKGADI-SUB)
             TO RSP06-PIAR-COUNTRY           OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                RSP06-PIAR-NATIONALITY       OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

AUSPOA     IF RSP06-PIAR-COUNTRY OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) = 'AU'  
AUSPOA        EVALUATE RSP06-PIAR-STATE OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
AUSPOA                 WHEN 'NS'
AUSPOA                       MOVE 'N1' TO RSP06-PIAR-STATE  
AUSPOA                                        OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
AUSPOA                 WHEN 'NT'
AUSPOA                       MOVE 'N2' TO RSP06-PIAR-STATE  
AUSPOA                                        OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
AUSPOA                 WHEN 'TA'
AUSPOA                       MOVE 'TS' TO RSP06-PIAR-STATE  
AUSPOA                                        OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
AUSPOA                 WHEN 'VI'
AUSPOA                       MOVE 'VC' TO RSP06-PIAR-STATE  
AUSPOA                                        OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
AUSPOA                 WHEN 'WA'
AUSPOA                       MOVE 'W1' TO RSP06-PIAR-STATE  
AUSPOA                                        OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
AUSPOA        END-EVALUATE  
AUSPOA     END-IF.  

       6300-BKGIMI-LOOP.

           IF WS-MAIL-PROMPT = 'MAIL'   
              GO TO 6300-EXIT.  

           ADD 1 TO BKGIMI-SUB. 

           IF BKGIMI-SUB > BKGIMI-SEGMENT-MAX   
              GO TO 6300-EXIT   
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGIMI-SEGMENT (BKGIMI-SUB) NOT > SPACES  
              GO TO 6300-EXIT   
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGIMI-PAX-SEQ-NUM (BKGIMI-SUB) NOT = 
              BKGINF-PAX-SEQ-NUM (BKGINF-SUB)   
              GO TO 6300-BKGIMI-LOOP
           END-IF.  

           IF (BKGIMI-DOC-NUM  (BKGIMI-SUB) > SPACES   AND  
               BKGIMI-DOC-NUM  (BKGIMI-SUB) NOT = '000000')       OR
              BKGIMI-DOC-ISSUE-DATE         (BKGIMI-SUB) > ZERO   OR
              BKGIMI-DOC-EXP-DATE           (BKGIMI-SUB) > ZERO   OR
              BKGIMI-DOC-ISSUE-COUNTRY-CODE (BKGIMI-SUB) > SPACES   
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 6300-BKGIMI-LOOP
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKGIMI-OCCUPATION-CODE (BKGIMI-SUB) 
             TO RSP06-PIAR-OCCUPATION    OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE 'Y' 
             TO RSP06-PIID-READ-IND      OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).
           MOVE 'P' 
             TO RSP06-PIID-DOC-TYPE      OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB 1).  
           MOVE BKGIMI-DOC-NUM (BKGIMI-SUB) 
             TO RSP06-PIID-DOC-NUM       OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB 1).  
           MOVE BKGIMI-DOC-ISSUE-DATE (BKGIMI-SUB)  
             TO DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE.
           PERFORM DPP10-MMDDYYYY-TO-YYYYMMDD.  
           MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE 
             TO RSP06-PIID-ISSUE-DATE    OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB 1).  
           MOVE BKGIMI-DOC-EXP-DATE (BKGIMI-SUB)
             TO DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE.
           PERFORM DPP10-MMDDYYYY-TO-YYYYMMDD.  
           MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE 
             TO RSP06-PIID-EXP-DATE      OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB 1).  
           MOVE BKGIMI-DOC-ISSUE-COUNTRY-CODE (BKGIMI-SUB)  
             TO RSP06-PIID-ISSUE-COUNTRY OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB 1).  
           MOVE 1 TO RSP06-PIID-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB). 

       6300-EXIT.   
           EXIT.
      *=================================================================
       6600-GET-ADDITIONAL-INFO.

           MOVE 'DOMESTIC CONSUMER ADVICE '      TO AIHR-CALC-KEY.  
           IF  TDP01-BASE-CURRENCY-CODE = 'AUD' 
               MOVE 'AUSTRALIA CONSUMER ADVICE ' TO AIHR-CALC-KEY   
           END-IF.  

           FETCH ADD-INFO-HDR-REC RECORD
              ON ERROR GO TO 6600-EXIT. 

           IF ADD-INFO-SET SET EMPTY
              GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.

           MOVE 0                 TO PIPOL2-SUB.
           MOVE 'CONSUMER ADVISE' TO PIPOL2-POLICY-DESC (1).

       6600-ADD-INFO-TEXT.  

           FETCH NEXT ADD-INFO-DESC-REC WITHIN ADD-INFO-SET SET 
             AT END   GO TO 6600-EXIT   
             ON ERROR GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.

           MOVE ZERO TO WS-SUB. 

       6600-LOOP-THRU-DATA. 

           ADD 1 TO WS-SUB. 

           IF WS-SUB > 9
              GO TO 6600-ADD-INFO-TEXT. 
           MOVE AIDR-DATA (WS-SUB) TO WS-CHAR-TABLE.
           IF WS-CHAR-TABLE NOT > SPACES
              GO TO 6600-LOOP-THRU-DATA.

           IF WS-CHR (1) = '.'  
              MOVE SPACE TO WS-CHR (1). 

           IF WS-CHR (1) = ' '  OR  ((CCMV-TEXT-PTR + 64) > 2000) OR
              PIPOL2-SUB = 0
              ADD 1       TO PIPOL2-SUB 
              MOVE 1      TO CCMV-TEXT-PTR  
              MOVE 35     TO PIPOL2-POLICY-CODE (PIPOL2-SUB)
              MOVE SPACES TO PIPOL2-POLICY-TEXT (PIPOL2-SUB).   

           MOVE 65 TO WS-LAST-CHAR. 

       6600-CHECK-FOR-LAST-CHAR.

           SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-LAST-CHAR.

           IF WS-LAST-CHAR = ZERO   
              GO TO 6600-LOOP-THRU-DATA.

           IF WS-CHR (WS-LAST-CHAR) > SPACES
              GO TO 6600-ADD-ENTRY. 

           GO TO 6600-CHECK-FOR-LAST-CHAR.  

       6600-ADD-ENTRY.  

           SUBTRACT WS-LAST-CHAR FROM 64 GIVING WS-LAST-CHAR.   

           STRING WS-CHAR-TABLE DELIMITED BY SIZE   
                  INTO PIPOL2-POLICY-TEXT (PIPOL2-SUB)  
                  POINTER CCMV-TEXT-PTR.

           SUBTRACT WS-LAST-CHAR FROM CCMV-TEXT-PTR 
                  GIVING CCMV-TEXT-PTR. 

           GO TO 6600-LOOP-THRU-DATA.   

       6600-DONE-WITH-DESCRIPTION.  

           STRING '                          ' DELIMITED BY SIZE
                  INTO PIPOL2-POLICY-TEXT (PIPOL2-SUB)  
                  POINTER CCMV-TEXT-PTR.

       6600-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

      *=================================================================
       6800-DETERMINE-RATE-CHANGE.  

           MOVE 'N'                     TO WS-NUM-PAX-TWO.  
           IF SSP03-O-PPC-COMPLAINT-FLG = 1 
             IF NOT CCL-CORP-GRP-IND
                IF CCL-OFFER-TYPE NOT = 'B' 
                   MOVE 'Y'                TO WS-RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT-SW 
                   GO TO 6800-EXIT  
                END-IF  
             END-IF 
           END-IF.  

           IF CCL-CORP-GRP-IND  
              IF CCL-GRP-RATE-WITH-VERSION  
                 MOVE CCR-GRP-VERSION-NUM TO WS-RATE-VERSION-NUM
                 IF RSP06-IBR-REQ-RATE-VERSION OF RSP06-OLD NOT =   
                    WS-RATE-VERSION-NUM 
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT-SW   
                    GO TO 6800-EXIT 
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 IF RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (1) NOT = 
                    WS-OFFER-CODE   
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT-SW   
                    GO TO 6800-EXIT 
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              IF SSP03-O-PPC-COMPLAINT-FLG = 0  
                 IF RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (1) NOT = 
                    WS-OFFER-CODE   
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT-SW   
                    GO TO 6800-EXIT 
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF BKCRV1-UPGRADE-CODE (1) > SPACES  
              IF RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD NOT = 
                 BKCRV1-UPGRADE-CODE (1)
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT-SW  
                 GO TO 6800-EXIT
              END-IF
              IF RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD NOT = 
                 BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE (1)   
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT-SW  
                 GO TO 6800-EXIT
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              IF RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD > SPACES  
                 IF RSP06-IBR-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD NOT =  
                    BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE (1)
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT-SW   
                    GO TO 6800-EXIT 
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 IF RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD NOT =  
                    BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE (1)
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT-SW   
                    GO TO 6800-EXIT 
                 END-IF 
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-NUM-PAX > (RSP06-IBR-NUM-ADULTS OF RSP06-OLD + 
                            RSP06-IBR-NUM-CHILDREN OF RSP06-OLD)
              IF WS-NUM-PAX = 2 
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-NUM-PAX-TWO 
              END-IF
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT-SW 
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-ADD-PAX-ONLY-SW
              GO TO 6800-EXIT.  

           IF ((RSP06-IBR-GTEE-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD  > SPACES)
