              AND (RSP06-IBR-GTEE-CATEGORY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD
                                         = BKCRV1-CATEGORY-CODE (1))
              AND (DPP34-CABIN-NUM (1:3) = 'TBA'))  
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              IF RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM  OF RSP06-OLD (1:1) = 'T'  
                 IF DPP34-CABIN-NUM (1:3) NOT = 'TBA'   
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT-SW   
                                WS-CHANGE-CABIN-ONLY-SW 
                    GO TO 6800-EXIT 
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM  OF RSP06-OLD  
                                              TO DPP34-CABIN-NUM
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 IF RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM  OF RSP06-OLD (1:1)= 'C'
                    IF DPP34-CABIN-NUM (1:3) NOT = 'CSV'
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT-SW
                                   WS-CHANGE-CABIN-ONLY-SW  
                       GO TO 6800-EXIT  
                    END-IF  
                 ELSE   
                    IF RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-OLD NOT =   
                                                         DPP34-CABIN-NUM
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT-SW
                                   WS-CHANGE-CABIN-ONLY-SW  
                       GO TO 6800-EXIT. 

           MOVE 'N' TO WS-RATE-DIFF-IBR-INPUT-SW
                       WS-ADD-PAX-ONLY-SW   
                       WS-CHANGE-CABIN-ONLY-SW. 
.   

       6800-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

      *=================================================================
       7000-PROCESS-OTHER-PAYMENT.  

           MOVE ZERO TO BKPCHK-SUB. 

           PERFORM 8500-CALC-PAYMENT-DATE THRU  
                   8500-CALC-PAYMENT-DATE-EXIT. 

       7000-GO-THRU-OTHER-PAYMENTS. 

           ADD 1 TO BKPCHK-SUB. 
           IF BKPCHK-SUB > BKPCHK-SEGMENT-MAX   
               GO TO 7000-EXIT. 

           IF AGENT-PHONE-NUM = ATCR-PHONE-NUM  
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              MOVE AGENT-PHONE-NUM      TO ATCR-PHONE-NUM   
              MOVE 'C'                  TO ATCR-COMPANY-CODE
              FETCH AGENT-TERMS-COMPANY-REC RECORD  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE ATCR-AGENCY-INDICATORS  TO MKP40A-INDICATORS.   

           IF BKPCHK-SEGMENT (BKPCHK-SUB) NOT > SPACES  
               GO TO 7000-EXIT. 

           ACCEPT DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE FROM DATE YYYYMMDD.   
           PERFORM DPP10-YYYYMMDD-TO-MMDDYY.

           IF BKPCHK-PAYMENT-TYPE (BKPCHK-SUB) = 'WR'                OR 
              BKPCHK-CHECK-NUM (BKPCHK-SUB) > SPACES
              IF WS-OTHER-PAY-DATE NOT > TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD   
                 CONTINUE   
              ELSE  
                 IF  BKPCHK-PAYMENT-TYPE (BKPCHK-SUB) = 'BA'       AND  
                     MKP40A-VOUCHERS-YES
                     CONTINUE   
                 ELSE   
                     ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                     MOVE 0108    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                     GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF BKPCHK-PAYMENT-TYPE (BKPCHK-SUB) NOT > SPACES 
              IF MKP40A-VOUCHERS-YES
                 MOVE 'V' TO WS-DEPOSIT-TYPE
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 'C' TO WS-DEPOSIT-TYPE
              END-IF
              GO TO 7000-CONTINUE.  

           EVALUATE BKPCHK-PAYMENT-TYPE (BKPCHK-SUB)
               WHEN 'CK'
                   MOVE 'CHK'            TO WS-PAYMENT-TYPE 
               WHEN 'WR'
                   MOVE 'BKW'            TO WS-PAYMENT-TYPE 
               WHEN 'CS'
                   MOVE 'CSH'            TO WS-PAYMENT-TYPE 
               WHEN 'BA'
                   MOVE 'VOU'            TO WS-PAYMENT-TYPE 
               WHEN OTHER   
                   ADD 1                 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                   MOVE 0138             TO 
                                MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                   MOVE 'CCR08UGC'       TO 
                               MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                   PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG THRU   
                           CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT   
                   GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
           END-EVALUATE.

           MOVE ZEROES                   TO WS-LRSUB.   

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB3 FROM 1 BY 1  
               UNTIL WS-SUB3 > RSP86-O-PAYMENT-TYPE-CNT 
               IF RSP86-O-PAYMENT-TYPE (WS-SUB3) =  
                   WS-PAYMENT-TYPE  
                   MOVE WS-SUB3          TO WS-LRSUB
                   MOVE RSP86-O-PAYMENT-TYPE-CNT
                                         TO WS-SUB3 
               END-IF   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF  WS-LRSUB = 0 
               ADD 1                     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
               MOVE 0139                 TO 
                                       MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
               MOVE 'CCR08UGC'           TO 
                                     MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
               PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG THRU   
                       CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT   
               GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
           END-IF.  


           IF BKPCHK-PAYMENT-TYPE (BKPCHK-SUB) = 'BA'   
              IF MKP40A-VOUCHERS-YES
                 MOVE 'V' TO WS-DEPOSIT-TYPE
                 GO TO 7000-CONTINUE
              ELSE  
                 ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                 MOVE 0055    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
              END-IF.   

           IF BKPCHK-PAYMENT-TYPE (BKPCHK-SUB) = 'CK'   
              IF (MKP40A-CASHIERSCHECK-NO) OR   
                 (RSP50-O-CURRENCY-CODE NOT = SSP05-O-CURRENCY-CODE)

                 ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                 MOVE 0056    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 'C' TO WS-DEPOSIT-TYPE
                 GO TO 7000-CONTINUE
              END-IF.   

           IF  BKPCHK-PAYMENT-TYPE (BKPCHK-SUB) = 'CS'  
               MOVE 'C' TO WS-DEPOSIT-TYPE  
           END-IF.  

           IF  BKPCHK-PAYMENT-TYPE (BKPCHK-SUB) = 'WR'  
               MOVE 'W' TO WS-DEPOSIT-TYPE  
           END-IF.  

       7000-CONTINUE.   

           IF RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-1 OF RSP06-NEW > SPACES
              MOVE WS-DEPOSIT-TYPE  
                TO RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-2  OF RSP06-NEW   
              MOVE BKPCHK-CHECK-NUM (BKPCHK-SUB)
                TO WS-DEPOSIT-COMMENT   
              MOVE SPACES   
                TO RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-2   OF RSP06-NEW   
              STRING WS-DEPOSIT-COMMENT ' ' DPP02-MMDDYY-DATE   
                     DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO 
                     RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-2 OF RSP06-NEW   
           ELSE 
              MOVE WS-DEPOSIT-TYPE  
                TO RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-1  OF RSP06-NEW   
              MOVE BKPCHK-CHECK-NUM (BKPCHK-SUB)
                TO WS-DEPOSIT-COMMENT   
              MOVE SPACES   
                TO RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-1   OF RSP06-NEW   
              STRING WS-DEPOSIT-COMMENT ' ' DPP02-MMDDYY-DATE   
                     DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO 
                     RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-1 OF RSP06-NEW   
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 7000-GO-THRU-OTHER-PAYMENTS.   

       7000-EXIT.   
           EXIT.
      *=================================================================
       7100-PROCESS-PAYMENTS.   

           MOVE 'N'  TO WS-GIFT-CARD-IND
                        WS-CC-PAYMENT-IND   
                        WS-UCC-APPROVED-IND.

           MOVE 'Y'  TO WS-ALL-CARD-DENIED-IND. 

           MOVE ZERO TO WS-NEW-CC-AMT   
                        CCP78-CC-SUB.   
           INITIALIZE   VLUCC2-SEGMENT-DATA 
                        VLADV2-SEGMENT-DATA.

       7120-CHECK-FOR-PAYMENT.  

           IF (RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-1 OF RSP06-OLD =  
               RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-1 OF RSP06-NEW)  AND  
              (RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-1  OF RSP06-OLD =  
               RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-1  OF RSP06-NEW)  AND  
              (RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-2 OF RSP06-OLD =  
               RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-2 OF RSP06-NEW)  AND  
              (RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-2  OF RSP06-OLD =  
               RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-2  OF RSP06-NEW)   
               GO TO 7140-PAYMENTS. 

           MOVE 'CGP' TO DPP04-HIST-ACTIVITY-TYPE.  
           MOVE SPACES TO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT.   

           MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE TO DPP04-TRF-LOCATION-CODE
           IF (RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-1 OF RSP06-OLD =  
               RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-1 OF RSP06-NEW)  AND  
              (RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-1  OF RSP06-OLD =  
               RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-1  OF RSP06-NEW)   
               NEXT SENTENCE
           ELSE 
               IF  RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'I'   
                   MOVE 'CP1' TO DPP04-HIST-ACTIVITY-TYPE   
                   MOVE INFO-USERID     TO DPP04-HIST-OPER-INITIAL  
                   MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE
                                        TO DPP04-HIST-AGENCY-CONN-CODE  
                   MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE   
                                        TO DPP04-TRF-LOCATION-CODE  

                   IF RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-1 OF RSP06-OLD > SPACES
                      STRING RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-1 OF RSP06-OLD ' '  
                             RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-1  OF RSP06-OLD  
                             DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT  
                      PERFORM IRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY  
                         THRU IRP15-TRF-EXIT
                   ELSE 
                      MOVE 'DEPOSIT-1 V/C/F/A/W'
                                               TO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT
                      PERFORM IRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY  
                                       THRU IRP15-TRF-EXIT  
               ELSE 
                   MOVE SPACES          TO GRP15-HISTORY-INFORMATION
                   MOVE 'CP1'           TO GRP15-ACTIVITY-CODE  
                   MOVE INFO-USERID     TO GRP15-USERID 
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM  OF RSP06-NEW
                                        TO GRP15-IBR-BOOK-NUM   
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                                        TO GRP15-CABIN-NUM  
                   MOVE RSP25-I-UPDATE-CONTACT  
                                        TO GRP15-AGENCY-CONTACT 
                   MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE   
                                        TO GRP15-LOCATION-CODE  
                   MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE
                                        TO GRP15-AGENCY-CONNECT-CODE
                   IF GRP15-USERID = '@IA' OR '@UA' OR '@AU'
                      MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID   
                                        TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID   
                   ELSE 
                      MOVE SPACES       TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID   
                   END-IF   
                   IF  RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-1 OF RSP06-OLD > SPACES   
                       STRING RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-1 OF RSP06-OLD ' ' 
                             RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-1  OF RSP06-OLD  
                             DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO GRP15-COMMENT   
                        PERFORM GRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY
                           THRU GRP15-GTRF-EXIT 
                   ELSE 
                      MOVE 'DEPOSIT-1 V/C/F/A/W'
                                               TO GRP15-COMMENT 
                      PERFORM GRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY  
                         THRU GRP15-GTRF-EXIT.  

           IF (RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-2 OF RSP06-OLD =  
               RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-2 OF RSP06-NEW)  AND  
              (RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-2  OF RSP06-OLD =  
               RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-2  OF RSP06-NEW)   
               NEXT SENTENCE
           ELSE 
               IF  RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'I'   
                   MOVE 'CP2' TO DPP04-HIST-ACTIVITY-TYPE   
                   MOVE INFO-USERID     TO DPP04-HIST-OPER-INITIAL  
                   MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE
                                        TO DPP04-HIST-AGENCY-CONN-CODE  
                   MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE   
                                        TO DPP04-TRF-LOCATION-CODE  
                   IF RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-2 OF RSP06-OLD > SPACES
                      STRING RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-2 OF RSP06-OLD ' '  
                             RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-2  OF RSP06-OLD  
                             DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT  
                      PERFORM IRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY  
                         THRU IRP15-TRF-EXIT
                   ELSE 
                      MOVE 'DEPOSIT-2 V/C/F/A/W'
                                               TO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT
                      PERFORM IRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY  
                         THRU IRP15-TRF-EXIT
               ELSE 
                   MOVE SPACES          TO GRP15-HISTORY-INFORMATION
                   MOVE 'CP2'           TO GRP15-ACTIVITY-CODE  
                   MOVE INFO-USERID     TO GRP15-USERID 
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM  OF RSP06-NEW
                                        TO GRP15-IBR-BOOK-NUM   
                   MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                                        TO GRP15-CABIN-NUM  
                   MOVE RSP25-I-UPDATE-CONTACT  
                                        TO GRP15-AGENCY-CONTACT 
                   MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE   
                                        TO GRP15-LOCATION-CODE  
                   MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE
                                        TO GRP15-AGENCY-CONNECT-CODE
                   IF GRP15-USERID = '@IA' OR '@UA' OR '@AU'
                      MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID   
                                        TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID   
                   ELSE 
                      MOVE SPACES       TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID   
                   END-IF   
                   IF RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-2 OF RSP06-OLD > SPACES
                      STRING RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-TYPE-2 OF RSP06-OLD ' '  
                          RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-NUM-2  OF RSP06-OLD 
                          DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO GRP15-COMMENT  
                      PERFORM GRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY  
                         THRU GRP15-GTRF-EXIT   
                   ELSE 
                      MOVE 'DEPOSIT-2 V/C/F/A/W'
                                               TO GRP15-COMMENT 
                      PERFORM GRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY  
                         THRU GRP15-GTRF-EXIT.  

       7140-PAYMENTS.   
           IF BKPUCC-SEGMENT (1) NOT > SPACES   
              GO TO 7550-NO-MORE-PAYMENTS.  
           MOVE 'N'                     TO WS-RESTRICT-PAYMENT-IND. 

           IF  RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (1 1) = '$' OR   
               RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (1 2) = '$' OR   
               RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (1 3) = '$' OR   
               RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (1 4) = '$' OR   
               RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (1 5) = '$'  
               MOVE 'Y'                 TO WS-RESTRICT-PAYMENT-IND  
           END-IF.  

           IF  IBR-LOCALITY-CODE          = '7'                      AND
               IBR-DIRECT-IND             = 'Y'                      AND
               ((IBR-LOCATION-CODE        = 'VP')                    OR 
                (WS-RESTRICT-PAYMENT-IND  = 'Y'))                    AND
               ((DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE        < IBR-OPTION-DATE)         OR 
207680          (DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE       = IBR-OPTION-DATE))         AND
                 ((IBR-OPTION-TIME        = ZEROS)                   OR 
207680         (DPP02-SYSTEM-TIME (1:4) <= IBR-OPTION-TIME))
               ADD 1                    TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
               MOVE 'CCR08UGC'          TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE 
                                                 (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
               MOVE 0230                TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   
                                                 (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
               MOVE SPACES              TO MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID 
               PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG
                  THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT   
               GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
           END-IF.  

           COMPUTE WS-TOTAL-CC-PMT-AMT =
                                 RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-UCC-PMT   OF RSP06-NEW 
                               + RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-CASH-PMT  OF RSP06-NEW 
                               + RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-NEW 
                               + RSP06-IBR-CABIN-UCC-PMT   OF RSP06-NEW 
                               + RSP06-IBR-CABIN-CASH-PMT  OF RSP06-NEW 
                               + RSP06-IBR-CABIN-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-NEW 
                               + RSP06-IBR-PAID-COMM-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW.

           MOVE 0 TO WS-DEPOSIT-AMT 
                     WS-TOTAL-DEPOSIT-AMT   
                     WS-REM-AMT.
           COMPUTE  WS-TOTAL-CC-PMT-AMT = WS-TOTAL-CC-PMT-AMT   
                               + RSP06-IBR-CREDIT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW. 
           COMPUTE WS-TOTAL-DEPOSIT-AMT =   
           RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW + 
           RSP06-IBR-INS-PREMIUM-AMT OF RSP06-NEW.  
           IF WS-TOTAL-CC-PMT-AMT >= WS-TOTAL-DEPOSIT-AMT   
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              COMPUTE WS-DEPOSIT-AMT =  
                      WS-TOTAL-DEPOSIT-AMT - WS-TOTAL-CC-PMT-AMT
           END-IF.  

           SUBTRACT RSP06-IB-PAX-GROSS-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-NEW
               FROM WS-TOTAL-CC-PMT-AMT.
           IF RSP06-IBR-MAX-OPT-PRG-FEE-AMT OF RSP06-OLD > ZEROES AND   
              RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-OLD = 'G'
              SUBTRACT RSP06-IBR-MAX-OPT-PRG-FEE-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW  
                  FROM WS-TOTAL-CC-PMT-AMT  
              ADD RSP06-IBR-PROGRAM-FEE-AMT OF RSP06-NEW
                  TO WS-TOTAL-CC-PMT-AMT
           END-IF.  

           MULTIPLY WS-TOTAL-CC-PMT-AMT BY -1 GIVING WS-REM-AMT.
       COPY SSP05INT.   

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-COMPANY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW TO  
                SSP05-I-COMPANY-CODE.   

           CALL 'SSC05GCD' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 SSP05-INPUT-PARAMS 
                                 SSP05-OUTPUT-PARAMS.   

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > 0 AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1
              GO TO 9500-RETURN-SUBROUTINE-ERROR.   

           PERFORM 8850-FETCH-CURRENCY THRU 8850-EXIT.  
           MOVE RSP94-O-LMN-CODE        TO WS-LMN-CODE. 

           MOVE 0 TO BKPUCC-SUB.

           MOVE 'N' TO WS-SPLITPAY-SCHD-REQUEST 
                       WS-SPLITPAY-CANCEL-REQUEST   
                       WS-SPLITPAY-CC-CHG-REQUEST.  

       7150-VALIDATE-PMTS-LOOP. 

           ADD 1 TO BKPUCC-SUB. 

           IF BKPUCC-SUB > BKPUCC-SEGMENT-MAX   
              GO TO 7300-PROCESS-PMTS.  

           IF BKPUCC-SEGMENT (BKPUCC-SUB) NOT > SPACES  
              GO TO 7300-PROCESS-PMTS.  

           IF  BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'N'   
               GO TO 7150-VALIDATE-PMTS-LOOP.   

           IF BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'C'
              GO TO 7201-CHECK-SPLITPAY-REQ 
           END-IF.  

           IF BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB) < ZERO
              DISPLAY BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB) UPON PRINTER  
              ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
              MOVE 0057    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR. 

           IF BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB) = ZERO
              IF  SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE NOT = 'IC'  
              ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
              MOVE 0121    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
              ELSE  
                  IF BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'Y' 
                     GO TO 4735-MODIFY-PYDR 
                  ELSE  
                     ADD 1      TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                     MOVE 0121  TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                     GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

LOYLTY*    IF BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'GC' OR 'PP'  
LOYLTY     IF BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'GC' OR 'PP' OR 'LP'  
              GO TO 7150-CONT.  
           IF BKPUCC-TRANSACTION-ID (BKPUCC-SUB) > SPACES   
              GO TO 7150-CONT.  

           IF MKP40A-CREDITCARDS-NO 
              ADD 1            TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
              MOVE 0098        TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE(CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

       7150-CONT.   
           SET ARP90-IDX DOWN BY 1. 
           PERFORM ARP90-FETCH-ACCT-PMT-SRC-REC 
              THRU ARP90-FETCH-ACCT-PMT-SRC-EXIT.   

           IF ARP90-PMT-SRC-REC-NOT-FOUND   
              GO TO 7200-CONT.  

           SET ARP90-IDX TO 1.  
           SEARCH ARP90-APRR-STATUSES   
                  AT END GO TO 7270-INVALID-CREDIT-CARD 
                  WHEN ARP90-APRR-STATUS-CODE (ARP90-IDX) = APRR-STATUS 
                       GO TO 7200-CONT. 

       7200-CONT.   

           ADD BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB) TO WS-TOTAL-CC-PMT-AMT.  
           ADD BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB) TO WS-TOTAL-PAYMENT-AMT. 

           IF AGENT-INTL-SALES-IND = 'Y' OR AGENT-REGION-CODE = 6   
              IF WS-TOTAL-CC-PMT-AMT < 2000.01  
                 NEXT SENTENCE  
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 7230-CC-PAYMENT-EXCEEDED 
           ELSE 
              IF AGENT-PHONE-NUM = 6194641000   
                 IF WS-TOTAL-CC-PMT-AMT < 300.01
                    NEXT SENTENCE   
                 ELSE   
                    GO TO 7230-CC-PAYMENT-EXCEEDED  
              ELSE  
                 IF WS-TOTAL-CC-PMT-AMT <  0.01 
                    NEXT SENTENCE   
                 ELSE   
                    GO TO 7230-CC-PAYMENT-EXCEEDED. 

           IF SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1  
              IF  BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = SPACES 
                  IF  BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB) NOT = 'GC'  
                      PERFORM 7240-VALIDATE-MIN-PAY-POL 
                      THRU 7240-VALIDATE-MIN-PAY-POL-EXIT   
                  END-IF
              END-IF
           END-IF.  
LOYLTY     IF BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB) NOT = 'LP'  
             IF ( BKPUCC-TOKEN-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB)(1:1) = '1' OR   
                BKPUCC-CARD-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB)(1:1) = '1' )   
                MOVE 'UT' TO BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB)  
             END-IF 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB)   
                                            TO ARP27-CARD-TYPE. 
           PERFORM ARP27-FETCH-CARD-TYPE-REC THRU ARP27-EXIT.   

       7201-CHECK-SPLITPAY-REQ. 
LOYLTY* SKIP FOR 'LP'   
LOYLTY     IF BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'LP'  
              GO TO 7229-FINISHED-SPLITPAY  
LOYLTY     END-IF.  
           IF BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) NOT > SPACES 
              GO TO 7229-FINISHED-SPLITPAY  
           END-IF.  

           IF BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'Y' OR 'N' 
              GO TO 7229-FINISHED-SPLITPAY  
           END-IF.  

           IF BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'X'
              IF WS-SPLITPAY-CC-CHG-REQUEST = 'N'   
                 GO TO 7202-SPLITPAY-VALIDATIONS
              ELSE  
                 ADD 1          TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                 MOVE 0181      TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
           END-IF.  

           IF BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'C'
              IF WS-SPLITPAY-CANCEL-REQUEST = 'N'   
                 GO TO 7202-SPLITPAY-VALIDATIONS
              ELSE  
                 ADD 1          TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                 MOVE 0180      TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
           END-IF.  

       COPY SSP09INT.   

           MOVE 'SPLITPAYMENT'  TO SSP09-I-CODE.

           STRING 'OPT' BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB)
                        DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO SSP09-I-TYPE.

           CALL 'SSC09GCD' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 SSP09-INPUT-PARAMS 
                                 SSP09-OUTPUT-PARAMS.   

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > 0 AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1
              IF (DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) = 'SSC09GCD' AND 
                     DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1) = 2) OR
                       (DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) = 'SSC10LCD' AND   
                           DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1) = 5) 
                  ADD 1         TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                  MOVE 0201     TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                  GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  


           IF WS-SPLITPAY-SCHD-REQUEST = 'Y'
              ADD 1             TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE 0182         TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

       7202-SPLITPAY-VALIDATIONS.   

           IF BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'GC'  
              ADD 1             TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE 0183         TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           IF BKPUCC-CREDIT-DEBIT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB)  NOT = 'C' AND   
              BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'M'  OR
              BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = ' '
              ADD 1             TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE 0183         TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-IBR-FP-PAID-IND OF RSP06-NEW = 1
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0184       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW NOT = 'I'
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0185       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           IF BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'X'
              GO TO 7205-SPLITPAY-CHANGE-CARD   
           END-IF.  

           IF BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'C'
              GO TO 7207-CANCEL-SPLITPAY
           END-IF.  

           IF (RSP06-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE OF RSP06-NEW > SPACES) AND
                 (RSP06-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE OF RSP06-NEW   
                                                   NOT = 'C' AND 'N')   
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0186       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-SCHEDULED-PAYMENT-IND NOT = 'Y'
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0187       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

       7203-CHECK-PLAN-VALIDITY.

           IF BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) NOT = 'M'
              MOVE FUNCTION NUMVAL (SSP09-O-DESCRIPTION)
                                         TO WS-INSTALMENTS-OPTED
              GO TO 7203-CHECK-PLAN-VALIDITY-CONT   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-FINAL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW   
                                         TO WS-FINAL-PAYMENT-DATE.  

           COMPUTE WS-INSTALMENTS-OPTED = ((WS-FINAL-DATE-YYYY  
                                        -  DPP01-SYSTEM-DATE-YEAR)* 12) 
                                        +  WS-FINAL-DATE-MM 
                                        -  DPP01-SYSTEM-DATE-MM 

           IF (WS-FINAL-DATE-DD <= DPP01-SYSTEM-DATE-DD)
                         AND (WS-FINAL-DATE-DD NOT = 29 AND 30 AND 31)  
              SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-INSTALMENTS-OPTED  
                         GIVING WS-INSTALMENTS-OPTED
           END-IF.  

       COPY SSP09INT.   

           MOVE 'EASYPAYMONTH'           TO SSP09-I-CODE.   
           MOVE 'PLAN'                   TO SSP09-I-TYPE.   

           CALL 'SSC09GCD' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 SSP09-INPUT-PARAMS 
                                 SSP09-OUTPUT-PARAMS.   

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > 0 AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1
              GO TO 9500-RETURN-SUBROUTINE-ERROR
           END-IF.  

           MOVE FUNCTION NUMVAL (SSP09-O-DESCRIPTION)   
                                       TO WS-MINIMUM-INSTALLMENTS.  

           IF WS-INSTALMENTS-OPTED < WS-MINIMUM-INSTALLMENTS
              ADD 1              TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
              MOVE 0221          TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           ADD RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-UCC-PMT   OF RSP06-NEW   
               RSP06-IBR-CABIN-UCC-PMT   OF RSP06-NEW   
               RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-CASH-PMT  OF RSP06-NEW   
               RSP06-IBR-CABIN-CASH-PMT  OF RSP06-NEW   
               RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-NEW   
               RSP06-IBR-CABIN-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-NEW   
               GIVING WS-PMTS-RECEIVED. 

           COMPUTE WS-NETDUE-AMT =  
                   RSP06-IBR-GROSS-FARE-AMT     OF RSP06-NEW +  
                   RSP06-IBR-MISC-AMT           OF RSP06-NEW +  
                   RSP06-IBR-PENALTY-AMT        OF RSP06-NEW +  
                   RSP06-IBR-TOTAL-FEES-AMT     OF RSP06-NEW +  
                   RSP06-IBR-ADMIN-FEE-AMT      OF RSP06-NEW +  
                   RSP06-IB-COURTESY-ORDER-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW +  
                   RSP06-IBR-PP-GRATS-AMT       OF RSP06-NEW +  
                   RSP06-IBR-OBC-CHARGE-AMT     OF RSP06-NEW +  
                   RSP06-IBR-NSF-AMT            OF RSP06-NEW +  
                   RSP06-IBR-DAMAGE-DEPOSIT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW -  
                   RSP06-IBR-CREDIT-AMT         OF RSP06-NEW -  
                   WS-PMTS-RECEIVED.

           IF WS-PMTS-RECEIVED >= (RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW   
                               + RSP06-IBR-INS-PREMIUM-AMT OF RSP06-NEW)

              DIVIDE WS-NETDUE-AMT BY WS-INSTALMENTS-OPTED  
                     GIVING WS-INSTALLMENT-AMOUNT ROUNDED   
           ELSE 
              COMPUTE WS-INSTALLMENT-AMOUNT ROUNDED 
                  = ((WS-NETDUE-AMT  + WS-PMTS-RECEIVED)
                  - (RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-AMT     OF RSP06-NEW 
                  +  RSP06-IBR-INS-PREMIUM-AMT OF RSP06-NEW))   
                  /  WS-INSTALMENTS-OPTED   
           END-IF.  

       COPY SSP09INT.   

           MOVE 'EASYPAYMONTH' TO SSP09-I-CODE. 
           MOVE 'INST'         TO SSP09-I-TYPE. 

           CALL 'SSC09GCD' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 SSP09-INPUT-PARAMS 
                                 SSP09-OUTPUT-PARAMS.   

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > 0 AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1
              GO TO 9500-RETURN-SUBROUTINE-ERROR
           END-IF.  

           MOVE FUNCTION NUMVAL (SSP09-O-DESCRIPTION)   
                                       TO WS-MINIMUM-INSTALLMENT-AMT.   

           IF WS-INSTALLMENT-AMOUNT < WS-MINIMUM-INSTALLMENT-AMT
              ADD 1              TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
              MOVE 0222          TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

       7203-CHECK-PLAN-VALIDITY-CONT.   

           MOVE DPP01-SYSTEM-DATE-YEAR   TO WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-YYYY.   

           COMPUTE WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-MM = DPP01-SYSTEM-DATE-MM +  
                                            WS-INSTALMENTS-OPTED.   

           IF WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-MM > 12   
              SUBTRACT 12 FROM WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-MM   
                       GIVING  WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-MM   
              ADD 1 TO WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-YYYY 
                       GIVING WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-YYYY  
           END-IF.  

           IF DPP01-SYSTEM-DATE-DD = 29  AND 30 AND 31  
              MOVE 28                    TO WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-DD  
           ELSE 
              MOVE DPP01-SYSTEM-DATE-DD  TO WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-DD  
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-LAST-INSTALMENT-DATE >=
                                RSP06-IBR-FINAL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW   
              ADD 1             TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE 0189         TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

       7204-CHECK-CARD-VALIDITY.

           IF BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'CC'  
              MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-EXP-MONTH (BKPUCC-SUB)   
                                TO WS1-EXP-MM   
              MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-EXP-YEAR  (BKPUCC-SUB)   
                                TO WS1-EXP-YY   

              IF WS1-EXP-YYMM < WS-LAST-INSTALMENT-DATE (3:4)   
                 ADD 1          TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                 IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND   
                                              SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1) 
                    MOVE 0203   TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE    (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                    MOVE 'CCR08UGC' 
                                TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE  (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 0190   TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE    (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                    GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'Y'            TO WS-SPLITPAY-SCHD-REQUEST. 

           GO TO 7229-FINISHED-SPLITPAY.

       7205-SPLITPAY-CHANGE-CARD.   

       COPY SSP09INT.   

           MOVE 'SPLITPAYMENT'  TO SSP09-I-CODE.

           STRING 'OPT' RSP06-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE OF RSP06-NEW 
                        DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO SSP09-I-TYPE.

           CALL 'SSC09GCD' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 SSP09-INPUT-PARAMS 
                                 SSP09-OUTPUT-PARAMS.   

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > 0 AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1
              IF (DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) = 'SSC09GCD' AND 
                     DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1) = 2) OR
                       (DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) = 'SSC10LCD' AND   
                           DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1) = 5) 
                  ADD 1         TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                  MOVE 0191     TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                  GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF BKPUCC-CREDIT-DEBIT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) NOT = 'C'
              ADD 1             TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE 0183         TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM 7228-CALC-LAST-INSTALMENT-DATE THRU 7228-EXIT.   

           IF DPP01-SYSTEM-DATE > WS-LAST-INSTALMENT-DATE   
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0192       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

       7206-CHECK-CARD-VALIDITY.

           MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-EXP-MONTH (BKPUCC-SUB)  TO WS1-EXP-MM.  
           MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-EXP-YEAR  (BKPUCC-SUB)  TO WS1-EXP-YY.  

           IF WS1-EXP-YYMM < WS-LAST-INSTALMENT-DATE (3:4)  
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND  
                                              SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1) 
                 MOVE 0204     TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC'
                               TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE  (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 0193     TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
                 GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'Y'            TO WS-SPLITPAY-CC-CHG-REQUEST.   

           GO TO 7229-FINISHED-SPLITPAY.

       7207-CANCEL-SPLITPAY.

       COPY SSP09INT.   

           MOVE 'SPLITPAYMENT' TO SSP09-I-CODE. 

           STRING 'OPT' RSP06-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE OF RSP06-NEW 
                        DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO SSP09-I-TYPE.

           CALL 'SSC09GCD' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 SSP09-INPUT-PARAMS 
                                 SSP09-OUTPUT-PARAMS.   

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > 0 AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1
              IF (DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) = 'SSC09GCD' AND 
                     DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1) = 2) OR
                       (DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) = 'SSC10LCD' AND   
                           DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1) = 5) 
                  ADD 1         TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                  MOVE 0206     TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                  GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM 7228-CALC-LAST-INSTALMENT-DATE THRU 7228-EXIT.   

           IF DPP01-SYSTEM-DATE >= WS-LAST-INSTALMENT-DATE  
              ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
              MOVE 0207       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'Y'           TO WS-SPLITPAY-CANCEL-REQUEST 

           GO TO 7150-VALIDATE-PMTS-LOOP.   

       7228-CALC-LAST-INSTALMENT-DATE.  

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW   TO PHR-PRODUCT-NUM
                                                     ARP90-PRODUCT-NUM. 

           PERFORM ARP90-LOAD-CALC-AREA-NAME THRU   
                   ARP90-LOAD-CALC-AREA-NAME-EXIT.  

           FETCH PAYMENT-HEADER-REC RECORD  
                         ON ERROR GO TO 7228-PAYMENT-ERROR. 

           FETCH NEXT PAYMENT-REC WITHIN PMT-SET SET
                              ON ERROR GO TO 7228-ERROR-IN-PAYMENT. 

           MOVE ZEROES                   TO WS-LAST-INSTALMENT-DATE.

           IF PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE = 'T'   
              GO TO 7228-GET-NEXT-INSTALLMENT   
           END-IF.  

           IF (IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE 
                                      = PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE)  
           OR (PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE = 'Z') 
               IF  PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE = IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE 
                   MOVE CURRENT-DBKEY    TO WS-PYDR-DBKEY   
               END-IF   
               MOVE PYDR-PAYMENT-DATE    TO WS-LAST-INSTALMENT-DATE 

           END-IF.  

       7228-GET-NEXT-INSTALLMENT.   

           FETCH NEXT PAYMENT-REC WITHIN PMT-SET SET
                              ON ERROR GO TO 7228-ERROR-IN-PAYMENT. 

           IF PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE = 'T'   
              MOVE ZEROES                TO WS-LAST-INSTALMENT-DATE 
              GO TO 7228-GET-NEXT-INSTALLMENT   
           END-IF.  

           IF (IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE = PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE)
           OR (PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE = 'Z') 

               IF  PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE = IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE 
                   MOVE CURRENT-DBKEY    TO WS-PYDR-DBKEY   
               END-IF   
               IF WS-LAST-INSTALMENT-DATE NOT > 0   
                  MOVE PYDR-PAYMENT-DATE  TO WS-LAST-INSTALMENT-DATE
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 7228-GET-NEXT-INSTALLMENT. 

       7228-ERROR-IN-PAYMENT.   

           IF ERROR-NUM = '0007' OR '0013'  
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE OF RSP06-NEW NOT = 'M' 
              MOVE FUNCTION NUMVAL (SSP09-O-DESCRIPTION)
                                         TO WS-INSTALMENTS-OPTED
              GO TO 7228-CONTINUE   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-FINAL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW   
                                         TO WS-FINAL-PAYMENT-DATE.  

           COMPUTE WS-INSTALMENTS-OPTED = ((WS-FINAL-DATE-YYYY  
                                        - WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-YYYY)* 12)
                                        + WS-FINAL-DATE-MM  
                                        - WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-MM

           IF (WS-FINAL-DATE-DD <= WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-DD)  
                         AND (WS-FINAL-DATE-DD NOT = 29 AND 30 AND 31)  
              SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-INSTALMENTS-OPTED  
                         GIVING WS-INSTALMENTS-OPTED
           END-IF.  

       7228-CONTINUE.   

           COMPUTE WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-MM = WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-MM +
                                            WS-INSTALMENTS-OPTED.   

           IF WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-MM > 12   
              SUBTRACT 12 FROM WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-MM   
                       GIVING  WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-MM   
              ADD 1 TO WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-YYYY 
                       GIVING WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-YYYY  
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-DD = 29 AND 30 AND 31 
              MOVE 28                   TO WS-LAST-INSTALLMENT-DD   
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 7228-EXIT. 

       7228-PAYMENT-ERROR.  

           IF ERROR-NUM NOT = '0007' AND '0013' 
              GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB. 
           MOVE 0194       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.

       7228-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       7229-FINISHED-SPLITPAY.  

           PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  

TOKENX     PERFORM 7900-SET-UP-DATA-FOR-ARO39PAY
              THRU 7900-EXIT.   

           MOVE 'CCVALIDATE'       TO ARP39-I-PROCESS-ID.   

           IF  BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'GC' 
               MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB)  TO ARP39-CARD-TYPE   
               MOVE ZEROES                   TO ARP39-PROCESSING-CODE   
           END-IF.  

           INITIALIZE DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS. 

TOKENX     CALL 'ARO39PAY' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS,  
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO, 
                                 ARP39-CO-TYPES,
                                 ARP39-CO-INPUT-DATA,   
                                 ARP39-CO-OUTPUT-DATA   
                                 P-MCB-PACKET.  

           IF ARP39-OUTPUT-MESSAGE  = 'Acceptable card' 
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              ADD 1                     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              IF ARP39-OUTPUT-MESSAGE  =
                'This card is blocked due to failed AVS'
                 MOVE ARP39-OUTPUT-MESSAGE TO DPP01-USER-MESSAGE
                 MOVE 0179 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              ELSE  
                 MOVE ARP39-OUTPUT-MESSAGE TO DPP01-USER-MESSAGE
                 MOVE 0106 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
              END-IF
              MOVE 2                    TO DPP01-MESSAGE-SW 
              GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           IF BKPUCC-CARD-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB) <= SPACES
              MOVE ARP39-CARD-NUM TO BKPUCC-CARD-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB)   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB) TO
                                   WS-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB).   

           MOVE SPACES             TO ARP38-INPUT-DATA. 
           MOVE ARP39-CARD-TYPE    TO ARP38-CARD-TYPE.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CURRENCY-CODE   OF RSP06-NEW  
                                   TO ARP38-CURRENCY-CODE.  
           MOVE BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB) 
                                   TO ARP38-PAYMENT-AMT.
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-LOCALITY-CODE   OF RSP06-NEW  
                                   TO ARP38-LOCALITY-CODE.  

           IF BKPUCC-CREDIT-DEBIT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'C' OR 'D' 
              MOVE BKPUCC-CREDIT-DEBIT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) 
                             TO ARP38-CREDIT-DEBIT-IND  
           ELSE 
              MOVE ARP39-O-CREDIT-DEBIT   TO ARP38-CREDIT-DEBIT-IND 
           END-IF.  
           MOVE TDP01-FINANCIAL-COMPANY-CODE
                                   TO ARP38-FINANCIAL-COMPANY-CODE  

           PERFORM ARP38-FETCH-LR-REC  THRU ARP38-EXIT. 
           MOVE ARP38-TOT-PAYMENT-AMT   
                                   TO   
                               WS-ARP38-TOT-PAYMENT-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB).   
           MOVE ARP38-CARD-USAGE-FEE-AMT
                                   TO   
                            WS-ARP38-CARD-USAGE-FEE-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB).   
           MOVE ARP38-CC-USAGE-FEE-PCT  
                                   TO   
                              WS-ARP38-CC-USAGE-FEE-PCT (BKPUCC-SUB).   
           MOVE ARP38-DB-USAGE-FEE-PCT  
                                   TO   
                              WS-ARP38-DB-USAGE-FEE-PCT (BKPUCC-SUB).   
           MOVE ARP38-DEBIT-CREDIT-IND  
                                   TO   
                              WS-ARP38-DEBIT-CREDIT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB).   

           IF ARP38-DEBIT-CREDIT-IND > SPACES   
             IF ARP26-CREDIT-AMT-PCT-CODE(WS-PT-SUB) = 'P'  
                COMPUTE CCP00-CC-USAGE-FEE  ROUNDED =   
                            ARP38-PAYMENT-AMT * ARP38-CC-USAGE-FEE-PCT  
             ELSE   
                MOVE ARP38-CC-USAGE-FEE-PCT TO CCP00-CC-USAGE-FEE   
             END-IF 
             IF ARP26-DEBIT-AMT-PCT-CODE(WS-PT-SUB) = 'P'   
                COMPUTE CCP00-DB-USAGE-FEE  ROUNDED =   
                            ARP38-PAYMENT-AMT * ARP38-DB-USAGE-FEE-PCT  
             ELSE   
               MOVE ARP38-DB-USAGE-FEE-PCT TO CCP00-DB-USAGE-FEE
            END-IF  
           END-IF.  

132689     MOVE BKPUCC-CURRENCY-CODE (BKPUCC-SUB)   
132689                             TO CCP00-CURR-CODE.  

           IF  SSP03-O-FORCE-PMT-FEE-ACPT-FLG = 1   
               IF  (BKPUCC-SERVICE-FEE-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB) NOT =   
                    ARP38-CARD-USAGE-FEE-AMT)   
                    ADD 1               TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                    MOVE 'CCR08UGC'     TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE 
                                                     (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                    MOVE 0145           TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   
                                                     (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                    GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR
               END-IF   
           ELSE 
               IF RSP86-O-CARD-USAGE-FEE-IND = 1
                   ADD 1                    TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                   MOVE 'CCR08UGC'          TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE 
                                                     (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                   MOVE 0146                TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   
                                                     (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE ARP27-PYMT-PMT-TYPE TO  WS-PAYMENT-TYPE.

           MOVE ZEROES                  TO WS-LRSUB.
           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB3 FROM 1 BY 1  
               UNTIL WS-SUB3 > RSP86-O-PAYMENT-TYPE-CNT 
               IF  RSP86-O-PAYMENT-TYPE (WS-SUB3)   
                   = WS-PAYMENT-TYPE
                   MOVE WS-SUB3         TO WS-LRSUB 
                   MOVE RSP86-O-PAYMENT-TYPE-CNT
                                        TO WS-SUB3  
               END-IF   
           END-PERFORM. 
           IF  WS-LRSUB = 0 
               ADD 1                    TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
               MOVE 0141                TO  
                                       MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
               MOVE 'CCR08UGC'          TO  
                                     MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
               PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG THRU   
                       CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT   
               GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
           END-IF.  

           MOVE ZEROES                  TO WS-LRSUB.

           IF ARP39-CARD-TYPE = 'GC'
                             OR 'PN'
                             OR 'PP'
LOYLTY                       OR 'LP'
              GO TO 7150-VALIDATE-PMTS-LOOP 
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB3 FROM 1 BY 1  
               UNTIL WS-SUB3 > RSP86-O-CARD-TYPE-CNT
               IF  RSP86-O-CARD-TYPE (WS-SUB3) =
                   ARP39-CARD-TYPE  
                   MOVE WS-SUB3         TO WS-LRSUB 
                   MOVE RSP86-O-CARD-TYPE-CNT   
                                        TO WS-SUB3  
               END-IF   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF  WS-LRSUB = 0 
               ADD 1                    TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
               MOVE 0140                TO  
                                       MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
               MOVE 'CCR08UGC'          TO  
                                     MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
               PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG THRU   
                       CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT   
               GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 7150-VALIDATE-PMTS-LOOP.   

       4735-MODIFY-PYDR.
               MOVE IBR-BOOK-NUM              TO PHR-PRODUCT-NUM
                                                 ARP90-PRODUCT-NUM. 
               PERFORM ARP90-LOAD-CALC-AREA-NAME THRU   
                       ARP90-LOAD-CALC-AREA-NAME-EXIT.  
              FIND PAYMENT-HEADER-REC RECORD
                  ON ERROR GO TO 5800-CHK-DEPOSIT.  
              MOVE BKPUCC-PMT-REF-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB) TO PYDR-PMT-REF-NUM. 
              MOVE 'A'               TO PYDR-CARD-APPROVAL-DENIAL-CODE. 
              FETCH PAYMENT-REC VIA PMT-SET 
                 USING PYDR-PMT-REF-NUM 
                       PYDR-CARD-APPROVAL-DENIAL-CODE.  

              MOVE 'A' TO PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE.
              MODIFY PAYMENT-REC RECORD.

           IF  TDP01-SHIP-CODE = RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW   
               AND TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD =   
                                 RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW   
               AND TDP01-DURATION-DAYS =
                                 RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW   
               CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW  
                                          TO TDP01-I-SHIP-CODE  
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW  
                                          TO TDP01-I-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD 
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW  
                                          TO TDP01-I-DUR-DAYS   
              PERFORM TDP01-RET-SAIL-DETAILS THRU   
                      TDP01-RET-SAIL-DETAILS-EXIT   
              IF TDP01-SAIL-SQLERR  
                 GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
              END-IF
              IF TDP01-SAIL-NOTFND OR   
                 TDP01-PROC-ERROR > ZEROES  
                 GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF TDP01-SCAT-SAIL-ID = TDP01-SAIL-ID AND
              TDP01-SAIL-CAT-CNT > 1
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              PERFORM TDP01-RET-ALL-SCAT THRU   
                      TDP01-RET-ALL-SCAT-EXIT   
              IF TDP01-SCAT-SQLERR  
                 GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  
           MOVE 'A' TO IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE 
                          RSP06-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE OF RSP06-NEW.  
           MOVE DPP02-SYSTEM-DATETIME  TO IBR-UPDATE-DATETIME.  
           IF IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE > SPACES 
              MOVE 0                   TO WS-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE-NI
           ELSE 
              MOVE -1                  TO WS-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE-NI
           END-IF.  
              FIND CURRENT INDV-BOOKING-REC RECORD  
                            ON ERROR GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR. 
              MODIFY INDV-BOOKING-REC RECORD.   

           EXEC SQL UPDATE CABIN_BOOK   
                SET (UPDATE_DATETIME = :WS-UPDATE-DATETIME, 
                     AUTO_PAY_IND    = :IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE
                                       :WS-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE-NI) 
              WHERE BOOK_NUM = :IBR-BOOK-NUM  AND   
                    SHIP_CODE = :IBR-SHIP-CODE AND  
                   (UPDATE_DATETIME <> :WS-UPDATE-DATETIME      OR  
                   ((:WS-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE-NI = -1       AND 
                   AUTO_PAY_IND IS NOT NULL)                    OR  
                   (:WS-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE-NI = 0         AND 
                   AUTO_PAY_IND IS NULL)                        OR  
                   AUTO_PAY_IND <> :IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE))  
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM DPP16-MODIFY-SAIL-INDIVIDUAL THRU DPP16-EXIT.

           GO TO 7570-VERIFY-PAYMENT.   

       7240-VALIDATE-MIN-PAY-POL.   
           MOVE 0                      TO WS-MIN-POL-AMT
                                          WS-PAY-AMT.   
           MOVE SPACES                 TO WS-MIN-PMT-AMT.   
           MOVE 'N'                    TO WS-PAY-ERR-SWITCH.

       COPY SSP09INT.   
           MOVE 'PMNT'                 TO SSP09-I-TYPE. 
           MOVE 'P'                    TO SSP09-I-CODE. 
           CALL 'SSC09GCD' USING          DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS  
                                          DPP01-COMMON-INFO 
                                          SSP09-INPUT-PARAMS
                                          SSP09-OUTPUT-PARAMS.  

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > 0 AND 
                 DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1   
                    GO TO 9500-RETURN-SUBROUTINE-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE SSP09-O-DESCRIPTION              TO WS-MIN-PMT-AMT. 
           MOVE FUNCTION NUMVAL (WS-MIN-PMT-AMT) TO WS-MIN-POL-AMT. 
           PERFORM VARYING WS-NUM-CARD FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL
              WS-NUM-CARD > BKPUCC-SEGMENT-MAX OR   
              BKPUCC-SEGMENT (WS-NUM-CARD) NOT > SPACES 
              ADD BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (WS-NUM-CARD)  
                                                 TO WS-PAY-AMT  
              IF  BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (WS-NUM-CARD) <  WS-MIN-POL-AMT
                  MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PAY-ERR-SWITCH 
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-DEPOSIT-AMT > ZEROES   
              IF ( (WS-PAY-AMT < WS-DEPOSIT-AMT) AND
                   (WS-PAY-ERR-SWITCH = 'Y') )  
                     GO TO 7250-CC-MIN-PAY-VIOLATION
              ELSE  
                     GO TO 7240-VALIDATE-MIN-PAY-POL-EXIT   
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              IF ( (WS-PAY-AMT < WS-REM-AMT) AND
                    (WS-PAY-ERR-SWITCH = 'Y') ) 
                       GO TO 7250-CC-MIN-PAY-VIOLATION  
              ELSE  
                       GO TO 7240-VALIDATE-MIN-PAY-POL-EXIT 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

       7240-VALIDATE-MIN-PAY-POL-EXIT.  
           EXIT.


       7230-CC-PAYMENT-EXCEEDED.

           ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB.
           MOVE 0059    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB).   
           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.

       7250-CC-MIN-PAY-VIOLATION.   
           ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB.
153688     IF IBR-CURRENCY-CODE = 'EUR' 
153688        MOVE 4011 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
153688     ELSE 
153688        IF IBR-CURRENCY-CODE = 'GBP'  
153688           MOVE 4012 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
153688        ELSE  
153688           MOVE 0088 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
153688        END-IF
153688     END-IF.  
153688*    MOVE 0088    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB).   
           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.

       7270-INVALID-CREDIT-CARD.

           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.

       7300-PROCESS-PMTS.   
           MOVE ZERO TO BKPUCC-SUB. 

       7350-PROCESS-CREDIT-CARDS.   

           ADD 1 TO BKPUCC-SUB. 

           IF BKPUCC-SUB > BKPUCC-SEGMENT-MAX   
              GO TO 7550-NO-MORE-PAYMENTS.  

           IF BKPUCC-SEGMENT (BKPUCC-SUB) NOT > SPACES  
              GO TO 7550-NO-MORE-PAYMENTS.  

           IF BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'GC'  
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-GIFT-CARD-IND  
              GO TO 7350-PROCESS-CREDIT-CARDS   
           ELSE 
               MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CC-PAYMENT-IND
           END-IF.  

           IF  BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'N'   
               GO TO 7350-PROCESS-CREDIT-CARDS. 

           IF BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'C'
              GO TO 7370-CANCEL-SPLITPAY
           END-IF.  

           IF  SSP03-O-GIFT-CARD-FLG = 1
               ADD 1        TO CCP78-CC-SUB 
               IF BKPUCC-TOKEN-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB) > SPACES
                  MOVE BKPUCC-TOKEN-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB)
                            TO VLUCC2-CARD-NUM            (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               ELSE 
               MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB)
                            TO VLUCC2-CARD-NUM            (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               END-IF   
               MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB)   
                            TO VLUCC2-CARD-TYPE           (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               MOVE WS-CURR-PROD-CURRENCY-CODE  
                            TO VLUCC2-CURRENCY-CODE       (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               MOVE BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB) 
                            TO VLUCC2-UNALLOC-UCC-PMT-AMT (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               MOVE ZEROES  TO VLUCC2-APPROVED-AMOUNT     (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               MOVE SPACES  TO VLUCC2-MP-APPROAVL-CODE    (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               IF BKPUCC-TRANSACTION-ID (BKPUCC-SUB) > SPACES   
                  MOVE 'PN' TO VLUCC2-CARD-TYPE           (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               END-IF   
               MOVE CCP78-CC-SUB
                            TO VLADV2-OWNER-SUB           (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               MOVE SPACES  TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS     (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                               VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE       (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                               VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT       (CCP78-CC-SUB)
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'N'    TO WS-APPROVED-MESSAGE   
                          WS-DENIED-MESSAGE 
                          WS-RESPONSE-MESSAGE.  

           MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB) TO WS-CARD-NUM.

           MOVE SPACES TO WS-STRING-AREA.   
           MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-HOLDER-NAME (BKPUCC-SUB) TO WS-STRING-AREA. 
           MOVE 38 TO WS-STRING-LENGTH. 
           PERFORM 8800-CONVERT-CASE THRU 8800-EXIT.
           MOVE WS-STRING-AREA TO BKPUCC-CARD-HOLDER-NAME (BKPUCC-SUB). 

           MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB)   
                            TO WS-HIST-COMMENT-CARD-TYPE.   

           IF BKPUCC-TRANSACTION-ID (BKPUCC-SUB) > SPACES   
              MOVE 'PN'     TO WS-HIST-COMMENT-CARD-TYPE.   

           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'G'
              PERFORM 7700-SET-UP-GRP-HIST     THRU 7700-EXIT   
           ELSE 
              PERFORM 7800-SET-UP-INDV-HISTORY THRU 7800-EXIT   
           END-IF.  

       7360-SPLITPAY-REQ-HISTORY.   


           IF (WS-SPLITPAY-SCHD-REQUEST = 'Y') AND  
                     (BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) > SPACES)
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 


              GO TO 7450-AFTER-SPLITPAY 
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM 7850-BUILD-HISTORY   
              THRU 7850-BUILD-HISTORY-EXIT. 

           MOVE 'RSP'                   TO DPP04-HIST-ACTIVITY-TYPE.

           MOVE SPACES                  TO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT.  
           STRING 'Easy Pay Request-' WS-INSTALMENTS-OPTED  
                   DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO  DPP04-HIST-COMMENT.  

           MOVE DPP04-HIST-COMMENT      TO DPP04-TRF-COMMENT1.  

           PERFORM IRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY 
              THRU IRP15-TRF-EXIT.  

           GO TO 7450-AFTER-SPLITPAY.   

       7370-CANCEL-SPLITPAY.

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO PHR-PRODUCT-NUM  
                                                   ARP90-PRODUCT-NUM.   

           PERFORM ARP90-LOAD-CALC-AREA-NAME THRU   
                   ARP90-LOAD-CALC-AREA-NAME-EXIT.  

           FETCH PAYMENT-HEADER-REC RECORD  
                         ON ERROR GO TO 7380-PAYMENT-ERROR. 

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE OF RSP06-NEW 
                                           TO PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE.

           FETCH PAYMENT-REC VIA PMT-SET
                         USING PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE
                         ON ERROR GO TO 7380-PAYMENT-ERROR. 

           MOVE 'T'                  TO PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE.  

           MODIFY PAYMENT-REC RECORD.   

           GO TO 7390-UPDATE-IBR.   

       7380-PAYMENT-ERROR.  

           IF ERROR-NUM NOT = '0007' AND '0013' 
              GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB. 
           MOVE 0197       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.

       7390-UPDATE-IBR. 

           IF (RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW = TDP01-SHIP-CODE) AND  
              (RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW =   
                                         TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD) AND  
              (RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                = TDP01-DURATION-DAYS)  
               GO TO 7400-UPDATE-IBR-CONT   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW
             TO TDP01-I-SHIP-CODE.  

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW
             TO TDP01-I-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD. 

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW
             TO TDP01-I-DUR-DAYS.   

           PERFORM TDP01-RET-SAIL-DETAILS THRU  
                   TDP01-RET-SAIL-DETAILS-EXIT. 

           IF TDP01-SAIL-SQLERR 
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF TDP01-SAIL-NOTFND OR  
              TDP01-PROC-ERROR > ZEROES 
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF TDP01-SCAT-SAIL-ID = TDP01-SAIL-ID AND
              TDP01-SAIL-CAT-CNT > 1
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              PERFORM TDP01-RET-ALL-SCAT THRU   
                      TDP01-RET-ALL-SCAT-EXIT   
              IF TDP01-SCAT-SQLERR  
                 GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

       7400-UPDATE-IBR-CONT.

           MOVE 'C'      TO IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE.   

           MOVE DPP02-SYSTEM-DATETIME  TO IBR-UPDATE-DATETIME.  

           IF IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE > SPACES 
              MOVE 0                   TO WS-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE-NI
           ELSE 
              MOVE -1                  TO WS-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE-NI
           END-IF.  

           FIND CURRENT INDV-BOOKING-REC RECORD.

           MODIFY INDV-BOOKING-REC RECORD.  

           EXEC SQL UPDATE CABIN_BOOK   
                SET (UPDATE_DATETIME = :WS-UPDATE-DATETIME, 
                     AUTO_PAY_IND    = :IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE
                                       :WS-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE-NI) 
               WHERE BOOK_NUM = :IBR-BOOK-NUM  AND  
                     SHIP_CODE = :IBR-SHIP-CODE AND 
                    (UPDATE_DATETIME <> :WS-UPDATE-DATETIME      OR 
                    ((:WS-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE-NI = -1       AND
                    AUTO_PAY_IND IS NOT NULL)                    OR 
                    (:WS-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE-NI = 0         AND
                    AUTO_PAY_IND IS NULL)                        OR 
                    AUTO_PAY_IND <> :IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE)) 
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM DPP16-MODIFY-SAIL-INDIVIDUAL THRU DPP16-EXIT.

       7410-SPLITPAY-CANCEL-HISTORY.

           PERFORM 7850-BUILD-HISTORY   
              THRU 7850-BUILD-HISTORY-EXIT. 

           MOVE 'SPD'                 TO DPP04-HIST-ACTIVITY-TYPE.  
           MOVE 'Cancel Easy Pay Plan'  
                                      TO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT 
                                         DPP04-TRF-COMMENT1.

           PERFORM IRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY 
              THRU IRP15-TRF-EXIT.  

           GO TO 7350-PROCESS-CREDIT-CARDS. 

       7450-AFTER-SPLITPAY. 

           INITIALIZE DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS. 

           MOVE WS-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB)   
                                            TO ARP27-CARD-TYPE. 
           PERFORM ARP27-FETCH-CARD-TYPE-REC THRU ARP27-EXIT.   

TOKENX     PERFORM 7900-SET-UP-DATA-FOR-ARO39PAY THRU 7900-EXIT.

           MOVE ZEROES                           TO WS1-EXP-YYMM.   
           IF  BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'Y'   
               IF BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'CC'  
                  MOVE WS-EXP-DATE-YY           TO WS1-EXP-YY   
                  MOVE WS-EXP-DATE-MM           TO WS1-EXP-MM   
                  IF  (WS1-EXP-YYMM < IBR-FINAL-DATE(3:4))  
                       MOVE 'CARD EXPIRY DATE CAN NOT BE LESS THAN FINAL'   
      -                     'PAYMENT DATE'       TO DPP01-USER-MESSAGE  
                       ADD 1                     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB
                       MOVE 0147                 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE  
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                       GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
                  END-IF
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE WS-ARP38-CARD-USAGE-FEE-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB)
                                      TO ARP39-CARD-USAGE-FEE-AMT.  
           MOVE WS-ARP38-DEBIT-CREDIT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB)  
                                      TO ARP39-DEBIT-CREDIT-IND.
           IF  WS-ARP38-DEBIT-CREDIT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'D' 
               MOVE WS-ARP38-DB-USAGE-FEE-PCT (BKPUCC-SUB)  
                                      TO ARP39-SCH-CARD-USAGE-FEE-PCT   
           ELSE 
               MOVE WS-ARP38-CC-USAGE-FEE-PCT (BKPUCC-SUB)  
                                      TO ARP39-SCH-CARD-USAGE-FEE-PCT   
           END-IF.  
           MOVE WS-ARP38-TOT-PAYMENT-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB)   
                TO ARP39-PAYMENT-AMT.   

PCI3  * Call token update subroutine to update the expiration date in   
PCI3  * the MPGS vault, otherwise token delegate calls might fail during
PCI3  * authorization with Worldpay. Only for previously used cards.
PCI3  * If it's a TokenX token no need to do token update call as we'll 
PCI3  * not be doing a token delegate call later.   
PCI3  *    IF ARP39-CARD-TYPE = 'GC' OR 'PP'
PCI3  *     IF ( ARP39-CARD-TYPE = 'GC' OR 'PP' ) OR
PCI3  *        (ARP39-TOKEN-NUM (1:1) = 'T')
PCI3  *        CONTINUE 
PCI3  *     ELSE
PCI3  *        IF BKPUCC-PMT-REF-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB) > SPACES  
PCI3  *           PERFORM CALL-TKUPD-SUB THRU CALL-TKUPD-EXIT   
PCI3  *        END-IF   
PCI3  *     END-IF. 

           MOVE 'CCPASSOFF'          TO ARP39-I-PROCESS-ID. 

TOKENX     CALL 'ARO39PAY' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS,  
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO, 
                                 ARP39-CO-TYPES,
                                 ARP39-CO-INPUT-DATA,   
                                 ARP39-CO-OUTPUT-DATA   
                                 P-MCB-PACKET.  

           IF  ARP39-OUTPUT-MESSAGE =   
                           ' PAYMENT HAS BEEN SUBMITTED FOR APPROVAL'   
               CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
               MOVE 2              TO DPP01-MESSAGE-SW  
               MOVE ARP39-OUTPUT-MESSAGE TO DPP01-USER-MESSAGE  
               ADD 1               TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
               MOVE 0106           TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
               MOVE 'CCR08UGC'    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
               PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG
                                THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT 
               MOVE 'Y'            TO WS-DENIED-MESSAGE 
               GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW TO ARP95-I-BOOK-TYPE.  
           PERFORM ARP26-FETCH-PMT-CTL   THRU ARP26-FETCH-PMT-CTL-EXIT. 
           MOVE ARP26-VISA-WAIT                  TO 
                                             ARP95-USCR-VISANET-WAIT-MS.
           MOVE ARP26-VISA-INI-WAIT              TO 
                                          ARP95-VISANET-INITIAL-WAIT-MS.
           MOVE ARP26-VISA-MAX-WAIT              TO 
                                           ARP95-VISANET-MAX-WAIT-MS
           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'I'
              MOVE ARP39-PYDR-DBK                TO ARP95-I-DBKEY   
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM  OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                 TO ARP95-I-BOOK-NUM
           ELSE 
              MOVE ARP39-UUPR-DBK                TO ARP95-I-DBKEY   
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-GROUP-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                                                 TO ARP95-I-BOOK-NUM
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM ARP95-VERIFY-VISANET-STATUS   THRU ARP95-EXIT.   
           IF ARP95-O-PROC-RETURN-CODE < 0  
               MOVE 'N'                 TO WS-ALL-CARD-DENIED-IND   
               IF WS-RESPONSE-MESSAGE NOT = 'Y' 
                  IF SSP03-O-GIFT-CARD-FLG = 1  
                     IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IC' or 'IM'
                        MOVE 0063       TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE
                        MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO CCP00-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                        PERFORM CCP10-GET-ADVISORY  
                           THRU CCP10-ADV-EXIT  
                        MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE  
                                 TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE (CCP78-CC-SUB) 
                        MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-TEXT  
                                 TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT (CCP78-CC-SUB) 
                        MOVE 'P' TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS  
                                                         (CCP78-CC-SUB) 
                        MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-RESPONSE-MESSAGE  
                     ELSE   
                        MOVE 0111       TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE
                        MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO CCP00-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                        PERFORM CCP10-GET-ADVISORY  
                           THRU CCP10-ADV-EXIT  
                        MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE  
                                 TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE (CCP78-CC-SUB) 
                        MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-TEXT  
                                 TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT (CCP78-CC-SUB) 
                        MOVE 'P' TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS  
                                                         (CCP78-CC-SUB) 
                        MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-RESPONSE-MESSAGE  
                     END-IF 
                  ELSE  
                     IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IC' OR 'IM'
                        ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                        MOVE 0063       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                        MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE 
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                        MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-RESPONSE-MESSAGE  
                     ELSE   
                        ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                        MOVE 0111       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                        MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE 
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                        MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-RESPONSE-MESSAGE  
                     END-IF 
                  END-IF
               END-IF   
              IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'I' 
                 MOVE ARP39-PTRR-DBK             TO ARP95-I-DBKEY   
              ELSE  
                 MOVE ARP39-UUPR-DBK             TO ARP95-I-DBKEY   
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              IF ARP95-O-VISANET-APPROVED   
                 MOVE 'N' TO WS-ALL-CARD-DENIED-IND 
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-UCC-APPROVED-IND
                  IF WS-APPROVED-MESSAGE NOT = 'Y'  
                     IF SSP03-O-GIFT-CARD-FLG = 1   
                        IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'I'   
                           MOVE PYDR-PAYMENT-AMT
                                           TO   
                                   VLUCC2-APPROVED-AMOUNT (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           IF  BKPUCC-DIGITAL-WALLET-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 
                               'Y'  
                               MOVE ARP95-O-VISANET-APPROVAL-CODE   
                                           TO   
                               VLUCC2-MP-APPROAVL-CODE (CCP78-CC-SUB)   
                           END-IF   
                        ELSE
                           MOVE UUPR-AMT   TO   
                                   VLUCC2-APPROVED-AMOUNT (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                        END-IF  
                        MOVE ARP95-VAULT-TOKEN-ID TO
                                  VLUCC2-VAULT-TOKEN-ID (CCP78-CC-SUB)  
                        IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IC' OR 'IM' 
                           MOVE 0062       TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE 
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO CCP00-ADVISE-SOURCE   
                           PERFORM CCP10-GET-ADVISORY   
                              THRU CCP10-ADV-EXIT   
                           MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE   
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE   (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-TEXT   
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT   (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE 'A' 
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-APPROVED-MESSAGE   
                        ELSE
                           MOVE 0112       TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE 
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO CCP00-ADVISE-SOURCE   
                           PERFORM CCP10-GET-ADVISORY   
                              THRU CCP10-ADV-EXIT   
                           MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE   
                               TO  VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE   (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-TEXT   
                               TO  VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT   (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE 'A' 
                               TO  VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-APPROVED-MESSAGE   
                        END-IF  
                     ELSE   
                        IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IC' OR 'IM' 
                           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                           MOVE 0062       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-APPROVED-MESSAGE   
                        ELSE
                           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                           MOVE 0112       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-APPROVED-MESSAGE   
                        END-IF  
                     END-IF 
                  END-IF
              ELSE  
                  IF WS-DENIED-MESSAGE NOT = 'Y'
                     IF SSP03-O-GIFT-CARD-FLG = 1   
                        IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IC' OR 'IM' 
                           IF PYDR-PAYMENT-TYPE = 'PBP' AND 
                              PYDR-CARD-DENIAL-CODE(1:4) = '0055'   
                                 MOVE 0152       TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE   
                                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO CCP00-ADVISE-SOURCE 
                                 PERFORM CCP10-GET-ADVISORY 
                                         THRU CCP10-ADV-EXIT
                                 MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE TO  
                                    VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE (CCP78-CC-SUB) 
                                 MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-TEXT TO  
                                     VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                                 MOVE 'D' TO
                                   VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                                 MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-DENIED-MESSAGE   
                           ELSE 
                             IF PYDR-PAYMENT-TYPE = 'UCC' AND   
                                PYDR-CARD-DENIAL-CODE (1:4) = '0204'
                                MOVE 0153       TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE
                                MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO CCP00-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                                PERFORM CCP10-GET-ADVISORY  
                                        THRU CCP10-ADV-EXIT 
                                MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE TO   
                                   VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE (CCP78-CC-SUB)  
                                MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-TEXT TO   
                                    VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT (CCP78-CC-SUB) 
                                MOVE 'D' TO 
                                  VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS (CCP78-CC-SUB) 
                                MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-DENIED-MESSAGE
                             ELSE   
                                 MOVE 0106       TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE   
                                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO CCP00-ADVISE-SOURCE 
                                 PERFORM CCP10-GET-ADVISORY 
                                    THRU CCP10-ADV-EXIT 
                                 MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE 
                                  TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                                 MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-TEXT 
                                  TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                                 MOVE 'D'   
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                                 MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-DENIED-MESSAGE   
                           END-IF   
                        ELSE
                           MOVE 0113       TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE 
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO CCP00-ADVISE-SOURCE   
                           PERFORM CCP10-GET-ADVISORY   
                              THRU CCP10-ADV-EXIT   
                           MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE   
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE   (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-TEXT   
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT   (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE 'D' 
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-DENIED-MESSAGE 
                        END-IF  
                     ELSE   
                        IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IC' OR 'IM' 
                           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                           MOVE 0106       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-DENIED-MESSAGE 
                        ELSE
                           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                           MOVE 0113       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-DENIED-MESSAGE 
                        END-IF  
                     END-IF
