                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

       7460-SPLITPAY-CHANGE-REQUEST.

           IF (WS-SPLITPAY-CC-CHG-REQUEST = 'Y') AND
                     (BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'X' )  
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 7350-PROCESS-CREDIT-CARDS   
           END-IF.  

           IF ARP95-O-VISANET-APPROVED  
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 7350-PROCESS-CREDIT-CARDS   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE WS-PYDR-DBKEY            TO GENERIC-DBK.

           FETCH PAYMENT-REC RECORD GENERIC-DBK 
                             ON ERROR GO TO 7470-PAYMENT-ERROR. 

           MOVE 'Z'                      TO PYDR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE.  

           MODIFY PAYMENT-REC RECORD.   

           GO TO 7480-SPLITPAY-CC-CHG-HISTORY.  

       7470-PAYMENT-ERROR.  

           IF ERROR-NUM NOT = '0007' AND '0013' 
              GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB. 
           MOVE 0199       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.

       7480-SPLITPAY-CC-CHG-HISTORY.

           PERFORM 7850-BUILD-HISTORY   
              THRU 7850-BUILD-HISTORY-EXIT. 

           MOVE 'CCU'                   TO DPP04-HIST-ACTIVITY-TYPE.

           MOVE 'Easy Pay-Credit Crd Updt'  
                                        TO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT   
                                           DPP04-TRF-COMMENT1.  

           PERFORM IRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY 
              THRU IRP15-TRF-EXIT.  

           GO TO 7350-PROCESS-CREDIT-CARDS. 

       7550-NO-MORE-PAYMENTS.   

           IF  GIFT-CARD-EXIST AND  
               (UCC-APPROVED OR NO-CC-PAYMENT-EXIST)
               CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
               GO TO 7570-VERIFY-PAYMENT
           END-IF.  

           MOVE ZERO TO BKPUCC-SUB. 

       7560-PROCESS-GIFT-CARDS. 

           ADD 1 TO BKPUCC-SUB. 

           IF  BKPUCC-SUB > BKPUCC-SEGMENT-MAX  
               GO TO 7570-VERIFY-PAYMENT
           END-IF.  

           IF  BKPUCC-SEGMENT (BKPUCC-SUB) NOT > SPACES 
               GO TO 7570-VERIFY-PAYMENT
           END-IF.  

           IF BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB) NOT = 'GC'  
              GO TO 7560-PROCESS-GIFT-CARDS 
           END-IF.  

           IF  BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'N'   
               GO TO 7560-PROCESS-GIFT-CARDS
           END-IF.  

           IF  SSP03-O-GIFT-CARD-FLG = 1
               ADD 1        TO CCP78-CC-SUB 
               IF BKPUCC-TOKEN-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB) > SPACES
                  MOVE BKPUCC-TOKEN-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB)
                            TO VLUCC2-CARD-NUM            (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               ELSE 
               MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB)
                            TO VLUCC2-CARD-NUM            (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               END-IF   
               MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB)   
                            TO VLUCC2-CARD-TYPE           (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               MOVE WS-CURR-PROD-CURRENCY-CODE  
                            TO VLUCC2-CURRENCY-CODE       (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               MOVE BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB) 
                            TO VLUCC2-UNALLOC-UCC-PMT-AMT (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               MOVE ZEROES  TO VLUCC2-APPROVED-AMOUNT     (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               MOVE CCP78-CC-SUB
                            TO VLADV2-OWNER-SUB           (CCP78-CC-SUB)
               MOVE SPACES  TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS     (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                               VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE       (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                               VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT       (CCP78-CC-SUB)
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'N' TO WS-APPROVED-MESSAGE  
                       WS-DENIED-MESSAGE
                       WS-RESPONSE-MESSAGE. 

           MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB) TO WS-CARD-NUM.

           IF  BKPUCC-CARD-HOLDER-NAME (BKPUCC-SUB) = SPACES     OR 
                                                      LOW-VALUES
               MOVE 'GIFT CARD' TO BKPUCC-CARD-HOLDER-NAME (BKPUCC-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB)   
                                 TO WS-HIST-COMMENT-CARD-TYPE.  

           MOVE BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB) TO ARP38-TOT-PAYMENT-AMT
           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'G'
              PERFORM 7700-SET-UP-GRP-HIST     THRU 7700-EXIT   
           ELSE 
              PERFORM 7800-SET-UP-INDV-HISTORY THRU 7800-EXIT   
           END-IF.  

           INITIALIZE DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS. 

           MOVE WS-CARD-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB)    TO ARP27-CARD-TYPE.
           PERFORM ARP27-FETCH-CARD-TYPE-REC THRU ARP27-EXIT.   

TOKENX     PERFORM 7900-SET-UP-DATA-FOR-ARO39PAY THRU 7900-EXIT.

           MOVE 'GC'                 TO ARP39-CARD-TYPE.
           MOVE ZEROES               TO ARP39-PROCESSING-CODE.  

           IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IC' OR 'IM'  
              MOVE 3    TO ARP39-CHANNEL-NUMBER 
           ELSE 
              IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IA'   
                 MOVE 4 TO ARP39-CHANNEL-NUMBER 
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 9 TO ARP39-CHANNEL-NUMBER 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'CCPASSOFF'          TO ARP39-I-PROCESS-ID. 

TOKENX     CALL 'ARO39PAY' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS,  
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO, 
                                 ARP39-CO-TYPES,
                                 ARP39-CO-INPUT-DATA,   
                                 ARP39-CO-OUTPUT-DATA   
                                 P-MCB-PACKET.  

           IF  ARP39-OUTPUT-MESSAGE =   
                           ' Redemption has been Submitted for Approval'
               CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
               MOVE 2             TO DPP01-MESSAGE-SW   
               MOVE ARP39-OUTPUT-MESSAGE TO DPP01-USER-MESSAGE  
               ADD 1              TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
               MOVE 0106          TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
               MOVE 'CCR08UGC'    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
               PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG
                                THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT 
               MOVE 'Y'           TO WS-DENIED-MESSAGE  
               GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW TO ARP95-I-BOOK-TYPE.  
           PERFORM ARP26-FETCH-PMT-CTL   THRU ARP26-FETCH-PMT-CTL-EXIT. 
           MOVE ARP26-VISA-WAIT                  TO 
                                             ARP95-USCR-VISANET-WAIT-MS.
           MOVE ARP26-VISA-INI-WAIT              TO 
                                         ARP95-VISANET-INITIAL-WAIT-MS. 
           MOVE  ARP26-VISA-MAX-WAIT             TO 
                                          ARP95-VISANET-MAX-WAIT-MS.
           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'I'
              MOVE ARP39-PYDR-DBK                TO ARP95-I-DBKEY   
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM  OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                 TO ARP95-I-BOOK-NUM
           ELSE 
              MOVE ARP39-UUPR-DBK                TO ARP95-I-DBKEY   
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-GROUP-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                                                 TO ARP95-I-BOOK-NUM
           END-IF.  
           PERFORM ARP95-VERIFY-VISANET-STATUS   THRU ARP95-EXIT.   

           IF ARP95-O-PROC-RETURN-CODE < 0  
              MOVE 'N'                  TO WS-ALL-CARD-DENIED-IND   
              IF WS-RESPONSE-MESSAGE NOT = 'Y'  
                  IF SSP03-O-GIFT-CARD-FLG = 1  
                     IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IC' OR 'IM'
                        MOVE 0063       TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE
                        MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO CCP00-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                        PERFORM CCP10-GET-ADVISORY  
                           THRU CCP10-ADV-EXIT  
                        MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE  
                                 TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE  (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                        MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-TEXT  
                                 TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT  (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                        MOVE 'P' TO 
                                   VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                        MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-RESPONSE-MESSAGE  
                     ELSE   
                        MOVE 0111       TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE
                        MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO CCP00-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                        PERFORM CCP10-GET-ADVISORY  
                           THRU CCP10-ADV-EXIT  
                        MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE  
                                 TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE (CCP78-CC-SUB) 
                        MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-TEXT  
                                 TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT (CCP78-CC-SUB) 
                        MOVE 'P' TO 
                                  VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS (CCP78-CC-SUB) 
                        MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-RESPONSE-MESSAGE  
                     END-IF 
                  ELSE  
                     IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IC' OR 'IM'
                        ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                        MOVE 0063       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                        MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE 
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                        MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-RESPONSE-MESSAGE  
                     ELSE   
                        ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                        MOVE 0111       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                        MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE 
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                        MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-RESPONSE-MESSAGE  
                     END-IF 
                  END-IF
              END-IF
              IF  RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'I'
                  MOVE ARP39-PTRR-DBK             TO ARP95-I-DBKEY  
              ELSE  
                  MOVE ARP39-UUPR-DBK             TO ARP95-I-DBKEY  
              END-IF
           ELSE 
             IF ARP95-O-VISANET-APPROVED
                 MOVE 'N'                  TO WS-ALL-CARD-DENIED-IND
                 IF WS-APPROVED-MESSAGE NOT = 'Y'   
                     IF SSP03-O-GIFT-CARD-FLG = 1   
                        IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'I'   
                           MOVE PYDR-PAYMENT-AMT
                                           TO   
                                   VLUCC2-APPROVED-AMOUNT (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                        ELSE
                           MOVE UUPR-AMT   TO   
                                   VLUCC2-APPROVED-AMOUNT (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                        END-IF  
                        IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IC' OR 'IM' 
                           MOVE 0062       TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE 
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO CCP00-ADVISE-SOURCE   
                           PERFORM CCP10-GET-ADVISORY   
                              THRU CCP10-ADV-EXIT   
                           MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE   
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE   (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-TEXT   
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT   (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE 'A' 
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-APPROVED-MESSAGE   
                        ELSE
                           MOVE 0112       TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE 
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO CCP00-ADVISE-SOURCE   
                           PERFORM CCP10-GET-ADVISORY   
                              THRU CCP10-ADV-EXIT   
                           MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE   
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE   (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-TEXT   
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT   (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE 'A' 
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-APPROVED-MESSAGE   
                        END-IF  
                     ELSE   
                        IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IC' OR 'IM' 
                           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                           MOVE 0062       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-APPROVED-MESSAGE   
                        ELSE
                           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                           MOVE 0112       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-APPROVED-MESSAGE   
                        END-IF  
                     END-IF 
                 END-IF 
               ELSE 
                   IF WS-DENIED-MESSAGE NOT = 'Y'   
                     IF SSP03-O-GIFT-CARD-FLG = 1   
                        IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IC' OR 'IM' 
                           MOVE 0106       TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE 
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO CCP00-ADVISE-SOURCE   
                           PERFORM CCP10-GET-ADVISORY   
                              THRU CCP10-ADV-EXIT   
                           MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE   
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE   (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-TEXT   
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT   (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE 'D' 
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-DENIED-MESSAGE 
                        ELSE
                           MOVE 0113       TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE 
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO CCP00-ADVISE-SOURCE   
                           PERFORM CCP10-GET-ADVISORY   
                              THRU CCP10-ADV-EXIT   
                           MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE   
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-CODE   (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-TEXT   
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-TEXT   (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE 'D' 
                                TO VLADV2-ADVISORY-STATUS (CCP78-CC-SUB)
                           MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-DENIED-MESSAGE 
                        END-IF  
                     ELSE   
                        IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IC' OR 'IM' 
                           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                           MOVE 0106       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-DENIED-MESSAGE 
                        ELSE
                           ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                           MOVE 0113       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE  
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                           MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-DENIED-MESSAGE 
                        END-IF  
                     END-IF 
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
            END-IF. 
           GO TO 7560-PROCESS-GIFT-CARDS.   

       7570-VERIFY-PAYMENT. 

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'  
              AND ALL-CARD-DENIED   
              AND BKPUCC-SEGMENT(1) > SPACES
                  PERFORM 8600-CANCEL-BOOKING   
                          THRU 8600-CANCEL-BOOKING-EXIT 
                  PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT   
                  IF SSP03-O-GIFT-CARD-FLG = 1  
                     PERFORM CCP78-OUTPUT-CCNEWBK2  
                           THRU CCP78-OUTPUT-CCNEWBK2-EXIT  
                     GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB  
                  ELSE  
                     ADD 1      TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                     MOVE 0106  TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                     MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO 
                                    MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                     PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG  
                           THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
                     GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB  
                  END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND 
               SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1)
               GO TO 7600-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           IF  SSP03-O-GIFT-CARD-FLG = 1 AND
               BKPUCC-SEGMENT(1) > SPACES AND   
               BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (1) NOT = 'N'
               ADD 1           TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
               MOVE 0144       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
               MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)   
           END-IF.  

       7600-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       7700-SET-UP-GRP-HIST.

           MOVE  SPACES               TO GRP15-HISTORY-INFORMATION. 
           MOVE  'G'                  TO GRP15-TYPE.

           MOVE ARP27-PMT-HIST-CODE   TO GRP15-ACTIVITY-CODE.   

           ADD   1                    TO GRP15-ITEM-NUM.
           MOVE  INFO-USERID          TO GRP15-USERID.  
           IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IA' OR 'UA' OR 'AU'  
              MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID     TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID 
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES             TO GRP15-GOCCL-ID 
           END-IF.  
           MOVE  ZERO                             TO GRP15-PAX-SEQ-NUM. 
           MOVE  WS-ARP38-TOT-PAYMENT-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB)  
                                                  TO GRP15-AMT. 
           MOVE  RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM  OF RSP06-NEW TO GRP15-IBR-BOOK-NUM.
           MOVE  RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO GRP15-CABIN-NUM.   
           MOVE 'PENDING'             TO GRP15-AGENCY-CONTACT.  

           MOVE WS-HIST-COMMENT           TO GRP15-COMMENT. 
           MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE     TO GRP15-LOCATION-CODE.   
           MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE  TO GRP15-AGENCY-CONNECT-CODE. 

           PERFORM GRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY THRU GRP15-GTRF-EXIT.   
           MOVE GBHR1-REC-SEQ-NUM     TO WS-GBHR1-REC-SEQ-NUM   
           MOVE GBHR1-ENTRY-CNT       TO WS-GBHR1-ENTRY-CNT.

           MOVE WS-AGT-CONTACT        TO GRP15-AGENCY-CONTACT.  

           MOVE ARP27-PAYER-HIST-CODE  TO GRP15-ACTIVITY-CODE.  

           MOVE ZEROS                 TO GRP15-AMT. 
           MOVE SPACES                TO GRP15-COMMENT. 
           STRING 'CH ' BKPUCC-CARD-HOLDER-NAME (BKPUCC-SUB)
                  DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO GRP15-COMMENT. 

           PERFORM GRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY THRU GRP15-GTRF-EXIT.   

       7700-EXIT.   
           EXIT.
      *=================================================================
       7800-SET-UP-INDV-HISTORY.

           MOVE INFO-USERID             TO DPP04-HIST-OPER-INITIAL. 

           MOVE ARP27-PMT-HIST-CODE TO DPP04-HIST-ACTIVITY-TYPE.

           MOVE  WS-ARP38-TOT-PAYMENT-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB)  
                                                  TO WS-INDV-HIST-AMT.  
           MOVE  WS-INDV-HIST-AMT  TO DPP04-HIST-AMT
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
             TO DPP04-HIST-CABIN-NUM.   

           MOVE 'PENDING'               TO DPP04-HIST-CONTACT.  

           MOVE WS-HIST-COMMENT         TO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT.  

           MOVE ZERO                    TO DPP04-HIST-PAX-NUM   
                                           DPP04-HIST-PAX-SEQ-NUM.  
           MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE TO DPP04-HIST-AGENCY-CONN-CODE.
           MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE    TO DPP04-TRF-LOCATION-CODE.

           PERFORM IRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY THRU IRP15-TRF-EXIT.

           MOVE IBHR-RECORD-SEQ-NUM    TO WS-IBHR-REC-SEQ-NUM.  
           MOVE ITRF-BOOK-HIST-SUB     TO WS-IBHR-ENTRY-CNT.

           MOVE WS-AGT-CONTACT          TO DPP04-HIST-CONTACT.  

           MOVE ARP27-PAYER-HIST-CODE   TO DPP04-HIST-ACTIVITY-TYPE.

           MOVE SPACES                  TO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT.  
           MOVE ZEROS                   TO DPP04-HIST-AMT.  

           STRING 'CH ' BKPUCC-CARD-HOLDER-NAME (BKPUCC-SUB)
                  DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT.

           PERFORM IRP15-CREATE-BOOKING-HISTORY THRU IRP15-HISTORY-EXIT.

       7800-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       7850-BUILD-HISTORY.  

           MOVE LOW-VALUES               TO DPP04-TRF-COMMENTS  
                                            DPP04-HISTORY-INFORMATION.  

           MOVE INFO-USERID              TO DPP04-HIST-OPER-INITIAL.

           IF MSGHDR-USER-NAME > SPACES 
              MOVE MSGHDR-USER-NAME      TO DPP04-HIST-CONTACT  
           ELSE 
               MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID       TO DPP04-HIST-CONTACT  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW
             TO DPP04-TRF-SAIL-DATE.

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW
             TO DPP04-TRF-SHIP-CODE.

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW
             TO DPP04-TRF-DURATION-DAYS.

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
             TO DPP04-HIST-CABIN-NUM.   

           MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE    TO DPP04-TRF-LOCATION-CODE.
           MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE TO DPP04-HIST-AGENCY-CONN-CODE.

       7850-BUILD-HISTORY-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

TOKENX 7900-SET-UP-DATA-FOR-ARO39PAY.   
           INITIALIZE                      ARP39-CO-INPUT-DATA  
                                           ARP39-OUTPUT-MESSAGE.

           MOVE ZEROES                      TO ARP39-PROCESSING-CODE.   

           MOVE ARP27-PYMT-PMT-TYPE          TO ARP39-PAYMENT-TYPE. 
           MOVE TDP01-FINANCIAL-COMPANY-CODE TO 
                                         ARP39-FIN-COMPANY-CODE.

           IF  BKPUCC-TOKEN-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB) > SPACES   
               MOVE 1                        TO ARP39-ATCR-IND  
           ELSE 
               MOVE ARP27-INDICATORS         TO ARP39-ATCR-IND. 
           MOVE MSGHDR-SESSION-ID            TO ARP39-CCL-SESSION-ID.   

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'  
              IF SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1   
                  IF  SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1  
                      IF  MSGHDR-SYSTEM-ID = 'CUK'  
                          MOVE 'NCU' TO ARP39-SOURCE-ID 
                      ELSE  
                          MOVE 'NCC' TO ARP39-SOURCE-ID 
                      END-IF
                  ELSE  
                      IF  MSGHDR-SYSTEM-ID = 'BUK'  
                          MOVE 'NBU' TO ARP39-SOURCE-ID 
                      ELSE  
                          MOVE 'NGC' TO ARP39-SOURCE-ID 
                      END-IF
                  END-IF
              ELSE  
                  MOVE 'NAP' TO ARP39-SOURCE-ID 
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              IF SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1   
                  IF  SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 1  
                      IF  MSGHDR-SYSTEM-ID = 'CUK'  
                          MOVE 'CCU' TO ARP39-SOURCE-ID 
                      ELSE  
                          MOVE 'CCC' TO ARP39-SOURCE-ID 
                      END-IF
                  ELSE  
                      IF  MSGHDR-SYSTEM-ID = 'BUK'  
                          MOVE 'CBU' TO ARP39-SOURCE-ID 
                      ELSE  
                          MOVE 'CGC' TO ARP39-SOURCE-ID 
                      END-IF
                  END-IF
              ELSE  
                  MOVE 'CAP' TO ARP39-SOURCE-ID 
              END-IF
            END-IF. 

           MOVE BKPUCC-CREATE-VAULT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB)
                                             TO ARP39-CREATE-VAULT-IND. 
           MOVE BKPUCC-PMT-TOKEN-ID-TYPE (BKPUCC-SUB)   
                                             TO ARP39-PMT-TOKEN-ID-TYPE.
           MOVE BKPUCC-PMT-TOKEN-ID  (BKPUCC-SUB)   
                                             TO ARP39-PMT-TOKEN-ID. 

           MOVE BKPUCC-PINNABLE-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) TO ARP39-PINNABLE-IND. 
           MOVE BKPUCC-TRANSACTION-ID (BKPUCC-SUB)  
                                      TO ARP39-TRANSACTION-ID.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW
                                        TO ARP39-BOOK-TYPE  
                                           ARP39-PRODUCT-TYPE.  

           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE      OF RSP06-NEW = 'I'   
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM  OF RSP06-NEW TO ARP39-BOOK-NUM   
                                                      ARP39-PRODUCT-NUM 
              MOVE WS-IBHR-REC-SEQ-NUM     TO ARP39-BK-HIST-SEQ-NUM 
              MOVE WS-IBHR-ENTRY-CNT       TO ARP39-BK-HIST-SUB 
           ELSE 
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-GROUP-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                                           TO ARP39-BOOK-NUM
                                              ARP39-PRODUCT-NUM 
                                              ARP39-GROUP-BOOK-NUM  
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM  OF RSP06-NEW 
                                           TO ARP39-GUESTDATA-BOOK-NUM  
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                                           TO ARP39-GUESTDATA-CABIN-NUM 
              MOVE WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM          TO ARP39-GUESTDATA-SEQ-NUM   
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW   
                                      TO ARP39-GUESTDATA-CABIN-SEQ-NUM  
              MOVE WS-GBHR1-REC-SEQ-NUM    TO ARP39-BK-HIST-SEQ-NUM 
              MOVE WS-GBHR1-ENTRY-CNT      TO ARP39-BK-HIST-SUB 
           END-IF.  

           IF (BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) > SPACES)   
               IF BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) = 'X'
                  MOVE RSP06-IBR-AUTOPAY-REQUEST-CODE OF RSP06-NEW  
                                        TO ARP39-AUTO-PAY-IND   
               ELSE 
                  MOVE BKPUCC-AUTO-PAYMENT-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) 
                                        TO ARP39-AUTO-PAY-IND   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW
                                        TO ARP39-SHIP-CODE. 
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW
                                        TO ARP39-SAIL-DATE. 
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW
                                        TO ARP39-DURATION-DAYS. 
           MOVE WS-LMN-CODE             TO ARP39-LMN-CODE.  
           MOVE SPACES                  TO ARP39-I-PROCESS-ID.  
           MOVE 'C'                     TO ARP39-TRANSACTION-TYPE.  
           MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB)
                                        TO ARP39-CARD-NUM   
                                           WS-CARD-NUM. 
           MOVE BKPUCC-TOKEN-NUM (BKPUCC-SUB)   
                                        TO ARP39-TOKEN-NUM  
                                           WS-CARD-NUM. 
           IF WS-CARD-NUM-1 = '3'   
              MOVE 'N'                  TO ARP39-AMEX-EXT-PMT-IND.  

           MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-EXP-MONTH (BKPUCC-SUB)  
                                        TO ARP39-EXP-DATE-MM
                                           WS-EXP-DATE-MM.  

           MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-EXP-YEAR (BKPUCC-SUB)   
                                        TO ARP39-EXP-DATE-YY
                                           WS-EXP-DATE-YY.  

           MOVE BKPUCC-PAYMENT-AMT (BKPUCC-SUB) 
                                        TO ARP39-PAYMENT-AMT.   

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CURRENCY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW
                                        TO ARP39-CURRENCY-CODE. 

LOYLTY     IF ARP39-CURRENCY-CODE = 'USD'   
LOYLTY        MOVE ARP39-PAYMENT-AMT    TO ARP39-PAYMENT-AMT-USD-EQ 
LOYLTY     ELSE 
LOYLTY        IF ARP39-PAYMENT-TYPE = 'LPP' 
LOYLTY           MOVE ARP39-PAYMENT-AMT    TO ARP50-IN-AMT  
LOYLTY           MOVE ARP39-CURRENCY-CODE  TO ARP50-CURR-CODE   
LOYLTY           MOVE 'FX'                 TO ARP50-PAYMENT-FUNCTION
LOYLTY           PERFORM ARP50-CALCULATE-EQUIVALENT-AMT 
LOYLTY              THRU ARP50-EXIT 
LOYLTY           IF ARP50-ERROR-NUM > 0 
LOYLTY               MOVE ARP39-PAYMENT-AMT TO ARP39-PAYMENT-AMT-USD-EQ 
LOYLTY           END-IF 
LOYLTY           MOVE ARP50-OUT-AMT         TO ARP39-PAYMENT-AMT-USD-EQ 
LOYLTY        ELSE  
LOYLTY           MOVE ARP39-PAYMENT-AMT     TO ARP39-PAYMENT-AMT-USD-EQ 
LOYLTY        END-IF
LOYLTY     END-IF.  

           MOVE SPACES                  TO WS-STRING-AREA.  
           MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-HOLDER-NAME (BKPUCC-SUB)
                                        TO WS-STRING-AREA.  

           MOVE FUNCTION UPPER-CASE (WS-STRING-AREA) TO 
                                BKPUCC-CARD-HOLDER-NAME (BKPUCC-SUB).   

           UNSTRING BKPUCC-CARD-HOLDER-NAME (BKPUCC-SUB)
                    DELIMITED BY SPACE  
                    INTO WS-FIRST-NAME WS-LAST-NAME.

           MOVE WS-FIRST-NAME           TO ARP39-CARD-HOLDER-FIRST-NAME.
           MOVE WS-LAST-NAME            TO ARP39-CARD-HOLDER-LAST-NAME. 
           MOVE BKPUCC-HOLDER-ZIP-CODE (BKPUCC-SUB) TO ARP39-9DIGIT-ZIP.
           MOVE BKPUCC-HOLDER-ADDRESS1 (BKPUCC-SUB) TO  
                                          ARP39-CARD-HOLDER-ADDRESS.

           MOVE SPACES                  TO ARP39-CID-NUM.   
           IF BKPUCC-CID (BKPUCC-SUB) > SPACES  
              MOVE BKPUCC-CID (BKPUCC-SUB) TO ARP39-CID-NUM 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE ARP39-ECOMMERCE-ORDER   TO ARP39-VPM-TRANS-TYPE.
           MOVE SPACES                  TO WS-TRANS-CODE.   

           MOVE INFO-PROG-APP             TO   DPP90-CURR-TXN-CODE. 
           MOVE 'AR'                      TO   DPP90-PASS-OFF-TXN-CODE. 
           PERFORM DPP90-FIND-TRANS-CODE  THRU DPP90-EXIT.  
           MOVE DPP90-PASS-OFF-TXN-CODE   TO   WS-TRANS-APP.

           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE     OF RSP06-NEW = 'I'
              MOVE 'IVU'                TO WS-TRANS-ID  
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'UGVU'               TO WS-TRANS-ID  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE MSGHDR-SOURCE-IP-ADDRESS    TO ARP39-IP-ADDRESS.

           MOVE WS-TRANS-CODE           TO ARP39-PASSBACK-TRANS-ID. 

           MOVE 'Y'                     TO ARP39-SAME-SCHEMA.   
           MOVE INFO-USERID             TO ARP39-OPER-INITIALS. 

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-DIRECT-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
                                        TO ARP39-DIRECT-BK-IND. 

           IF  BKPUCC-DIGITAL-WALLET-IND (BKPUCC-SUB) > SPACES  
               MOVE BKPUCC-DIGITAL-WALLET-IND (BKPUCC-SUB)  
                 TO ARP39-DIGITAL-WALLET-IND
           END-IF.  

TOKEN3     IF ARP39-OPER-INITIALS = '@IC' AND   
TOKEN3        (ARP39-CURRENCY-CODE = 'EUR' OR 'GBP') AND
TOKEN3         ARP39-PAYMENT-TYPE = 'UCC'   
TOKEN3         MOVE 2              TO DPP01-MESSAGE-SW  
TOKEN3         MOVE 'EUR GBP PAYMENT NOT ALLOWED' TO DPP01-USER-MESSAGE 
TOKEN3         ADD 1               TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
TOKEN3         MOVE 0106           TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
TOKEN3         MOVE 'CCR08UGC'    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
TOKEN3         PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG
TOKEN3                          THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT 
TOKEN3         MOVE 'Y'            TO WS-DENIED-MESSAGE 
TOKEN3         GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB.

       8400-FIND-A-SENIOR.  

           MOVE 0                       TO WS-SR-SUB.   
           MOVE 'N'                     TO WS-SR-CTZ-FLAG.  

       COPY SSP05INT.   

           MOVE TDP01-COMPANY-CODE   TO SSP05-I-COMPANY-CODE.   

           CALL 'SSC05GCD' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 SSP05-INPUT-PARAMS 
                                 SSP05-OUTPUT-PARAMS.   

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > 0 AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1
              GO TO 9500-RETURN-SUBROUTINE-ERROR.   

       8400-FIND-A-SENIOR-CONT. 

           ADD 1 TO WS-SR-SUB.  

           IF WS-SR-SUB > 5 
              GO TO 8400-FIND-A-SENIOR-EXIT 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE BKGINF-AGE (WS-SR-SUB)  
                                        TO CCMV-PAX-AGE-X.  
           MOVE BKGINF-AGE-AMOUNT  (WS-SR-SUB)  
                                        TO CCMV-PAX-AGE.
           MOVE BKGINF-AGE-CODE    (WS-SR-SUB)  
                                        TO CCMV-AGE-CODE.   
           PERFORM CCP75-DETERMINE-SEX-AGE THRU CCP75-DSA-EXIT. 
           MOVE CCMV-PAX-AGE
                                        TO WS-BKGINF-AGE (WS-SR-SUB).   

           IF  BKGACT-ACTION-CODE (WS-SR-SUB) NOT  = 'D'
                  IF CCMV-AGE-CODE NOT = 'M'
                      ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
                      MOVE 0081          TO 
                           MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                      GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
                   END-IF   
           END-IF   

           GO TO 8400-FIND-A-SENIOR-CONT.   

       8400-FIND-A-SENIOR-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

       8500-CALC-PAYMENT-DATE.  

           MOVE DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE       TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE. 
           MOVE ZEROES                  TO WS-BUS-DAY-CNT.  

           PERFORM UNTIL WS-BUS-DAY-CNT >  SSP05-O-CONN-OTH-PAY-CUTOFF  
               PERFORM DPP10-YYYYMMDD-WEEK-DAY  
               IF  DPP02-DAY-ABBV (DPP02-TEMP-DATE) NOT = 'SAT' AND 
                                                          'SUN' 
                   ADD 1                TO WS-BUS-DAY-CNT   
               END-IF   
               MOVE 1                   TO DPP02-NUMBER-DAYS
               PERFORM DPP10-ADVANCE-YYYYMMDD-DATE  
           END-PERFORM. 

           MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE     TO WS-OTHER-PAY-DATE.   

           MOVE DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE       TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE. 
           MOVE SSP05-O-CONN-CHECK-CUTOFF-DAYS  
                                        TO DPP02-NUMBER-DAYS.   
           PERFORM DPP10-ADVANCE-YYYYMMDD-DATE. 
           MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE     TO WS-CHECK-PAY-DATE.   

       8500-CALC-PAYMENT-DATE-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

       8600-CANCEL-BOOKING. 

           COPY RSP24INT.   

           INITIALIZE DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS. 
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                                        TO RSP24-I-BOOK-NUM.
           MOVE 'C'                     TO RSP24-I-CANCEL-ACTION.   
           MOVE 'N'                     TO RSP24-I-PENALIZE-CABIN-IND.  
           MOVE RSP50-I-CONNECTION-CODE TO RSP24-I-CONNECTION-CODE. 
           MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID          TO DPP01-USERID,
                                           RSP24-I-USERID.  

           STRING '@' SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE DELIMITED BY SIZE   
             INTO DPP01-3CHAR-USERID.   

           IF SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE = 'IA' OR 'UA' OR 'AU'  
              MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID      TO RSP24-I-GOCCL-ID  
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES              TO RSP24-I-GOCCL-ID  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'N'                     TO RSP24-I-CANCEL-GROUP-IND.
           MOVE WS-AGT-CONTACT          TO RSP24-I-AGENT-CONTACT.   
           MOVE 'B'                     TO RSP24-I-CANCEL-REASON-CODE.  
           MOVE 'API CANCELLATION'      TO RSP24-I-CANCEL-REASON-TEXT.  
           MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE   TO RSP24-I-LOCATION-CODE.   

           MOVE 'N'                     TO RSP24-I-MASS-CXF-CDT-IND.

           CALL 'RSO24CGC' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 RSP24-CANCEL-CABIN-INPUT   
                                 RSP24-CANCEL-CABIN-OUTPUT  
                                 RSP24-CANCEL-CABIN-OUTPUT. 

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR        > ZERO AND   
              DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1  
              ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) 
                TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX  (1) 
                TO CCP00-ADVISE-CODE
              MOVE CCP00-ADVISE-CODE
                TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
              PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG 
                 THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT
              GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB 
           END-IF.  

       8600-CANCEL-BOOKING-EXIT.
           EXIT.

       8800-CONVERT-CASE.   

           MOVE 1 TO WS-STRING-SUB. 
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-STRING-SUB > WS-STRING-LENGTH   
              EVALUATE WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB)   
                WHEN 'a' MOVE 'A' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'b' MOVE 'B' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'c' MOVE 'C' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'd' MOVE 'D' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'e' MOVE 'E' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'f' MOVE 'F' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'g' MOVE 'G' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'h' MOVE 'H' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'i' MOVE 'I' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'j' MOVE 'J' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'k' MOVE 'K' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'l' MOVE 'L' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'm' MOVE 'M' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'n' MOVE 'N' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'o' MOVE 'O' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'p' MOVE 'P' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'q' MOVE 'Q' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'r' MOVE 'R' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 's' MOVE 'S' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 't' MOVE 'T' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'u' MOVE 'U' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'v' MOVE 'V' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'w' MOVE 'W' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'x' MOVE 'X' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'y' MOVE 'Y' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN 'z' MOVE 'Z' TO WS-STRING-CHAR (WS-STRING-SUB) 
                WHEN OTHER  CONTINUE
              END-EVALUATE  
              ADD 1 TO WS-STRING-SUB
           END-PERFORM. 

       8800-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       8850-FETCH-CURRENCY. 

           PERFORM DPP10-ACCEPT-SYSTEM-DATE-TIME.   

           COPY RSP94INT.   

           MOVE TDP01-BASE-CURRENCY-CODE TO RSP94-I-BASE-CURRENCY-CODE. 
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CURRENCY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW TO 
                RSP94-I-CURRENCY-CODE.  

           CALL 'RSC94GCD' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
                                 RSP94-INPUT-PARAMS 
                                 RSP94-OUTPUT-PARAMS.   


           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > ZERO AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1 
              GO TO 9500-RETURN-SUBROUTINE-ERROR.

       8855-NO-CITY-NAME.   
           IF  ERROR-NUM = '0013' OR '0007' 
               ADD 1               TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
               MOVE 0149           TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
               MOVE 'CCR08UGC'     TO   
                        MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
               PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG
                                   THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
               GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
           ELSE 
               GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR
           END-IF.  

      *=================================================================
       8400-CHECK-TRAVEL-WITH-LOOP. 
           IF (BKDINP-DINING-WITH-BOOK-NUM (WS-BKG-SUB) NOT > SPACES)   
            OR (BKDINP-DINING-WITH-BOOK-NUM (WS-BKG-SUB) = '000000')
               GO TO 8400-EXIT. 

           MOVE BKDINP-DINING-WITH-BOOK-NUM (WS-BKG-SUB)
             TO IBR-BOOK-NUM
                WS-BOOK-NUM.
           MOVE 'A' TO IBR-BOOK-TYPE.   
           PERFORM IRP66-FETCH-IBR THRU IRP66-FETCH-IBR-EXIT.   
           IF IRP66-ERROR-NUM > 0   
              MOVE 2 TO DPP01-MESSAGE-SW
              MOVE IRP66-ERROR-MESSAGE (IRP66-ERROR-NUM)
                   TO DPP01-USER-MESSAGE
                      DPP42-HOLD-MSG-DESC   
              GO TO 8400-INVALID-TRAVEL-WITH.   

           IF IBR-CANCEL-DATE > ZERO
              GO TO 8400-INVALID-TRAVEL-WITH.   

           IF IBR-SAIL-DATE = WS-INP-SAIL-DATE AND  
              IBR-SHIP-CODE = WS-INP-SHIP-CODE AND  
              IBR-DURATION-DAYS = WS-INP-SAIL-DUR-DAYS  
               NEXT SENTENCE
           ELSE 
              GO TO 8400-INVALID-TRAVEL-WITH.   

           PERFORM 7500-CALL-RSO23-FOR-TW THRU 7500-EXIT.   

           IF RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-OLD (1)  > SPACE   
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-OLD (1) TO
                                             WS-TW-DINING-CODE  
           ELSE 
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-OLD (1) TO 
                                            WS-TW-DINING-CODE   
           END-IF.  
           IF WS-TW-DINING-CODE = WS-NEWBK-DINING-CODE  
               NEXT SENTENCE
           ELSE 
               IF  WS-DIN-ADV-SHOWN = 'Y'   
                   NEXT SENTENCE
               ELSE 
                   ADD 1      TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                   MOVE 'Y'   TO WS-DIN-ADV-SHOWN   
                   MOVE 0115  TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE   (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                   MOVE 'CCR08UGC'  
                              TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB).   

           MOVE BKDINP-DINING-WITH-BOOK-NUM (WS-BKG-SUB)
                              TO WS-INP-BOOK-NUM(WS-BKG-SUB)
                                 WS01-BOOK-NUM(WS-BKG-SUB)  
           IF RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-OLD (1)  > SPACE   
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-OLD (1) TO
                           WS-INP-PAX-DINING-PREF (WS-BKG-SUB)  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-OLD (1) TO
                           WS-INP-PAX-DINING-CNF  (WS-BKG-SUB). 

           GO TO 8400-EXIT. 

       8400-INVALID-TRAVEL-WITH.

           ADD 1        TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
           MOVE 0054    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
           GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.
