           EXIT.
      *=================================================================
       7500-CALL-RSO23-FOR-TW.  
       COPY RSP23INT.   

           MOVE WS-BOOK-NUM            TO RSP23-I-INDV-BOOK-NUM.
           MOVE 'A'                    TO RSP23-I-BOOK-TYPE.
           MOVE ZERO                   TO DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR.
           MOVE MSGHDR-USER-ID         TO DPP01-USERID. 
           MOVE 'Y'                    TO RSP23-I-READ-IBR-IND  
                                          RSP23-I-READ-IFR-IND  
                                          RSP23-I-READ-I-PIAR-IND   
                                          RSP23-I-READ-I-PIER-IND   
                                          RSP23-I-READ-PIER-IND 
                                          RSP23-I-READ-PAX-IND  
                                          RSP23-I-READ-PTFR-IND 
                                          RSP23-I-READ-PPR-IND  
                                          RSP23-I-READ-TIR-IND  
                                          RSP23-I-READ-PXFR-IND 
                                          RSP23-I-READ-PIAR-IND 
                                          RSP23-I-READ-PIID-IND 
                                          RSP23-I-READ-PICR-IND 
                                          RSP23-I-READ-PPIR-IND 
                                          RSP23-I-READ-CIF-IND  
                                          RSP23-I-READ-I-TOAR-IND   
                                          RSP23-I-READ-POBC-IND 
                                          RSP23-I-READ-IBPIR-IND
                                          RSP23-I-READ-PICF-IND 
                                          RSP23-I-READ-PICC-IND 
                                          RSP23-I-READ-CSSXR-IND
                                          RSP23-I-READ-GST-OFR-IND  
                                          RSP23-I-READ-GST-OFR-QUL-IND  
                                          RSP23-I-READ-PPHOR-IND.   

           CALL 'RSO23RGC'        USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS
                                        DPP01-COMMON-INFO   
                                        RSP23-RGC-INPUT 
                                        RSP06-OLD.  

           IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > 0 AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1
              GO TO DPP12-RETURN-SUBROUTINE-ERROR.  

       7500-EXIT.   
            EXIT.   
      *=================================================================
       7600-SEND-MESSAGES.  

           IF  BKCRV1-GRP-RPTS-SEC-EMAIL > SPACES OR
               BKCRV1-GRP-RPTS-SEC-FAX   > ZEROS
               MOVE BKCRV1-GRP-RPTS-SEC-EMAIL   
                           TO MSMQ-EMAIL-ADD
                              WS-EMAIL-ADD  
               MOVE BKCRV1-GRP-RPTS-SEC-FAX 
                           TO MSMQ-FAX-NUM  
               IF  WS-EMAIL-ADD > SPACES
                   MOVE 'E' 
                           TO MSMQ-DELIVERY-IND 
               ELSE 
                   MOVE 'F' 
                           TO MSMQ-DELIVERY-IND 
               END-IF   
               MOVE LOW-VALUES  
                           TO DPP01-SEND-CLEAR  
               PERFORM DPP12-SEND-MSMQ-MESSAGE THRU 
                       DPP12-SEND-MSMQ-MESSAGE-EXIT 
           ELSE 
               PERFORM VARYING WS-EMAIL-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                   UNTIL WS-EMAIL-SUB > RSP49-O-COMMUN-ENTRY-CNT
                   MOVE RSP49-O-C-EMAIL (WS-EMAIL-SUB)  
                           TO MSMQ-EMAIL-ADD
                              WS-EMAIL-ADD  
                   MOVE RSP49-O-C-FAX-NUM (WS-EMAIL-SUB)
                           TO MSMQ-FAX-NUM  
                   IF  WS-EMAIL-ADD > SPACES
                       MOVE 'E' 
                           TO MSMQ-DELIVERY-IND 
                   ELSE 
                       MOVE 'F' 
                           TO MSMQ-DELIVERY-IND 
                   END-IF   
                   MOVE LOW-VALUES  
                           TO DPP01-SEND-CLEAR  
                   PERFORM DPP12-SEND-MSMQ-MESSAGE THRU 
                           DPP12-SEND-MSMQ-MESSAGE-EXIT 
               END-PERFORM  
           END-IF.  

       7600-SEND-MESSAGES-EXIT. 
           EXIT.
      *=================================================================
       8200-CHECK-PPHOR-EXIST.  
           MOVE ZEROES             TO WS-PHONE-TYPE-MOBILE-SUB  
                                      WS-PHONE-TYPE-HOME-SUB.   

           IF  RSP06-PPHOR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) = 'Y'   
               PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                       UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT 
                             OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB )
                  EVALUATE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE   
                      OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-SUB) 
                     WHEN 'C'   
                        MOVE WS-SUB TO WS-PHONE-TYPE-MOBILE-SUB 
                     WHEN 'H'   
                        MOVE WS-SUB TO WS-PHONE-TYPE-HOME-SUB   
                  END-EVALUATE  
               END-PERFORM  
               GO TO 8400-BUILD-PHONE-NUMBERS   
           END-IF.  

       8300-BUILD-RSP06-PPHOR.  

           IF WS-PHONE-NUM > ZEROES OR WS-MOBILE-NUM > ZEROES   
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 8200-CHECK-PPHOR-EXIST-EXIT 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'Y' TO RSP06-PPHOR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW(NEW-PAX-SUB). 

           MOVE RSP06-PAX-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)   
             TO  RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).   

           MOVE RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)
             TO  RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).

           MOVE ZEROES  
             TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB).   

       8400-BUILD-PHONE-NUMBERS.

           IF WS-PHONE-TYPE-HOME-SUB <= ZEROES  
            IF WS-PHONE-NUM > ZEROES
              ADD 1       TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                     (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
              MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                          TO WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT 

              MOVE 'H'    TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW
                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)   

              MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)   

              MOVE WS-PHONE-NUM (5:16)  
                          TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)   

              MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND  
                          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT) 
                             RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-NEW  
                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)   

              MOVE ZEROES TO RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG   
                          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT) 
                             RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG   
                          OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT) 
            END-IF  
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-TYPE-HOME-SUB)   
              IF WS-PHONE-NUM <= ZEROES 
                 IF RSP06-PAX-GUEST-INFO-IND
                                    OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) = 1  
                    ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                    MOVE 0151   
                          TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                    MOVE 'CCR08UGC' 
                          TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                    PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG   
                       THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
                    GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB   
                 END-IF 
                 IF WS-PHONE-TYPE-HOME-SUB =
                      RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                   SUBTRACT 1 FROM RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT
                                            OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                 ELSE   
                   MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT   
                                            OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                          TO WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT 
                   MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TBL OF RSP06-NEW  
                                (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)
                          TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TBL OF RSP06-NEW 
                                (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-TYPE-HOME-SUB)
                   SUBTRACT 1 FROM RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT
                                            OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 MOVE WS-PHONE-NUM (5:16)   
                          TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                                (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-TYPE-HOME-SUB)
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF SSP03-O-CHK-TXT-PREF-FLG NOT = 1  
              GO TO 8200-CHECK-PPHOR-EXIST-EXIT 
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-PHONE-TYPE-MOBILE-SUB <= ZEROES
            IF WS-MOBILE-NUM > ZEROES   
              ADD 1       TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW 
                                                     (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
              MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB) 
                          TO WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT 

              MOVE 'C'    TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW
                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)   

              MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)   
              MOVE WS-MOBILE-NUM (5:16) 
                          TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)   
              MOVE WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT   
                          TO WS-PHONE-SUB   
              PERFORM 8500-BUILD-MOB-PREF-IND   
                 THRU 8500-BUILD-MOB-PREF-IND-EXIT  
            END-IF  
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                             (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-TYPE-MOBILE-SUB) 
              IF WS-MOBILE-NUM <= ZEROES
                 IF WS-PHONE-TYPE-MOBILE-SUB =  
                      RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                   SUBTRACT 1 FROM RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT
                                            OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                 ELSE   
                   MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT   
                                            OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                          TO WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT 
                   MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TBL OF RSP06-NEW  
                                (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)
                          TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TBL OF RSP06-NEW 
                                (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-TYPE-MOBILE-SUB)  
                   SUBTRACT 1 FROM RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT
                                            OF RSP06-NEW (NEW-PAX-SUB)  
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 MOVE WS-MOBILE-NUM (5:16)  
                          TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                                (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-TYPE-MOBILE-SUB)  
                 MOVE WS-PHONE-TYPE-MOBILE-SUB  
                          TO WS-PHONE-SUB   
                 PERFORM 8500-BUILD-MOB-PREF-IND
                    THRU 8500-BUILD-MOB-PREF-IND-EXIT   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

       8200-CHECK-PPHOR-EXIST-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

       8500-BUILD-MOB-PREF-IND. 

           MOVE 'N'          TO WS-ALLOW-TEXT-PREF-IND  
           IF RSP06-PAX-RESIDENCE-COUNTRY OF RSP06-NEW  
                                     (NEW-PAX-SUB) > SPACES 
              IF RSP06-PAX-RESIDENCE-COUNTRY OF RSP06-NEW   
                                     (NEW-PAX-SUB) = 'US' OR 'CA'   
                 MOVE 'Y'    TO WS-ALLOW-TEXT-PREF-IND  
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              IF RSP06-PAX-NATIONALITY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW
                                     (NEW-PAX-SUB) = 'US' OR 'CA'   
                 MOVE 'Y'    TO WS-ALLOW-TEXT-PREF-IND  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  
           IF WS-ALLOW-TEXT-PREF-IND = 'N'  
              IF BKGINF-TEXT-PREF-IND (BKGINF-SUB) = 'Y'
                    ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                    MOVE 0160 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                    MOVE 'CCR08UGC' 
                              TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE(CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                    PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG   
                       THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
                    GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB   
              END-IF
              IF BKGINF-SAIL-TEXT-OPT-FLAG (BKGINF-SUB) = 'Y'   
                    ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                    MOVE 0161 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                    MOVE 'CCR08UGC' 
                              TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE(CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                    PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG   
                       THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
                    GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB   
              END-IF
              IF BKGINF-PAYM-TEXT-OPT-FLAG (BKGINF-SUB) = 'Y'   
                    ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                    MOVE 0162 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                    MOVE 'CCR08UGC' 
                              TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE(CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                    PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG   
                       THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
                    GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB   
              END-IF
              MOVE SPACES   
                       TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW
                                    (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-SUB)  
                          RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
                                    (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-SUB)  
              MOVE 0   TO RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
                                    (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-SUB)  
                          RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
                                    (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-SUB)  
             GO TO 8500-BUILD-MOB-PREF-IND-EXIT 
           ELSE 
              IF (BKGINF-PAYM-TEXT-OPT-FLAG (BKGINF-SUB) = 'Y'  OR  
                  BKGINF-SAIL-TEXT-OPT-FLAG (BKGINF-SUB) = 'Y') AND 
                 (BKGINF-TEXT-PREF-IND (BKGINF-SUB) <= SPACES OR = 'N') 
                     ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                     MOVE 0163 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                     MOVE 'CCR08UGC'
                               TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE(CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                     PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG  
                        THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT 
                     GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB  
               END-IF   
              IF BKGINF-PAYM-TEXT-OPT-FLAG (BKGINF-SUB) = 'Y'   
                 IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW NOT = 'I'  
                    ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                    MOVE 0166 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                    MOVE 'CCR08UGC' 
                              TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE(CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                    PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG   
                       THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
                    GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB   
                 END-IF 
                 IF WS-AGENT-DIRECT-IND NOT = 'Y'   
                    ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                    MOVE 0167 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)  
                    MOVE 'CCR08UGC' 
                              TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE(CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                    PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG   
                       THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT  
                    GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB   
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF BKGINF-TEXT-PREF-IND (BKGINF-SUB) = 'Y'   
              MOVE 'Y' TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW
                          (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-SUB)
              IF BKGINF-SAIL-TEXT-OPT-FLAG (BKGINF-SUB) NOT = 'Y' AND   
                 BKGINF-PAYM-TEXT-OPT-FLAG (BKGINF-SUB) NOT = 'Y'   
                 ADD 1    TO CCMV-ADV-SUB   
                 MOVE 0156 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC'
                          TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE(CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                 PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG  
                    THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT 
                 GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB  
              END-IF
              IF BKGINF-SAIL-TEXT-OPT-FLAG (BKGINF-SUB) = 'Y'   
                 MOVE 'Y'   
                       TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
                          (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-SUB)
                 MOVE 1 
                       TO RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
                          (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-SUB)
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 'N'   
                       TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
                          (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-SUB)
                 MOVE 0 
                       TO RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
                          (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-SUB)
              END-IF
             IF BKGINF-PAYM-TEXT-OPT-FLAG (BKGINF-SUB) = 'Y'

                 MOVE 1 
                       TO RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
                          (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-SUB)
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 0 
                       TO RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
                          (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-SUB)
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'N' TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW
                          (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-SUB)
                          RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
                          (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-SUB)
              MOVE 0   TO RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
                          (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-SUB)
                          RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
                          (NEW-PAX-SUB WS-PHONE-SUB)
           END-IF.  

       8500-BUILD-MOB-PREF-IND-EXIT.
           EXIT.

       8600-FIND-CHG-IN-MOB.

           IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'  
              GO TO 8630-FIND-SAME-MOB-NUM  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 0 TO WS-NEW-SUB 
                     WS-PAX-MOB-CNT.
       8610-NEW-NEXT-PAX.   

           ADD 1 TO WS-NEW-SUB  
                    WS-PAX-MOB-CNT. 

           IF WS-NEW-SUB > NEW-PAX-SUB  
              SUBTRACT 1              FROM WS-PAX-MOB-CNT   
              GO TO 8630-FIND-SAME-MOB-NUM  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE SPACES                TO WS-PAX-MOB-TBL (WS-NEW-SUB).   

           IF RSP06-PPHOR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB)= 'N'  
              GO TO 8610-NEW-NEXT-PAX   
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-NEW-PH-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
                   UNTIL WS-NEW-PH-SUB >
                         RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB)
                      OR RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW
                                        (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) = 'C'
                 CONTINUE   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-NEW-PH-SUB >   
                         RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB)
              GO TO 8610-NEW-NEXT-PAX   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 0 TO WS-OLD-SUB.

       8620-OLD-NEXT-PAX.   

           ADD 1 TO WS-OLD-SUB. 

           IF WS-OLD-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD   
              GO TO 8610-NEW-NEXT-PAX   
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PPHOR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)= 'N'  
              IF RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) =   
                 RSP06-PAX-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB) AND   
                 RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) =
                 RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB) 
              END-IF
              GO TO 8620-OLD-NEXT-PAX   
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-OLD-PH-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
                   UNTIL WS-OLD-PH-SUB >
                         RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)
                      OR RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE OF RSP06-OLD
                                        (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB) = 'C'
                 CONTINUE   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-OLD-PH-SUB >   
                         RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)
              IF RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) =   
                 RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB) AND 
                 RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) =
                 RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)  
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB) 
              END-IF
              GO TO 8620-OLD-NEXT-PAX   
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                            (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT =
              RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-OLD
                            (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB)  
              IF RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                                    (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) (7:10) = 
                 RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-OLD 
                                    (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB) (7:10)   
                 AND
                 (RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-OLD
                                    (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB) (1:6) =  
                                                   '000000' OR '000001')
                 AND
                 (RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                                    (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)(1:6) =   
                                                   '000000' OR '000001')
                 CONTINUE   
              ELSE  
                 IF RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) =
                    RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB) AND  
                    RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) = 
                    RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)   
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB)  
                 END-IF 
                 GO TO 8620-OLD-NEXT-PAX
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) =  
              RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB) AND
              RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) =   
              RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB) 
              IF RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW  
                                       (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT = 
                 RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-OLD  
                                       (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB) OR
                 RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW  
                                       (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT = 
                 RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-OLD  
                                       (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB) OR
                 RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-NEW  
                                       (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT = 
                 RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-OLD  
                                       (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB)   
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB) 
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 'N' TO WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB) 
              END-IF
              IF (RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW
                                       (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)   
                                                      = 'Y' OR 'I') AND 
                 (RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-OLD
                                       (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB)   
                                                      = 'Y' OR 'I') 
                 CONTINUE   
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB) 
              END-IF
           END-IF   

           MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-OLD  
                            (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB)  
                  TO WS-OLD-PREF-IND (WS-NEW-SUB).  

           GO TO 8620-OLD-NEXT-PAX. 

       8630-FIND-SAME-MOB-NUM.  

           MOVE 0 TO WS-CUR-SUB.

       8640-NEXT-CURR-PAX.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-CUR-SUB. 

           IF WS-CUR-SUB > NEW-PAX-SUB  
              GO TO 8600-FIND-CHG-IN-MOB-EXIT   
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PPHOR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (WS-CUR-SUB)= 'N'  
              GO TO 8640-NEXT-CURR-PAX  
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-CUR-PH-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
                   UNTIL WS-CUR-PH-SUB >
                         RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-CUR-SUB)
                      OR RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW
                                        (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) = 'C'
                 CONTINUE   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-CUR-PH-SUB >   
                         RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-CUR-SUB)
              GO TO 8640-NEXT-CURR-PAX  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 0 TO WS-NEW-SUB.

       8650-NEXT-NEW-PAX.   

           ADD 1 TO WS-NEW-SUB. 

           IF WS-NEW-SUB > NEW-PAX-SUB  
              GO TO 8660-UPDATE-PREF-IND
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PPHOR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB)= 'N'  
              GO TO 8650-NEXT-NEW-PAX   
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-NEW-SUB = WS-CUR-SUB   
              GO TO 8650-NEXT-NEW-PAX   
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-NEW-PH-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
                   UNTIL WS-NEW-PH-SUB >
                         RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB)
                      OR RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW
                                        (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) = 'C'
                 CONTINUE   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-NEW-PH-SUB >   
                         RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB)
              GO TO 8650-NEXT-NEW-PAX   
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                            (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) NOT =
              RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                            (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)  
              IF RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                                    (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) (7:10) = 
                 RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) (7:10)   
                 AND
                 (RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) (1:6) =  
                                                   '000000' OR '000001')
                 AND
                 (RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                                    (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)(1:6) =   
                                                   '000000' OR '000001')
                 CONTINUE   
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 8650-NEXT-NEW-PAX
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
                                    (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT =
              RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) OR   
              RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
                                    (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT =
              RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) OR   
              RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
                                    (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT =
              RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DIFF-PREF-IND  
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'N' TO WS-DIFF-PREF-IND  
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW
                                    (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT =
              RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW
                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
              IF (RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW
                                    (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)  
                                                      NOT = 'Y' AND 'I')
                 OR (RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
                                                      NOT = 'Y' AND 'I')
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DIFF-PREF-IND   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  


           IF WS-DIFF-PREF-IND = 'Y'
              IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCNEWBK1'   
                 ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                 MOVE 0168 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC'
                           TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE(CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                 PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG  
                    THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT 
                 GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB  
              END-IF
              IF (WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-CUR-SUB) = 'Y' AND  
                  WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB) = 'Y') 
                 ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                 MOVE 0169 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
                 MOVE 'CCR08UGC'
                           TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE(CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                 PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG  
                    THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT 
                 GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB  
              END-IF
              IF (WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-CUR-SUB) = 'N' AND  
                  WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB) = 'Y') 
                 MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW
                                          (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)
                    TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
                                          (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)
                 MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
                                          (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)
                    TO RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW
                                          (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)
                 MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
                                          (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)
                    TO RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW
                                          (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)
                 MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
                                          (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)
                    TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-NEW
                                          (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 8650-NEXT-NEW-PAX. 

       8660-UPDATE-PREF-IND.
           IF RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW
                                        (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
                                                               <= SPACES
              MOVE SPACES   
                    TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
                                        (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
           ELSE 
              IF RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
                                        (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) = 'N'
                 MOVE 'N'   
                    TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
                                        (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
              ELSE  
                 IF WS-OLD-PREF-IND (WS-CUR-SUB) = 'Y'  
                    MOVE 'Y'
                    TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
                                        (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 'I'
                    TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
                                        (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 8640-NEXT-CURR-PAX.

       8600-FIND-CHG-IN-MOB-EXIT.   
           EXIT.
      *=================================================================
       9800-VALIDATE-REF-NUM.   
TOKEN2     MOVE SPACES TO WS-CHARS. 
P54170     MOVE BKPUCC-PMT-REF-NUM (USUB) TO ARP90-PMT-REF-NUM. 
P54170  
P54170     PERFORM ARP90-FETCH-ACCT-PMT-REF-REC THRU
P54170             ARP90-FETCH-OWNER-PMT-SRC-EXIT   
P54170  
TOKEN      IF ARP90-PMT-TOKEN-NUM > SPACES  
TOKEN         MOVE ARP90-PMT-TOKEN-NUM TO WS-CHARS  
TOKEN      ELSE 
P54170     MOVE ARP90-PMT-ACCOUNT-NUM TO WS-CHARS   
TOKEN      END-IF   
TOKEN2     INSPECT WS-CHARS TALLYING
TOKEN2             WS-UCC-LENGTH FOR CHARACTERS BEFORE SPACE.   
P54170     PERFORM UNTIL WS-CHAR (WS-UCC-LENGTH) NOT = SPACE
P54170             SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-UCC-LENGTH
P54170     END-PERFORM  



TOKEN      IF  BKPUCC-TOKEN-NUM (USUB) > SPACES 
TOKEN          MOVE BKPUCC-TOKEN-NUM (USUB) TO  WS-CHARS
TOKEN      ELSE IF BKPUCC-CARD-NUM (USUB) > SPACES  
162639     MOVE BKPUCC-CARD-NUM (USUB) TO  WS-CHARS 
TOKEN      END-IF   
TOKEN2     END-IF.  

TOKEN2     INSPECT WS-CHARS TALLYING
TOKEN2             WS-UCC-INPLEN FOR CHARACTERS BEFORE SPACE
P54170     PERFORM UNTIL WS-CHAR (WS-UCC-INPLEN) NOT = SPACE
P54170                   SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-UCC-INPLEN  
P54170     END-PERFORM  
TOKEN2*    We will check if only the ref-num is provided we will populate the   
TOKEN2*    input BKPUCC-CARD-NUM field. 

P54170     IF ARP90-PMT-ACCOUNT-NUM (WS-UCC-LENGTH - 3:4) = 
P54170                     WS-CHARS (WS-UCC-INPLEN - 3:4)   
TOKEN2        OR (WS-UCC-INPLEN = 0)
TOKEN         IF  ARP90-PMT-ACCOUNT-NUM  > SPACES   
TOKEN2           MOVE SPACES                TO BKPUCC-CARD-NUM (USUB)   
TOKEN            MOVE ARP90-PMT-TOKEN-NUM   TO BKPUCC-TOKEN-NUM (USUB)  
TOKEN         END-IF
P54170     ELSE 
P54170        ADD 1 TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
P54170        MOVE 0152 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
P54170        GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
P54170     END-IF.  

       9800-VALIDATE-REF-NUM-EXIT.  
           EXIT.

198118 9810-CHECK-BOOK-NUM. 
198118  
198118     IF CCMH-MESSAGE-ID = 'CCCHGBK1'  
198118        IF BKCRV1-BOOK-NUM > SPACES   
198118          MOVE BKCRV1-BOOK-NUM (1:6) TO WS-BKCRV1-BK-NUM  
198118          MOVE BKCRV1-BOOK-NUM (7:8) TO WS-BKCRV1-BKEXT-NUM   
198118          IF WS-BKCRV1-BKEXT-NUM > SPACES 
198118             ADD 1               TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
198118             MOVE 0238           TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE
198118                                    (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
198118             GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR 
198118          END-IF  
198118  
198118          PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB-BOOK FROM 1 BY 1 
198118             UNTIL WS-SUB-BOOK > 6
198118             IF WS-BKCRV1-BK-NUM (WS-SUB-BOOK : 1) = SPACES   
198118                ADD 1         TO CCMV-ADV-SUB 
198118                MOVE 0238     TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
198118                GO TO 8900-PROGRAM-ERROR  
198118             END-IF   
198118          END-PERFORM 
198118        END-IF
198118     END-IF.  
198118  
198118 9810-CHECK-BOOK-NUM-EXIT.
198118     EXIT.


      *=================================================================
       8900-PROGRAM-ERROR.  
           MOVE 'CCR08UGC' TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (CCMV-ADV-SUB).  
           IF CCP45-SECURITY-ID = 'T'   
              MOVE SPACES TO MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID   
           END-IF.  
           PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG
              THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT.  
           GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB.   
      *=================================================================
       9000-END-OF-JOB. 
           GO TO DPP12-END-OF-JOB.  
      *=================================================================
PCI3  * CALL-TKUPD-SUB. 
PCI3  *     INITIALIZE ARP38-TUP-INPUT-DATA.
PCI3  *     MOVE ARP39-TOKEN-NUM TO ARP38T-IN-TOKEN-NUM.
PCI3  *     STRING ARP39-EXP-DATE-YY DELIMITED BY SIZE  
PCI3  *            ARP39-EXP-DATE-MM DELIMITED BY SIZE  
PCI3  *               INTO ARP38T-EXP-DATE-YYMM.
PCI3  *     CALL 'ARO38TUP' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS, 
PCI3  *                           DPP01-COMMON-INFO,
PCI3  *                           ARP38-TUP-INPUT-DATA, 
PCI3  *                           ARP38-TUP-OUTPUT-DATA.
PCI3  *     IF ARP38T-ERROR-NUM > 0 
PCI3  *        DISPLAY 'ARP38T-ERROR-NUM    : ' ARP38T-ERROR-NUM
PCI3  *            UPON PRT WITH NO ADVANCING   
PCI3  *        DISPLAY 'ARP38T-ERROR-MESSAGE: ' ARP38T-ERROR-MESSAGE
PCI3  *            UPON PRT WITH NO ADVANCING   
PCI3  *     END-IF. 
PCI3  * 
PCI3  * CALL-TKUPD-EXIT.
PCI3  *     EXIT.

       9100-DATABASE-ERROR. 
           DISPLAY 'ERROR-STATUS ' ERROR-STATUS        UPON PRINTER.
           DISPLAY 'ERROR-NUM '    ERROR-NUM           UPON PRINTER.
           DISPLAY 'ERROR-RECORD ' ERROR-RECORD        UPON PRINTER.
           DISPLAY 'ERROR-AREA   ' ERROR-AREA          UPON PRINTER.
           DISPLAY 'ERROR-SET '    ERROR-SET           UPON PRINTER.

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-NUMERIC-DISPLAY.   
           DISPLAY 'DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR = '   
                              DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR        UPON PRINTER.
           DISPLAY 'DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) = '
                              DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) UPON PRINTER.
           DISPLAY 'DPP01-SUB-MSG-DESC   (1) = '
                              DPP01-SUB-MSG-DESC   (1) UPON PRINTER.
           MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX   (1) TO WS-NUMERIC-DISPLAY.
           DISPLAY 'DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX      (1) = '
                              WS-NUMERIC-DISPLAY       UPON PRINTER.
           MOVE DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS  (1) TO WS-NUMERIC-DISPLAY.
           DISPLAY 'DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS     (1) = '
                              WS-NUMERIC-DISPLAY       UPON PRINTER.
           MOVE DPP01-SUB-PAX-SEQ-NUM (1) TO WS-NUMERIC-DISPLAY.
           DISPLAY 'DPP01-SUB-PAX-SEQ-NUM    (1) = '
                              WS-NUMERIC-DISPLAY       UPON PRINTER.

           IF RB-ERROR-CODE = '02'  
              ADD 1                     TO WS-DMS-ROLLBACK-CNT  
              IF WS-DMS-ROLLBACK-CNT > 2
                 CONTINUE   
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 1000              TO WS-WAIT-MILLISECONDS 
                 CALL 'ERTWAIT' USING WS-WAIT-MILLISECONDS  
                 GO TO 0100-INIT-DATABASE   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 0064          TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (1).
           MOVE 'CCR08UGC'    TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE (1).  

           IF CCP45-SECURITY-ID = 'T'   
               MOVE SPACES TO MSGHDR-AGENCY-ID  
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG
              THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT.  

           GO TO DPP12-DATABASE-ERROR.  

       9150-RDMS-ERROR. 
           IF SQLSTATE = '40001'
              ADD 1                     TO WS-DMS-ROLLBACK-CNT  
              IF WS-DMS-ROLLBACK-CNT > 2
                 CONTINUE   
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 1000              TO WS-WAIT-MILLISECONDS 
                 CALL 'ERTWAIT' USING WS-WAIT-MILLISECONDS  
                 GO TO 0100-INIT-DATABASE   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           GO TO DPP12-RDMS-ERROR.  

       9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG. 
           PERFORM DPP12-EDIT-ERROR-MSG 
              THRU DPP12-EDIT-ERROR-MSG-EXIT.   

           GO TO DPP12-END-OF-JOB.  

       9210-EDIT-CONTINUE-MSG.  
           PERFORM DPP12-EDIT-ERROR-MSG 
              THRU DPP12-EDIT-ERROR-MSG-EXIT.   

       9300-SEVERE-ERROR-MSG.   
           MOVE 3 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-STATUS. 
           GO TO DPP12-SEVERE-ERROR-MSG.

       9400-INFO-MSG.   
           PERFORM DPP12-INFO-MSG   
              THRU DPP12-INFO-MSG-EXIT. 

       9500-RETURN-SUBROUTINE-ERROR.
           GO TO DPP12-RETURN-SUBROUTINE-ERROR. 

       9600-SAVE-SUBROUTINE-ERRORS. 
           PERFORM DPP12-SAVE-SUBROUTINE-MSGS   
              THRU DPP12-SAVE-SUBROUTINE-MSGS-X.
